kḷpto jyotiṣṭomo'tirātro'ṣoḍaśikaḥ 111
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suta upāsmai gāyatā nara upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ davidyutatyā rucaite āsṛgramindavaḥ pavamānasya te vayaṃ pavamānasya te kava upa pra yanto adhvaraṃ pra vo mitrāya gāyatendrā yāhi citrabhāno indre agnā namo bṛhadvṛśā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ caiḍaṃ ca sauparṇa m̐! rohitakūlīyaṃ ca punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ tisṛṣu samantamekasyāṃ plava ekasyāṃ dairghaśravasamekasyāmiti vā rathaṃtaraṃ tisṛṣu gauṅgavam̐ rauravaṃ triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ ta ekarcāḥ pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca mā cidanyadviśam̐satetyabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyaṃ yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ maukṣaṃ jarābodhīyaṃ pavasvendramaccheti saphasujñāne purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca pro ayāsīditi kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāmāgniṃ vo vṛdhantamiti satrāsāhīyam̐ saubharamudvam̐śīyaṃ caturvi śam̐ stomaḥ 112
upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavamānasya te vayaṃ pavamānasya te kave agna ā yāhi vītaya ityājyāni pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ cāśvaṃ ca pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca taṃ vo dasmamṛtīṣahamityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyam̐ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasam̐hite ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato haririti saphasujñāne kāśītaṃ vai pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ tisṛṣu gaurīvitamekasyāmauṣṇihamokonidhanamekasyāmaudalamekasyāmiti vā sādhraṃ tisṛṣu kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma jyotiṣṭoma stomaḥ 12
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā pavamānasya te kave agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyāni vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca yauktāśvamuttareṣu yaduttaraṃ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti mādhucchandasaṃ ca bhargaśca yaśo vāyāsye vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ cābhi pra vaḥ surādhasamityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyaṃ yastemado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe hāviṣmatamuttareṣu pavasvendramaccheti śaṅku sujñāne ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma sākamaśva mabhrātṛvyo anā tvamityāmahīyavamāṣṭādam̐ṣṭraṃ yaduhovadgoṣṭoma stomaḥ 13
davidyutatyā rucaite asṛgramindavaḥ pavamānasya te kave agnināgniḥ samidhyata ityājyānyuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca vairupaṃ cābhi somāsa āyava iti rauravaṃ ca gautamaṃ cāñjaśca vairūpamagnestriṇidhanaṃ tisro vāca ityaṅgirasām̐ saṃkrośo'ntyo rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cā tvā sahasramā śatamityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyaṃ tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ cāsotā pari ṣiñcata sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaśca sāma sujñānaṃ ca daivodāsaṃ vā sutāso madhumattamā iti gaurīvitaṃ cāndhīgavaṃ ca pavitraṃ ta iti sāmarājamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthānyāyuṣṭoma stomaḥ 14
pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ pavamānasya te kave agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanaditi hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇi pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛcca parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti pṛśni cātharvaṇaṃ cābhīśavaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cohu vā asyeti vāsiṣṭham antyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca yo rājā carṣaṇīnāmityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyaṃ pari priyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca yadīnidhanaṃ tvam̐ hyaṅga daivya soma punāna ūrmiṇeti bṛhatkasujñāne purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāma ca yadūrdhvedaṃ pro ayāsīditi lauśamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma saindhukṣitam̐ saubharaṃ vīṅkam̐ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ vā yadīḍābhiraiḍaṃ goṣṭoma stomaḥ 15
pavamānasya viśvajid yatsoma citramukthyaṃ pavamānasya te kave agne stomaṃ manāmaha iti hoturājyaṃ svānyuttarānyityājyāni arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatraṃ ca yaṇvaṃ cāpatyam̐ santani śākvaravarṇaṃ tānyuttareṣvabhisomāsa āyava iti mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ ca vāṃraṃ cāgnestriṇidhanamabhi tripṛṣṭhamiti sampāntyā rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca tvamindra yaśa asityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyamasāvyam̐śurmadāyeti gayatraṃ ca gauṣūktaṃ cābhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti cyāvanasujñāne daivodāsaṃ vā pavasva vājasātaya iti gaurīvitaṃ ca rayiṣṭhaṃ cāsāvi somo aruṣo vṛṣā haviriti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma yajiṣṭhaṃ tvā vavṛmaha iti sadhyam̐ sāṃvartaṃ mārutamāyuṣṭoma stomaḥ 16
asṛkṣata pra vājina etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavamānasya te kave yamagne pṛtsu martyamiti hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇī ndrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca sakṛdiṣovṛdhīyaṃ kuryān mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍaṃ caukṣṇorandhraṃ triṇidhanamāyāsyam̐ sakṛtsamantaṃ kuryāt sākamukṣa itīhavadvāsiṣṭhamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca pibā sutasya rasina ityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyaṃ pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ caidhmavāhaṃ ca yadihavat sa sunve yo vasūnāṃ taṃ vaḥ sakhāyo madāyeti dīrghasujñāne kāśītaṃ vā somāḥ pavanta indava iti gaurīvitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca yatsvayoni trirasmai sapta dhenavo duduhrira iti marutāṃ dhenvantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma gūrdas traikakubhaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni yadyukthyo'gniṣṭomastveva jyotiṣṭoma stomaḥ 17
evamanye'pi trayo'bhiplavāḥ kartavyāḥ teṣu viśeṣaḥ dvitīye'hani hāviṣmatasya sthāne yauktāśvamuttaram maukṣasya hāviṣmataṃ dvitīye'bhiplave pañcame'hani yaṇvasya sthāne'patyam tṛtīye'bhiplave pañcame'hani mānavātpūrvamvāmraṃ ca mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ ceti pañcame'hani yadahargrāmegeyaṃ santani syān mānavāt pūrvaṃ vāmraṃ syādetaccājāmyarthaṃ tatra vāmram prathamāyāṃ mānavaṃ tṛce caturthe'bhiplave pañcame 'hani yaṇvasya sthāne śākvaravarṇam tatraiva ṣaṣṭhe'hanyāśvasūktasyeṣovṛdhīyaṃ triṇidhanasya samantam bhargayaśasorvīṅkaśuddhāśuddhīyayośca vyavasthāprakāra ukto na prasmartavyaḥ anyatsarvaṃ prathamavat evaṃ caturo'bhiplavānupetya pṛṣṭhyaṃ ṣaḍahamupayantīti tasya kḷptimāha pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā pṛṣṭhyasya yadvyūḍhatvaṃ tad yadi daśarātraprayuktaṃ tatra samūḍho'yaṃ bhavitumarhatyavicchinnatvāt atha pṛṣṭhyaprayuktastato vyūḍha iti saṃdehādvikalpa iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ siddhāntamāha samūḍhastveva daśarātraprayukto vyūha ity bhāvaḥ tathā ca nidānamathāpi vilupto vyūḍhaḥ ṣaḍaha ityādi śrutyantaraṃ ca narte chandomebhyaḥ pṛṣṭhyo vyūhamānaśa iti tasya tasya samūḍhasya kḷptirvakṣyata ityāha tasya kalpa iti prathamamaharāha upāsmai gāyatā nara upoṣu jātamapturaṃ pavamānasya te kava iti prathamasyāhno bahiṣpavamānaṃ pra soma devavītaya ityabhīvarta ekasyāṃ pajramekasyāṃ yaudhājayamekasyām̐ samānamitaraṃ dvitīyasyāhnaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayetyaidamāyāsyamekasyāmabhīvarta ekasyāṃ kāleyamekasyaṃ vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ madhyaṃdinasya samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyasyāhno bhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgaṃ prathamāyāṃ tasyāmevābhīvartaḥ kāleyaṃ dvitīyāyāṃ gautamaṃ tṛtīyāyām̐ samānamitaram 18
caturthasyāhnaḥ pavamāno ajījanatpunāno akramīdabhūti stotrīyānurūpāvagnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanaditi hoturājyaṃ tavāham̐ soma rāraṇetyāṣṭādam̐ṣṭramekasyāmabhīvarta ekasyāṃ kāleyamekasyām̐ somaḥ pavate janitā matīnāmiti janitramantyaṃ madhyaṃdinasya pra ta āśvinīḥ pavamāna dhenava iti lauśamantyamārbhavasya samānamitaraṃ 19

pañcamasyāhnaḥ pavamānasya viśvajidyatsoma citramukthyamiti stotrīyānurūpāvagne stomaṃ manūmaha iti hoturājyam̐ soma u ṣvaṇaḥ sotṛbhiriti mānavānupe tṛcayorvāmramekasyām abhīvarta ekasyāṃ kāleyamekasyāmindurvājī pavate gonyoghā iti saṃpāntyā madhyāṃdinasya pārthasya loke tvāṣṭrīsāma yad dvyanutodaṃ govitpavasveti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśamantyamārbhavasya samānamitaram̐ 192
ṣaṣṭhasyāhno 'sṛkṣata pra vājina etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipa iti stotrīyānurūpau yamagne pṛtsu martyamiti hoturājyaṃ mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyeti svāramaukṣṇorandhraṃ tisṛṣvaiḍamaukṣṇorandhraṃ ekasyāmabhīvarta ekasyāṃ kāleyamekasyāmayā pavā pavasvainā vasūnītīhavadvāsiṣṭhamantyaṃ madhyaṃ dinasya jyotiryajñāsya pavate madhu priyam marutāṃ dhenvantyamārbhavasya samānamitaram̐ samānamitaram 193

caturviṃśaḥ prāyaṇīyaḥ
uktau jyotiṣṭomadvādaśāhau | atha tadupajīvanena kalpakāra ṛksamāmnāye daśarātrānantaram adhītasya brāhmaṇe ca dāśarātrikaviṣṭutisamāmnāyānantaraṃ gāvo vā etat satramāsate tyārabhya adhyāyadvayena vihitasya saṃvatsarasatrasya gavāmayanasyastotraklṛptimadhyāyadvayenāhaklṛpto jyotiṣṭoma ityādinā ||
iha gavāmayanasya ekaṣaṣṭyadhikaṃ śatatrayaṃ sautyānyahāni santi | tāni ca satraprakaraṇe śatarātrānantaramatirātraścaturviṃśaṃ prāyaṇīyamaharityārabhya saṃvatsarabrāhmaṇamityantenānuvākena saṃgṛhya darśitāni | tadyathāatirātraścaturviśaṃ prāyaṇīyamahaścatvāro'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahāḥ | pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ | iti prathamo māsaḥ |
sa dvitīyaḥ | sa tṛtīyaḥ | sa caturthaḥ | sa pañcamaḥ | iti pañca māsāḥ | atha ṣaṣṭhe māsi trayo'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahāḥ | pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaho'bhijit trayaḥ svarasāmāna ityaṣṭāviṃśatirahāni | atirātraścaturviṃśābhyāṃ saha ṣaṣṭho māsa iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | madhye viṣuvān divākīrtyamahaḥ | ātmā vā eṣa saṃvatsarasya yadviṣuvān pakṣāvetāvabhito bhavataḥ iti śruteḥ ||
athottaraḥ pakṣaḥ | trayaḥ svarasāmāno viśvajit pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ trayastriṃśārambhaṇaḥ trayo'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā ityaṣṭāviṃśatirahāni | vratātirātrābhyāṃ saha prathamo māsaḥ | vratātirātrābhyāṃ prathama uttarasminniti vacanāt | pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahastrayastriṃśārambhaṇaścatvāro'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā iti dvitīyo māsaḥ | tathaiva tṛtīyastathaiva caturthastathaiva pañcamaḥ | trayo'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā āyuśca gauśca dve ahanī dvādaśāhasya dvādaśāhāni iti ṣaṣṭho māsaḥ ||
atha mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca | tau ca prathamamāsapūrakāvityuktam | tasyaitasya gavāmayanasya satrabhūtadvādaśāhavat prayogaḥ | uktakāle dīkṣā | brāhmaṇena gavāmayanasyetyādinā dīkṣākālādikamuktam | aupasathyāntaṃ kṛtvā prāyaṇīyamatirātramupayanti | tasya satrasyarddhyādi subrahmaṇyāntaṃ dvādaśāhavat | stotraklṛptimāha

samantādīnāmekarcānāṃ gauṅgavādīnāṃ ca sāmatṛcaḥ | trīnekatṛce nānāstotrīyāsviti vacanāt | atraivaṃbhūtāyā ṛksāmaklṛpternidānakāreṇa darśitamatha khalu ya ūrdhvaṃ jyotiṣṭomadaśāhābhyāmityādinā | tattu vistarabhayādiha nocyate | antyaśabdo'yaṃ mādhyadinasya pavamānasyāntyamāha | evamuttarasyārbhavasya || 4 ||

madhyāsyāyām | sādhyāsyāyāṃ caiko'dhyāsyāyāmevāntato dvau tasyāṃ caivāditaśca sā śaśakarṇaklṛpteti
vacanāt | evaṃ tṛcaikarcakaraṇam | bhargayaśasorvyavasthāṃ sūtrakāra āhaābhiplavikasya dvitīye'hani bhargo yaśa iti prāg viṣuvato vyatyāsaṃ syātāmiti gautama ityādinā yaśa uttarasminnaparamityantena ||
vṛṣā śoṇa pārtham antyam ||3||
iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
atha pṛṣṭhāni

iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe hāviṣmatam uttareṣu | pavasvendramacche ti śaṅkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca | vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyam || 5 ||
ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||

iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | |

prathamāyām | agnestriṇadhanamadhyāsyāyām || 3 ||

asāvyaṃśurmadāye ti gāyatraṃ ca goṣūktaṃ cābhidyumnaṃ vṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti cyāvana sujñāne | daivodāsaṃ vā | pavasva vājasātaya iti gaurīvitaṃ ca rayiṣṭhaṃ ca asāvi somo aruṣo vṛṣā harir iti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśam antyam | |5 ||

|| ityājyāni ||
prati vāṃ sūra udite bhindhi viśvā apa dviṣo yajñasya hi stha ṛtvijā 
ityuttarāṇyājyāni || 2 ||
indrāyendo marutvateti gāyatraṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca sakṛdiṣovṛdhīyaṃ kuryāt mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍaṃ caukṣṇorandhraṃ triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ sakṛtsamantaṃ kuryāt | sākamukṣa itīhavadvāsiṣṭham antyam || 3 ||
iti mādhyaṃdinaṃ pavamānam ||
sakṛdiṣovṛdhīyaṃ kuryāditi | ante'bhiplava iṣovṛdhīyamāśvasūktasya sthāne kuryādityarthaḥ | evaṃ triṇidhanasthāne samantam | tadidamuktamiṣovṛdhīyasamante pṛṣṭhyānantarye ṣaṣṭha iti | 3
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca | pibā sutasya rasina ityabhīvartaḥ | svāsu kāleyam || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pari stavāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ caidhmavāhaṃ ca yadihavat sa sunve yo vasūnāṃ taṃ vaḥ sakhāyo madāyeti
dīrghasujñāne | kāśītaṃ vā | somāḥ pavanta indava iti gaurīvitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca yatsvayoni | trirasmai sapta dhenavo duduhrire iti marutāṃ dhenu antyam || 5 ||
ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||

yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 6 ||
gūrdam traikakubhaṃ nārmedham ityukthāni | yadyukthyaḥ ||

agniṣṭomastve || 7 ||

pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ
evamanye'pi trayo'bhiplavāḥ kartavyāḥ | teṣu viśeṣaḥ | dvitīye'hani hāviṣmatasya sthāne yauktāśvamuttaram | maukṣasya hāviṣmatam | dvitīye'bhiplave pañcame'hani yaṇvasya sthāne'patyam | tṛtīye'bhiplave pañcame'hani saṃtani | mānavāt pūrvaṃ vāmraṃ ca mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ ceti | pañcame'hani yadahaḥ grāmegeyaṃ saṃtāne syāt mānavāt pūrvaṃ vāmraṃ syāt iti vacanāt | etaccājāmyartham | tatra vāmraṃ prathamāyām | mānavaṃ tṛce | caturthe'bhiplave pañcame'hani yaṇvasya sthāne śākvaravarṇam | tatraiva ṣaṣṭhe'hanyāśvasūktasyeṣovṛdhīyam | triṇidhanāyāsyasya samantam | bhadrayaśasorvīṅkaśuddhāśuddhīyayośca vyavasthāprakāra ukto na prasmartavyaḥ | anyat sarvaṃ prathamavat ||
evaṃ caturo'bhiplavānupetya pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍahamupayantīti tasya
klṛptimāha
pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā || 1 ||
brāhmaṇoktaṃ pṛṣṭhyasya yad vyūḍhatvaṃ tadyadi daśarātraprayuktaṃ tatra samūḍho'yaṃ bhavitumarhati | avicchinnatvāt | atha pṛṣṭhyaprayuktaṃ tato vyūḍha iti saṃdehādvikalpa iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ || 1 ||
siddhāntamāha
samūḍhastveva || 2 ||
iti | daśarātraprayukto vyūha iti bhāvaḥ | tathā ca nidānamathāpi vilupto vyūḍhaḥ ṣaḍaha ityādi | śrutyantaraṃ ca narte chandomebhyaḥ pṛṣṭhayo vyūhamānaśa iti||2 ||
tasya samūḍhasya klṛptirvakṣyata ityāha
tasya kalpaḥ || 3 ||
iti || 3 ||
prathamamaharāha
upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ upoṣu jātamapturam pavamānasya te kave iti prathamasyāhno bahiṣpavamānam || 4 ||
asya pratnānavarcasya navāhayogābhāvānnivṛttiḥ | pratnavatībhiścopavatībhiścetyārabhya nava bhavanti | navāhasya yuktyā iti śruteḥ ||
pra soma devavītaya ityabhīvarta ekasyām | pajramekasyām | yaudhājayamekasyām | samānamitarat || 5 ||
daśarātrikeṇa prathamenāhnā samānamitaradityarthaḥ ||
agna ā no mitrā ā yāhiityājyāni | pra somāso vipaścitaḥ iti gāyatramāśvaṃ somasāma iti tṛcaḥ | pra soma devavītaye ityabhīvartapajrayaudhājayaiḥ sāmatṛcaḥ | pra tu drava ityauśana mantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | asminnapi pṛṣṭhe rathaghoṣādayaḥ pṛṣṭhadharmāḥ kāryāḥ | saha dharmaiḥ sarvatra pṛṣṭhaṃ syāt iti vacanāt || pra somāso madacyuta iti gāyatramekasyām | tasyāmeva
gavāmayanam pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ 
saṃhitam | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir ityekarcayoḥ saphaākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti prathamāyāṃ gaurīvitagautame | kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvaṃ trivṛt | atredamanusaṃdheyam | yatsāmāvasṛjeyuravasvargāllokāt padyeran ityabhīvartaprakaraṇe śravaṇāt pūrvasmin pakṣasi sarvatra kartavyo'bhīvarta iti sthite pṛṣṭhasya nānābrahmasāmatvāt na tatrābhiplavavat brahmasāmatvena saṃkalpayituṃ śakyata iti pāvamānīkīṣu vṛhatīṣu niveśyate | nityānugraheṇaiva ca stomopapattyarthamāgantubhirnityaiḥ sahaikarcaṃ kriyate | tatra dvitīyādiṣvahassu kāleyamāgantu kalpayiṣyate | taṃ khalu vṛhatīṣu kāleyamanukalpayāmaḥ | sato'nurūpamasmin bhavatīti | asmiṃstvahani kāleyasyācchāvākasāmatvāt saṃcāradoṣo mā bhūditi
pajramāgantu kalpitamiti || 5 |||| 8 ||
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ
punānaḥ soma dhāraya ityaiḍamāyāsyamekasyām abhīvarta ekasyām kāleyamekasyām vṛṣā śoṇa iti
pārtham antyam | iti mādhyaṃdinasya | samānamitarat || 1 ||
iti | mādhyaṃdinaśabdena kalpe sarvatra mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamāna ucyate | ihavadvāsiṣṭhasya ṣaṣṭhe'hani mādhyaṃdināntyatvena kalpayiṣyamāṇatvāt asaṃcārāya pārthamatra kalpitam | etatstomaṃ kṣatrasāma bṛhatīpṛṣṭhe'bhirūpam iti nidānam | tatreyaṃ klṛptiḥ | pavasva vaco pavasvendo vṛṣā soma vṛṣā hyasi pavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtaṃ mitraṃ vayam indramidgā caturṛcam | indre agnā ityājyāni | caturṛce'ntyāyā uddhāraḥ | vṛṣā pavasva dhāraya iti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhāraya ityaiḍamāyāsyam abhīvartaḥ kāleyamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ | tṛce | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārtha mantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | bṛhatstotre dundubhimāhanyuḥ | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | pavasvendramaccheti śaṅkusujñāne ekarce | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvita mekasyām | krauñcaṃ tṛce | vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvam |
gavāmayanam pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ 
evāhyasi vītyāmahīyavam | āṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam ||6||||9| 
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ
abhi somāsa āyavaḥ iti paurumadgaṃ prathamāyām | tasyāmevābhīvartaḥ | kāleyaṃ dvitīyāyām | gautamaṃ tṛtīyāyām | samānamitarat || 1 ||
iti | caturṇāṃ prathamāyāṃ dvāvitarau nānottarayor iti nyāyādevamekarcakaraṇam | atha klṛptiḥ | davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramidavo rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam iṣe pavasva dhāraya iti bahiṣpavamānam | agninā mitraṃ huva indreṇa tā huva ityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca | abhisomāsa āyavaḥ iti paurumadgaṃ prathamāyām | abhīvartakāleya gautamaiḥ sāmatṛcaḥ antarikṣaṃ tisṛṣu | pra hinvāna ityasyādhyāsyāyām āṣkāraṇidhanam | tisro vāca ityaṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo 'ntyaḥ | vairūpaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | vayaṃ gha tve mahāvaiṣṭambham | taraṇiritsiṣāsati iti
rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | upavājayamānā vairūpeṇa stuvīranni tyādi | tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca pāṣṭhauhaṃ ca | ā sotā pariṣiñcata sakhāya ā niṣīdata iti vācaḥsāma śauktaṃ caikarcayoḥ | sutāso madhumattamā iti gaurīvitaṃ ca triṇidhanaṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāma | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni || sarvaṃ saptadaśam || 1 |||| 10 ||
caturthasyāhnaḥ
pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi iti stotrīyānurūpau | agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat hoturājyam | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇa ityāṣṭādaṃṣṭrottaramekasyām | abhīvarta ekasyām | kāleyamekasyām | somaḥ pavate janitā matīnām iti janitramantyam | mādhyadinasya pra ta āśvinīḥ pavamāna dhenavaḥ iti lauśamantyam ārbhavasya | samānamitarat || 1 ||
gavāmayanam pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ 
iti | stotrīyo bahiṣpavamānasyādyastṛcaḥ | dvitīyo'nurūpaḥ | atra gāyatrītriṣṭubjagatīnām anyonyalokapariharaṇena prakṛtivadavasthānaṃ samūha ityasmiṃstryahe vaiyūhike'hareva jagatītriṣṭubhorārbhavānte mādhyaṃdinānte ca kṛtayorbahiṣpavamāne prakṛtivadavasthāne vaiyūhiko yūpaḥ tatsthāne ca mādhyadināntyo gāyatrastṛco yathāpratyāsaktiriṣyate | hoturājyāni ca | vaiyūhikānyuddhṛtya gāyatrāṇi tadvibhaktīni pratinidhīyanta ityanusaṃdheyam ||
athāsya klṛptiḥ | pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇaya āśurarṣabṛhanmate hinvanti sūramusraya iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā indro dadhīco asthabhiḥ iyaṃ vāmasya manmana ityājyāni | pavasva dakṣa sādhana iti gāyatraṃ cātharvaṇaṃ ca nidhanakāmaṃ ca | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇa ityāṣṭādaṃṣṭramabhīvartaḥ kāleyamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | ābhīśavaṃ tisṛṣu |
svaḥpṛṣṭhaṃ tisuṣu | somaḥ pavate janitā matīnām iti janitramantyam | vairājaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ceti pṛṣṭhāni | vairājasya stotramupākṛta ityādi | paripriyā divaḥ kavir iti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivyam soma | punāna ūrmiṇā iti bṛhatkātīṣādīye | purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadāndhīgave | pra ta āśvinīr iti lauśam antyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saindhukṣitaṃ saubharaṃ vasiṣṭhasya priyamityukthāni | indra juṣasva iti gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | sarvamekaviṃśam || 1 |||| 11 ||
pañcamasyāhnaḥ
pavamānasya viśvavit yat soma citramukthyam iti stotrīyānurūpau | agne stomaṃ manāmahe iti hoturājyam | soma uṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhiḥ iti mānavānūpe tṛcayoḥ | vāmramekasyām abhīvarta ekasyām | kāleyamekasyām | indurvājī pavate gonyoghā iti saṃpāntyā mādhyadinasya | pārthasya loke tvāṣṭrīsāma yad dvyanutodam | govit pavasva iti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśam antyam ārbhavasya | samānamitarat || 11 ||
gavāmayanam pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ 
iti | pārthasya loke tvāṣṭrīsāmeti saṃcārārthamuktam | pavamānasya viśvavit | yatsoma citramukthyam | pra somāso adhanviṣuḥ pra kavirdevavītaye yavaṃ yavanno andhasā yāste dhārā madhuścutaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | agne stomaṃ manāmahe purūruṇā ciddhyasti uttiṣṭhannojasā saha indrāgnī yuvām ityājyāni | arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatraṃ ca yaṇvaśākalavārśāni | soma uṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhir iti mānavānūpe tṛcayoḥ | vāmrābhīvartakāleyaiḥ sāmatṛcaḥ | agnestriṇidhanaṃ tisṛṣu | indurvājī pavate iti saṃpāntyāntyam | mahānāmnyaśca vāmadevyaṃ ca bārhadgiraṃ ca rāyovājīyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | apaḥ sāvakā upanidhāya mahānāmnībhiḥ stuvīranni tyādi | asāvyaṃśurmadāye ti gāyatraṃ ca saṃtani ca | abhidyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇāśiśurmahīnām iti cyāvana krośe | pavasva vājasātaye iti gaurīvitaṃ ca ṛṣabhaścaśākvaraṃ tvāṣṭrīsāma ca dvyanutodamaṣṭeḍaśca padastobhaḥ | govitpavasveti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saṃjayaṃ ca saumitraṃ mahāvaiśvāmitraṃ cokasthāni | sarvaṃ triṇavam || 12 ||
ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ
asṛkṣata pra vājina etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ iti stotrīyānurūpau | yamagne pṛtsu martyam iti hoturājyam | mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyeti svāramaukṣṇorandhraṃ tisṛṣu aiḍamaukṣṇorandhramekasyām abhīvarta ekasyām kāleyamekasyām ayā pavā pavasvainā vasūnīti ihavadvāsiṣṭhamantyaṃ mādhyaṃdinasya | jyotiryajñasya pavate madhu priyam iti marutāṃ dhenvantyam ārbhavasya | samānamitaram | samānamitaram || 1 ||
iti ārṣeyakalpasūtre prathamo'dhyāyaḥ || 1 ||
asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ etamutyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavasva devavīrati sa nā ca soma jeṣi ca taratsamandī dhāvati ete somā asṛkṣateti bahiṣpavamānam | yamagne pṛtsu martyaṃ prati vāṃ sūra udite bhindhi viśvā apa dviṣo yajñasya hi stha ṛtvijetyājyāni | indrāyendo marutvate iti gāyatraṃ ceṣovṛdhīyaṃ gāyatrīkrauñcaṃ ca vājadāvaryaśca 
gavāmayanam pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ 
revatyaśca | mṛjyamāna iti svāramaukṣṇorandhraṃ tisṛṣvaiḍamaukṣṇorandhramabhīvartaḥ kāleyamiti sāmatṛce | vājajid varuṇasāma | aṅgirasāṃ goṣṭhaśca tisṛṣu | ayā pavasvainā vasūnītīhavadvāsiṣṭhamantyam | revatīṣu vāravantīyam | svāsu vāmadevyam | surūpakṛtnumūtaye ityṛṣabhoraivatam | ubhe yadindreti śyenaśca pṛṣṭhāni | vāravantīyasya stotre dhenurityādi pṛṣṭhadharma uktaḥ | parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca trīṇi ca vaidanvatāni (tṛtīyaṃ vaidanvatam, ādyaṃ vaidanvatam, caturthaṃ vaidanvatam) | sasunveyo vasūnām taṃ vaḥ sakhāyo madāye ti dīrgha kārṇaśravase ca | somā pavanta iti gaurīvitaṃ ca madhuścinnidhanaṃ ca krauñce ca vāṅnidhanaṃ aiḍaṃkrauñcama | jyotiryajñasyeti marutāṃ dhenva ntyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | agne tvaṃ no antama iti gūrdaḥ | imā nu kam iti bhadram | pra va indrāyetyudvaṃśaputra ityukthāni | sarvaṃ trayastriṃśam ||
ṣaṣṭhe'hani saṃsthite ityādi pūrvavat | atra sūtramābhīvartastotrīyānābhiplāvikān pṛṣṭhye saṃśayet | brāhmaṇācchaṃsinā kāleyarco'cchāvākena sarvatra yadā pavamāne syātāviti | tatra prathamadvitīyayorahnorābhiplāvikasyābhīvartasya
pārṣṭikayornaudhasaśyaitayośca samānastotrīyāviti na tayoḥ pṛthaganuśāsanam | prathame'hani kāleyasya ca | evaṃ caturbhirabhiplavaiḥ pṛṣṭhyena caiko māsaḥ | sa dvitīyaḥ | sa tṛtīyaḥ | sa caturthaḥ | sa pañcamaḥ | ṣaṣṭhe'pi māse trayo'bhiplavāḥ prayoktavyāḥ | caturthasya lopaḥ | pūrvasmin pakṣasi triṣu caturtho'bhiplavo lupyeteti vacanāt | tṛtīye'bhiplave ṣaṣṭhe'hani iṣovṛdhīyasamante syātām | pṛṣṭhyānantarye ṣaṣṭha iti vacanāt ||
pūrvavat pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ prayoktavyaḥ || 1 ||| 13 ||
iti śrīvāmanāryasutavaradarājaviracitāyām ārṣeyakalpasūtravyākhyāyāṃ prathamo'dhyāyaḥ || 1 ||

pavasva vāco agriyaḥ punāno akramīdabhi pavamānasya te kave agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ā no mitrāvaruṇābhi tvā vṛṣabhā suta indrāgnī ā gatam̐ sutamuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatramamahīyavam̐ satrāsāhīyaṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayetyabhīvarto rauravayaudhājaye abhi vāyuṃ vītyarṣā gṛṇāna iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasam̐hite pavasvendramaccheti saphasujñāne purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ tisṛṣu gaurīvitamekasyāṃ madhuścunnidhanamekasyām̐ śyāvāśvamekasyāmiti vāndhīgavaṃ tisṛṣu yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca bṛhaccāgneyaṃ kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyasyarkṣu rathaṃtaramagniṣṭomasāma sarvastomasya satastryāvṛta stomāścatuḥpraṇayāḥ 21
upāsmai gāyatā nara upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhrataṃ pavamānasya te vayaṃ pavamānasya te kave agna ā yāhi vītaya ityājyāni pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ cāśvaṃ cābhi somāsa āyava iti pauruhanmanaṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca gauṅgavaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyaṃ yajjāyathā apūrvyeti rathaṃtaraṃtaram̐ svāsu vāmadevyaṃ taṃ vo dasmamṛtīṣahamityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyam̐ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ cāyā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato haririti saphasujñāne kāśītaṃ vā pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ tisṛṣu gaurīvitamekasyāṃ gautamamekasyāmaudalamekasyāmiti vā svaraṃ tisṛṣu yatpayonidhanamevamabhi priyāṇītyaiḍaṃ kāvamantyam̐ svāraṃ yadi vikalpayedyajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāmauśanamaupagavaṃ vasiṣṭhasya priyaṃ tairaścyaṃ vetyukthāni yadyukthyo'gniṣṭomastveva 22
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asyutte śuṣmāsa īrate pavamānasya te kave agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyāni vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ ca yatpṛṣṭhye parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ cāyāsye vṛṣā śona iti pārthamantyaṃ matsyapāyi te maha iti bṛhat svāsu vāmadevyam abhi pra vaḥ surādhasamityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyaṃ yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca pavasvendramaccheti śaṅkusujñāne ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitaṃ tisṛṣu gaurīvitamekasyāṃ krauñcamekasyām āsitamekasyāmiti vā svaraṃ tisṛṣu yadbṛhannidhanayoḥ pūrvamuttaramāvṛtteṣu vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti aiḍaṃ yāmamantyam̐ svāraṃ yadi vikalpayedyajñāyajñīyamagnistomasāma sākamaśvam̐ saubhara0māṣṭādam̐ṣṭramityukthāni yadyukthyo'gniṣṭomastveva 23
davidyutatyārucaite asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate pavasva vṛṣṭimā su naḥ pavamānasya te kave agnināgniḥ samidhyata ityājyānyuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cābhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgaṃ ca gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ cāgneśca triṇidhanaṃ tisro vāca ityaṅgirasām̐ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ pratyasmai pipīṣata iti rathaṃtaram̐ svāsu vāmadevyaṃ vayaṃ gha tvā sutāvanta ityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyaṃ tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatrasam̐hite ā sotā pariṣiñcata sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaśca sāma sujñānaṃ ca daivodāsaṃ vā sutāso madhumattamā iti gaurīvitaṃ tisṛṣu gaurīvitamekasyāmāndhīgavamekasyāṃ tvāṣṭrīsāmaikasyamiti vā svaraṃ tisṛṣu yatprathamaṃ pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam̐ svāram̐ sāmarājaṃ yadi vikalpayedyajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma pramam̐hiṣṭhīyam̐ hārivarṇamudvam̐śīyamityukthāni yadyukthyo'gniṣṭomastveva sarve saptadaśāḥ 24
upāsmai gāyatā naro | babhrave nu svatavase pra svānāso rathā iva pavamānasya te kave agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanadyad adya sūra udita udghedabhi śrutāmaghamiyaṃ vāmasya manmano asya pratnāmanu dyutamiti gāyatraṃ bhrājam̐ satrāsāhīyaṃ tavāham̐ soma rāraṇetyutsedhastisṛṣvābhīśavamekasyāmabhīvarta ekasyāṃ pṛśnyekasyām̐ rathaṃtaraṃ tisṛṣu śiśuṃ jajñānam̐ haryataṃ mṛjantīti pārthamantyaṃ mahādivākīrtyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ceindra kratuṃ na ā bhareti vikarṇam̐ svāsu kāleyaṃ pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ cābhrājaṃ cābhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti saphaśrudhye ekarcayoḥ paryū ṣviti śyāvāśvamekasyāṃ purojitī vo andhasa iti bṛhattisṛṣu gaurīvitamekasyāmāndhīgavamekasyāṃ niṣedhamekasyāṃ yajñāyajñīyaṃ tisṛṣu dhartā diva iti kāvamantyaṃ mūrdhānaṃ divo aratiṃ pṛthivyā iti bhāsaṃ daśastobhamagniṣṭomasāmaikavim̐śa stoma 251
indra kratuṃ na ā bhareti mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ śyaitanaudhase etāni brahmasāmānyāvṛttānām̐ svarasāmnāmabhīvartaloke pauṣkalam̐ rāthaṃtarayoḥ śrudhyaṃ bārhatasya sujñānaloke 252
upa tvā jāmayo giro janīyanto nvagrava uta naḥ priyā priyāsu tatsaviturvareṇyam̐ somānām̐ svaraṇamagna ā yāhi pavase pavamānasya te kave suṣamiddho na ā vaha tā naḥ śaktaṃ pārthivasya yuñjanti bradhnamaruṣaṃ tamīḍiṣva yo arciṣāsya pratnāmanu dyutamiti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti kāleyamekasyām̐ rathaṃtaraṃ tisṛṣu dairghaśravasaṃ tisṛṣu yaudhājayamekasyāmayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyaṃ vairājaṃ ca mahānāmnyaśca vairūpaṃ ca revatyaśca pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatrasam̐hite pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye paryū ṣviti vāmadevyaṃ purojitī vo andhasa ityāndhīgavamekasyāṃ gaurīvitamekasyāṃ yadvāhiṣṭhīyamekasyāṃ yajñāyajñīyaṃ tisṛṣu pari pra dhanveti dvipadāsu vāravantīyam̐ sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyaṃ tvamagne yajñānām̐ hoteti bṛhadagniṣṭomasāma sarvastomasya sataścaturāvṛta stomāstriḥpraṇayāḥ 26
paurumīḍhaṃ mānavaṃ janitraṃ bhāradvājaṃ vāsiṣṭhaṃ gauṅgavam̐ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ vaita ekarcā āvṛttasya pṛṣṭhyasyābhīvartaloka indra kratuṃ na ā bhareti paurumīḍhaṃ mānavaṃ janitraṃ bhāradvājam̐ śyaitanaudhase etāni brahmasāmānyāvṛttasyābhiplavasyābhīvartaloke pauṣkalam̐ rāthaṃtarāṇām̐ śrudhyaṃ bārhatānām̐ sujñānaloke 27
jyotiṣṭomamājyabahiṣpavamānaṃ gāyatrī cābhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca vārkajambhaṃ ca yadbṛhannidhanaṃ maidhātithaṃ ca rauravaṃ vā yaudhājayamauśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cendra kratuṃ na ā bhareti naudhasam̐ svāsu kāleyam̐ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasam̐hite pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitāndhīgave kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma pramam̐hiṣṭhīyam̐ hārivarṇamudvam̐śīyamiti ukthānyāyuṣṭoma stomaḥ 28
pavasva vāco agriyo davidyutatyā rucā pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi pavamānasya te kave agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyānyasya pratnāmanu dyutamiti gāyatraṃ cāāmahīyavaṃ ca parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ ca vārkajambhaṃ ca yadīnidhanaṃ dairghaśravasaṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ vā yaudhājayamayam̐ soma iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ cendra kratuṃ na ā bhareti śyaitam̐ svāsu kāleyaṃ yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitāndhīgave sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma sākamaśvam̐ saubharamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni goṣṭoma stomaḥ 29
upāsmai gāyatā nara upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto davidyutatyā rucaite asṛgramindavo yavaṃyavaṃ no andhasā pavamānasya te kave hotā devo amārtyastā naḥ śaktaṃ pārthivasya surūpakṛtnumūtaya ā sute siñcata śriyamasya pratnāmanu dyutamiti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca satrāsāhīyaṃ ca parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca rauravayaudhajaye ayam̐ soma iti pārthamantyaṃ vrataṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ trayodaśa parimādo brāhmaṇakḷptā indramidgāthino bṛhad indro dadhīco asthabhirudghedabhi śrutāmaghamiti trivṛcchiro gāyatram pañcadaśam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ saptadaśaṃ bṛhaduttara ekavim̐śaṃ bhadraṃ pucchaṃ tadidāsa bhuvaneṣu jyeṣṭhamiti pañcavim̐śa ātmā rājanaṃ pañcavim̐śamitaram̐ sarvaṃ vāmadevyaṃ maitrāvaruṇasāma trikadrukeṣu mahiṣo yavāśiramiti pañcanidhanaṃ vāmadevyaṃ brahmasāma svāsu kāleyaṃ pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāṃ cārkaḥ pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca madhuścunnidhanaṃ ca śyāvāśvāndhīgave sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma 2101

pavasva vāco agriyaḥ punāno akramīdabhi pavamānasya te kave ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam || 1 ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ā no mitrāvaruṇā abhi tvā vṛṣabhā sutam indrāgnī āgataṃ sutam ||
ityājyāni || 2 ||
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatram āmahīyavaṃ satrāsāhīyam | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti abhīvarto rauravayaudhājaye | abhi vāyuṃ vīti pārtham antyam ||

svarasāmnāṃ prathamaḥ

auśanam aupagavam vasiṣṭhasya priyaṃ tairaścyam ityukthāni | yadyukthyaḥ agniṣṭomastveva || 7 || ekaviṃśatyahakāriṇāṃ pakṣe svarasāmāna ukthyā bhavanti | tathā ca nidānamaathaite svarasāmānastānagniṣṭomān navāhakāriṇaḥ kuryuḥ | ukthyānekaviṃśatyahakāriṇa iti | sūtraṃ caekaviṃśatyahakāriṇa upariṣṭādabhijita pṛṣṭhyamupayanti | prāk ca viśvajitaḥ svarasāmānaścokthyān iti | agniṣṭomastveveti navāhakāritvādasmākamiti bhāvaḥ | saptadaśa bhavanti iti śruteḥ trayāṇāṃ svarasāmnāṃ saptadaśa stomāḥ kartavyāḥ || 7 ||

indra kratuṃ na ābhara iti mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ śyaitanaudhase | etāni brahmasāmānyāvṛttānāṃ svarasāmnāmabhīvartaloke | pauṣkalaṃ rāthantarayoḥ | śrudhya bārhatasya sujñānaloke || 1 |
yat purastāt samāna pragātho bhavati | anyadanyat sāmeti śrute. | abhīvartasthāne indrakratau nānābrahmasāmāni vihitāni | sujñānamapi paurvapakṣikameveti tatsthāne rāthantarayoḥ svarasāmnoḥ pauṣkalam | bārhatasya ca svarasāmnaḥ śrudhyaṃ vihitam | tatrāvṛttānāṃ svarasāmnāṃ prathamo davidyutatyā rucetyādikaḥ | tasyendra

gavāmayanampṛṣṭhyābhiplavau
paurumīḍhaṃ mānavaṃ janitraṃ bhāradvājaṃ vāsiṣṭhaṃ gauṅgavaṃ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ vaita ekarcāḥ | āvṛttasya pṛṣṭhasyābhīvarttalokaḥ || 1 ||
tatra prathamaṃ trayastriṃśamaharasṛkṣata pravājina ityādikam | tasya pavamānābhyarṣasītyabhīvartasthāne paurumīḍham | ṣaṣṭhe'hani saṃsthite ityādinidānoktāḥ ṣaḍahasaṃsthādharmā asminnahani kartavyāḥ | kathamāvṛtte pṛṣṭhe saṃsthādharmā ityupakramya trayastriṃśesya tveva saṃsthāyāmityācāryā ityādinidānavacanāt | pavamānasya viśvavidityādi triṇavamahardvitīyam | tasya mandrayā yāti dhārayetyabhīvartasthāne pūrvaṃ mānavam | pavamāno ajījanatpunāno akramīdabhi ityādyekaviṃśamahastṛtīyam | tasya paridhim̐ rati tām abhīvartaloke janitram | davidyutatyādi saptadaśamahaścaturtham | tasyābhisomāsa āyava iti prathamāyāmabhīvartaloke bharadvājam | pavasva vāco agriya ityādikaṃ pañcadaśamahaḥ pañcamam | tasminnutso devo hiraṇyaya ityabhīvartasthāne vāsiṣṭham | upāsmai gāyatā nara ityādikaṃ trivṛdahaḥ ṣaṣṭham | tasya pra soma devavītaya iti prathamāyāmabhīvartasthāne gauṅgavaṃ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ vā | anyat sarvaṃ pūrvavat || 1 ||
athāvṛttasyābhiplavasya ṣaṇṇāmahnāṃ kramādviśeṣamāhaindra kratuṃ na ā bhare ti paurumīḍhaṃ mānavaṃ janitraṃ bhāradvājaṃ śyaita naudhase | etāni brahmasāmānyāvṛttasyābhiplavasyābhīvartaloke | pauṣkalaṃ rāthantarāṇāṃ śrudhyaṃ bārhatānāṃ sujñānaloke || 2 ||
iti | tatra prathamasyāhnaḥ kalpaḥasṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipa pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānamityādi | indra kratuṃ na ābhareti paurumīḍhaṃ brahmasāmābhīvartasthāne | taṃ vaḥ sakhāya iti sujñānasya śrudhyam ||
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ -- pavamānasya viśvavid ityādi | indrakratumiti mānavādyaṃ brahmasāma | prāṇā śiśur iti sujñānaloke pauṣkalam ||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥpavamāno ajījanat ityādi | indra kratau janitraṃ brahmasāma | somaḥ punāna iti sujñānaloke śrudhyam ||
caturthasyāhnaḥdavidyutatyā ruce tyādi | indrakratumiti bhāradvājaṃ brahmasāma | sakhāya ā niṣīdateti sujñānaloke pauṣkalam ||
pañcamasyāhnaḥpavasva vāco agriya ityādi | indrakratau śyaitaṃ brahmasāma | indramaccheti sujñānasthāne śrudhyam ||
gavāmayanamāyuḥ
ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥupāsmai gāyatā nara ityādi | indrakatumiti naudhasaṃ brahmasāma | pavate haryata iti sujñānaloke pauṣkalam || anyatsarvamuktam || atra ca triṣvabhiplaveṣu kartavyeṣu āvṛttānāṃ caturṇāmabhiplavānāṃ yaḥ prathamaḥ śākvaravarṇavām̐stasya lopaḥ | uttare abhiplave daśarātrasamīpe triṣu caturṇāmāvṛttānāṃ prathamo lupyeteti dhanaṃjaya uttama iṣovṛdhīya samante syātāmiti | viparītametacchāṇḍilyāyanasya | viṣuvatsamīpe ca | triṣvabhāva evamuttarasmin pakṣasi prathamasya māsasya aṣṣṭāviṃśatirahāni | dvātriṃśaduttame | dve tvahanī purastāt bhaviṣyataḥ | punarāvṛttaṃ pṛṣṭhyamāvṛttāṃścābhiplavān kuryuḥ | sa māsaḥ | tathaiva apare trayo māsāḥ | atha ṣaṣṭhe māsi trayo'bhiplavāḥ pūrvavat ||
iti pṛṣṭhyābhiplavāvāvṛttau || 8 ||
āyuḥ
āyuṣṭomamāha
jyotiṣṭomamājyabahiṣpavamānam | gāyatrī ca | 1 ||
gāyatrīśabdenātra mādhyaṃdinādyo gāyatrastṛcaḥ sasāmā kathyate | upadavipaveti bahiṣpavamānam | agnaāno mitrāāyāhi indrāgnīityājyāni | uccā teiti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca || 1 |
abhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca vārkajambhaṃ ca yad bṛhannidhanaṃ maidhātithaṃ ca rauravaṃ vā yaudhājayam | auśanam antyam || 2 ||
iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
dvihiṃkāraṃ prathamāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | | 2 ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | indra kratuṃ na ā bhareti nodhasaṃ svāsu kāleyam | 3 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhaye iti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo iti gaurīvitāndhīgave | kāvam antyam | 4 |
ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 5 ||
pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇam udvaṃśīyam ityukthāni || 6 ||
gavāmayanamgauḥ a. 2. kha. 10
āyuṣṭomastomaḥ || 7 ||
trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśānyājyāni | saptadaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | ekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanam | sokthamiti stomaḥ | | 7 || iti āyuḥ || 9 | |
gauḥ
goṣṭomamāhapavasva vāco agriyo davidyutatyā rucā pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe || 2 ||
ityājyāni ||
asya pratnāmanu dyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti samantaṃ ca vārkajambhaṃ ca yadīnidhanam dairghaśravasaṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ vā | yaudhājayam | ayaṃ soma iti pārtham antyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
samantaṃ prathamāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | 3 ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca | indra kratuṃ na ā bhare ti śyaitaṃ svāsu kāleyam || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣai | pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye | purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitas ndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
yajñāyajñoyamagniṣṭomasāma || 6 ||
sākamaśvaṃ saubharam udvaṃśīyam ityukthāni || 7 ||
goṣṭomastomaḥ || 8 ||
pañcadaśaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | trivṛntyājyāni | saptadaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | ekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanam | sokthamiti stomaḥ || 8 ||
atha dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni trivṛdaharārabhyāvivākyaparyantāni satrabhūtavyūḍhadvādaśāhavat kartavyāni ||
mahāvratam
upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ upoṣu jātamapturaṃ pavasva vāca agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto davidyutatyā rucaite asṛgramidavo yavaṃyavaṃ no andhasā pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
gavāmayanammahāvratam a. 2. kha. 11
hotā devo amartya. tā naḥ śaktaṃ pārthivasya surūpakṛtnumūtaye ā sute ṣiñcata priyam || 2 ||
ityājyāni ||
asminnahani sarveṣāṃ stotrāṇāṃ pañcaviṃśastomaḥ | tasyaivaṃ viṣṭutiḥ | aṣṭābhyo hiṃkaroti | sa tisṛbhiḥ | sa catasṛbhiḥ |
sa ekayā | aṣṭābhyo hiṃkaroti | sa ekayā | sa tisṛbhiḥ | sa catasṛbhi | navabhyo hiṃkaroti | sa catasṛbhiḥ | sa dvābhyām |
sa tisṛbhiḥ | sa pañcabhiḥ | sa ekayā | sa tisṛbhiriti vā | taduktaṃ pañcaviṃśecaturviśasyottame paryāye paricarāyāmāvapet iti gautamaḥ | āvāpasthāna iti dhānaṃjayyaḥ iti || 2 ||
asya pratnām anu dyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca satrāsāhīyaṃ ca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca rauravayaudhājaye | ayaṃ soma iti pārtham antyam || 3 ||
iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ || yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | eko'dhyāsyāyāmevāntataḥ iti vacanāt || 3 || vrataṃ pṛṣṭham || 4 || vakṣyamāṇaṃ vratasaṃjñaṃ hotuḥ pṛṣṭhamityarthaḥ || 4 ||
trayodaśa parimādo brahmaṇaklṛptāḥ || 5 ||
prāṇena purastādityādi brāhmaṇena trayodaśa parimātsaṃjñakāni pṛṣṭhāṅgatvena klṛptānītyarthaḥ | eteṣāṃ pṛṣṭhāṅgatvaṃ pṛṣṭhasya pañcaviṃśasaṃpatsiddhyarthamuktam | tathā copagranthaḥ śiraḥpakṣapucchānāṃ parimādbhistraya pañcaviṃśā iti | tadarthamevaiṣāṃ parisāmatve'pi sakṛdgāna sūtre vihitam || 5 ||
indramidgāthino bṛhat indro dadhīco asthabhiḥ udghedabhi śrutāmagham iti trivṛcchiro gāyatram | pañcadaśaṃ rathantaraṃ dakṣiṇaḥ pakṣaḥ | saptadaśaṃ bṛhad uttaraḥ | ekaviṃśaṃ bhadraṃ puccham | tadidāsa bhuvaneṣu jyeṣṭham iti pañcaviṃśa ātmā rājanam ||6|| iti | vratasaṃjñakaṃ pṛṣṭhamuktam | pṛṣṭhahome mahāvrataṃ pibatu somyam iti viśeṣaḥ | mahāvratāmiti vrata iti vacanāt | gāyatrarathantara0bṛhadrājanāni pibatu somyaṃ madhvāyurdadhanti yajñapatāviti vā | yāvanti pṛṣṭhasthāni syuḥ sarvāṇyeva prasaṃcakṣīta | pibatu dadhaditi ca yathārthamūhediti vacanāt | pūrvaṃ tu gautamīyaṃ matam | indramidgāthina iti tṛcatrayam, rathantarabṛhadbhadrāṇāṃ tṛcatrayam, tadidāseti tṛcaṃ sadasyeva japeyuḥ | athavā tāni tattatstotradeśa eva japeyuḥ |
gavāmayanammahāvratam 
aṅgānāṃ mukhyadeśatvāt | bṛhadrathantarayostu ṛkjapānantaraṃ yathāyogaṃ daśavyāhṛtijapo yaste goṣviti japaśca kāryaḥ | vyapaiti vai khalu gāyatraṃ bṛhadrathantarayorjapānvrate ityupagranthavacanāt | athopākṛte pṛṣṭhe yuktvā stomaṃ parimādo gāyediti bhāḍitāyano manyate | ṛgjapaṃ kṛtvā pratigṛhya tṛṇe ayuktveti gautamaśāṇḍilyau dhānaṃjayyaśca | ṛgjapaṃ kṛtvā prāgeva stotropakaraṇādityeke | pūrvayā dvārā niṣkramya dakṣiṇenāgnīdhrīyaṃ mahāgnicayanamuttareṇa gatvā prāṇaprabhṛtibhirupatiṣṭheran | sarvāṇi sāmānyudgātā sakṛt sakṛt gāyet | nidhanaṃ prastotṛpratihartārāvanūpeyātām | madhyenidhaneṣvapi sāmasu madhyenidhanānyanūpeyātām | ādyantastubdheṣu pāramātsu padāya padāya stobhet | agnicayanasya purastāt pratyaṅmukhāstiṣṭhantaḥ prāṇena sāmnāhavanīyamupatiṣṭheran | tasya vasiṣṭhastriṣṭubindra iti ṛṣayaḥ | hāuhāuhāu śvāsaṃ kṛtvā āyuḥ satyamityādistobhān padāya padāya brūyāt | tenaiva pathā pratyāvṛjya pucchasya paścāt tiṣṭhanto'pānena sāmnā pucchamupatiṣṭheran | vasiṣṭhastriṣṭubindraḥ | hāuhāuhāu nirucchavāsaṃ kṛtvā ā no āyāhītyetyādi padāya padāya stobhet | hāu 3 | hiṃ hiṃ hiṃ | ihā | hiṃ iti vratapakṣayoḥ pūrveṇa
sāmnā dakṣiṇaṃ pakṣamupatiṣṭheran | uttareṇa sāmnottaram | prajāpatistriṣṭubindraḥ | padāya padāya stobhet | prāṅmukhā eva prajāpatehṛdayenāpi kakṣamupatiṣṭheran | paścimena pakṣāvapi kakṣāvityuktam | tasya ṛṣiḥ prajāpatiḥ | devatā ca | chando nāsti | ehṛdayāmiti triruktamantarnidhanam | prajārūpamityādi nidhanam | viśve devā iti vasiṣṭhasya nihavena cātvālamupatiṣṭheran | vasiṣṭho jagatī viśvedevāḥ āihītyādi padāya padāya stobhet | cātvālamupasthāyottareṇāgnīdhrīyaṃ gatvā tasya paścāt prāṅmukhastiṣṭhanto'pi cātvālasthānasthā eva pratyaṅmukhāḥ satrasyardhyāgnīdhrīyamupatiṣṭheran | tasya ṛṣayo devatāśca viśvedevāḥ | aganma jyetirityādibhyo devatāpadebhyaḥ pratyekam auhoveti stobhet | teṣāṃ padavatpravṛtteḥ | aganma jyotiḥ | amṛtā abhūma | antarikṣaṃ pṛthivyā adhyāruhāma | divamantarikṣādadhyāruhāma | avidāma devān | samu devairaganmahi | suvarjyotiriti nidhanāni | tasyottamaṃ nidhanaṃ yāvaducchvāsaṃ brūyuḥ | hāu svaratā brahmāṇa indramiti ślokena sāmnā havirdhāne upatiṣṭheran | purastāt pratyaṅmukhā | hāu abhisvarateti anuślokena ca paścāt prāṅmukhāḥ | tayoḥ prajāpatiḥ ṛṣiḥ | dvipadāditriṣṭup chandaḥ | indro devatā | tata evekṣamāṇā auhoi nāke suparṇamiti yāmena dvandveṣvadhīyamānena mārjālīyamupatiṣṭheran | yama ṛṣiḥ | triṣṭup chandaḥ | suparṇavaruṇayamā devatāḥ | āyurnavastobhayoḥ pūrveṇa sāmnā upatiṣṭheran | purastāt pratyaṅmukhāḥ | uttareṇa paścāt
gavāmayanammahāvratam 
prāṅmukhāḥ upatiṣṭheran | hāuhāuhāuvā | viśvato dāvannityāyurnavastobhe | prajāpatirdvipadā viṣṭārapaṅktirindra | tayorantarnidhanānyuktāni | antarvedi pratyaṅmukhāstiṣṭhantaḥ ṛśyasya sāmnā gārhapatyamupatiṣṭheran | tasyaiva paścāt prāṅmukhā vā | gārhapatyaḥ śālāmukhīyaḥ | harī ta indre iti ṛśyasya sāmnaḥ kaśyapo'nuṣṭubindraḥ | ṛṣyāsa indretyādi nidhanam | atra sūtram -- indraprabhṛti pratyakṣaṃ nidhanamupeyuḥ iti indra tasarapūteti saṃbuddhya taṃ brūyurityartha | nidānaṃ tu hrasvaṃ pratyakṣaṃ drāghitaṃ parokṣam ityādi | evaṃ parimādbhiścaritvā rājānaṃ saṃnāhayeyuḥ | varmapratimokavyatiriktaṃ khaṅgadhanurādibandhanaṃ kārayedudgātā | tasya rājño dvayavarārdhyau dvau rathāvanuyāyinau syātām | rājño'nye pūrveṇa devayajanaṃ saṃnahyeran | rājā ca sacivāśca dakṣiṇena devayajanaṃ parīyuḥ | pūrveṇa patnīśālāmudgātā gatvā dakṣiṇe vedyante prāco darbhān saṃstīrya teṣvenaṃ prāṅmu'khamupaveśayet | athāsmai kavacaṃ pratimuñcet | uttiṣṭha rājanniti goptetyantenānyasmin vā pratimuñcati | rājānamanumantrayetānena mantreṇa | athodgātā paścimena paryāhītyukto rājā sacivaiḥ saha devayajanasya paścimena pradakṣiṇaṃ parigacchet | udgātā pūrveṇa patnīśālāṃ pratyāvṛjyottarasmin vedyante pūrveṇāgnīdhrīyaṃ rājānamavasthāpya brūyāt | hastatramityādi caturthamityantam | tasyārthaḥ | hastatrāṇaṃ carma haste badhāna | udgatajyaṃ dhanuḥ kuru |
trīniṣūnayomayānupakalpayasva | caturthamiṣuṃ yameva kaṃcanāniyamena kalpayasveti pratidhatsva kāṇḍadhanuṣīti brūyāt | pratidadhānaṃ rājānamanumantrayeta vaiṇāvatāyeti dhārayātretyantena | sahasā tiṣṭheti ca brūyāt | rājā ca dhanuṣaḥ kāṇḍamanupanīyānuyāyināmanyatamaṃ rathamātiṣṭhet | yajamānaparicārakā uttareṇāgnīdhrīyaṃ pūrvāpare carmaṇī vibadhnīyuḥ | dakṣiṇena rathapathaṃ śiṣṭvā | athodgātā rājānaṃ brūyāt | pradakṣiṇaṃ devayajanaṃ parīyādityādi visrambhayeyurityantam | tasyārthaḥ | devayajanaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ parigacchanneva carmaṇoḥ pūrvaṃ carmāgamaneṣu vidhyet | kathamekaikenāyomayeneṣuṇottarottaram prathamasyopari dvitīyam tasyopari tṛtīyam anatipātayan atiśayapatanamakurvan aparasmai carmaṇe sacivāya yathāsaukaryamasyeyuḥ | tṛtīyeneṣuṇā vidhyodaṅ prayāyāḥ sacivaiḥ saha | tathā caturthamiṣuṃ yāṃ diśaṃ manyethāstāṃ pratyasyerava brahmadviṣo jahīti | gāṃ dṛṣṭvā avatiṣṭhethāḥ | tatra kvāpi gataḥ saṃnāhaṃ kuryuriti | tato'nyaṃ brāhmaṇaṃ pratipradakṣiṇaṃ devayajanaṃ parīyādityādi praiṣārthaḥ | hiṃkāravelāyāmimaṃ rājānaṃ kārayetyuktvā udgātā bhūmidundubhimāvrajet | paścādāgnīdhrīyasya śvabhraṃ
gavāmayanammahāvratam 
dundubhyākṛtikamardhamantarvedi ardhaṃ bahirvedi khātaṃ bhavati | sa bhūmidundubhiḥ ārṣabheṇottaralomnā carmaṇā ācchāditaḥ syāt | taṃ ca tvaṃ vāgasītyādistribhiḥ mantraiḥ pratimantramudgātāro valadhānenāhanyuḥ | anyaṃ vā ghnantametairmantrairanumantrayeran | aikāhikavrate āhīnikavrate ca tvaṃ vāgasītyādimantre satraśabdasthāne yajñaśabdaḥ prayoktavyaḥ | yajñe anandiṣuriti anumantryāpa upaspṛśya yathaitaṃ pratyāvṛjya paścāttiṣṭhantaścityamagnimupatiṣṭherana | namaste gāyatryādinā śiraḥ; namaste rathantarāyeti dakṣiṇaṃ pakṣam, namaste yajñāyajñīyāyetyādinā puccham, namasta vāmadevyāyetyādinā madhyam upatiṣṭheran | brāhmaṇe pakṣāntaratvena yajñāyajñīyaṃ pucchaṃ vāmadevyaṃ cātmā vihitamiti talliṅgābhyāṃ mantrābhyāṃ pucchamadhyayorupasthānam | sūtrakāreṇoktamyadā tu bhadrarājane pucchātmane tadā namaste bhadrāyeti pucchaṃ namaste rājanāyeti madhyaṃ copatiṣṭheran | atha sarvamagniṃ namaste gāyatrāyetyādi madhyamityantama | tena samastena mantreṇa tasmai ta iti dhehītyantenopasthāya sadaḥ praviśeyuḥ | tatra sarve sahartvijo mahāvratena stuvīran iti brāhmaṇenokte vihṛtya stavanapakṣe agnyupasthānānantaramava sadaḥpraveśa | tasmin hi pakṣe sadasyeva śiraḥprabhṛtibhiḥ sarvaiḥ stavanam | udgātaiva sarveṇodgāyedityasmiṃstu pakṣe havirdhāne śirasā stutvetyādikrameṇa sadaḥ praviśeyu | tatrāyaṃ prayogaḥ | śiraḥpakṣapucchānāṃ bhedena kuśāvidhānam | kuśāvidhānavasanāni ca bhinnāni bhinnāni | agnyupasthānaṃ kṛtvā havirdhānasya pūrvayā dvārā prapadya havirdhānamadhye somapātrāṇāmuttarataḥ indramidgāthino bṛhad indro dadhīco asthabhir udghedabhi śrutāmaghamiti
gāyatreṇāniruktena trivṛtstomena stuvīran | indramidgā caturṛce antyāyā uddhāraḥ | saṃdhiṣāmavatparivartinyā viṣṭutyā kuśāvidhānam | gāyatrādīnāṃ śiraḥprabhṛtisaṃjñayā pakṣirūpaṃ varṇayati śrutiḥ | śiraḥsaṃjñakasya gāyatrasya puṣkalo gāyatrīndra ityṛṣyādayaḥ | yajamānavācanam | acchidrajapāśca rājanena stute sarveṣāṃ tantreṇa kartavyam | na tu tattatstyutyanantaram | evaṃ havirdhāne śirasā stutvā saṃrabdhāḥ pratyañca eyuraparayā dvārā | te dakṣiṇena mārjālīyadhiṣṇyaṃ parītya paścānmaitrāvaruṇadhiṣṇyasyopaviśya rathantareṇa pañcadaśena stuvīran | stutvā varacodanā | tathā copagranthaḥakevalastotre vai khalu bṛhadrathantare vrate dharmāllabheteteti udañcaḥ sarpeyurjaghanena hoturdhiṣṇyam paścādbrāhmaṇācchaṃsino dhiṣṇyasyopaviśya bṛhatā saptadaśena stuvīran | te yenaiva prasarpeyustena punaḥ niḥsṛpya havirdhānasya pūrvayā dvārā niṣkramyottareṇāgnīdhrīyaṃ parītya paścādgārhapatyasyopaviśya pucchenaikaviṃśena stuvīran | ekaviṃśaṃ bhadraṃ pucchamiti | bhadreṇa stavanam | te yenaiva nisarpeyuḥ tena punaḥ prasṛpyottareṇāgnīdhrīyaṃ parītya havirdhānasya pūrvayā dvārā prapadya saṃrabdhāḥ pratyañca eyuḥ | te dakṣiṇena dhiṣṇyān parītya
gavāmayanam mahāvṛtam paścānmaitrāvaruṇasya dhiṣṇyaṃ gatvā yathāyātamupaviśeyuḥ | athāparayā dvāraudumbarīmāsandīṃ mauñjena pūrvaṃ kṛtabandhanāṃ sadasi praviśeyuḥ
yajamānaparicārakā. | udumbarābhāve yajñiyasya vṛkṣasya palāśavyatiriktasyāsandī kartavyā | muñjābhāve darbharaśanayā vā bandhanaṃ kartavyam | āsandyāḥ prādeśamātrāḥ pādāḥ kāryāḥ | aratnimātrāṇītarāṇyaṅgāni | evaṃbhūtāmāsandīṃ dakṣiṇenaudumbarīṃ hṛtvā tasyā uttarato nidadhyuḥ | tāmuttareṇodgātā gatvā paścādupaviśya bhūmispṛśo'syāḥ pādān kṛtvā kūrcāvadhastādupohyābhimṛśet | bṛhadrathantare te pūrvau pādāviti pūrvapādadvayam | śyaitanaudhase aparau | vairūpavairāje anūcī iti prācīnaphalakadvayam | śākvararaivate tiraścī ityudīcīnaphalakadvayam | evaṃ pṛthagaṅgānyabhimṛśya vivayanaṃ mauñjarajjubhirbandhanam | ālabhya japedṛcaḥ | prāñca ā tā nā iti yajñāyajñīyamityantam | tatra vivayanānāṃ yathāliṅgamālambhaḥ kāryaḥ | tāmāsandīmukhenorasā bāhubhyāṃ ca spṛṣṭvārohet | vasavastvetyādibhiścaturbhirmantraiḥ sāmrājyāyetyantairārohaṇam | āruhya japet | syonāmāsadaṃ sukhadāmāsadaṃ namaste'stu mā mā hiṃsāriti | evamārohatyudgātari prastotṛpratihartārau brahmā ca gṛhapatiścāhīnapakṣe satre sarve kūrcānāroheyuḥ | āsandyā vyākhyātaṃ dravyaṃ vāṇasya | vāṇaśabdena vīṇāviśeṣa ucyate | sa udumbarasya yajñiyavṛkṣasya vā kartavyaḥ | tasya
daṇḍasyādhastādbhāge parimaṇḍalākāraṃ daṇḍaparimāṇāt sthaviṣṭhaṃ vakrarūpaṃ bilaṃ bhavati | tasmin pradeśe lohitenānaḍuhenottaralomnā carmaṇā pracchādito bhavati | tasyopari bhāge daśa chidrāṇi bhavanti | teṣūparyupari daśa tantryo baddhāḥ syuḥ mauñjyo dārbhyo vā | yadvā | daśānāmapi chidrāṇāṃ madhye śatatantryo baddhāḥ syuḥ | katham | catustriṃśanmadhyame | trayastriṃśāvabhitaḥ | tadagre tvekaikaśo bandhanaṃ kartavyam | tāstraidhaṃ vibhajya bhūrbhuvaḥsvarityekaikayā vyāhṛtyottarottarayodūhet | bhūriti trayastriṃśatamudūhet | bhuvariti catustriṃśatamudūhet | svariti trayastriṃśatamudūhet | etaccodūhanaṃ pakṣadvaye'pi kāryam | taṃ vāṇamabhimṛśet | vadovadeti vāṇetyantena | śithilāṃstantūnāyacchet | ebhirno vāṇetyaśīmahītyantena | vāk sarvaṃ mano jyotirmano bhadra iti japitvā vāṇamindraṇatayeṣikayā vetasaśākhayā ca sapalāśayā mūladeśe vādayet | yā svayaṃ vakrā sendraṇatā | prāṇāya tvetyūrdhvamullikhet | apānāya tvetyavāñcamavalikhet | vyānāya tvā vyānāya tveti triḥsaṃlikhyodañcaṃ vāṇaṃ prohet | imaṃ vāṇamullikhannāsveti brāhmaṇamuktvā āhatadundubhīn pravadantu | vīṇā iti brūyāt | alābuvīṇāṃ vakrākapiśīrṇyau ca pūrvasyāṃ dvāri bahissadasaṃ vīṇāvādino vādayeyuḥ |
gavāmayanammahāvratam alābuvīṇā prasiddhā | vakrā samatantrīkā vetravīṇetyucyate | kapiśīrṣṇī markaṭaśīrṣṇī | vakrākapiśīrṣṇyau dundubhīṃśca pratimantrayeta yo vakrāyāmityādi vadetthantena | mahāvīṇāpiśīlavīṇe ca aparasyāṃ dvāryantarbahirvā vādayeyuḥ | caturdaśatantrikā alābuvīṇā mahāvīṇā | śūrpā vīṇā piśīlavīṇā | kṛṣṇatantrikā kanakatantrikā vā | te ca pratimantrayeta | alābavī piśīlī cetyādi mahayiṣyata ityantena | paścimenodgātṝn patnyaḥ ekaikā dve dve vīṇe kāṇḍavīṇāṃ picchoravīṇāṃ ca vyatyāsaṃ vādyeyuḥ | kāṇḍavīṇā śaramayī |
picchorā vaiṇavī | mayūrapucchamayī vā | kāṇḍamayīvādanena picchorāmupamukhaṃ vādayet | anayorapaghāṭileti saṃjñā | tāśca pratimantrayeta | yāṃ patnyapaghāṭilāmiti tvagityantena sarvamanuvīkṣamāṇo japet | ākrandā ulūlaya iti gīyata ityantena | atha japedudgātā suparṇo'sīti yajamānānityantam | ekayajamānake svargamayiṣyannimaṃ yajamānamityūhaḥ kāryaḥ | brāhmaṇaḥ pūrvasyāṃ sadaso dvāri pratyaṅmukha upaviśet | so'bhigara ityucyate | vṛṣalo'parasyāṃ dvāri prāṅmukha upaviśet | so'pagaraḥ | sa śūdro brūyānnārātsurime satriṇa iti | arātsurityabhigaro brūyāt| ekāhe'hīne ca nārātsīdayaṃ yajamāna ityapagaraḥ | arātsīdityabhigaraḥ | dakṣiṇena mārjālīyaṃ vaiśyo'ntarvedi dakṣiṇāmukhastiṣṭhet | bahirvedi śūdra udaṅmukhastiṣṭhet vaiśyābhāve yaḥ kaścāryo varṇaḥ | tau śvetaṃ parimaṇḍalaṃ carma prativyāyacchetām | śūdraḥ pūrvo vyāyacchet | āryaḥ (aryaḥ?) paścāt | pūrveṇāgnidhrīyaṃ brahmacāryantarvedyudaṅmukhastiṣṭhet | bahirvedi puṃścalī vyabhicāriṇī strī dakṣiṇāmukhī tiṣṭhet | sā brahmacāriṇaṃ brūyāt | duścaritinnavakīrṇiniti | brahmacārī ca tāṃ pratibrūyāt | dhiktvā jālmīti śiśnaprāṇe janītyantam | punaḥ puṃścalī pūrvavat | brahmacārī ca | punaḥ puṃścalī pūrvavat |
gavāmayanammahāvratam brahmacārī ca | sarveṣāmabhigarāpagarādīnāṃ karmaṇi niṣṭhite abhigaro'rātsurime satriṇa iti brūyāt | ekayajamāne tvarātsīdayaṃ yajamāna iti carmamaṇḍalaṃ muktvā śūdraḥ pradravet | taṃ tenaiva carmaṇā vaiśyastāḍayet | yathākāmaṃ puṃścalīṃ brahmacārī paruṣaṃ vadet | paścimenāgnidhrīyaṃ bahirvedi pariśrite mithunau saṃbhavetām | yau varṇau labheran | saṃbhavo maithunam | gṛhapaterdāsyo'bhīṣṭā vā striyaḥ pañcāvarārdhyāḥ pañcaśataṃ parārdhyāḥ pañcaviṃśatirvā navān kumbhānudakaiḥ pūrayitvā pradakṣiṇaṃ mārjālīyaṃ parīyuḥ haimahā idaṃ madhvidaṃ madhviti vadantyaḥ | tābhyo'pi vīṇā vādayeyuḥ | sadaso vyatiriktāsu sarvāsu sraktiṣu dundubhīnābadhnīyuḥ | vedavedāṅgetihāsapurāṇādivāco laukikāni vādyādighoṣāṃśca yathālābhamuttarottari vyāhareyuḥ | rājanasya hiṃkāraṃ prati abhigaraprabhṛtayaḥ svaṃ svaṃ karma kṛtvā gaccheyuḥ | brāhmaṇamuktvemaṃ hiṃkāravelāyāṃ kārayedityuktam devayajanapradakṣiṇādigodarśanāntaṃ hiṃkāravelāyāṃ kuryāt | ā stotrāntāt kumbhinyaḥ sarve ca ghoṣā vīṇādayo vartante | atha rājanavratam | pañcaviṃśastomaḥ | pañcaviṃśa ātmā rājanamiti vacanāt | rājanaṃ
pañcaviṃśaṃ viduḥ | indrastriṣṭubindraḥ | bhaktaya uktāḥ | uttamāyāṃ stotrīyāyāṃ prastutāyāṃ pādau prasārayet | nidhanavelāyāṃ bhūmau pratiṣṭhāpayet | yajamānavacanāt ṛgjapāntaṃ kṛtvā āhriyamāṇe bhakṣe pratilomairārohaṇīyairmantrairavarohayet | viśve tvā devā ānuṣṭubhena chandasāvarohantu | tānanvavarohāmi sāmrājyāyeti prathamo mantraḥ | ādityāstvā jāgatena chandasāvarohantu tānanvavarohāmi svārājyāyeti dvitīyaḥ | rudrāstvā traiṣṭubhena vairājyāyeti tṛtīyaḥ | vasavastvā gāyatreṇa rājyāyeti caturthaḥ | etaiścaturbhiravaruhya japet | mahī dyauriti| bharīmabhirityantam | atigrāhyāṇāṃ bhakṣaṇam triṣṭup chandasā pūrvaṃ camasebhyaḥ | atha nārāśaṃsabhakṣaṇam || 6 ||
vratasaṃjñakāt pṛṣṭhādanyeṣāṃ stomaklṛptimāhapañcaviṃśamitarat sarvam || 7 ||
iti || 7 ||
vāmadevyaṃ maitrāvaruṇasāma trikadrukeṣu mahiṣo yavāśiram iti pañcanidhanaṃ vāmadevyaṃ brahmasāma svāsu kāleyam || 8 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
adha āsīnā vāmadevyādibhiḥ stuvīran | vāmadevo'ticchandā indraḥ || 8 ||
gavāmayanammahāvratam 
athārbhavaḥ
parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaś ca | pavasvendramacche ti sapha śrudhye | purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca madhuścunnidhanaṃ ca śyāvāśvāndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvam antyam || 9 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ]
gaurīvitamekasyām | ārbhave cedeko'nuṣṭubhīti vacanāt | ekamekarcaṃ kurvan prathamāyāmāditaḥ kuryāditi ca || 9 ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 10 ||
ilāndaṃ vā | ilāndamagniṣṭomasāma kārya miti śruteḥ | satavaśyāvīyāni sāmāni vrata iti nidānavacanācca | agniḥ siddhā viṣṭārapaṅktiḥ agnirityṛṣyādayaḥ | bhaktayastu pūrvamevoktāḥ | ādyenānugānena kuśāstaraṇam | ilāndapakṣe anuṣṭubhi śyāvāśvasthāne yajñāyajñīyaṃ kāryam | atha yatrānyadagniṣṭomasāma bhavati tadyajñāyajñīyamanuṣṭubhi bhavati iti nidānam | agniṣṭomānte stomavimocanādyapsuṣomāntam | atra viśvajipṛṣṭhyadaśarātramahāvrateṣu astūyamānamapīndrakratuṃ na ābhareti brāhmaṇācchaṃsinānu
śaṃsayet | sarvatrendrakratu brāhmaṇācchaṃsinordhvaṃ viṣuvata prāgatirātrāditi vacanāt || 10 ||
iti mahāvratam ||
udayanīyaḥ
athodayanīyātirātrasya tasya sarvatra satrasyarddhyādi | tasya klṛptimāha
klṛpta udayanīyo'tirātro yathā prāyaṇīyo yathā prāyaṇīyaḥ || 1 | iti | tasya stomavimokādi dadhibhakṣaṇānte kṛte'vabhṛthamabhyaveyuḥ | taduktamudayanīye'tirātre saṃsthite'vabhṛtamabhyaveyu riti | udavasanīyānte'nyānṛtvijo vṛtvā pṛthak pṛṣṭhaśamanīyairyajerana | uktaḥ pṛṣṭhaśamanīyaḥ || 1 ||
iti udayanīyaḥ || 12 ||
itivāmanācāryasūnuḥ kuśikānvayasaṃbhavo varadarājaḥ |
saṃvatsarakalpavyākhyāṃ cakre saha prayogeṇa ||
gavāmayanabhedā ye stotroktāsteṣu kecana |
viniyogajñāpanārthamūhasāmnāmihoditāḥ ||
ye tvanye vistarabhayādatrāsmābhirna darśitāḥ |
te sūtra eva draṣṭavyāḥ prīyatāṃ puruṣottamaḥ ||
iti ārṣeyakalpasūtravyākhyāyāṃ śrīvāmanāryasutavaradarājakṛtāyāṃ dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ || 2 ||
<3>
kḷpto jyotiṣṭomo 311 3.1
gorvarkajambhasya loke dairghaśravasaṃ dairghaśravasasya bārhaduktham̐ svāsu śyaitaṃ gaurīvitasya śyāvāśvamudvaṃśīyasya nārmedhaṃ yadi rātriḥ samānamitaram̐ sāṃvatsarikeṇa 312 3.2
āyuṣo vārkajambhasya loke maidhātithaṃ maidhātithasya rauravam̐ svāsu naudhasaṃ gaurīvitasya śyāvāśvam udvam̐śīyasya nārmedhaṃ yadi rātriḥ samānamitaram̐ sāṃvatsarikeṇa 313 3.3
abhi pra gopatiṃ giretyabhijito brahmaṇa ājyaṃ yadyatirātraḥ sāmatṛcasya loke śyāvāśvaṃ pramam̐hiṣṭhīyam̐ hārivarṇaṃ nārmedhaṃ pratyasmai pipīṣata iti nānadam̐ ṣoḍaśisāma rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ samānamitaram̐ sāṃvatsarikeṇa 314 3.4
viśvajitaḥ pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye saphaśrudhye yadyatirātraḥ sāmatṛcasya loka āndhīgavaṃ jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimāntsamānamitaram̐ sāṃvatsarikeṇa 315 3.5
vratasyā no viśvāsu havyamiti śyaitaṃ brahmasāma gaurīvitasya loka audalam̐ samānamitaram̐ sāṃvatsarikeṇa 316 3.6
pavasva vāco agriyo davidyutatyā rucā pavamānasya te kave agna ā yāhi vītaya iti rāthaṃtaraṃ trīṇi bārhatānyasya pratnāmanu dyutamiti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ ca rathaṃtaraṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cāyam̐ soma iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca svādoritthā viṣūvata iti śyaitam̐ svāsu kāleyam̐ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasam̐hite pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśatrivṛntyājyāni saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamāno hotuśca pṛṣṭhaṃ pañcadaśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya pañcavim̐śaṃ brāhmaṇāccham̐sinaḥ saptadaśamacchāvākasyaikavim̐śa ārbhava ekavim̐śo'gniṣṭomaḥ 32 3.7
eṣaivottarasya kḷptistasya trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśānyājyāni saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānastrīṇi ca pṛṣṭhāni pañcavim̐śaṃ brāhmaṇāccham̐sinaḥ saptadaśa ārbhava ekavim̐śo'gniṣṭomo 331 3.8
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityubhayānyājyāni śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe āndhīgavāduttaramātharvanam̐ sākamaśvam̐ saubharaṃ traikakubhaṃ vodvam̐śīyamityukthāni caturvim̐śa ārbhava ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ sokthyaḥ samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa sastomam 332 3.9
upāsmai gāyatā nara upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ pavasva vāco agriyo davidyutatyā rucā pavamānasya te kave agna ā yāhi vītaye purūruṇā cidhyastīndramidgāthino bṛhadindre agnā namo bṛhaduccā te jatamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca pavasva madhumattama itīḍānām̐ saṃkṣāra ekasyāṃ pratnaṃ pīyūṣaṃ pūrvyaṃ yadukthyamiti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu punānaḥ soma dhārayeti rauravamekasyām̐ rathaṃtaraṃ tisṛṣu śiśuṃ jajñānam̐ haryataṃ mṛjantīti vāmadevyamantyaṃ bṛhatpṛṣṭhaṃ vāmadevyasyarkṣu svāram̐ sauparṇam̐ śrāyantiyaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gayatrasam̐hite pari pra dhanvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśāni trīṇyājyāni trivṛnmaitrāvaruṇasya saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamāno hotuśca pṛṣṭhaṃ pañcadaśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya pañcavim̐śaṃ brāhmaṇāccham̐sinaḥ saptadaśamacchāvākasya saptadaśa ārbhava ekavim̐śo'gniṣṭomaḥ 34 3.10
upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavamāno ajījanat pavamānasya te kava itīmamū ṣu tvamasmākaṃ mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe ahamiddhi pituṣparīyaṃ vāmasya manmana uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatramekasyām āmahīyavamekasyām̐ śākvaravarṇamekasyāṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti vaiṣṭaṃbhamekasyāṃ kāleyaṃ ekasyāṃ yaudhājayamekasyām auśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyasyarkṣu saubharaṃ kāleyasyarkṣu vāravantīyam̐ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāṃ cārkaḥ pavasvendramaccheti saphāyāsye purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma 35 3.11
eṣaivottarasya kḷptistasyaikavim̐śamagniṣṭomasāmobhau trivṛtau 361 3.12
śākvaravarṇātpūrvam̐ svāram̐ sauparṇamāyāsyasya loke pauṣkalam̐ śyāvāśvātpūrvamaiḍamāyāsyaṃ caturviṃśāvuttarau pavamānau tṛcakḷptau samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa sastomam̐ 362 3.13
rathaṃtaradairghaśravase antaraiḍamāyāsyamekasyām̐ sam̐hitāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyam̐ samānamitaraṃ viśvajitaikāhikenāgniṣṭomenāṣṭādaśāvuttarau pavamānāvekavim̐śam̐ hotuḥ pṛṣṭhamekaviṃśo 'gniṣṭomastrivṛditaram̐ sarvam 363 3.14
pavamānasya jighnataḥ punāno akramīdabhi pavamānasya te kave agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghananmitram̐ huve pūtadakṣamu tvā mandantu somāstā huve yayoridamarṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatramekasyāṃ vārṣāharaṃ ekasyām̐ śākvaravarṇamekasyāṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti vaṣaṭkāraṇidhanamekasyām̐ rauravamekasyāṃ yaudhājayamekasyāmauśanamantyam̐ ratham̐taraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca bṛhacca kāleyaṃ ca yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatra vārṣāhare pavasvendramaccheti saphaupagave purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadāndhīgave kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma trivṛtaḥ pavamānā agniṣṭomasāma ca tisṛbhirājyapṛṣṭhāni 37 3.15
upāsmai gāyatā nara ityekayā bahiṣpavamānam̐ sādyaḥkrāṇyājyānīndrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti utsedha auśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyasyarkṣi niṣedhaḥ svāsu kāleyamayā rucā hariṇyā punāna iti gāyatrapārśvaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāmaikatrika stoma ekayātha tisṛbhiḥ 38 3.16
jyotiṣṭomaṃ bahiṣpavamānamagne viśvebhiragnibhirā no mitrāvaruṇā tvetā niṣīdatendrāgnī āgatam̐ sutamuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca pavasva madhumattama iti kāleyaṃ tve soma prathamā vṛktabarhiṣa iti yaudhājayaṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti rauravamauśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cādhā hīndra girvaṇa endumindrāya siñcateti dyautānamārute svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasam̐hite pari pra dhanva nadam̐ vā odatīnāmiti saphaśrudhye prayū ṣviti marutām̐ sam̐stobhaḥ purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave kāvamantyaṃ devo vo draviṇodā iti yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśāni trīṇyājyāni ṣoḍaśamacchāvākasya ṣoḍaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ saptadaśāni trīṇi pṛṣṭhāni ṣoḍaśamacchāvākasya ṣoḍaśa ārbhava ekavim̐śo'gniṣṭomaḥ 39 3.17
ete asṛgramindavo yavaṃyavaṃ no andhaseti purastātparyāsasya tṛcacaturṛce āyuṣa ukthāni ṣoḍaśāḥ pavamānā acchāvākasāmāni caikavim̐śo'gniṣṭomo dve cokthe samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa sastomam 3101 3.18
indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca pavasva madhumattama iti kāleyaṃ tve soma prathamā vṛktabarhiṣa iti dairghaśravasaṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti raurava yaudhājaye śrudhyasya loke maruām̐! sam̐stobho marutām̐ sam̐stobhasya śyāvāśvaṃ purojitī vo andhasa ityāndhīgavaṃ trivṛtāvuttarau pavamānau samānamitaraṃ prathamena vrātyastomena sastomam 3102 3.19
agriyavatī pratipat pūrvasya prātaḥsavanam uccā te jātamandhasa iti gayatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca pavasva madhumattama iti kāleyaṃ tve soma prathamā vṛktabarhiṣa iti dairghaśravasaṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti rathaṃtaram̐ rauravayaudhājaye vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ cādhā hīndra gīrvaṇa endumindrāya śiñcateti dyautānamārute svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasam̐hite jarābodhīyaṃ ca svāśirāṃ cārkaḥ pari pra dhanva nadaṃ va odatīnāmiti saphaśrudhye paryū ṣviti marutām̐ sam̐stobhaḥ purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave ātharvaṇaṃ ca bṛhaccāgneyaṃ kāvamantyaṃ devo vo draviṇodā iti yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma dve trivṛtī stotre dve pañcadaśasaptadaśe dve ekavim̐śacaturviṃśe dve catuścatvārim̐śāṣṭācatvārim̐śe dve triṇavatrayastrim̐śe dve dvātrim̐śe 311 3.20
agna āyūm̐ṣi pavase agne pāvaka rociṣāgnirmurdhā divaḥ kakudagna ā yāhi vītaye kaste jāmirjanānāmīḍenyo namasyo 'gnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanadutte bṛhanto arcaya iti gāyatramekasyāmāmahīyavamekasyāṃ jarābodhīyamekasyāṃ pāhi no agna ekayeti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣvino rājannaratiḥ samiddha ityauśanamantyamenā vo agniṃ namasā kayā te agne aṅgira iti rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cāgna ā yāhyagnibhiracchā naḥ śīraśociṣamiti naudhasakāleye adābhyaḥ pura0eteti gāyatrasam̐hite bhadro no agnirāhuto 'gne vājasya gomata iti sapha pauṣkale viśoviśo vo atithimiti śyāvāśvāndhīgave samiddhamagnim̐ samidhā girā gṛṇa iti yajñāyajñīyamantyamupa tvā jāmayo gira iti vāyavyāsu vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma 312 3.21
eṣaivottarasya kḷptistasya revatīṣu vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāmo ubhau trivṛtāv 313 1 3.22
uddharati jarābodhīyaṃ yaudhājayātpūrvam̐ rauravaṃ pauṣkalasya loke śrudhyam̐ śyāvāśvasya yajñāyajñīyaṃ yajñāyajñīyasya kāvam̐ samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa jyotiṣṭoma stomo 313 2 3.23
avā no agna ūtibhiragnim̐ hinvantu no dhiya iti purastātparyāsasya tṛcapañcarce pāhi no agna ekayeti rauravaṃ tisṛṣu dairghaśravasamekasyām̐ samantaṃ viṣṭārapaṅktau yaudhjayaṃ tisṛṣu 19
samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa sarvaḥ saptadaśaḥ 3133 3.24
pavasvendo vṛṣā suta ete asṛgrajindavaḥ pavamānasya te kave vrātyānyājyānyuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatramekasyāmāmahīyavamekasyāṃ jarābodhīyamekasyāṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu svāsu naudhasasyarkṣvabhīvarto brahmasāma samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena 3134 3.25
eṣa evottarasya madhyaṃdino jyotiṣṭomamitaram̐ sarvam̐ 3135 3.26
śākvaravarṇasya loke jarabodhīyaṃ vaṣaṭkāraṇidhanasya saptahaṃ bṛhato vaṣaṭkāraṇidhanam̐ samānamitaracchyenena 3136 3.27
pra somāsa adhanviṣuḥ kpra svānāso rathā ivā sṛgramindavaḥ patho paprayanto adhvaraṃ pra vo mitrāya gāyata pra va indrāya mādanaṃ pra vāmarcantyukthinaḥ pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ pra soma devavītaya iti gauṅgavaṃ pra tu dravetyauśanamantyaṃ vāmadevyaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca hārāyaṇaṃ ca kaulmalabarhiṣaṃ ca pra somāso madacyuta iti gāyatraṃ cāśu bhārgavam abhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ pra dhanva soma jāgṛviriti sapha satrāsāhīye pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti śyāvāśvaudale pro ayāsīditi kāvamantyaṃ pra daivodāso agniriti yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma dairghaśravasam̐ svāsu yadi jīvet sarvaḥ svāraḥ sarve trivṛtaḥ śāntirvāmadevyam̐ śāntirvāmadevyam 314 3.28

tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ
atha ye brāhmaṇe'dhyāyaiścaturbhiḥ ṣoḍaśādibhiḥ |
ekāhā vihitāsteṣāṃ klṛptistribhirihocyate || 1 ||
jyotiṣṭomaḥ
ārambhakāladīkṣopasaddivasadakṣiṇādikaṃ sūtre udagayanapūrvapakṣetyādyaiḥ paṭalaiścaturbhiruktam | tatraudgātratantraṃ pravakṣyate'smābhiratra | na tvanyat | taddhyāpastambādyairadhvaryupratyayaṃ kathitam | tatra tāvat prajāpatirvā idameka āsīt ityanuvākoktasya jyotiṣṭomasya klṛptimāha
klṛpto jyotiṣṭomaḥ || 1 ||
prajāpatirakāmayata bahu syāṃ prajāyeti | sa etamagniṣṭomamapaśyat ityādi brāhmaṇena jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomasaṃsthaḥ klṛpta ityarthaḥ | tasyaivaikāhaprakaraṇe prathamaṃ saṃsthātvavidhānaṃ dakṣiṇāvidhānaṃ ca brāhmaṇena kriyate | taduktamśeṣavidheḥ punarāmnānamiti | tasya ca prayogaḥ prāgevāsmābhirdarśitaḥ || 1 ||
iti jyotiṣṭoma; || 1 ||
gauḥ
athaiṣa gaur ityanuvākena gauruktaḥ |
tasya klṛptimāha -- 
gauḥ || 1 ||
vārkajambhasya loke dairghaśravasam | dairghaśravasasya bārhaduktham | svāsu śyaitam | gaurīvitasya śyāvāśvam | udvaṃśīyasya nārmedham | yadi rātriḥ || 2 ||
samānamitaraṃ sāṃvatsarikeṇa || 3 ||
iti | gaurgavāmayane dvitīyādhyāyopāntyakhaṇḍena klṛptaḥ | tatra cāvṛttasyābhiplavasya yat ṣaṣṭhamaho rāthantaram tasya rāthantareṇāyuṣā saṃnipāte jāmitā syāditi tatparihārārthamāyuṣo mādhyaṃdine bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambhaṃ kalpitam | uktaṃ hi nidāneatha yatra samānasāmānte saṃnipātastajjāmītyācakṣate | yatra vā samānapṛṣṭhe'hanī iti | tatraitānyaṣṭau jāmikaraṇāni | saubharaṃ pratnavatyo bṛhadityeṣo'bhivyāhāro vārkajambhaṃ bṛhannidhanam | vṛhannidhanānītyāha rathantarasaṃnipāteṣviti ca | īnidhanaṃ tu vārkajambham | dvayoḥ sāmnorviprayogo mā bhūditi | āyuḥsahacarasya gormādhyaṃdine kṛtam | aikāhike tvāyuṣi jāmyabhavāt bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambhamuddhariṣyata iti goṣṭome tatsahacaramīnidhanamapyuddhṛtya tasya sthāne dairghaśravasaṃ nityaṃ kartavyam | asaṃcārāya ca tasya bārhaduktham | indrakratorgavāmayanottarapakṣaprayuktatvādiha svāsuśyaitam | gaurīvitasya satraprayuktatvāt tasya sthāne prākṛtaṃ śyāvāśvameva | yadi goṣṭomo'tirātrasaṃsthaḥ syāt tadodvaṃśīyasya nārmedhaṃ vicchandasa ṛco bhavanti | ahorātrayo rūpamiti tasya rātryanurūpatvaśravaṇāt | anyatsarvaṃ gavāmayanikena gavā samamityarthaḥ | tasyeyaṃ klṛptiḥpavasva vāco agriyodavidyutatyā rucā pavasvendo vṛṣā sutovṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi pavamānasya te kavaiti bahiṣpavamānam | agnindūmitraṃ vayamindramidgācaturṛcam | indre agnetyājyāni | asya pratneti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | parītoṣīti samantaṃ prathamāyām | dairghaśravasabārhadukthe tisṛṣu | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyam iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ | vṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramacche ti sapha śrudhye | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvaśvāndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyamityārbhavaḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśva saubhara mudvaṃśīyamityukthāni | atirātrapakṣe nārmedhamukthānāmantyam | pañcadaśaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | trivṛntyājyāni | saptadaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | ekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyaṃ savanam | soktham | pañcadaśī rātristrivṛtsaṃdhiriti stomaklṛptiḥ ||
yadyapyatirātrāṇāmahīnaprakaraṇe brāhmaṇāmnānāttatraivābhidhānaṃ yuktam, tathāpi prātisviko viśeṣo lāghavārthamiha kathyate | sāmānyatastvāhīnikī rātristatra vakṣyati | tatrānuktaṃ sarvaṃ jyotiṣṭomavat || 3 || iti gauḥ || 2 ||
āyuḥ
athaiṣa āyurityanuvākenāyuruktaḥ |
āyuṣaḥ || 1 ||
vārkajambhasya loke maidhātitham | maidhātithasya rauravam | svāsu naudhasam | gaurīvītasya śyāvāśvam | udvaṃśīyasya nārmedham | yadi rātri || 2 ||
samānamitaraṃ sāṃvatsarikeṇa || 3 ||
iti |
gavāmayane jyotiṣṭomamājyabahiṣpavamānamityanuvākenāyuḥ klṛptaḥ | tasyaikāhikasyokte dravyavikāre | nidānaṃ pūrvānusāreṇa sugamam | upadavipaveti bahiṣpavamānam | agnaā no mitra āyāhi indrāgnītyājyāni || uccā ta iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca abhisomāsa iti dvihiṃkāraṃ prathamāyām | maidhātithaṃ tisṛṣu | rauravaṃ tisṛṣu | yaudhājaya -- madhyāsyāyām | auśanamantyam ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyamiti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale | purojitīti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | kāvamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni | pramaṃhiṣṭhīyasya kakup chandaḥ | hārivarṇasyoṣṇik | atirātraścedāyuḥ | tadodvaṃśīyasya sthāne nārmedham ||
trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśānyājyāni ||
saptadaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | ekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanam | soktham | pañcadaśī rātriḥ | trivṛtsaṃdhiriti stomaḥ | uktasya jyotirgaurāyuriti trikasya trikadrukā iti saṃjñā | ete vai trikadrukā stomā iti śruteḥ || 3 ||
iti āyuḥ || 3 ||

abhi pra gopatiṃ giretyabhijito brahmaṇa ājyam | yadyatirātraḥ || 1 ||
brahmaśabdena brāhmaṇācchaṃsī gṛhyate | tadanuśaṃsanīyatvāt | tṛtīyasyājyasya tena saṃbandhaḥ | atra nidānam abhijito'tirātrī bhavato brahmaṇa ājyamuddharati | ārṣabhastotrīyaṃ mā cīcaran iti || 1 ||

iti | gaurīvitamekasyām | madhuścunnidhanamekasyām | śyāvaśvamekasyām iti yo'yaṃ sāmatṛcaḥ tisṛṣu stotrīyāsu kramāt kriyamāṇairekarccaiḥ sāmabhiḥ kalpitastṛcaḥ tasya sthāne śyāvāśva mityarthaḥ | atra ca yadyatirātra ityanuṣajyate tenāgniṣṭomātirātrayordvayorapi śyāvāśvameva | gaurīvitaṃ hi satraprayuktam || 2 ||

ukthādikaṃ sāma syādatirātraviṣayam ||
tatrāgniṣṭomasyeyaṃ klṛptiḥ | pavasva vāco agriyaḥ punāno akramīdabhi pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ā no mitrāvaruṇāabhi tvā vṛṣabhā suta indrāgnī āgataṃ sutam ityājyāni ||
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca satrāsāhīyaṃ ca | punānaḥ sometyabhīvartaḥ rauravayaudhājaye | abhi vāyumiti pārtha mantyam ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti
