pavamānasya jighnataḥ punāno akramīdabhi pavamānasya te kave || 1 || iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat mitraṃ huve pūtadakṣam u tvā mandastu somā tā huve yayoridam || 2 ||
ityājyāni ||
arṣā soma yumattama iti gāyatramekasyām | vārṣāharamekasyām | śākvaravarṇamekasyām | punāna. soma dhārayeti vaṣaṭkāraṇidhanamekasyām | rauravamekasyām | yaudhājayamekasyām | auśana mantyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
gāyatrībṛhatyau sāmatṛcau || 3 ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca bṛhacca kāleyam || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
rathantarabṛhatī parācīṣu kārye | tathā caikāheṣvadhīmahe | śrutiścātra parācīṣu rathantaraṃ bhavatīti || 4 ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatravārṣāhare | pavasvendramacche ti saphaupagave | purojitī vo andhasaḥ iti nānadāndhīgave | kāvam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ]
kāvavarjamakercāḥ || 5 ||
yajñāyajñīyamaniṣṭomasāma || 6 ||
stomaklṛptimāha
trivṛtaḥ pavamānāḥ | agniṣṭomasāma ca | tisṛbhirājyapṛṣṭhāni || 7 || iti | ekasyai hiṃkaroti | sa prathamayā | ekasyai hiṃkaroti sa prathamayā | ekasyai hiṃkaroti sa prathamayeti trikastomasya viṣṭutiḥ | ekasyai hiṃkaroti | sa prathamayā | ekasyai hiṃkaroti | sa madhyamayā | ekasyai hiṃkaroti sa uttamayeti vā | taduktamekinyāvartisthānagate triṣpurastāddhiṃkāraṃ śāṇḍilyaḥ trike ca prathamayā stomapūraṇam | tṛcena dhānaṃjayyaḥ sakṛccaikini hiṃkāram iti | viṣṭāvābhāvāttṛcabhāgasthāne kuśāvidhānam | ekaḥ paryāyaḥ | ekasyai hiṃkaroti sa prathamayā | tisṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa madhyamayā | pañcabhyo hiṃkaroti sa uttamayeti trivṛto viṣṭutiḥ | iṣorvyatyāsamiṣubhyām | tayoḥ pūrvā śyenasyāgniṣṭomasāmanīti vacanāt | vrātīnānāṃ yaudhānāṃ putrā anūcānā abhicāraṇīyāyāmṛtvijaḥ pracaranti | arhatāmeva putrā iti dhānaṃjayyaḥ | te ca lohitoṣṇīṣā lohitavāsaso nivītāḥ pracaranti | gṛhītakāṇḍā āropitadhanvānaśca dakṣiṇāvelāyāṃ
dakṣiṇārthānāṃ gavāṃ lohitaṃ janayeyuḥ | tīkṣṇaiḥ kaṇṭakairvitudeyuriti tatropāyaḥ sūtrāntaroktaḥ |
śyeneṣuvajrasaṃdaṃśā abhicāraprayojanāḥ |
sahaiva tadviṣṭutibhiruddhṛtāḥ pañcaviṃśakāt ||
ṣaḍviśabrāhmaṇe'dhītāḥ puruṣairiha gamyate |
kalpe tattatpradeśeṣu teṣāṃ klṛptirvidhānataḥ ||7||
iti śyenaḥ ||15||
ekatrikaḥ
athaiṣa ekatrika ityanuvākenaikatrika uktaḥ | ekatrike sadya. sarvaṃ kuryuḥ | ekadīkṣastryupasatkaḥ iti gautamaḥ | tasya klṛptimāha
upāsmai gāyatā nara ityekayā bahiṣpavamānam || 1 ||
sā caikā retasyaiva kāryā | retasyaikatrikasya bahiṣpavamānamiti vacanāt ||
sādyaskrāṇyājyāni || 2 ||
imamūṣu tvamasmākam ahamiddhi pituḥ pari iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni || 2 ||
indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatram punānaḥ soma dhāraye ti utsedhaḥ | auśanam antyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
ekarcāḥ sarve || 3 ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | śrāyantīyasyarkṣu niṣedhaḥ | svāsu kāleyam || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni | |
athārbhavaḥ
ayā rucā hiraṇyā iti gāyatrapārśvam || 5 ||
[ityārbhava pavamānaḥ ||]
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 6 ||
ākhyāsiddha evāsya stoma iti darśayati
ekatrikaḥ stomaḥ || 7 ||
etadeva vyācaṣṭe
ekayātha tisṛbhiḥ || 8 ||
iti | bahiṣpavamānam ekayā stotrīyayā | prathamaṃ tisṛbhiḥ | ḍitīyamekayā | tṛtīyaṃ tisṛbhiriti | evaṃ vyatyāsena ekatrikau stomāvityarthaḥ | ekasyai hiṃkaroti | sa prathamayeti ekastomasya
viṣṭutiḥ | sa ca hiṃkārastrirvā | sakṛdeva vā | trikasya
viṣṭutiruktā || 8 ||
iti ekatrikaḥ || 16 ||
prathamo vrātyastomaḥ
atha vrātyastomāścatvāro nikāyinaḥ | vrātyā upanayanādisaṃskārahīnāḥ | teṣāṃ prāyaścittārthā ime | vrātyastomaiḥ yakṣyamāṇāḥ ananyapūrvām asamānagotrāṃ bhāryātvena amantrakaṃ parigṛhya pṛthakpṛthagagnīnādhāya yajñasādhanāni ca saṃpādya dīkṣeran | jyotiṣṭomenāniṣṭveti nidānakārasya matam | iṣṭvaiveti sūtrakārasya | ya eṣāmadhyayanakuśalo bandhumānarthavān sa gṛhapatiḥ | bhakṣāṃśca tamanu bhakṣayeyuḥ | devā vai svargaṃ lokamāyan ityanuvākena prathamo vrātyastomaścatuṣṣoḍaśisaṃjña uktaḥ | tasya kalpe -- 
jyotiṣṭomaṃ bahiṣpavamānam || 1 ||
agne viśvebhiragnibhi ā no mitrāvaruṇā ā tvetā niṣīdatendrāgnī āgataṃ sutam || 2 ||
ityājyāni ||
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | pavasva madhumantam iti kāleyam | tve soma prathamā vṛktabarhiṣa iti yaudhājayam | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti rauravam auśanam antyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
kāleyamekarce | kakubyatra madhyaṃdina ekarcaḥ | tasyāmanyatrottamād vrātyastomāditi vacanāt | tve someti sato bṛhatī || 3 ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cādhāhīndra girvaṇaḥ endumindrāya siñcateti dyautānamārute || 4 | iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
dyautānaraya kakup prathamāthoṣṇik atha purauṣṇiganuṣṭubiti chandāṃsi | mārutasyoṣṇik || 4 ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pari pra dhanva nadam̐ va odatīnām iti saphaśrudhye | paryūṣviti marutām̐ saṃstobhaḥ | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave kāvam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
pari pra dhanveti akṣarapaṅktiḥ | nadaṃ va ityuṣṇik | paryūṣvityanuṣṭup pipīlikāmadhyā | atra caturṣvekarceṣu kartavyeṣu dviprabhṛtīn kakupaprabhṛtyānantaryeṇeti vacanāt kakupsthānāpannāyāṃ dvipadāyāṃ kriyamāṇaṃ saphamārabhya caturṇāmekatve prāpte marutām̐ saṃstobhasyāraṇyegeyatvenāpacitatvāt tṛce kāryaṃ | gāyatraṃ caikarce | prathamātikrame hetvabhāvāditi gāyatrasaṃhitasaphaśrudhyaśyāvaśvānyekarcāni ityekaṃ matam | gāyatramukhyatvāt tṛca eva kāryamiti | saphādīni catvāri nairantaryeṇaikarcānītyaparam | taduktamgāyatramekarce dhānañjayyaḥ | tṛce marutāṃsaṃstobhaṃ viparītaṃ śāṇḍilyaḥ iti || 5 ||
devo vo drāvaṇoda iti yajñāyajñīyavai 4. 2..
maniṣṭomasām || 6 ||
atha stomavidhiḥ
trivṛd bahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśāni trīṇyājyāni | ṣoḍaśamacchāvākasya | ṣoḍaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | saptadaśāni trīṇi pṛṣṭhāni | ṣoḍaśamacchāvākasya | ṣoḍaśa ārbhavaḥ | ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ || 7 || iti | ṣoḍaśastomasyaivaṃ viṣṭutiḥ | pañcabhyo hiṃkaroti | sa tisṛbhiḥ | sa ekayā | sa ekayā | pañcabhyo hiṃkaroti | sa ekayā | sa tisṛbhiḥ | sa ekayā | ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa dvābhyām | sa ekayā | sa tisṛbhiriti | ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti |
sa ekayā | sa dvābhyām | sa tisṛbhiriti vā | taduktamadāśarātrikebhya ekayā jyāyaḥsu tasmāt pūrvasyottame paryāya āvapet | brahmāyatanīyā tu teṣu kṣatrāyatanīyā veti prathamāyāḥ prayoge bhūyiṣṭhe brahmāyatanīyā | madhyamāyāḥ kṣatrāyatanīyeti || 7 ||
iti prathamo vrātyastomaḥ || 17 ||
dvitīyaḥ ṣaṭṣoḍaśī vā vrātyastomaḥ
athaiṣa ṣoḍaśīti vihitaṃ dvitīyaṃ vrātyastomamāhaete asṛgramindavo yavaṃ yavaṃ no andhase ti purastāt paryāyasya tṛcacaturṛce | āyuṣa
ukthāni || 1 ||
ṣoḍaśāḥ pavamānāḥ | acchāvākasāmāni caikaviṃśo'gniṣṭomo dve cokthe || 2 ||
samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa sastomam || 3 ||
iti | paryāso bahiṣpavamānasyottamastṛcaḥ | upāsmai gāyatā naro davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo yavaṃ yavaṃ no andhasāpavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | athāgniṣṭomasāmāntaṃ pūrvavat | pramahiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni | ṣoḍaśaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśāni trīṇyājyāni | ṣoḍaśamacchāvākasya | ṣoḍaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | saptadaśāni trīṇi pṛṣṭhāni | ṣoḍaśamacchāvākasya | ṣoḍaśa ārbhavaḥ | ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | dve cokthe | ṣoḍaśamacchāvākasyeti
stomaklṛptiḥ || 3 ||
iti dvitīyaḥ vrātyastomaḥ || 18 ||
tṛtīyo dviṣoḍaśī vrātyastomaḥ
athaiṣa ṣoḍaśītyuktaṃ vrātyastomamāhaindrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | pavasva madhumattama iti kāleyam | tvaṃ soma prathamā vṛktabarhiṣa iti dairghaśravasam | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti raurava yaudhājaye | śrudhyasya loke marutām̐ saṃstobho marutāṃ saṃstobhasya śyāvāśvam | purojitī vo andhasa iti āndhīgavam || 1 ||
trivṛtāvuttarau pavamānau || 2 ||
samānamitaraṃ prathamena vrātyastomena sastomam | 3 ||
iti | upadavipaveti trivṛd bahiṣpavamānam | agne viśvebhir ā no mitrāvaruṇā ā tvetā niṣīdata indrāgnī āgataṃ sutamityājyāni | trīṇi pañcadaśāni | antyaṃ ṣoḍaśam | indrāyendo iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | pavasva madhumattama iti kāleyam | tve soma iti dairghaśravasam | punānasso rauravayaudhājaye | svāsvauśanamantyam | iti mādhyaṃdinastrivṛt | antyavarjamekarcāḥ ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cādhāhīndra girvaṇaḥ endumindrāyeti dyautānamārute | iti pṛṣṭhāni | trīṇi saptadaśāni | uttamaṃ ṣoḍaśam | svādiṣṭhaye ti gāyatrasahite | pari pra dhanve ti sapham | nadaṃ va omarutāṃ saṃstobhaḥ | paryūṣviti śyāvāśvam | purojitīti āndhīgavam | kāvamantyam | ityārbhavastrivṛt | antyavarjamekarcāḥ | chandāṃsi prathamavat | devo vo draviṇādā ḍati yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāmaika
viṃśam || 3 ||
iti tṛtīyo vrātyastomaḥ || 19 ||
caturtho vrātyastomaḥ (śamanīcāmeḍhrāṇāṃ stomaḥ)
athaiṣa śamanīcāmeḍhrāṇāṃ stomaḥ | ityuktaṃ caturthavrātyastomamāha -- 
agriyavatī pratipat | pūrvasya prātaḥsavanam || 1 ||
pavasva vāco davidyutatyāpavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agne viśvebhir ā no mitrāvaruṇā ā tvetā niṣīdatendrāgnī āgataṃ sutamityājyāni || 1 ||
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | pavasva madhumattama iti kāleyam | tve soma prathamā vṛktabarhiṣe iti dairghaśravasam | punānaḥ soma dhāraye ti rathantaraṃ rauravayaudhājaye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārtham antyam || 2 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ]
sarve tṛcāḥ || 2 ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ cādhāhīndra girvaṇa endumindrāya siñcateti dyautānamārute || 3 || iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā iti gāyatra0 saṃhite | jarābodhīyaṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pari pra dhanvanadaṃ va odatīnām iti saphaśrudhye | paryūṣu iti marutām̐ saṃstobhaḥ | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | ātharvaṇaṃ bṛhaccāgneyaṃ kāvam antyam || 4 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
saphaśrudhye ekarce | śiṣṭāstṛcaḥ | chandāṃsyuktāni || 4 ||
devo vo draviṇodā iti yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 5 ||
atha stomakalpaḥdve trivṛtī stotre | dve pañcadaśasaptadaśe | dve ekaviṃśacaturviṃśe | dve catuścatvāriṃśāṣṭācatvāriśe | dve triṇavatrayastriṃśe | dve dvātriṃśe ||6|| iti | dvātriṃśe tisro viṣṭutīḥ kuryāditi dhānaṃjayya | tāsāṃ paryāyakṛtiprayogau catuścatvāriṃśena vyākhyātau iti sūtram | tatra prathamā viṣṭutiryathā -- ekādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti | sa tisṛbhiḥ sa saptabhiḥ sa ekayā daśabhyo hiṃkaroti | sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ | sa ṣaḍbhirekādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti | sa saptabhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiriti | evamuttare'pi draṣṭavye | tāsāṃ ca savanasamīṣantī stotrasamīṣantīti prayogadvayaṃ ca catuścatvāriṃśavat | iha tu dvātriṃśacatuścatvāriṃśayordvayorapi prathamaikaviṣṭutiḥ kāryā | na tu samīṣantī | tathā kuryādaharbhāji pathyāmanyatreti vacanāt || 6 || iti caturtho vrātyastomaḥ || 20 ||
agniṣṭut prathamaḥ
athāgniṣṭutaścatvāro nikāyinaḥ | indro vai triśirasam ityanuvāke sāmānyenāgniṣṭutāṃ samaṣṭirvarṇyate | trivṛdaniṣṭomaḥ ityanuvākena prathama uktaḥ | etasyaiva revatīṣu dvitīyaḥ | jyotiṣṭomanāgniṣṭutā yajñavibhraṣṭo yajeteti tṛtīyaḥ | saptadaśenāgniṣṭutāṃ nādyakāma iti caturthaḥ |
sūtre tu ṣaṭ pañca vāgniṣṭuta ityapi pakṣadvayamupanyasya catvāra ityayameva pakṣaḥ sthāpitaḥ | ṣaḍagniṣṭutaḥ trivṛjjyotiṣṭomaścādyau svarkavāravantīyāvityādinā | agniṣṭutsu somāpyayanamantre āpyāyatāmagnaya ekadhanavida ā tubhyamagniḥ pyāyatāmā tvamagna pyāyasvetyūhaḥ | subrahmaṇyāyāṃ tu subrahmaṇyomiti trirvacanānantaramāgneyo nigado vaktavyaḥ | tatra gautamīyaṃ matamagna āgaccha rohitābhyāṃ bṛhadbhāno dhūmaketo jātavedo vicarṣaṇa āṅgirasabrāhmaṇāṅgirasa bruvāṇetyuktvā tryahe sutyāmagnayo brahmāṇa āgacchatetyādi brūyāditi | dhānaṃjayyamataṃ tu -- agna āgaccha rohitava āgaccha bharadvājasyājasahasassūnā vārāvaskandinnuṣasojāra āṅgirasabrāhmaṇāṅgirasabruvāṇa tryahe sutyāmāgacchāgnayo brahmāṇa āgacchatetyādi brūyāditi | āgacchateti pūrvaṃ devāhvānāddhānaṃjayya iti vacanāt | dhānaṃjayyamate'gnaya ityasmāt pūrvamāgacchateti vaktavyam | na tu gautamamate | tatra pūrvasminnigade vicarṣaṇetyatra viramet | uttarasmin jāretyatra | śeṣaṃ prakṛtivat ||
prathamamagniṣṭutamāha
agna āyūṃṣi pavase agne pāvaka śociṣāgnirmūrdhā divaḥ kakut ||1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṣṭutsu sarveṣāṃ stotrāṇāmāgnirdevatā || 1 ||
agna āyāhi vītaye kaste jāmirjanānām īḍenyo namasyo' gnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat || 2 ||
ityājyāni ||
utte bṛhanto arcaya iti gāyatram ekasyām | āmahīyavamekasyām | jarābodhīyamekasyām | pāhi no agna ekayā iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | ino rājannaratiḥ samiddhaḥ iti auśanam antyam || 3 || [iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | |]
enā vo agniṃ namasā kayā te agne aṅgiraḥ iti rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | agna āyāhyagnibhir acchā naḥ śīraśociṣam iti naudhasakāleye ||4 || iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
adābhyaḥ pura ete ti gāyatrasaṃhite | bhadro no agnirāhuto' gne vājasya gomataḥ iti saphapauṣkale | viśoviśo vo atithim iti śyāvāśvā ndhīgave | samiddhamagnim iti yajñāyajñīyam antyam || 5 |
[iti ārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ | |]
yajñāyajñīyaṃ tisṛṣu | śiṣṭānyādyāsu | saṃbhavati stome'ntyam sarvatra tṛca iti vacanāt | ekamekarcaṃ kurvan prathamāyāmāditaḥ kuryāt | dvau tasyāmeve ti ca || 5 ||
upa tvā jāmayo gira iti vāyavyāsu vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 6 ||
vāyavyāsviti vacanamuttaravyavahārārtham | yasya vāyavyāsvagniṣṭoma iti hi satre vakṣyati | sarvāṇi stotrāṇi trivṛnti | ubhau trivṛtau iti vakṣyamāṇatvāt || 6 ||
[iti agniṣṭut prathamaḥ || 21 ||]
agniṣṭut dvitīyaḥ
prathamasya klṛptiṃ dvitīyasyātidiśatieṣaivottarasya klṛptiḥ || 1 ||
iti | viśaṣamāha
tasya revatīṣu vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma | 2 | iti | tasyendro devatā || 2 ||
prathamadvitīyayostomamāhaubhau trivṛtau | 3 |
iti | itaḥprabhṛti subrahmaṇyāyāṃ devā brahmāṇa iti vā | uttareṣvagnaya iti sthāne subrahmaṇyāyāṃ devā iti brūyāt | aindratvādagniṣṭomasāmna ityeke iti nidānavacanāt ||3||
iti agniṣṭut dvitīyaḥ || 22 ||
agniṣṭut tṛtīyaḥ tṛtīyamagniṣṭutamāha uddharati jarābodhīyam | yaudhājayāt pūrvaṃ rauravam | pauṣkalasya loke śrudhyam | śyāvāśvasya yajñāyajñīyam | yajñāyajñīyasya kāvam || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa || 2 ||
jyotiṣṭomāḥ stomāḥ || 3 ||

agna āyūṃṣi pavase agne pāvaka rociṣā avā no agna ūtibhir agniṃ hinvantu no dhiyo'gniṃ mūrddhā iti bahiṣpavamānam || agna ā yāhi kaste jāmir īḍenyo namasyo'gnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat ityājyāni || utte bṛhanto gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | pāhi no agna iti rauravaṃ tisṛṣu | dairghaśravasamekasyām | samantaṃ viṣṭārapaṅktau | pāhi viśvasmād ityasyāmṛci yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | ino rājan iti auśanamantyam || enā vo kayā te agna iti rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | agna āyāhyagnibhir acchā naḥ śīraśociṣam iti naudhasakāleye iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
adābhyaḥ pura eteti gāyatrasaṃhite | bhadro no agne vājasyeti sapha śrudhye | viśoviśo vo atithim iti yajñāyajñīyāndhīgave | samiddhamagniṃ samidhā girā iti kāvamantyam | saphaśrudhya ekarce ||

prajāpaterapūrvaḥ

bṛhaspatisavaḥ
bṛhaspatisavo dvitīyaḥ | trivṛdagniṣṭomastasya prātaḥsavane ityanuvākenoktaḥ | yaṃ brāhmaṇāḥ svarājānaḥ puraskurvīran sa bṛhaspatisavena yajete tyādi sūtram | tena brāhmaṇasyaivasminnadhikāro na rājanyavaiśyayoḥ | tasya klṛptimāhaeṣa evottarasya mādhyaṃdinaḥ || 1 ||

yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvaṃ trivṛt | kecidatra kalpe mādhyaṃdinaśabdaṃ mādhyadinasavanavācinaṃ matvā prajāpaterapūrvasya mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanaṃ vṛhaspatisave'tidiśyate ityaṅgīkurvāṇā naudhasasyarkṣu abhīvarto brahmasāmetyāhuḥ | tadayuktam | yato'yaṃ mādhyaṃdinaśabdaḥ kalpe sarvatra mādhyaṃdinasya pavamānasyaiva vācako na tu savanasya | tathā hisomaḥ pavate janitramantyaṃ mādhyaṃdinasya | prata āśvinīriti lauśamantyam ārbhavasya | ityādau tāvat pavamānasyaiva vācaka iti suspaṣṭam | tathā dvitīyamekastomamadhikṛtya vakṣyati | gorājye bahiṣpavamānamindrastomasya mādhyaṃdinaḥ | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti bṛhatī | svāsu śyaitam | samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇeti | tatra svāsu śyaitamiti vacanāt avagamyate pavamānasyaivāyaṃ vācaka iti | yadi hi savanasya vācakaḥ syāttadā indrastome svāsveva śyetasya vidhānāditi tenaiva siddhatvādvacanamanarthakaṃ syāt | kiṃ ca bṛhaspatisavamevādhikṛtya madhye pṛṣṭhye vakṣyatiklṛptamā bṛhaspatistomāt | tasyābhīvarto brahmasāmeti | tatra yadi pūrvameva brahmasāmatvena vihitaḥ syāttadā punaḥ kiṃ tadvidhānena? tasmāttantaudhasameva brahmasāma bṛhaspatisavasyeti siddham || 2 ||
iti bṛhaspatisavaḥ || 26||

agna āyūm̐ṣi pavasa upāsmai gāyatā nara upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ pavamānasya te vayaṃ pavamānasya te kave śagdhyu ṣu śacīpata ityabhīvarto brahmasāma samānamitaraṃ bṛhaspatisavena sastomam 411
imaṃ me varuṇa śrudhī havamupāsmai gāyatā nara upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ pavamānasya te vayaṃ pavamānasya te kave sam̐hitasya loke hāviṣkṛtaṃ januṣaikarcayoḥ saphapauṣkale śyāvāśvasyaudalam̐ samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśamitaram̐ sarvaṃ 412
kayā tvaṃ na ūtyā pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyānyasya pratnāmanu dyutamiti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ cāyāsye ayam̐ soma iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca vaikhānasaṃ ca yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatrasam̐hite pavasvendrdamaccheti satrāsāhīya śrudhye ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti śyāvāśva krauñce dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyo yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma sākamaśvam̐ saubharamāṣṭādam̐ṣṭraṃ trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśam̐ hoturājyam̐ saptadaśamitaram̐ sarvam 42
upa tvā jāmayo gira upāsmai gāyatā nara upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ pavamānasya te vayaṃ pavamānasya te kave agna ā yāhi vītaya ityājyāni pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatramāśvamāśu bhārgavaṃ pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ gauṅgavaṃ yaudhājayam auśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cendramiddevatātaya iti yauktasrucam̐ svāsu kāleyam̐ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāṃ cārkaḥ pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale | sādhre pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudalasādhre audalagautame vā kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśam̐ hoturājyam̐ saptadaśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasyaikavim̐śamitaraṃ sarvam 43
indrāyendo marutvate pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ṃ! asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā gniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyāni vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ cā jigaṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇaṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ cā yāsye vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ca yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatram agnerarkaḥ surūpaṃ tvaṃ hyaṅga pavasva devavītaya iti śaṅkusujñāne ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti āsitaṃ krauñcaṃ bhargo vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma sākamaśvam̐ saubharam udvam̐śīyaṃ trivṛdbahiṣkpavamānaṃ pañcadaśam̐ hoturājyam̐ saptadaśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasyaikavim̐śaṃ brāhmaṇāccham̐sinastriṇavamitaram̐ sarvam 44
viśvakarmanhaviṣā vāvṛdhāno davidyutatyā rucai te asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyata iṣe pavasva dhārayā hotā devo amartyaḥ purūruṇā ciddhyasti tamindraṃ vājayāmasīyaṃ vāmasya manmana uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ carṣabhaśca pavamāno gauṣūktamabhisomāsa āyava iti paurumadgaṃ ca gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ cotsedhastisro vāca ityaṅgirasām̐ saṃkrośo'ntyo rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca pari svano girīsthā iti gāyatram̐ sam̐hitaṃ vāravantīyam̐ sa sunve yo vasūnām prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti dīrgha śrudhye purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvā ndhīgave niṣedhaḥ pari pra dhanveti vājadāvaryo yā ruceti nityavatsā dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyo yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma pramam̐hiṣṭhīyam̐ hārivarṇaṃ nārmedhaṃ gaurīvitam̐ ṣoḍaśisāma rātriḥ saṃdhistrivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśam̐ hoturājyam̐ saptadaśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasyaikavim̐śaṃ brāhmaṇāccham̐sinastriṇavamacchāvākasya trayastrim̐śamitaram̐ sāgniṣṭomasāmaikavim̐śānyukthāni saṣoḍaśikāni pañcadaśi rātristrivṛtsaṃdhiḥ 451
kḷpto'gniṣṭomaḥ śunāsīryasya loke 452
pavasvendo vṛṣā suta ete asṛgramindava uta no goṣaṇiṃ dhiyaṃ śaśamānasya vā nara upa naḥ sūnavo gira utte śuṣmāsa īrate pavamānasya te kave hotā devo amartyaḥ pra vāṃ mahi dyavī ayamu te samatasi gāva upa vadāvate pavamānasya jighnata iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ ca sākamukṣa iti pārthamantyaṃ mā bhema ma śramaṣmeti bṛhatsvāsu vāmadevyamimā u tvā purūvaso yasyāyaṃ viśva ārya iti śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca pavamāno ajījanaditi gāyatra sam̐hite ā sotā pari ṣiñcata gomanna indro aśvavaditi sapha śrudhye abhi no vājasātamamiti śyāvāśvā ndhīgave añjata iti kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma 46
dvitīyasya svarasāmna ājyabahiṣpavamānaṃ bārhadukthasya loka āṣṭādam̐ṣṭraṃ maukṣasya hāviṣmatam̐ śrudhyasya sujñānam uddharatyukthāni samānamitaraṃ gavaikāhikena 471
ete asṛgramindava utte śuṣmāsa īrata iti purastatparyāsasya tṛcapañcarce svāsu śyaitam̐ samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇa 472
pavamānasya viśvaviddavidyutatyā rucai te asṛgramindavastaṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhrataṃ pavamānasya te kave viśvebhiragne agnibhirmitraṃ vayam̐ havāmaha indraṃ vo viśvataspari tā huve yayoridamabhi tvā śūra nonuma iti kaṇvarathaṃtaraṃ pṛṣṭham ā no viśvāsu havyamiti sadoviśīyaṃ brahmasāma januṣaikarcayoḥ sapha pauṣkale pari tyam̐ haryatam̐ harimityanuṣṭuppavitraṃ ta iti kāvamantyamuddharatyukthāni samānamitaramāyuṣaikāhikena 473
ete asṛgramindavastaṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratamiti purastātparyāsasya tṛcapañcarce śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma saṃhitasya loke maukṣaṃ pauṣkalasya śrudhyam̐ śyāvāśvasya yajñāyajñīyaṃ yajñāyajñīyasyarkṣu viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma samānamitaraṃ āyuṣaikāhikena 474
pūrvasya bahiṣpavamānaṃ vrātyāni trīṇyājyāni vaiśvajitamuttamaṃ bārhadukthasya loke devasthānam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ pṛṣṭhamabhīvarto brahmasāma maukṣasya loke svāśirāmarkaḥ śyāvāśvasya yajñāyajñīyaṃ tvaṃ naścitra ūtyeti vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma samānamitaraṃ gavaikāhikena gaurīvitam̐ ṣoḍaśisāma viṣṇoḥ śipiviṣṭavatiṣu bṛhat sarve saptadaśāḥ kḷpto jyotiṣṭomaḥ pratyavarohaṇīyaḥ 475
kḷpto'bhyārohaṇīyaḥ 476
vāyo śukro ayāmi ta iti tṛcaḥ ṣaṭ saṃbhāryā vaiśvajityaḥ pavasva vāco agriyo davidyutatyā rucaitamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suta utte śuṣmāsa īrate pavamānasya te kavedhā kṣapā pariṣkṛto vaiśvajitānyājyānyuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cā mahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca svāraṃ ca saindhukṣitaṃ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ ca rathaṃtaraṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca devasthānaṃ ca varuṇasāma ca raurava yaudhājaye vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vamadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatra maukṣe kākṣīvataṃ ca svāśirāṃ cārkaḥ pavasvendaramaccheti sapha daivodāse purojitī vo andhasa iti vādhryaśvaṃ ca vaitahavyaṃ ca somasāma ca trāsadasyavaṃ cā ndhīgavaṃ ca kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāmā gne tvaṃ no antama iti satrāsāhīyam̐ saubharam udvam̐śīyaṃ dvātrim̐sāḥ pavamānāḥ pañcadaśānyājyāni saptadaśāni pṛṣṭhānyekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ sokthaḥ 48
rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratamiti purastātparyāsasya tṛcapañcarce maidhātithasya loke sadoviśīyam̐ rauravasya maidhātitham̐ śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma pauṣkalasya loke śrudhyam̐ śyāvāśvasya yajñāyajñīyaṃ vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsūddharatyukthāni samānamitaramāyuṣaikāhikena sarvaḥ saptadaśaḥ 491
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto davidyutatyā rucai te asṛgramindavo vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā pavamānasya te kave 'gniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyāni parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ ca yaśaścābhīśavaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ ca vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhatpṛṣṭhaṃ tvamindra pratūrtiṣvityatrābhīvarto brahmasāma samānamitaramāyuṣātirātreṇai kavim̐śaṃ prātaḥsavanam̐ saptadaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinam̐ savanaṃ pañcadaśaṃ tṛtīyasavanam̐ soktham̐ sarātrikaṃ trivṛtsaṃdhir 492
jyotiṣṭomamājyabahiṣpavamānam̐ stomāścā tha yadeva dvidivasasya pūrvamahastadetat 493
pavasva vāco agriya eṣa devo amartya eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvamagniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyānyasya pratnāmanu dyutamiti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca rṣabhaśca pavamānaḥ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ dairghaśravasamutsedho 'yam̐ soma iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca vaikhānasaṃ ca pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatra sam̐hite pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīya viśoviśīye ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti niṣedhaḥ śyāvāśvaṃ krauñcaṃ dhartā diva iti dīgrhatamaso'rko'ntyo yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimām̐ścaturvim̐śāḥ pavamānāḥ pañcadaśānitrīṇyājyāni saptadaśamacchāvākasyaikaviṃśāni trīṇi pṛṣṭhāni triṇavamacchāvākasya trayastrim̐śo'gniṣṭoma ekavim̐śānyukthāni saṣoḍaśikāni pañcadaśī rātristrivṛtsaṃdhiḥ 4101

upahavyaḥ
atha saptadaśāḥ ṣaṭ | teṣāṃ prathama upahavyaḥ saptadaśo'gniṣṭomo devāścāsurāśca ityanuvākenoktaḥ | tatra śrūyate so'niruktamāharat iti | tadaniruktatvaṃ sūtrakāreṇa pradarśitam | upahavye devatānāmadheyāni parokṣaṃ brūyuḥ svasthānāsu | pratyakṣam asvasthānāsu | devaśabdaṃ sarvatra varjayeyu iti | atropahavyagrahaṇamaniruktavidherupalakṣaṇam | tasmin svasthānānāṃ devatānāṃ nāmadheyāni parokṣaṃ brūyuḥ | asvasthānānāṃ pratyakṣam | devaśabdaṃ sarvatra svasthānāsu asvasthānāsu ca varjayeyuriti | prākṛtena prakāreṇa yā prāptā yatra devatā | svasthānaṃ(nā?)socyate tatra hyasvasthānā tato'parā ||
tatra kasya devatānāmadheyasya kena śabdena parokṣaṃ kartavyamityapekṣāyāmuktamhotā devo mahī mitrasyetyete hotā yajñe mahī yajñasyeti brūyuriti indurityatra somam iti | hoturājye agneḥ svasthānatvāt hotā devo amartya ityagnivācino devaśabdasya sthāne yajña iti brūyuḥ | maitrāvaruṇājye mitrasya svasthānatvāt mahāṃ mitrasya sādhaya ityatra mitrasyetyapohya yajñasyeti vaktavyam | pavamāne svasthānatvāt somo ya uttamaṃ havir ityatra suṣāva somamadribhira ityatra ca somaśabdasthāne induśabdaḥ prayoktavyaḥ | evamṛdhak soma svastaye ityādiṣu ca | parisvānaścakṣase devamādanaḥ ityatra devanāmasvasthāne'pi devaśabdasya viśvaśabdena pārokṣyaṃ kāryam | viśva iti devān iti vacanāt | devatāntarāṇāṃ tu kalpakāravihitāsvṛkṣu parokṣāṇyeva nāmadheyāni | yajñāyajñīye punaragniśabdasya pārokṣyaṃ na kartavyam | sa uttame stotre devo vo draviṇodā iti devānabhiparyāvartata iti brāhmaṇenoktastotre devaśabdaprayogābhyanujñānāt liṅgāt saṃsadāmayane tvaniruktasya trayastriṃśasya bahiṣpavamāne sa vāyumindramaśvina ityatrendravāyvādīnāmasvasthānatvāt pārokṣyaṃ na kāryam | vājapeye tamīḍiṣva yo arciṣa iti yadaindrāgnamājyaṃ tatrendrāgnīśabdayoraniruktagāne naiva pārokṣyaṃ siddham | ṛgjape tu stotrāṅgabhūtaṃ pārokṣyaṃ kāryameva | stotraṃ sāmaviṣayaṃ hīdam | ata eva parisāmasu na bhavati | tasmāt pārokṣyaviṣaye na kiñcidvaktavyamavaśiṣyate iti sūtrakārasyābhiprāyaḥ | subrahmaṇyāyāstu devatāhvānārthatvena stotravatprādhānyāt tatra pārokṣyaṃ syādeva | tathā coktamindraṃ śakreti brūyāt | viśva iti devānanirukteṣu iti ||
stomaklṛptimāha
pavasvendo vṛṣā suta ete asṛgramindavaḥ uta no goṣaṇiṃ dhiyam śaśamānasya vā naraḥ upa naḥ sūnavo giraḥ utte śuṣmāsa īrate pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
uta no goṣaṇiṃ dhiyamityādayastraya ekarcāḥ | teṣāṃ krameṇa marutaḥ pūṣā viśvedevā iti devatāḥ | tathā ca nidānamatha khalvāha mārutī bhavati pauṣī bhavati vaiśvadevī bhavatīti | teno vidyādetaddevatyā imā bhavantītyetaddeveṣu sūkteṣu samāmnātā iti || 1 || hotā devo amartyaḥ pra vāṃ mahi dyavi ayamu te samatasi gāva upa vadāvaṭe || 2 ||
ityājyāni ||
pavamānasya jighnataḥ iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ ca | sākamukṣa iti pārtham antyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
samantaṃ prathamāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām || 3 ||
mā bhema mā śramiṣmeti bṛhat svāsu vāmadevyam | imā u tvā purūvaso yasyāyaṃ viśva ārya iti śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pavamāno ajījanad iti gāyatrasaṃhite | ā sotā pariṣiñcata gomanna indro aśvavad iti saphaśrudhye | abhī no vājasātamam iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | añjata iti kāvam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
saphaśrudhye ekarce || 5 ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 6 ||
sarvo'yamupahavyaḥ saptadaśaḥ | sarve saptadaśā iti vakṣyati || 6 || iti upahavyaḥ || 8 ||
ṛtapeyaḥ
dvitīyaḥ ṛtapeyaḥ |
saptadaśo'gniṣṭomastasya dvādaśa dīkṣopasada ityanuvākoktaḥ | tatra sūtram ṛtapeye tasya dvādaśa dīkṣopasada ityādi |
atha kalpaḥ dvitīyasya svarasāmna ājyabahiṣpavamānam || 1 ||
bārhadukthasya loka āṣṭādaṃṣṭram | maukṣasya
hāviṣmatam | śrudhyasya sujñānam || 2 ||
ūddharatyukthāni | 3 || 0 samānamitaraṃ gavaikāhikena || 4 ||
iti | pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto
vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi utte śuṣmāsa īrate
pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam || agniṃ dūtam mitraṃ vayam indramidgā caturṛcam | indre agne tyājyāni | caturṛcasya tṛtīyāmuddharet | tṛtīyāṃ sarvasvārasvarasāmnoriti| vacanāt | atra ca svarasāmno nirdeśāt | dhiṣṇyopasthānaṃ kṛtvā sadaḥprasarpaṇakāle ṛtamuktvā prasarpeyuḥ | iyaṃ bhūmirasāvāditya udetā prātarāditya iti somabhakṣaṇakāle caitaduktvā prakṛtamantreṇa bhakṣaṇam ||
asya pratnāmanu dyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam miti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca āṣṭādaṃṣṭraṃ ca yaudhājayam | ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyam | samantamādyāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | pavasvendramacche ti saphasujñāne | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam | saphasujñāne ekarce ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvaḥ saptadaśaḥ ||
iti ṛtapeyaḥ || 9 ||
dūṇāśaḥ
tṛtīyo dūṇāśaḥ | saptadaśo'gniṣṭomastasya dīkṣaṇīyāyāmiṣṭauityanuvākenoktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ
ete asṛgramindavaḥ utte śuṣmāsa īrate iti purastāt paryāsasya tṛcapañcarce || 1 ||
svāsu śyaitam || 2 ||
samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇa || 3 ||
iti | pavasva vāco agriyo davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavaḥ utte śuṣmāsa īrate pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna āyāhi vītaye mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe indramidgāthino bṛhad indre agnā namo bṛhad ityājyāni ||
asya pratnā iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam samantaṃ ca rathantaraṃ ca
dairghaśravasaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ ca | ayaṃ soma hati pārtham antyam || samantamādyāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti sapha śrudhye | purojitīti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam | saphaśrudhye ekarce ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
sarvāṇi stotrāṇi saptadaśāni || 3 ||
iti dūṇāśaḥ || 10 ||
vaiśyastomaḥ
caturtho vaiśyastomaḥ saptadaśo'gniṣṭomastasya prātaḥsavanīyānītyanuvākenoktaḥ | tatra vaiśyasyaivādhikāraḥ | na brāhmaṇakṣatriyayoḥ | tasya kalpaḥ
pavamānasya viśvavid davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavastaṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhrataṃ pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
viśvebhiragne agnibhiḥ mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe indraṃ vo viśvataspari tā huve yayoridam || 2 ||
ityājyāni ||
abhi tvā śūra nonumaḥ iti kaṇvarathantaṃ pṛṣṭham | ā no viśvāsu havyam iti sadoviśīyaṃ brahmasāma | januṣaikarcayoḥ saphapauṣkale | pari tyaṃ haryataṃ harim ityanuṣṭup | pavitram iti kāvamantyam || 3 ||
uddharatyukthāni || 4 ||
samānamitaramāyuṣaikāhikena || 5 ||
iti | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyavaḥ iti dvihiṃkāraṃ vāmadevyaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ | dvihiṃkāraṃ vāmadevyaṃ prathamāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām |
abhi tvā śūra iti kaṇvarathantaraṃ pṛṣṭham | svāsu vāmadevyam | ā no viśvāsu havyam iti sadoviśīyaṃ brahmasāma | svāsu kāleyam iti pṛṣṭhāni | kaṇvarathantaraṃ sarvabṛhatīṣu | tathā ca nidānam -- sarvabṛhatīṣu
nānyad vṛhadrathantarābhyāmarhati yat kakubuttarāsu syāt 
iti ||
svādiṣṭhaye ti gāyatrasaṃhite |
ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir iti ekarcayoḥ sapha pauṣkale 
pari tyaṃ haryataṃ harim hati śyāvāśvāndhīgave | pavitraṃ ta iti kāvam antyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || sarvaḥ saptadaśaḥ || 5 ||
iti vaiśyastomaḥ || 11 |
tīvrasut
pañcamastīvrasut saptadaśa ukthya indro vṛtramahan ityanuvākenoktaḥ | tasya sūtrama sa pañcamena tīvrasuti somātipavitaṃ yājayeda ityādi | atha kalpaḥ
ete asṛgramindavas taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam iti purastāt paryāsasya tṛcapañcarce || śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma | saṃhitasya loke maukṣam pauṣkalasya loke śrudhyam | śyāvāśvasya yajñāyajñīyam || yajñāyajñīyasyarkṣu viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 1 ||
samānamitaramāyuṣaikāhikena || 2 ||
iti | upāsmai gāyatā davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavaḥ taṃ tvā tṛmṇāni bibhratam pavamānasya te kava iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna ā no mitrā yāhīndrāgnītyājyāni ||
atra somabhakṣaṇakāle bhakṣāvṛtā camasānavajighreyuḥ | yānavajighreyusteṣvevābhyunnayeyuḥ | tānacchāvākasya stotre bhakṣayeyuḥ | evaṃ sarvasavaneṣu kartavyam ||
uccā te jātam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam | dvihiṃkāramādyāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramaccheti sapha śrudhye | purojitīti yajñāyajñīyāndhīgave | kāvam antyam | yajñāyajñīyasyarkṣu viśoviśīya
magniṣṭomasāma | pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇam ṭhadvaṃśīyam ityukthāni ||
sarvaḥ saptadaśaḥ || 2 ||
iti tīvrasut || 12 | |
vājapeyaḥ
ṣaṣṭho vājapeyaḥ saptadaśa ukthyaḥ ṣoḍaśimān saptadaśī ityanuvākadvayenoktaḥ | tasya sūtramyaṃ brāhmaṇā sa rājānaḥ puraskurvīran sa vājapeyena yajetetyādi khaṇḍadvayam | subrahmaṇyāyāṃ śakrāgaccha viśve brahmāṇa iti viśeṣaḥ | aniruktaprātaḥsavanatvāt | stotraklṛptimāhapūrvasya bahiṣpavamānam | vrātyāni trīṇyājyāni | vaiśvajitamuttamam | bārhadukthasya loke devasthānam | rathantaraṃ pṛṣṭham | abhīvarto brahmasāma | maukṣasya loke svāśirāmarkaḥ | śyāvāśvasya yajñāyajñīyam tvaṃ naścitra ūtyeti vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 1 | samānamitaraṃ gavaikāhikena || 2 ||
gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma viṣṇo śipiviṣṭavatīṣu bṛhat || 3 ||
sarve saptadaśāḥ || 4 ||
iti | upāsmai davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavas taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | ṛdhagindo suvastayomiti viśeṣaḥ | induriti somamiti vacanāt | hotā devo amartyas tā naḥ śaktaṃ pārthivasya surūpakṛtnumūtaye tamīḍiśva yo arciṣetyājyāni | hotā yajño amartyomiti prastāvaḥ | hotā yajño mahī yajñasyeti brūyāditi vacanāt | uttametyājyaṛgjape pārokṣyamindrāgnīśabdayorna kāryamiti upahavya evoktam ||
atha mādhyaṃdinaḥ | asya pratneti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti samantaṃ prathamāyām | dairghaśravasaṃ tisṛṣu | devasthānaṃ tisṛṣu | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | ayaṃ soma iti pārtham antyam | dakṣiṇāsu yathāliṅgaṃ mantraprayogaḥ sūtroktaprakāreṇa kāryaḥ ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cābhīvarttaśca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye ekarcayoḥ |
purojitī va hati yajñāyajñīyāndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvam antyamityārbhavaḥ ||
tvaṃ naścitra ūtyeti vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma | tasya sarvabṛhatīkāraḥ kakubuttarākāro veti pakṣadvayaṃ vāravantīyaprasaṅge pratihāravyākhyāyāmasmābhirdarśitam | atra ca pavamānasthayajñāyajñīye yajñāyajñīyadharmā na kāryāḥ | vāravantīye tu kāryāḥ | pratihāravelāyāṃ patnyavekṣaṇam || sākamaśvaṃ saubharam ṭhadvaṃśīyam ityukthāni | indra juṣasve ti gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | ṣoḍaśigrahāvekṣaṇādi camasabhakṣaṇāntamatirātravat | nipkramaṇādi yajamānopahavāntaṃ kṛtvā
vājapeya sāma Vajapeya sama
kimitte viṣṇo iti bṛhatā vājapeyasāmnā stuvīran | tasya bharadvājastriṣṭup vipṇurityādayaḥ | ṣoḍaśastotrīyāsu bhāiḥ sādā iti pāṭhaḥ | saptadaśyāṃ bhāiḥ sadā iti | uttamaṃ na ruhyāditi ācāryā iti nidānavacanāt | bhakṣa āhṛte śyena ityavekṣyendavindrapītasyendriyāvatastriṣṭupchandasassarvagaṇasyeti bhakṣaṇam | triṣṭupchandasā vājapeyasāmni bhakṣayediti vacanāt | atha stomavimocanādyudavasanīyāntaṃ prakṛtivat | saṃsthāvājapeyastu sūtrakāreṇoktaḥ | anatirātro vā ṣoḍaśimān || tasya bṛhatsaptadaśaṃ stotraṃ syāt | pañcadaśaṃ svāsviti | tatra ṣoḍaśicamasabhakṣaṇāntaṃ jyotiṣṭomavadavikāreṇa kṛtvā niṣkramaṇādiyajamānāpahavānte kṛte svāsu bṛhatyā pañcadaśastomena stavanam | kecittu kimitte iti bṛhatyā saptadaśena stomena stavanamicchanti | tatra mūlaṃ mṛgyam | anyattu sarvaṃ sūtrata evāvadhāryam | pratyavarohaṇīyenaivāntato yajetetyuktam | pratyavarohaṇīyamāha
klṛpto jyotiṣṭomaḥ pratyavarohaṇīyaḥ || 5 || iti | viśvarūpājyotirgānayordhūrgānānāṃ ca nivṛttiḥ | śeṣaṃ prakṛtivat || 5 ||
iti vājapeyaḥ || 13 ||
rājasūyaḥ
atha rājasūyaḥ | agniṣṭomaṃ prathamamāharatītyādibhiścaturbhiranuvākairuktaḥ | rājā rājasūyena yajeta | tasya sapta sutyāḥ | abhyārohaṇīyaḥ abhiṣecanīyaḥ daśapeyaḥ keśavapanīyaḥ vyuṣṭidvirātraḥ kṣatrasya dhṛtiriti | phālgunīpakṣasya prathamāyāṃ dīkṣeta abhyārohaṇīyāya jyotiṣṭomāya | tasya klṛptimāha
klṛpto'bhyārohaṇīyaḥ || 1 ||
iti | jyotiṣṭoma ityanuvartate | sa cāgniṣṭomasaṃsthaḥ | agniṣṭomaṃ prathamamāharatīti śruteḥ || 1 ||
iti abhyārohaṇīyaḥ || 141 ||
rājasūyaḥabhiṣecanīyaḥ
pratyavarohaṇīyeneṣṭvā saṃvatsarādūrdhvamabhiṣecanīyena yajeta | tasya brāhmaṇamathaiṣo'bhiṣecanīya ityādi | stotraklṛptimāha
vāyo śukro ayāmi ta iti tṛcaḥ ṣaṭ saṃbhāryā vaiśvajityaḥ || 1 || iti | upa tvā jā janīyanto uta naḥ priyā priyāsu tatsaviturvareṇyam somānāṃ svaraṇam agna āyūṃṣi ṣaḍṛcaṃ saṃbhāryāḥ | daśatayebhyo nānādeśebhya āhṛtyātra saṃbhriyanta iti kṛtvā || 1 ||
pavasva vāco agriyo davidyutatyā rucā etamutyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suta utte śuṣmāsa īrate pavamānasya te kave adha kṣapā pariṣkṛtaḥ || 2 || iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
vāyo śukra ityanuṣṭup | adha kṣapā pariṣkṛta iti ca | tamasya marjayāmasi ityuttamā kāryā | tāsāṃ madhyamāmuttamāṃ kurma iti nidānavacanāt | saṃbhāryāṇām ṛcāṃ krameṇa agniḥ sarasvān sarasvatī savitā brahmaṇaspatiragnipavamānāviti devatāḥ | śiṣṭānāṃ soma eva | rathantaravarṇāyāṃ dvādaśākṣarāṇyabhiṣṭobhet | catvāryeva vā | dvādaśākṣarāṇyabhiṣṭobhedanuṣṭubhi | sarvatra catvārīti śāṇḍilya iti vacanāt || 2 || vaiśvajityājyāni || 3 || iti | suṣamiddho na āvaha tā naḥ śaktaṃ pārthivasya yuñjanti bradhnamaruṣaṃ carantam tamīḍiṣva yo arciṣā ityājyāni || 3 ||
uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca svāraṃ ca saindhukṣitam | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti samantaṃ ca rathantaraṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca devasthānaṃ ca varuṇasāma ca rauravayaudhājaye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārtham antyam || 4 ||
[iti mādhyadinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
samantamādyāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām || 4 ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca || 5 || iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe | kākṣīvataṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramaccheti saphadaivodāse | purojitī vo andhasa iti vādhryaśvaṃ ca | vaitahavyaṃ ca somasāma ca trāsadasyavaṃ cāndhīgavaṃ ca kāvamantyam || 6 || ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
saphadaivodāse ekarce || 6 || yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 7 ||
agne tvaṃ no antama iti satrāsāhīyaṃ saubharam udvaṃśīyam || 8 || ityukthāni ||
dvātriṃśāḥ pavamānāḥ | pañcadaśānyājyāni | saptadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni | ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ sokthaḥ || 8 ||
iṣṭāhotrīyamavabhṛthasāma | samasya manyave viśa iti vā | iṣṭāhotrīyamabhiṣecanīye | samasyeti veti vacanāt | abhiṣecanīyeneṣṭvā tasya sākṣādavabhṛthamakṛtvā taddīkṣita eva daśāhe tato daśapeyena yajeta | abhiṣecanīyadaśapeyayormadhye saṃsṛpeṣṭayaḥ santi | tāsāmanteṣu sanāmagrahāṃ subrahmaṇyāmāhvayet | abhiṣecanīyadaśapeyāvantareṇa sadā nāmagrahaḥ iti vacanāt || 9 ||
iti abhiṣecanīyaḥ ||
rājasūyaḥdaśapeyaḥ
varuṇasya suṣuvāṇasyetyādi daśapeyasya brāhmaṇam | sūtraṃ tutasya brāhmaṇaṃ daśa daśa camasamabhiyantīti | subrahmaṇyacaturthā udgātṛcamasaṃ bhakṣayeyuḥ ṣaḍ ttvānye ityādi ||
stotraklṛptimāha
rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam iti purastāt paryāsasya tṛcapañcarce | maidhātithasya loke sadoviśīyam | rauravasya maidhātitham | śrāyantīyaṃ brahmasāma | pauṣkalasya loke śrudhyam | śyāvāśvasya yajñāyajñīyam | vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsu || 1 ||
uddharatyukthāni || 2 ||
samānamitaramāyuṣaikāhikena || 3 ||
sarvaḥ saptadaśaḥ || 4 ||
iti | upāsmai gāyatā davidyutatyā rucā rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna ā no mitrā āyāhīndrāgnī ityājyāni ||
uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca sadoviśīyaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam | dvihiṃkāramādyāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām || rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni || svādiṣṭhayā iti gāyatrasaṃhite pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye ekarcayoḥ | purojitī vo andhasa iti yajñāyajñīyaṃ cāndhīgavaṃ ca kāvamantyam || svāsu vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvo'yaṃ daśapeyaḥ saptadaśaḥ || 4 ||
iti daśapeyaḥ || 143 ||
rājasūyaḥ -- keśavapanīyaḥ pratīcīnastomāparanāmā
atha keśavapanīyaḥ | tasya brāhmaṇamyadeṣo'rvācīnaḥ stomaḥ keśavapanīyo bhavati ityādi | saṃvatsarādūrdhvaṃ keśavapanīyāya dīkṣeta | tasya paurṇamāsyāmatirātra ityādi sūtram | pratīcīnaḥ stomaḥ uttaraḥ iti nidānam | atha kalpaḥ -- pavasva vāco agriya pavasvendo vṛṣā suto davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha || 2 ||
ityājyāni ||
parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti samantaṃ ca yaśaścābhīśavaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ ca | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārtham antyam || 3 ||
bṛhacca pṛṣṭham | tvamindra pratūrtiṣu iti abhīvarto brahmasāma ||4|| samānamitaramāyuṣo'tirātreṇa || 5 ||
iti | tatra mādhyaṃdinaḥ | uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | parītoṣiñcatā sutam iti samantaṃ ca yaśaścābhīśavaṃ ca | yaudhājayaṃ ca | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | samantamādyāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām ||
vṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca | tvamindra pratūrtiṣu abhīvartaḥ | svāsu kāleyam iti pṛṣṭhāni || svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | kāvamantyam | saphapauṣkalaśyāvāśvānyekarcāni || yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ nārmedham ityukthāni || ṣoḍaśī nāsti | āyuratirātre tasyābhāvāt | rātriḥ saṃdhiśca prakṛtivat | āyuratirātrasya yadyapyahīnikīṃ rātriṃ vakṣyati tathāpi ekāhaprakaraṇa tasyāpyakṛtatvāt nātrātideśaḥ || 5 ||

iti || 6 ||

iti | upadavipaveti bahiṣpavamānam | agna ā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma iti rauravayaudhājaye | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrahāviṣkṛte | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir ityekarcayoḥ saphapauṣkale | purojitī va ityaudalāndhīgave | kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | jyotiṣṭomastomaḥ | idamahardadhibhakṣāntam | rātrau vasatīvarīpariharaṇakāle sanāmagrahaṇaṃ subrahmaṇyāhvānam || 1 ||

tṛcasūktānāmādigrahaṇena vidhiḥ || 1 ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni ||2|| asya pratnāmanudyutam iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣkṛtaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ carṣabhaśca pavamānaḥ parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti samantaṃ dairghaśravasam utsedho'yaṃ soma iti pārtham antyam || 3 || [iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca vaikhānasaṃ ca || 4 || iti pṛṣṭhāni ||

caturviśāḥ pavamānāḥ | pañcadaśāni trīṇyājyāni | saptadaśamacchāvākasya | ekaviṃśāni trīṇi pṛṣṭhāni | triṇavamacchāvākasya | trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | ekaviṃśānyukthāni saṣoḍaśikāni | pañcadaśī rātriḥ | trivṛtsaṃdhiḥ || 8 ||

iti ārṣeyakalpavyākhyāyāṃ śrīvāmanāryasutavaradarājaviracitāyāṃ vivṛtyām ekāheṣu dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ āditaścaturthaḥ || 4 ||

rājā medhābhirīyata utte śuṣmāsa īrata iti purastātparyāsasya tṛcapañcarce jyotiṣṭomī gāyatrī vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ yo rājā carṣaṇīnāmityabhīvarto brahmasāma samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇa saptadaśāḥ pavamānāstrivṛntyājyāni pañcadaśāni pṛṣṭhānyekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomo 511
yavaṃyavaṃ no andhasetyanurūpa āmahīyavāduttaramābhīkamāndhīgavādarkapuṣpam̐ samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena sarvo daśadaśy 512
eṣa śuṣmyasiṣyadaditi purastātparyāsasyaikarca āmahīyavāduttaram pavasva madhumattama iti cyāvanaṃ naudhasasyarkṣu janitraṃ pari pra dhanveti saphaṃ paryū ṣviti śyāvāśvaṃ purojitī vo andhasa ityāndhīgavaṃ ca janitraṃ ca samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena sastomamekaikā stotriyopajāyate 513
rauravayaudhājaye antarā maidhātithaṃ naudhasasyarkṣu śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ brahmasāma yat padanidhanam̐ sam̐hitasya loke gauṣūktaṃ pauṣkalasyāśvasūktaṃ pramām̐hiṣṭhīyam̐ hārivarṇam̐ śuddhāśuddhīyam̐ svāsu yadaiḍam̐ samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena dvādaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinam̐ savanaṃ trivṛtī abhito 521
ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti tisro'nuṣṭubhaścatasro gāyatrīḥ karoti punastomasyājyamadhyaṃdinaṃ tīvrasutaḥ pṛṣṭhāni jyotiṣṭoma ārbhava āndhīgavāduttaraṃ yajñāyajñīyaṃ yajñāyajñīyasyarkṣu vāravantīyena ṣaḍbhiḥ sākamaśvasya ṣaḍbhirhārivarṇasya ṣadbhistairaścyasya ṣaḍbhiranubrāhmaṇam̐ stomās 522
taratsa mandī dhāvatīti catasṛbhirbahiṣpavamānamindrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca yajñāyajñīyasyarkṣu vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma sākamaśvam̐ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyaṃ gaurīvitam̐ ṣoḍaśisāma samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇānubrāhmaṇam̐ stomāḥ 523
upo ṣu jātamapturamityanurūpo yajña indramavardhayaditi brahmaṇa ājyaṃ pibā sutasya rasina ityutsedho brahmasāma januṣaikarcayoḥ saphaṃ ca valabhicca śyāvāśvasya loka audalamañjata iti kāvamantyamuddharatyukthāni samānamitaramāyuṣaikāhikena saptisaptadaśau stomau viparyāsaṃ 531
pratīcīnastomasya bahiṣpavamānaṃ yajña indramavardhayaditi brahmaṇa ājyaṃ pibā sutasya rasina iti niṣedho brahmasāma maukṣasya loke valabhitsaphasya satrāsāhīyamayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti śyāvāśvakrauñce agrego rājāpyastaviṣyata iti kāvamantyaṃ uddharatyukthāni samānamitaraṃ gavaikāhikenai kavim̐śaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ tisṛbhirhoturājyam̐ saptisaptadaśamitaram̐ sarvaṃ 532
valabhido bahiṣpavamānamubhau paryāsāvabhijito gāyatrī punānaḥ soma dhārayeti bṛhati jyotiṣṭome antye tvamindra yaśā asīti yaśo brahmasāma sam̐hitāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyamāndhīgavādbhargaḥ samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇa caturvim̐śaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ trivṛtpañcadaśānyājyāni caturvim̐śo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ saptadaśaikavim̐śāni pṛṣṭhāni triṇava ārbhava ekavim̐śo'gniṣṭomaḥ 533
sāhasraṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ yathā prathamasyā mahīyavāduttaramaiḍam̐ sauparṇaṃ jarābodhīyātsvāśirāmarka ānuṣṭubhebhya uttarampari pra dhanveti dvipadāsu vāravantīyam̐ sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam̐ samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśam̐ hoturājyam̐ saptadaśe dve ekavim̐śamacchākāvasya triṇavo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaśchandomāḥ pṛṣṭhāni trayastrim̐śa ārbhava ekavim̐śo'gniṣṭoma 541
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇamagneśca vrataṃ yaudhājayātpūrvam̐ rauravaṃ yaśaso loke śyaitam̐ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāṃ cārkaḥ samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa jyotiṣṭomamata ūrdhvam̐ ṣaḍūrdhvā ṣaḍarvāñco 542
jyotiṣṭomamājyabahiṣpavamānam uddharati jarābodhīyam̐ rathaṃtararaurave vājapeyasya pṛṣṭhāni purojitī vo andhasa iti audalamekasyāmāndhīgavamekasyām̐ śyāvāśvamekaśyāṃ bṛhattisṛṣu samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa trivṛdbahiṣpavamānaṃ pañcadaśāni trīṇyājyāni trivṛnmaitrāvaruṇasya caturvim̐śo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ saptadaśāni trīṇi pṝṣṭhāni pañcadaśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya saptadaśa ārbhava ekavim̐śo'gniṣṭomaḥ 543
punānaḥ soma dhārayeti rauravaṃ tisṛṣu sadoviśīyamekasyām̐ samantaṃ viṣṭārapaṅktau yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣvabhīvarto brahmasāma purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitamekasyāmāndhīgavamekasyām̐ śyāvāśvamekasyāṃ bṛhattisṛṣu saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena sastomam 551
upāsmai gāyatā nara iti tṛcaḥ ṣaṭsaṃbhāryā vaiśvajityaḥ pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto davidyutatyā rucai te asṛgramindavo vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunai tamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavamānasya te vayaṃ pavamānasya te kave punānaḥ soma dhārayeti bṛhatī samānamitaramabhiṣecanīyena sarvaḥ ṣaṭtrim̐śo 552
yā jyotiṣṭomasya sā marutstomasya 553
yā bṛhaspatisavasya sendrāgnyoḥ kulāyasya nubrāhmaṇam̐ stomāḥ 554
indrāyendo marutvate pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā pavamānasya te kava īḍenyo namasyo yadudya sūra udita indramidgāthino bṛhad indre agnā namo bṛhaduccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca tavāham̐ soma rāraṇeti samantaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ ca vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca revatīṣu ca vāmadevyam̐ śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatra maukṣe pavasvendramaccheti sapha śrudhye pari tyam̐ haryatam̐ harimiti śyāvāśvāndhīgave sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyaṃ pra daivodāso agniriti yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsu satrāsāhīyaṃ tava tyad indriyaṃ bṛhaditi saubharaṃ svāsūdvam̐śīyam̐ sarvaḥ pañcadaśaḥ 56
pavasva vāco agriya upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavamānasya te kave gniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityubhayānyājyāni punanaḥ soma dhārayeti rauravamekasyāṃ yaudhājayamekasyāṃ dairghaśravasamekasyām̐ rathaṃtaraṃ tisṛṣu bṛhatpṛṣṭham̐ śyaitasyarkṣvabhīvarto brahmasāma samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena trivṛtpañcadaśau stomau viparyāsam̐ 571
svāsu śyaitam̐ sam̐hitāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyam̐ śrudhyasya loke viśoviśīyamāndhīgavāduttaram̐ somasāmā tharvaṇaṃ vāsamānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇa dve trivṛtī stotre dve dvādaśe dve pañcadaśasaptadaśe dve ekavim̐śanaradaśe dve caturvim̐śe dve triṇave 572
pavasva vāco agriya upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavamānasya te kave namaste agna ojase mitraṃ vayam̐ havāmahe samasya manyave viśastā huve yayoridampratnaṃ pīyūṣaṃ pūrvyaṃ yadukthyamiti dvayoḥ satobṛhatyorgāyatraṃ tisṛṣvāmahīyavamekasyāmaiḍam̐ sauparṇamekasyām̐ satrāsāhīyamekasyāṃ yaikā pariśiṣyate tasyām̐ sadoviśīyaṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti rauravamekasyāṃ yaudhājayamekasyām̐ samantamekasyām̐ rathaṃtaraṃ tisṛṣvadha yadime pavamāna rodasī ityutsedha ekasyāmayam̐ soma iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca tadidāsa bhuvaneṣu jyeṣṭhamiti śyaitam̐ svāsu kāleyam̐ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ tisṛṣu saṃhitamekasyām̐ surūpamekasyām̐ svāśirāṃ cārka ekasyāṃ pavasvendramaccheti saphaviśoviśīye purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ekasyāṃ niṣedha ekasyām̐ śyāvāśvāmekasyāmāndhīgavaṃ tisṛṣu madhuścunnidhanaṃ tisṛṣu sumanmā vasvī rantī sunarīti vāravantīyaṃ tisṛṣu kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma dve trivṛtī stotre dve dvādaśe dve pañcadaśasaptadaśe dve navadaśacaturvim̐śe dve caturvim̐śaikavim̐śe dve triṇave 58
śyenamājyabahiṣpavamānamarṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatra vārṣāhare punānaḥ soma dhārayeti vaiyaśvamekasyāṃ vaṣaṭkāraṇidhanamekasyām̐ rauravamekasyāṃ yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣvauśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca saptahaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ vārṣāharam̐ satrāsāhīyaṃ pavasvendramaccheti kāśītau pagave purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadā ndhīgave sāmatrayāṇāṃ yatprathamaṃ kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma dve trivṛtī stotre dve dvādaśe dve pañcadaśe dve ekavim̐śe dve caturvim̐śe dve triṇave 591
pavasvendo vṛṣā suto 'paghnanpavate mṛdha iti purastātparyāsasya tṛce uddharati satrāsāhīyaṃ purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadamekasyām̐ tasyāmevāndhīgavam̐ śyāvāśvamekasyām̐ sāmaikasyāṃ trayāṇāṃ yaddvitīyaṃ pramām̐hiṣṭhīyamaupagavamudvam̐śīyaṃ mahānāmnyaḥ ṣoḍaśisāma samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa sarvaḥ pañcadaśaḥ kḷpto jyotiṣṭomaḥ śāntyarthaḥ śāntyarthaḥ 592

athaiṣa virāṭ ityanuvākena virāḍuktaḥ| tasya kalpaḥ --

uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cābhīkaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma rauravayaudhājaye | auśanamantyam | auśanayaudhājaye tṛcayoḥ| ekarcāḥ itare | saṃbhavati stome'ntyaṃ sarvatra | tṛce dvitīyaṃ cedaraṇyegeyam | tadabhāve yadantyāt pūrvam iti vacanāt |
gāyatryāmahīyavābhīkāni sāmatṛcaḥ| trīnekatṛce nānāstotrīyāsu iti vacanāt ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā iti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramacchā iti saphapauṣkale | purojitī iti śyāvāśvāndhīgavārkapuṣpāṇi | kāvamantyam | antyavarjamekarcāḥ| anuṣṭubhi sāmatṛcaḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvāṇi stotrāṇi daśīni| tisṛbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa parācībhiścatasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa ekayā sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā tisṛbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa parācībhiriti daśino viṣṭutiriti| taduktamdaśini catuṣko madhye parivarttinī paryāyāvabhitaḥ iti ||2||
iti virāṭ || 2||
aupaśadaḥ
athaiṣa aupaśadaḥ ityanuvākena aupaśadaḥ uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ
eṣa śuṣmyasiṣyadad iti purastāt paryāsasyaikarcaḥ | āmahīyavāduttaram | pavasva madhumattama iti cyāvanam | naudhasasyarkṣu janitram | pari pradhanveti sapham | paryūṣu iti śyāvāśvam, | purojitī vo andhasa ityāndhīgavaṃ ca janitraṃ ca ||1||
samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena sastomam | ekaikā stotrīyopajāyate ||2||
iti | upāsmai davidyutatyā rucā eṣa śuṣmyasiṣyadat pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna ā no mitrāyāhi indrāgnī ityājyāni ||
uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| pavasva madhumattama iti cyāvanam | punānaḥ soma dhāraya iti rauravayaudhājaye | auśanamantyam | cyāvanamekasyām | kakubyatra mādhyaṃdina ekarcaḥ | tasyāmiti vacanāt ||
rathantaraṃ vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasasyarkṣu janitram | svāsu kāleyam iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā iti gāyatrasaṃhite | pari pra dhanva indramaccha iti saphapauṣkale | paryūṣu iti śyāvāśvam | purojitī iti āndhīgavaṃ ca janitraṃ ca kāvamantyam | saphapauṣkalaśyāvāśvānyekarcāni pari pra dhanveti dvipadā| paryūṣviti pipīlikāmadhyānuṣṭup | pṛṣṭheṣu pūrvaṃ janitram | ārbhave tūttamam | taduktamupagrantheanupūrvamaupaśadanavasaptadaśayoḥ pūrvamato'nyatra iti ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | ekaikā stotrīyopajāyata ityasyāyamarthaḥ | bahiṣpavamānaprabhṛtiyajñāyajñīyānteṣu stotreṣu jyotiṣṭomastomā ekaikayā stotrīyayā vṛddhāḥ kāryā iti | tataśceyaṃ stomaklṛptiḥ| daśi bahiṣpavamānam | ṣoḍaśānyājyāni | ṣoḍaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | aṣṭādaśāni pṛṣṭhāni | aṣṭādaśa ārbhavo dvāviṃśo'gniṣṭoma iti | tatra ṣoḍaśasya viṣṭutiḥ | pañcabhyo hiṃkaroti | sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiriti | daśarātrikebhya ekayā jyāyaḥsu tasmāt pūrvasyottame paryāya āvapet | brahmāyatanīyā tu teṣu iti vacanāt | aṣṭādaśaṃ saptadaśa āvapediti gautamaḥ | nyāyavihitaḥ syāditi dhānaṃjayyaḥ | tatrāvāpapakṣe garbhiṇīviṣṭutyā uttame paryāye aṣṭābhyo hiṃkaroti | sa dvābhyāṃ sa tisṛbhiḥ sa tisṛbhiriti vidadhyāt | samaparyāyatvaṃ nyāyavihitatvam | ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa dvābhyāṃ ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiriti tasmin pakṣe viṣṭutiḥ | dvāviṃśasya saptinyā viṣṭutyā uttame paryāye āvapet | aṣṭābhyo hiṃkaroti| sa ca tisṛbhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiriti || 2 ||
iti aupaśadaḥ ||3||
punastomaḥ
aupaśadānantaram āpastambena sarvatomukha uktaḥ | sarvatomukhena yajeta yaḥ kāmayeta sarvamidaṃ bhaveyam iti | madhye gārhapatyaḥ | pratidiśaṃ saumikā vihārāḥ | trivṛt prācyāṃ diśi | pañcadaśo dakṣiṇataḥ| saptadaśaḥ paścāt | ekaviṃśa uttarataḥ iti | tāni ca trivṛdādīni 15 catvāri mukhāni bṛhadrathantarapṛṣṭhasya ṣaḍahasya caturbhirahobhiḥ kartavyāni| stomānvayāt | yatra hi ṣaḍahasya saṃpūrṇatā tatraiva vairūpādīnāṃ pravṛttiḥ| nyūnatāyāṃ tu bṛhadrathantarayoreva vyatyāsena prayogaḥ | tathā ca kalpakāraḥ -- caturvīrādiṣu bṛhadrathantareṇaiva pṛṣṭhena kalpayati | na tu vairūpādīni yathāsaṃbhavam | [iti] | nidānakāraścāha -- 
athaiṣa caturvīro bṛhadrathantarapṛṣṭha eva | na hi ṣāḍahikāni anubhavanti | nānyo vyalolupsītiti | chandomeṣu cāha -- bṛhadrathantare pṛṣṭhe na hi ṣāḍahikānyanubhavanti no vyalolupsīt iti | bṛhadrathantarapṛṣṭhaṃ ca ṣaḍahaṃ pṛṣṭhyastomasaṃjñaṃ parastāt kalpayiṣyati| tataśca pṛṣṭhyastomasya caturbhirahobhirasya catvāri mukhāni kāryāṇi | tatra prathamasya mukhasya klṛptiḥ -- upāsmai gāyatā nara upoṣu jātamapturam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna ā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnī ityājyāni||
pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ prathamāyām | abhi droṇāni babhrava ityāśvaṃ dvitīyāyām | sutā indrāya vāyava iti somasāma tṛtīyāyām | pra soma devavītaya iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | pra tu drava iti auśanam antyam ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pra somāso madacyuta iti gāyatraṃ prathamāyām| tasyāmeva saṃhitam | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato hariḥ iti januṣaikarcayoḥ saphākṣāre | pra sunvanāya ityaudalaṃ prathamāyām | tasyāmeva gautamam kāvam antyaṃ tisṛṣu ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvāṇi stotrāṇi trivṛt ||
pavasva vāco agriyo pavasvendo vṛṣā soma vṛṣā hyasi pavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam | dvitīyādīnāmapi mukhānāṃ bahireva bahiṣpavamānastavanam | ekāhaprakaraṇe vidhānāt | kālabhedābhāvācca ||
agni dūtaṃ mitraṃ vayam indramidgācaturṛcam | indre agna ityājyāni | caturṛce antyāyā uddhāraḥ ||
vṛṣā pavasva dhāraya iti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhāraya ityaiḍaṃtriṇidhane āyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti vāsiṣṭham antyam| pārthaṃ kecidicchanti |
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | pavasvendramaccha iti śaṅkusujñāne ekarcayoḥ| ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhagaḥ ityāsitādyaṃ prathamam | krauñcaṃ tisṛṣu| vṛṣā matīnām iti yāmam antyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
sākamaśvam | evāhi ityāmahīyavam | indraṃ viśvā ityāṣṭādaṃṣṭraṃ pūrvam ityukthāni | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam || atha tṛtīyasya -- 
davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam iṣe pavasva dhāraya iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnināgniḥ mitraṃ huve indreṇa tā huve ityājyāni ||
uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca| abhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgaṃ prathamāyām | gautamaṃ tisṛṣu | antarikṣaṃ tisṛṣu | āṣkāraṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām | tisro vāca ityaṅgirasāṃ krośamantyam ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | vayaṃ gha tvā iti mahāvaiṣṭambham | taraṇiritsiṣāsati iti rauravam iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca pāṣṭhauhaṃ ca | ā sotā pari ṣiñcatasakhāya ā niṣīdata iti vācaḥ sāma śauktaṃ caikarce| sutāso madhumattama iti tvāṣṭrīsāmanī svārādyatriṇidhane | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭam antyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni || sarvaḥ saptadaśaḥ ||
atha caturthasya
pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi prayadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ āśurarṣa bṛhanmate hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā indro dadhīco asthabhiḥ iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni||
pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatraṃ cātharvaṇaṃ ca nidhanakāmaṃ ca | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇa ityāṣṭādaṃṣṭram ābhīśavaṃ ca svaḥpṛṣṭhaṃ cāṅgirasam somaḥ pavate janitram antyam ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pari priyā divaḥ kaviḥ iti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca| tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punānaḥ ūrmiṇā iti bṛhatkātīṣādīye | purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadāndhīgave | pra ta
aśvinīr iti lauśam antyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
agniṃ vo vṛdhantām iti saindhukṣitam | vayamu tvām iti saubharam |
imamindra iti vasiṣṭhasya priyam ityukthāni ||
pratyasmai pipīṣata iti gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | pṛṣṭhyastome hi kalpakāro vakṣyati -- caturthasyāhnaḥ pratyasmai pipīṣata iti gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāme ti | sūtrakāraścāha -- 
virāḍapāye cāpaityanyāsu ceti | ādyetyabhiyuktā catuṣṭomavadasya jyotiṣṭomenaiva rathantarapṛṣṭhena yathāstomaklṛptena catvāri mukhāni
kāryāṇītyāha | trivṛdādistomasya klṛptiḥ viśvasṛjāmayane vakṣyate ||
athaiṣa punastoma ityanuvākena punastoma uktaḥ| tasya kalpaḥ
rauravayaudhājaye antarā maidhātitham | naudhasasyarkṣu śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ brahmasāma yat padanidhanam | saṃhitasya loke gauṣūktam | pauṣkalasyāśvasūktam pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ svāsu yadaiḍam ||1||
samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena ||2||
iti | upadavipaveti bahiṣpavamānam | agna ā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | punānaḥ so rauravaṃ prathamāyām | maidhātithaṃ dvitīyāyām | yaudhājayaṃ tṛtīyāyām | auśanamantyam | trīṇyanyatarasyāṃ saṃbhaveyur iti vacanāt bṛhatyāmekarcakaraṇam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasasyarkṣu padanidhanaṃ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ | svāsu kāleyam iti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatragauṣūkte | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | kāvam antyam | kāvavarjamekarcāḥ || 2 ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||3||
pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ svāsu yadaiḍamityukthāni || 3 ||
atha stomaklṛptiḥ -- 
dvādaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | trivṛtī abhitaḥ || 4 ||
iti | prātaḥsavanaṃ sarvaṃ trivṛt | mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ trivṛdityarthaḥ| dvādaśastomasya viṣṭutiḥ| catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā sa ekayā catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa ekayā sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa ekayā sa ekayā sa dvābhyāmiti | āvāpasthāneṣu dvikāviṣṭāvāḥ | taduktam -- dvādaśe tṛcabhāgasthāneṣu dvikāviṣṭāvāḥ | āvāpasthāneṣu veti || 4 ||
iti punastomaḥ ||4||
catuṣṭomau
prathamaḥ
athaiṣa catuṣṭoma ityanuvākena pūrvaścatuṣṭoma uktaḥ| atha yasya catvāri stotrāṇītyuttaraḥ | etau ca gautamau catuṣṭomāviti sūtrāntareṣu prasiddhiḥ | tayoḥ pūrvasya kalpaḥ -- 
ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhagaḥ iti tisro'nuṣṭubhaścatasro gāyatrīḥ karoti | punastomasyājyamadhyaṃdinam | tīvrasutaḥ pṛṣṭhāni | jyotiṣṭoma ārbhavaḥ | āndhīgāduttaraṃ yajñāyajñīyam | yajñāyajñīyasyarkṣu vāravantīyena ṣaḍbhiḥ sākamaśvasya ṣaḍbhiḥ hārivarṇasya ṣaḍbhiḥ tairaścyasya ṣaḍbhir anu brāhmaṇastomāḥ ||1||
iti | ayaṃ pūṣeti tisro'nuṣṭubhaḥ| sa tisṛbhistribhiḥ pādaiścatasro gāyatrīḥ kuryāt | tadyathā ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhagom | viyakhyadrodasī ubhom | somāsaḥ kṛṇvate pathom | pavamāna śravāyiyom | prathamā retasyā | uttamā rathantaravarṇā | iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna ā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni ||
uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| punānaḥ so rauravaṃ maidhātithaṃ yaudhājayam iti sāmatṛcaḥ | auśanam antyam ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramacchā iti ekarcayoḥ saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | kāvam antyam ||
yajñāehyūṣu taṃ te śrudhī iti caturṣu tṛceṣu vāravantīyena ṣaḍbhiḥ stotrīyābhiragniṣṭomasāma | ādyayoryajñāyajñīyayoragnirdevatā| parayorindraḥ| anubrāhmaṇaṃ stomaklṛptiḥ | catasṛbhiḥ bahiṣpavamānam | aṣṭābhirājyāni | dvādaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | ṣoḍaśāni pṛṣṭhāni | viṃśa ārbhavaḥ |
caturviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ iti | catuṣṭomayoścatuḥparyāyāḥ kāryāḥ | tatrāṣṭistomasya viṣṭutiḥ | dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti | sa prathamayā dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti | sa prathamayā dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti | sa madhyamayā dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti | sa uttamayeti brahmāyatanīyāpakṣe | kṣatrāyatanīyāpakṣe tu madhyamāyāṃ dvau paryāyau | taduktam -- catuṣke dvau prathamāyāṃ paryāyau syātāṃ yadi brahmāyatanīyāṃ kuryāt | madhyamāyāṃ kṣatrāyatanīyāṃ cet tathāṣṭīni | dvikāstu tasya paryāyāḥ iti | tatra prathamadvitīyau paryāyau tattṛcabhāgasthānayornidhāya dvitīyasya paryāyasyottarataḥ pratyagagreṇa kuśādvayena tṛtīyaṃ paryāyaṃ nidadhyāt | uttamasya paryāyasya
tṛcabhāgasthāna evottamaṃ paryāyam | evaṃ catuṣparyāyeṣu sarveṣu kuśāvidhānasthānam | atha ṣoḍaśasya catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā sa ekayā| catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa ekayā sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā| catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa ekayā sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā | catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa ekayā sa ekayā sa dvābhyāmiti | ṣoḍaśe catvāro dvādaśa paryāyāḥ | teṣāṃ madhyamau sadṛśāviti | atha caturviṃśasya ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti sa dvābhyāṃ sa dvābhyāmiti prathamaḥ paryāyaḥ | evamanye trayaḥ | tatra yajñātṛce dvirabhyastayaikaikayā stotrīyayā prathamaḥ paryāyaḥ | evamitareṣvapi tṛceṣvitare paryāyāḥ | taduktaṃ -- caturviṃśe catvāraḥ ṣaṭkāḥ paryāyā dvikaviṣṭāvāḥ | pṛthaktṛceṣu catustṛca iti | atrāṣṭicaturviṃśayorviṣṭutikalpe pakṣāntarāṇi sūtroktāni vistarabhayādatra na likhitāni ||1||
iti prathamaścatuṣṭomaḥ || 5||
dvitīyaḥ
uttaraṃ catuṣṭomamāha -- 
tarat sa mandī dhāvati iti catasṛbhirbahiṣpavamānam | indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | yajñāyajñīyasyarkṣu vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyam | gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma || 1||
samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa | anubrāhmaṇaṃ stomāḥ || 2 ||
iti | taratsamandī dhāvati iti caturṛcaṃ bahiṣpavamānam || agna ā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni || indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | punānaḥ so rauravaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam | auśanameva tṛce| śiṣṭā ekarcāḥ ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā iti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramacchā iti saphapauṣkale | purojitī va iti śyāvāśvāndhīgava| yajñāyajñīyāni | kāvamantyam | kāvayajñāyajñīye tṛcayoḥ | ekarcā itare | saṃbhavati stome'ntyaṃ sarvaṃ tṛce | dvitīyaṃ cedaraṇyegeyam |
tadabhāve yadantyātpūrvam iti vacanāt | yajñāyajñīyasyarkṣu vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma sākamaśvaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyam ityukthāni | gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma ||
atha catvāri stotrāṇi catasṛbhiḥ ityādibrāhmaṇoktāḥ stomāḥ| tadyathā -- bahiṣpavamānam | trīṇi cājyāni catuṣkastomāni | uttamamājyam | mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamāno rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevye cāṣṭīni uttamaṃ pṛṣṭhadvayam ārbhāvo'gniṣṭomasāma ca dvādaśāni| ukthāni ṣoḍaśī ca ṣoḍaśāni | tatra catuṣkasya viṣṭutiḥ| ekasyai hiṃkaroti sa prathamayaikasyai hiṃkaroti sa prathamayaikasyai hiṃkaroti sa madhyamayaikasyai hiṃkaroti sa uttamayeti | kṣatrāyatanīyāpakṣe madhyamāyāṃ paryāyadvayam | aṣṭistomasya pūrvasmin viṣṭutiruktā | atha dvādaśasya catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā sa ekayā | dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti sa parācībhyāṃ pūrvābhyāṃ dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti | sa parācībhyāmuttarābhyāṃ catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti | sa ekayā sa ekayā sa dvābhyāmiti | uktaṃ ca dvādaśe vyākhyātau prathamottamau madhyamau dvikau pūrvayoḥ pūrva uttarayoruttara iti ṣoḍaśasya pūrvasminnevoktā ||2||
iti dvitīyaścatuṣṭomaḥ ||6||
udbhid
asurāṇāṃ vai balaḥ ityanuvākenodbhidbalabhidāvuktau | udbhidbalabhidbhyāmaviprayogeṇa yajete tyādi sūtram | tatrodbhidaḥ klṛptimāha -- 
upoṣu jātamapturam ityanurūpo yajña
indramavardhayad iti brahmaṇa ājyam | pibā
sutasya rasina ityutsedho brahmasāma januṣaikarcayoḥ saphaṃ ca udbhicca śyāvāśvasya loka audalam | añjata iti kāvam antyam ||1||
uddharatyukthāni ||2||
samānamitaram āyuṣaikāhikena ||3||
iti| upāsmai upoṣu pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | udbhidyanurūpamekarcaṃ śāṇḍilyaḥ | stotrīyaṃ gautamadhānaṃjayyau ||
agna āyāhi vītaye ā no mitrāvaruṇā yajña indramavardhayat indrāgnī āgataṃ sutam ityājyāni ||
uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | abhi somāsa iti dvihiṃkāraṃ prathamāyām| maidhātitharaurave tisṛṣu | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | auśanamantyam ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | pibā sutasya rasina ityutsedhaḥ | svāsu kāleyam iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā iti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato hariḥ iti saphaṃ ca udbhicca |
purojitī vo andhasa iti audalāndhīgave | añjata iti kāvamantyam | ekarcāḥ sarve | saṃbhavati stome'ntyaṃ sarvatra tṛca ityatra saṃbhavatīti vacanāt | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 3 ||
saptisaptadaśau stomau viparyāsam || 4 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam | saptiprathamamājyaṃ saptadaśamityevaṃ vyatyāsena saptisaptadaśau stomau bhavataḥ| tatra saptistomasya jātisaṃhārapakṣe viṣṭutiḥ| ekasyai hiṃkaroti sa prathamayā tisṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa parācībhistisṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa parācībhiriti | yugmāyuksaṃhārapakṣe tu dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti sa prathamayā dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti sa prathamayā dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti sa madhyamayā tisṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa parācībhiriti || 4 ||
iti udbhid || 7 ||
balabhid
balabhidaḥ klṛptimāha
pratīcīnastomasya bahiṣpavamānam | yajña indramavardhayad iti brahmaṇa ājyam | pibā sutasya iti niṣedho brahmasāma | maukṣasya loke balabhit | saphasya satrāsāhīyam | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti śyāvāśvakrauñce | agrego rājāpyastaviṣyata iti kāvam antyam || 1||
uddharatyukthāni || 2||
samānamitaraṃ gavaikāhikena ||3||
iti | pratīcīnastomaḥ keśavapanīyaḥ | pavasva vāco pavasvendo davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe iti mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe yajña indramavardhayat indre agnā namo bṛhad ityājyāni ||
asya pratnāmanudyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| parītoṣi samantamādyāyām | dairghaśravasaṃ tisṛṣu | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyam ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca| pibā sutasya iti niṣedhaḥ | svāsu kāleyam iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca balabhicca | pavasvendramaccha iti satrāsāhīya śrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā iti śyāvāśvakrauñce | agrego rājāpyastaviṣyata iti kāvamantyam || ekarcāḥ sarve | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||3||
ekaviṃśaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | tisṛbhirhoturājyam | saptisaptadaśamitaraṃ sarvam ||4||
iti | stomavidhiḥ | trikastomasya śyene viṣṭutiruktā saptistomasyodbhidi || 4 ||
iti balabhid || 8 ||
apacitiḥ
athaiṣo'pacitir ityanuvākena prathamo'pacitiruktaḥ | athaiṣa sarvastomo'pacitir iti dvitīyaḥ | rājāpacitikāma etābhyāṃ yajeteti dhānaṃjayyaḥ | yaḥkaścāpacitikāma iti śāṇḍilyaḥ | tayoḥ prathamasya kalpaḥ
balabhido bahiṣpavamānam | ubhau paryāsau | abhijito gāyatrī | punānaḥ soma dhāraya iti bṛhatī | jyotiṣṭome antye | tvamindra yaśā asi iti yaśo brahmasāma | saṃhitāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyam | āndhīgavādbhargaḥ || 1||
samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasroṇa || 2 ||
iti | pavamānasya te vayam pavamānasya te kave iti bārhatarathantarāvubhau paryāsāvatra bhavataḥ | tatra bṛhataḥ pṛṣṭhena pradhānyādbārhatasya pūrvaṃ prayogaḥ | jyotiṣṭome'ntye iti mādhyaṃdinārbhavayorantye auśanakāve ityarthaḥ | 2 || tatreyaṃ klṛptiḥ -- 
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā
pavamānasya te vayam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam |
agna āyāhi mitraṃ vayam indramidgāthinaḥ indre agna ityājyāni ||
uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ satrāsāhīyaṃ ca | punānaḥ so samantamādyāyām |
rathantaraṃ dairghaśravasaṃ tisṛṣu | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām auśanamantyam |
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca | tvamindra yaśā asi iti yaśaḥ | svāsu kāleyamiti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā iti gāyatraṃ ca saṃhitaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca | pavasvendramacchā iti saphaśrudhye | purojitī vo iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | bhargaśca | kāvamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 2 ||
caturviṃśaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | trivṛtpañcadaśānyājyāni | caturviṃśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | saptadaśaikaviṃśāni pṛṣṭhāni | triṇavaḥ ārbhavaḥ | ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ || 3 ||
iti | trivṛtpañcadaśayorājyeṣu vyatyāsena prayogaḥ | pṛṣṭheṣu saptadaśaikaviṃśayośca ||3||
| iti apacitiḥ || 6 ||
sarvastomo'pacitiḥ
dvitīyamapacitimāha -- 
sāhasraṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ yathā prathamasya | āmahīyavāduttaramaiḍaṃ sauparṇam | jarābodhīyāt svāśirāmarkaḥ | ānuṣṭubhebhya uttaram | pari pra dhanva iti dvipadāsu vāravantīyam | sūryavatīṣu kāvam antyam || 1||
samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa ||2||
iti | pavasva vāco agriya davidyutatyā rucā pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna āyāhi mitraṃ vayam indramidgāthina indre agna ityājyāni ||
uccā te gāyatraṃ ca āmahīyavaṃ caiḍaṃ ca sauparṇaṃ satrāsāhīyaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhāraya iti samantaṃ ca rathantaraṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca yaudhājayamauśanamantyam ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca | tvamindra yaśā iti yaśaḥ | svāsu kāleyamiti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā iti gāyatraṃ ca saṃhitaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramacchā iti saphaśrudhye | purojitī va iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave bhargaḥ | pari pradhanva iti dvipadāsu vāravantīyam | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 2 ||
stomavidhimāha -- 
trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśaṃ hoturājyam | saptadaśe dve | ekaviṃśamacchāvākasya | triṇavo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | chandomāḥ pṛṣṭhāni | trayastriṃśa
ārbhavaḥ | ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ ||3||
iti | chandomāḥ pṛṣṭhānīti | caturviṃśacatuścatvāriṃśāṣṭācatvariṃśa10 caturviṃśastomāni kramāt pṛṣṭhānītyarthaḥ || 3 ||
iti sarvastomo'pacitiḥ || 10 ||
agneḥ stomaḥ
prathamaḥ -- pakṣītyaparākhyaḥ
pakṣī vā eṣa stomaḥ ityanuvākena pakṣī vihitaḥ| athaiṣa jyotiḥ iti anuvākena jyotiḥ | tāvagnistomāviti cocyate | yathoktam -- aikāhikena vratenoktaḥ upāyo'gneḥ stomayoḥ iti | jyotiṣo'pi pakṣisaṃjñā'sti | yathoktamāpastambenapakṣibhyāṃ sāgnicityau iti | prathamamagneḥ stomamāha -- 
uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇam agneśca vratam | yaudhājayāt pūrvaṃ rauravam | yaśaso loke śyaitam | svādiṣṭhayā gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ ||1||
samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa | jyotiṣṭomamata ūrdhvam || 2||
iti | samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇetyāderayamarthaḥ | ārbhavāt pūrvamanuktaṃ pūrveṇa samānam | svāśirāmarkādūrdhvaṃ tu kakubādijyotiṣṭomena samamiti | pavasva vāco davidyutatyā rucā pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna ā yāhi mitraṃ vayam indramidgāthina indre agna ityājyāni ||
uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca svāraṃ sauparṇamagneśca vratam | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca rathantaraṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca rauravayaudhājaye | auśanamantyam ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
stomāsvādiṣṭhayā iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale | purojitīti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | kāvamantyam || saphapauṣkalaśyāvāśvāndhīgavānyekarceṣu ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 2 ||
ṣaḍūrdhvāḥ stomāḥ ṣaḍarvāñcaḥ || 3||
pārṣṭhikāḥ stomāḥ ṣaṭpūrvamūrdhvā bhavanti | tatasta evārvāñca ityarthaḥ| tadyathā trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśa saptadaśaikaviṃśatriṇavānyājyāni | yathāsaṃkhyaṃ trayastriṃśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | trayastriṃśatriṇavaikaviṃśasaptadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni| yathāsaṃkhyaṃ pañcadaśa ārbhavaḥ | trivṛdagniṣṭomasāma | iti || 3 ||
iti prathamaḥ agneḥ stomaḥ || 11 |
agneḥ stomaḥ
dvitīyaḥ -- jyotirityaparākhyaḥ
dvitīyamagneḥ stomamāha -- 
jyotiṣṭomamājyabahiṣpavamānam | uddharati jarābodhīyaṃ rathantararaurave | vājapeyasya pṛṣṭhāni | purojitī vo andhasa iti audalamekasyām | āndhīgavamekasyām | śyāvāśvamekasyām | bṛhattisṛṣu || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa ||2||
iti | upāsmai davidyutatyā pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna ā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni ||
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇamagneśca vratam punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca yaudhājayamauśanamantyam || jarābodhīyarathantararauravāṇāmuddhāraḥ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cābhīvartaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale ekarcayoḥ | purojitī vo andhasa iti audalam āndhīgavaṃ śyāvāśvamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | bṛhattisṛṣu kāvamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 2 ||
atha stomaklṛptiḥ 
trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśāni trīṇyājyāni | trivṛnmaitrāvaruṇasya
caturviṃśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | saptadaśāni trīṇi pṛṣṭhāni |
pañcadaśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya | saptadaśa ārbhavaḥ | ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ || 3||
iti ||3||
iti dvitīyaḥ agneḥ stomaḥ || 12 ||
ṛṣabhaḥ
atha ṛṣabho rājanyaḥ | athaiṣa ṛṣabha ityanuvākenoktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
punānaḥ soma dhāraya iti rauravaṃ tisṛṣu | sadoviśīyamekasyām | samantaṃ viṣṭārapaṅktau | yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | abhīvarto brahmasāma | | purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitamekasyām | āndhīgavamekasyām | śyāvāśvamekasyām | bṛhattisṛṣu ||1||
saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena
sastomam || 2||
iti | upadavipaveti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna ā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni ||
uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| punānaḥ so rauravaṃ tisṛṣu | sadoviśīyamekasyām | duhāna ūdhariti viṣṭārapaṅktau samantam | yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu auśanamantyam ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cābhīvarttaśca | kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni || svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti sapha pauṣkale ekarce| purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitam āndhīgavaṃ śyāvāśvam iti sāmatṛcaḥ | bṛhattisṛṣu kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśānyājyāni | saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ | saptadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni | ārbhava ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomasāma | iti stomaklṛptiḥ || 2||
iti ṛṣabhaḥ || 13 ||
gosavaḥ
yo vai vājapeya ityanuvākena gosava uktaḥ | vaiśyaṃ yaṃ viśaḥ sarājānaḥ puraskurvīran | sa gosavena yajeta | tasya bahiṣpavamānam -- 
upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ iti tṛcaḥ ṣaṭ saṃbhāryā vaiśvajityaḥ | pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi | vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavamānasya te vayam pavamānasya te kave ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
upa tvā jāmayo gira ityādyā agna āyūṃṣi pavasa iti [antāḥ] ṣaḍṛcaḥ saṃbhāryāḥ vaiśvajityaḥ||1||
punānaḥ soma dhāraya iti bṛhatī | samānamitaramabhiṣecanīyena || 2||
iti | suṣamiddho na āvaha | tā naḥ śaktaṃ pārthivasya | yuñjanti bradhnamaruṣaṃ carantam | tamīḍiṣva yo arciṣetyājyāni || suṣamiccaturṛce yathāgotramuddhāraḥ | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca svāraṃ ca saindhukṣitaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca rathantaraṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca devasthānaṃ ca varuṇasāma rauravayaudhājaye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca kāleyaṃ śyaitaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe | kākṣīvataṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramaccheti saphadaivodāse | purojitīti vādhryaśvaṃ ca vaitahavyaṃ ca somasāma ca trāsadasyavaṃ cāndhīgavaṃ kāvamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | agne tvanno antama iti satrāsāhīyaṃ saubharam udvaṃśīyam ityukthāni ||2||
atha stomaklṛptiḥ
sarvaḥ ṣaṭtriṃśaḥ || 3||
iti | dvādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ so'ṣṭābhiḥ sa ekayā dvādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ so'ṣṭābhiḥ dvādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti | so'ṣṭābhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ| iti ṣaḍ triṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ triṇavādyāvat| samā iti vacanāt | triṇavasyādyā viṣṭutiryā tadvat samāḥ stomāḥ kartavyā ityarthaḥ || 3||
iti gosavaḥ || 14||
marutstomaḥ indrāgnyoḥ kulāyaśca
athaiṣa marutstoma ityanuvākena marutstoma uktaḥ | athaiṣa indrāgnyoḥ kulāya ityanuvākena indrāgnyoḥ kulāyaḥ| tayoḥ kalpaḥ
yā jyotiṣṭomasya sā marutstomasya ||1||
yā bṛhaspatisavasya sendrāgnyoḥ kulāyasya ||2||
iti| yeti klṛptirviśeṣyā| ubhayoḥ stomaklṛptimāha
anubrāhmaṇaṃ stomāḥ || 3||
iti | tatra marutstomo bahuyajamānakaḥ | etenaiva trīnyājayet
iti śruteḥ | sakhāyo bhrātaro vā ye saṃpādayeyuste marutstomena yajeran iti vacanācca | tasya sarvaṃ jyotiṣṭomavat | dhūrjyotirviśvārūpāṇāṃ nivṛttiḥ | trīṇi trivṛnti stotrāṇi | trīṇi pañcadaśāni | trīṇi saptadaśāni | trīṇyekaviṃśāni | ārbhave sarvāṇi sāmāni tṛcasthāni| indrāgnyoḥ kulāyasya bṛhaspatisavavat sarvam | ṣaṭ
stotrāṇi trivṛnti | dve stotre pañcadaśe| saptadaśe dve ekaviṃśe iti stomāḥ | ārbhave sarve tṛcāḥ | etenaiva dvau yājayet iti śruteḥ || 3 ||
iti marutstomaḥ indrāgnyoḥ kulāyaśca || 15||
indrastomaḥ
athaiṣa pañcadaśa indrastoma ukthyaḥ ityanuvākenendrastoma uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
indrāyendo marutvate pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā pavamānasya te kave || 1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
īḍenyo namasyo yadadya sūra udita indramidgāthino bṛhad indre agnā namo bṛhad || 2||
ityājyāni ||
uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇeti samantaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ ca | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārtham antyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ||]
bṛhacca revatīṣu ca vāmadevyam | śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā iti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramaccheti sapha śrudhye | pari tyaṃ haryataṃ harim iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvam antyam || 5 ||
saphaprabhṛtīni trīṇyekarcāni || 5 ||
pra daivodāso agnir iti yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 6||
svāsu satrāsāhīyam | tava tyadindriyaṃ bṛhad iti saubharam svāsūdvaṃśīyam || 7 ||
ityukthāni||
sarvaḥ pañcadaśaḥ || 8 ||
iti indrastomaḥ || 16 ||
indrāgnyoḥ stomaḥ
athaiṣa indrāgnyoḥ stomaḥ ityanuvākena indrāgnyoḥ stoma uktaḥ| rājapurohitau saha yajeyātām indrāgnyoḥ stomena | pṛthagvā | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavamānasya te kave ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityubhayānyājyāni || 2 ||
agniṃ dūtam ā no mitrāvaruṇendramidgāthino bṛhad indrāgnī āgataṃ sutam ityājyāni ||
punānaḥ soma dhārayeti rauravamekasyām | yaudhājayamekasyām | dairghaśravasamekasyām | rathantaraṃ tisṛṣu | bṛhatpṛṣṭham | śyaitasyarkṣu abhīvarto brahmasāma || 3 ||
samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena ||4||
iti | uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | punānaḥ sorauravaṃ yaudhājayaṃ 15 dairghaśravasamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | rathantaraṃ tisṛṣu| auśanamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitasyarkṣu abhīvarto brahmasāma | svāsu kāleyam iti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | kāvamantyam | kāvamekatṛce | ekarcā itare | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 4 ||
trivṛtpañcadaśau stomau viparyāsam || 5 ||
iti stomavidhiḥ ||5||
iti indrāgnyoḥ stomaḥ || 17 ||
vighanaḥ prathamaḥ
athaiṣa vighana ityanuvākena vighana uktaḥ | indramadevyo māyā asacanta iti dvitīyaḥ | vighanābhyāṃ paśukāmo yajetābhicaran vā | prathamaṃ vighanamāha -- 
svāsu śyaitaṃ | saṃhitāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyam | śrudhyasya loke viśoviśīyam āndhīgavāduttaraṃ somasāma ātharvaṇaṃ vā || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇa ||2||
iti | somasāmātharvaṇaṃ veti vikalpasya vyavasthā sūtroktā | ātharvaṇaṃ paśukāmayajñe kuryāt | somasāmābhicarataḥ iti | pavasva vāco davidyutatyāpavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna āyāhi mitraṃ vayam indramidgāthina indre agnetyājyāni ||
asya pratnāmanudyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti samantaṃ prathamāyām | rathantaraṃ dairghaśravase tṛcayoḥ | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhitajarābodhīyāni | pavasvendramaccheti saphaviśoviśīye | purojitīti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | somasāmātharvaṇaṃ vā | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||2||
stomaklṛptimāha -- 
dve trivṛtī stotre | dve dvādaśe| dve pañcadaśasaptadaśe| dve
ekaviṃśanavadaśe | dve caturviṃśe| dve triṇave ||3||
iti | dvādaśasya viṣṭutiḥ punaḥstome darśitā | atha navadaśasya | tisṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa parācībhiḥ | trayodaśabhyo hiṃkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa saptabhiḥ sa tisṛbhiḥ | tisṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa parācībhiriti| taduktam -- ekānnaviṃśe ca parivartini paryāyāvabhitastrayodaśīmadhye'smin prathamāyāstrirvacanaṃ tathottamāyā iti ||3||
iti prathamo vighanaḥ ||18||
dvitīyo vighanaḥ
dvitīyaṃ vighanamāha -- 
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
namaste agna ojase mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe samasya manyase viśas tā huve yayoridam ||2||
ityājyāni |
