pratnaṃ pīyūṣaṃ pūrvyaṃ yadukthyam iti dvayoḥ satobṛhatyorgāyatraṃ tisṛṣu āmahīyavamekasyām | aiḍaṃ sauparṇamekasyām | satrāsāhīyamekasyām | yaikā pariśiṣyate tasyāṃ sadoviśīyam | punānaḥ soma dhāraya iti rauravamekasyām | yaudhājayamekasyām | samantamekasyām | rathantaraṃ tisṛṣu | adha yadime pavamāna rodasī ityutsedha ekasyām | ayaṃ soma iti pārtham antyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ || ]
sato dve bṛhatyau dvāsaptatyakṣare | tatra caturviṃśaticaturviṃśatyakṣaraistrisro gāyatrīḥ kṛtvā tāsu gāyatraṃ kāryam | tadyathā -- pratnaṃ pīyūṣaṃ pūrvyom | indramabhijāyamānom | vasuruco divyā abhyom iti | yathā -- pratnaṃ pīyūṣaṃ pūrvyām ityāmahīyavam | indramabhovetyaiḍaṃ sauparṇam | vasuruca iti satrāsāhīyam | yā tu satobārhate tṛce pariśiṣṭā satobṛhatī adha yadima iti tasyāṃ sadoviśīyam | śiṣṭaṃ spaṣṭam ||
atha taittirīyakam | satobṛhatīṣu stuvate vyatiṣiktābhiḥ stuvate iti | vyatiṣaṅgaḥ satobṛhatīnāṃ bṛhatībhirvyavadhānam | tataśca vyatiṣaṅgasaṃpādanārtho'dha yadima ityasyāḥ punaḥ prayoga ityarthakāritaḥ saṃcāro na doṣāya | uktaṃ copagranthe -- na punaḥ samānāhani stotriyāsaṃcāro vidyate anyatra vighanāditi || 3||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca tadidāsa bhuvaneṣu jyeṣṭham iti śyaitaṃ svāsu kāleyam || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā iti gāyatraṃ tisṛṣu saṃhitamekasyām surūpamekasyām | svāśirāmarka ekasyām | pavasvendramaccheti saphaviśoviśīye | purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitamekasyām | niṣedha ekasyām | śyāvāśvamekasyām |
āndhīgavaṃ tisṛṣu madhuścunnidhanaṃ tisṛṣu | sumanmā vasvīrantī sūnarīti vāravantīyaṃ tisṛṣu | kāvam antyam || 5
ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||6||
atha stomāḥ -- 
dve trivṛtī stotre | dve dvādaśe | dve pañcadaśasaptadaśe| dve navadaśacaturviṃśe | dve caturviṃśaikaviṃśe| dve triṇave || 7||
iti dvitīyaḥ vighanaḥ ||18||
saṃdaṃśaḥ
athaiṣa saṃdaṃśo'bhicaranyajeta iti ṣaḍviṃśabrāhmaṇe saṃdaṃśa uktaḥ | athaiṣa vajro'bhicaranyajeta iti vajraḥ| rājānaṃ saṃdaṃśenābhicaret | janapadaṃ vajreṇa | tatra saṃdaṃśamāha -- 
śyenamājyabahiṣpavamānam || 1 ||
iti | pavamānasya jighnataḥ punāno akramīdabhi pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnirvṛtrāṇi mitraṃ huve u tvā mandantu somāḥ tā huve yayoridam ityājyāni ||1||
arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatravārṣāhare | punānaḥ soma dhāraya iti vaiyaśvamekasyām | vaṣaṭkāraṇidhanamekasyām | rauravamekasyām | yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanam antyam || 2||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca saptahaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ||3||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ vārṣāharaṃ satrāsāhīyam | pavasvendramacchā iti kāśītaupagave | purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadāndhīgave | sāmatrayāṇāṃ yat prathamaṃ | kāvam antyam ||4||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
sāmatrayāṇāṃ yat prathamamiti | hāu hāu hāu as phaḍityādi || 4 ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||5||
stomānāha -- 
dve trivṛtī stotre | dve dvādaśe | dve pañcadaśe | dve ekaviṃśe |
dve caturviṃśe | dve triṇave ||6||
iti | trivṛtpañcadaśaikaviṃśatriṇavānāmabhicaraṇīyā viṣṭutayaḥ ṣaḍviṃśe'dhītāḥ| tatra trivṛtorviṣṭutyorvikalpaḥ || 6 ||
iti saṃdaṃśaḥ ||19||
vajraḥ
vajramāha -- 
pavasvendo vṛṣā suto'paghnan pavate mṛdha iti purastāt paryāsasya tṛce | uddharati satrāsāhīyam | purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadamekasyām | tasyāmevāndhīgavam | śyāvāśvamekasyām | sāmaikasyāṃ trayāṇāṃ yad dvitīyam | pramaṃhiṣṭhīyam aupagavam udvaṃśīyam || 1||
mahānāmnyaḥ ṣoḍaśisāma | samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa ||2||
iti | pavamānasya jighnataḥ punāno akramīdabhi pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ apaghnan pavate mṛdhaḥ pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnirvṛtrāṇi mitraṃ huve pūtadakṣam u tvā mandantu somāḥ tā huve yayoridam ityājyāni ||
arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatravārṣāhare | punānaḥ so iti vaiyaśvaṃ vaṣaṭkāranidhanaṃ rauravamiti sāmatṛcau| yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanamantyam ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca saptahaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatravārṣāhare | pavasvendramaccheti kāśītaupagave ekarcayoḥ | purojitī vo iti nānadāndhīgave prathamāyām | śyāvāśvaṃ dvitīyāyām | abhyābhūr ityādikaṃ mahāsāma tṛtīyāyām | kāvamantyam |
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma |
pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ tvaṃ na indra ityaupagavamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni ||
mahānāmnyaḥ ṣoḍaśisāma | tāsāṃ stutivelāyāmudaghoṣaḥ kartavyaḥ | na tvapāṃ nidhanaṃ yajuṣā syāt || 2 ||
stomavidhimāha -- 
sarvaḥ paścadaśaḥ ||3||
iti | abhicaraṇīyā viṣṭutiḥ || 3||
saṃdaṃśavajrayorantataḥ śāntyarthaṃ jyotiṣṭomena yaṣṭavyamityāha -- 
klṛpto jyotiṣṭomaḥ śāntyarthaḥ | śāntyarthaḥ || 4 ||
iti ||4||
iti vajraḥ || 20 ||
iti vāmanāryasūnuḥ kuśikānvayasaṃbhavo varadarājaḥ |
ekāhānāṃ kalpaṃ vyācaṣṭa yathāgamaṃ yathābuddhi |
iti śrīvamanāryasūnuvaradarājaviracitāyāmārṣeyakalpavyākhyāyāṃ
pañcamo'dhyāyaḥ ||5||
ekāhaḥ samāptaḥ ||
<6>
kḷpto jyotiṣṭomo'tirātro'ṣoḍaśiko yadi vā ṣoḍaśimānbrāhmaṇe ṣaṣṭhādibhiścaturbhiradhyāyairatirātrasaṃstho jyotiṣṭomo'ṣoḍaśikaḥ kḷptaḥ yadi vā ṣoḍaśimāniti tu brāhmaṇāntarānusāreṇoktaṃ asya cātirātrasya prayoga ādavevāsmābhirdarśitaḥ vakṣyamāṇānāṃ rātrerviśeṣamāha ahīnikyām̐ rātrau paṇyaṃpaṇyamitsotāra iti śrautakakṣaṃ daivodāsaṃ hotṛṣāma kautsasya loka āṣṭādam̐ṣṭramā tvā viśantvindava ityetāsu samānamitaram̐ sāttrikyā 611
sarvastomasyopavatī pratipat svāsu śyaitaṃ parisvānārbhavīyā sam̐hitāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyamāndhīgavādātharvaṇam̐ samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇa tasya jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān 612
aptoryāmnastṛtīyasya sāhasrasyājyabahiṣpavamānaṃ jarābodhīyasya loke surūpamuddharatyātharvaṇaṃ pari pra dhanveti svarvannidhanam̐ sauhaviṣam̐ samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa vaiśvajitāni pṛṣṭhāni pṛṣṭhānāṃ garbhā anubrāhmaṇamatiriktastotrāṇi 613
navasaptadaśasyā mahīyavāduttaraṃ pavasva madhumattama iti sākamaśvamabhi somasa āyava iti janitram̐ raurava yaudhājaye naudhasasyarkṣu janitraṃ pari pra dhanveti sapham̐ samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomenātirātreṇāṣoḍaśikena 614
yā jyotiṣṭomasya sā viṣuvatastasya saptadaśānyukthānyaṣoḍaśiko'tirātraḥ 615
kḷpte go āyuṣī 616
kḷptau viśvajidabhijitau 617
yā bṛhaspatisavasya saikastomānāṃ prathamasya tasyaiva jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān 618
gorājyabahiṣpavamānamindrastomasya madhyaṃdinaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti bṛhatī svāsu śyaitam̐ samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇa tasya jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimām̐s 621
tīvrasuta ājyabahiṣpavamānaṃ madhyaṃdinaśca samānamitaraṃ prathamenaikastomena 622
pratīcīnastomasya bahiṣpavamānamabhijito gāyatrī jyotiṣa uttarasya bṛhatī samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenaikastomena ṣoḍaśāḥ ṣoḍaśino'nubrāhmaṇaṃ vā 623
ye vyuṣṭidvirātrasyāhanī te āṅgirasadvirātrasyo ttarādahna ṛṣabhajarābodhīye uddharati samantadairghaśravase antarātharvaṇam̐ sam̐hitāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyaṃ viśoviśīyasya loke śrudhyaṃ 624
yāṅgirasadvirātrasya pūrvā sā caitrarathasya tasyāyuṣa ukthāni iti pūvamahaḥ pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suta ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam̐ śrudhyasya loke jarābodhiyaṃ nārmedhādrātrimupayantyatha yadeva dvidivasasyottamamahastadetadityuttaram 625
ye caitrarathasyāhanī te kāpivanasya trivṛtpañcadaśo'gniṣṭomaḥ pūrvamaharāyuratirātra uttaram 626
ābhiplavikasya prathamasyāhna ājyabahiṣpavamānaṃ madhyaṃdinaśca rathaṃtaram̐ svāraṃ ca sauparṇam̐ śagdhyū ṣu śacīpata ityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyam̐ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatram̐ svāśirāṃ cārko 'yā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato haririti saphākṣāre pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti audalaṃ cartanidhanamājyadohamabhi priyāṇīti vaikhānasamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma 63
upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ davidyutatyā rucā pavasvendo vṛṣā suto rājā medhābhirīyata iṣe pavasva dhārayā kayā te agne aṅgiraḥ purūruṇā ciddhyasti tamindraṃ vājayāmasīyaṃ vāmasya manmāno 'sya pratnāmanu dyutamiti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cābhi somāsa āyava iti gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ cāyam̐ soma itīnidhanamājyadohamantyam̐ svāsu vāmadevyaṃ mo ṣu tvā vāghataścaneti gāyatrapārśvamastāvi manma pūrvyamiti saṃtani śrāyantiyasyarkṣu saṃkṛti yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ cā gneścārko 'bhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ ca paryū ṣvityāndhīgavam indrāya soma pātava iti yajñāyajñīyaṃ pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyaṃ viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsvāyuṣa ukthāni 64
pavasva vāco agriya eṣa devo amartya eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā hotā devo amartyo mitraṃ vayam̐ havāmahe mahām̐ indro ya ojase ndre agnā namo bṛhaduccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣūktaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ carṣabhaśca pāvamānaḥ pratnaṃ pīyūṣaṃ pūrvyaṃ yadukthyamityutsedhaḥ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamityābhīśavamātharvaṇaṃ pṛśni tisro vāca iti triṇidhanamājyadohamantyaṃ bṛhacca satrāsāhīyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca sa sunve yo vasūnāṃ prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti dīrgha śrudhye ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti niṣedhaśca śyāvāśvaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca pari pra dhanveti vājadāvaryo 'yā ruceti nityavatsā dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyo vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsu jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān 65
pavasva vājasātaya iti tisro'nuṣṭubhaścatasro gāyatrīḥ karoti pajrayaudhājaye antarā gauṅgavamaudalājyadohe gautamam̐ samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya 661
upāsmai gāyatā nara upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ davidyutatyā rucai te asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asīṣe pavasva dhārayābhi somāsa āyava iti gautamamantarikṣaṃ dairghaśravasaṃ ta ekarcāḥ santani tisṛṣu paurumadgaṃ gāyatrapārśvaṃ maidhātithaṃ ta ekarcā dvihiṃkāraṃ tisṛṣu mahānāmnyaśca vāmadevyaṃ ca pārthuraśmaṃ ca śrāyantīyasyarkṣu saṃkṛti samānamitaraṃ madhyamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya 662
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ pavamānasya te kave gāyatrāduttare catvāra ekarcā uttamaṃ ca bārhatamanuṣṭubhi traya ekarcā uttamaṃ tṛce samānāmitaramuttamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya 663
pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatramekasyāmāśvamekasyāṃ jarābodhīyamekasyāṃ pra soma devavītaya iti gauṅgavamekasyāṃ pajramekasyāṃ yaudhājayamekasyāmauśanamaupagavamāṣṭādam̐ṣṭraṃ pratyasmai pipīṣata iti nānadam̐ ṣoḍaśisāma rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya 671
upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ davidyutatyā ruce ṣe pavasva dhārayā paryū ṣviti śyāvāśvamindrāya somapātava ityāndhīgavampavitraṃ ta iti yajñāyajñīyamantyamudvam̐śīyasya loke tairaścyamudvam̐śīyam̐ ṣoḍaśisāma rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā gargatrirātrasya 672
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suta ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvamuddharati jarābodhīyam̐ satobṛhatīdvipadāticchandasaśca pratilomaṃ bārhatānyayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti dīrghatamasaścārkaḥ krauñcaṃ ca dhartā diva iti yajñāyajñīyamantyam̐ samānamitaramuttamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya 673
sāṃvatsarikasya viṣuvato bahiṣpavamānamubhau paryāsāvāśvāduttare somasāma ca rohitakūlīyaṃ ca pavasvendramaccheti saphā kṣāre samānamitaraṃ prathamenāśvatrirātrasya 681
vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunaite somā abhi priyam̐ somaḥ punāno arṣatyutte śuṣmāsa īrata iti purastātparyāsasyāharatyāmahīyavāduttarāṇyāśubhārgavaprabhṛtīni ṣaḍ dvihiṃkārāduttarāṇyacchidraṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca pauruhanmanamagneścārkāduttarāṇi surūpaprabhṛtīni catvāri yajñāyajñīyātpūrvāṇyāsitaṃ ca kautsaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca rayiṣṭham̐ samānamitaraṃ madhyamenāhnā gargatrirātrasyā 682
aṣṭarcāduttarannavamādahnaścaturaḥ ṣaḍṛcānupaharatyā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvamiti paryāsa ṛṣabhātpavamānāduttarāṇi surūpaṃ cā dārasṛcce ḍānāṃ ca saṃkṣāraḥ svāraṃ ca saindhukṣitaṃ pṛśnina uttarānyarkapuṣpaṃ kaulmalabarhiṣaṃ devasthānam̐ hāviṣmatāduttarāṇi śāṃmadaprabhṛtīni catvāri yajñāyajñīyāduttaraṃ yadvāhiṣṭhīyam̐ samānamitaramuttamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya 683
ye gargatrirātrasya prathamottame ahanī te antarvasoḥ 691
upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ davidyutatyā rucā pavasvendo vṛṣā suto rājā medhābhirīyata ete asṛgramindavo vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānuneṣe pavasva dhārayāatha yadeva chandomapavamānasya madhyamamahastadetattasya tṛcakḷpta ārbhavo 692
yāntarvasoḥ prathamā sā parākasya 693
vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānuneti purastātparyāsasyāharatyāmahīyavāduttare āśu ca bhārgavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ cā gneścārkāduttare surūpa bhāse yajñāyajñīyātpūrve āsita kautse samānamitaraṃ madhyamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya 694
vyuṣṭeruttarasyāhno bahiṣpavamānamatha yadeva chandomapavamānasyottamamahastatetattasya śaśakarṇakḷptā bṛhatī vivṛtijyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ
atha viṃśaprabhṛtibhiradhyāyairbrāhmaṇe tribhiḥ |
ye'hīnā vihitāsteṣāṃ klṛptiḥ ṣaṣṭhādiṣu triṣu ||
paurṇamāsīdīkṣāmāsāpavargāhīnā ityādi paṭalatrayam | teṣāṃ sūtramsarve'tirātrā ahīnāḥ | dvirātraprabhṛtayaścāhargaṇāḥ iti | cāturmāsyarājasūyau tu nāhīnau | tayorekāhaprakaraṇa eva samāmnānāt | ahnāṃ saṃpātābhāvācca | tata eva vakṣyamāṇo'hīnarātrerviśeṣaḥ tayorna bhavati ||
atra trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam ityādi prathamānuvākenoktasya jyotiṣṭomātirātrasya klṛptimāha
klṛpto jyotiṣṭomo'tirātro'ṣoḍaśiko yadi vā ṣoḍaśimān || 1 ||
iti | brāhmaṇe ṣaṣṭhādibhiścaturbhiradhyāyairatirātrasaṃstho jyotiṣṭomo'ṣoḍaśikaḥ klṛptaḥ | yadi vā ṣoḍaśamān iti tu
brāhmaṇāntarānusāreṇoktam | asya cātirātrasya prayoga ādāvevāsmābhirdarśitaḥ || 1 || vakṣyamāṇānāṃ rātrerviśeṣamāha 
āhīnikyāṃ rātrau | panyaṃpanyamit sotāra iti | śrautakakṣaṃ daivodāsaṃ hotṛṣāma kautsasya loka āṣṭādaṃṣṭram ā tvā viśantvindava ityetāsu || 2 ||
samānamitaraṃ sātrikyā || 3 ||
iti | daivodāsaṃ hotṛṣāmeti | iha khalvājyāni pṛṣṭhāni sāgniṣṭomāni cokthāni | pratyekaṃ rātriparyāyā iti catvāri catvāri sāmāni yathākramaṃ hotṛmaitrāvaruṇabrāhmaṇācchaṃsyacchāvākairanuśaṃsyanta iti tattatsaṃbandhena vyavahriyante | tataśca śrautakakṣāt paraṃ yaddhotṛṣāma madhyamasya rātriparyāyasya prathamaṃ sāma taddaivodāsaṃ niyatam | na tvaurvasadmanena saha vaikalpikamityarthaḥ | sātrikyā iti tulyārthayoge caturthīprayogaḥ chāndasaḥ ! sātrikyā rātryāḥ samānamanyadityarthaḥ | satreṣu dvādaśāhādiṣu yā rātriḥ sā sātrikī | evaṃ ca jyotiṣṭomena samānamiti vaktavye sātrikyeti vacanaṃ satreṣu sarvastomātirātrādīnāmāhinikānāmapyatideśe prākṛtyeva rātri
riti pradarśanārtham | brāhmaṇe ca ṣaṣṭhādibhiradhyāyairvihito jyotiratirātraḥ svatantraḥ eva | nanu dvādaśāhābhūtaḥ tasmiṃśca nāhinikīrātririti pratipāditam | sūtre jyotiṣṭomavidhiranantarāmnānāt ityādinā || 2 ||
iti jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ || 1 |||
sarvastomo'tirātraḥ
atha sarvastomo'tirātraḥ | trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam| pañcadaśānyajyāni | saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | ekaviṃśāni pṛṣṭhāni | triṇava ārbhavaḥ | trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | pratyavarohīṇyukthāni | triṇavaṃ prathamam | dve ekaviṃśe saṣoḍaśike | pañcadaśī rātriḥ | trivṛtsaṃdhiḥ | sarvastomenātirātreṇa bubhūṣan yajeta ityanuvākenoktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
sarvastomasyopavatī pratipat | svāsu śyaitam parisvānārbhavīyā | saṃhitāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyam | āndhīgavādātharvaṇam || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇa || 2 ||
tasya jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśamān || 3 ||
iti | ārbhavīyetyārbhavādyā gāyatrītyarthaḥ | upadavipaveti bahiṣpavamānam | agnaā no mitrāmitraṃ vayamaindramidgāthinaḥindre agnetyājyāni ||
asya pratneti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti samantamādyāyām | rathantara dairghaśravase tṛcayoḥ | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni || parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatrasaṃhite jarābodhīyam | pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye | purojitī va iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | ātharvaṇaṃ ca | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharaṃ nārmedham mi tyukthāni | gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | āhīnikī rātriḥ | saṃdhiḥ prākṛtaḥ | ataḥprabhṛti brāhmaṇenaiva stomānāṃ
vidhānāt ārṣayakalpe noktaḥ || 3 ||
iti sarvastomo'tirātraḥ ||2||
aptoryāmaḥ
athāptoryāmaḥtrivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśānyājyāni | saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | ekaviṃśaṃ hotuḥ pṛṣṭham | chandomā itarāṇi | triṇava ārbhavaḥ | trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | pratyavarohīṇyukthāni | triṇava prathamamathaikaviṃśamatha saptadaśam | ekaviṃśaḥ ṣoḍaśī pañcadaśī rātristrivṛtsaṃdhiḥ trivṛtprathamātiriktastotramatha pañcadaśamatha saptadaśamathaikaviṃśam ityanuvākenoktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ
aptoryāmnastṛtīyasya sahasrasyājyabahiṣpavamānam | jarābodhīyasya loke surūpam | uddharatyātharvaṇam | pari pra dhanva iti svarnidhanaṃ sauhaviṣam || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa || 2 ||
vaiśvajitāni pṛṣṭhāni pṛṣṭhānāṃ garbhāḥ || 3 ||
anubrāhmaṇamatiriktastotrāṇi | 4 ||
iti | garbhaprakāro nidānakāreṇa vyākhyātaḥ | tatra garbhavidhānamāhurmadhyamaṃ paryāyaṃ sastrotrīyaṃ garbhaṃ kuryāt | prathamottamau mukhyasya | evaṃ garbhopapattirbhavatīti | so'yaṃ bṛhatpṛṣṭho'ptoryāmaḥ kalpakāreṇoktaḥ | sūtrakāreṇa tu bahavo'ptoryāmavikalpā darśitāḥ | aptoryāme kṛtā garbhāḥ kalpena | rathantaraṃ vā pṛṣṭham | tasya sthāne bārhataṃ vājajit | bṛhadvāravantīye uttare pṛṣṭhe | kāleyasyarkṣu vāravantīyam | tasya sthāne pūrvam | samantasya ca kāleyam | pārthasyauśanam | ta eva garbhāḥ pṛṣṭheṣu ityekaḥ pakṣaḥ ||
athāsya pakṣasya kalpoktasya ca sādhāraṇamāha -- vaiśvajitānyājyāna ājyeṣu garbhān kuryāditi dhānaṃjayyaḥ | ṣāṣṭikānyukthānyuktheṣu iti | asmin api pakṣe yajñāyajñīyasya garbho nāsti | pakṣadvayepi pṛṣṭhānāmeva garbhā iti śāṇḍilyasya matam | tadāha
yathaivārṣeyakalpena kṛtamiti śāṇḍilyaḥ iti | tasyaiva matena kalpāntaramāhajyotiṣṭome vātirātre'tiriktastotrāṇyādadhyāt ṣoḍaśimatīti śāṇḍilyaḥ | ta eva garbhāḥ pṛṣṭheṣu yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca bṛhadgarbha miti | so'yaṃ jyotiraptoryāma ucyate | nidānakārastvāhaapi vā sarvāṇyāvarttīni vaiśvajitairāvarttibhirgarbhavanti kuryāt | tatra bṛhat saṃcarate tasya vairūpaṃ vidhṛtyā iti | tatra tāvannidānakāramatamāśritya bṛhatpṛṣṭhasya prayoga ucyate | tatra bṛhatpṛṣṭhātirātravat sarvam | viśeṣastupavasva vāco davidyutatyā rucāpavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtam ā no mitrāindramidgāthinaḥ indrāgnī āgatam ityājyāni | caturṛce'ntyāyā uddhāraḥ | suṣamiddho na āvaha tā naḥ śaktaṃ pārthivasya yuñjanti bradhnam tamīḍiṣva yo arciṣā ityājyānāṃ garbhāḥ | suṣamiddha ityatra śunakakaṇvasaṃkṛtivādhryaśvānāṃ dvitīyāmṛcamuddharet | tṛtīyāmanyeṣām | ṛgjapakāle garbhiṇāṃ garbhāṇāṃ ca krameṇa ṛgjapaḥ | tatra prathamamājyasya pañcadaśastomasya prathamaṃ paryāyam agniṃ dūtam iti tṛcena stutvā suṣamiddha iti tṛcena dvitīyaṃ paryāyaṃ stuyuḥ | punaragniṃ dūtam ityanena tṛtīyam | evamuttareṣvapyājyeṣu draṣṭavyam ||
atha mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ| asya pratnāmanudyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | parīto ṣi iti samantaṃ prathamāyām | rathantaraṃ tisṛṣu | dairghaśravasaṃ tisṛṣu | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyamiti saptadaśaḥ | rathantarasya kakubuttarā bṛhatī chandaḥ | pṛṣṭhye rathamativaheyurityādinoktā rathaghoṣādayaḥ pṛṣṭhadharmā atra kāryāḥ | kathaṃ dharmāḥ kartavyā ityeke | garbhabhūtāni bhavantyanupabhūtānyadharmā garbho bhavatīti kartavyā ityācāryā ityādinidānavacanāt | atra pavamāne rathantarastavanakāle bahirvedi paścāt santaṃ rathaṃ prāñcaṃ vaheyuḥ | bṛhatpṛṣṭhaṃ tasya vairājaṃ garbhaḥ | ekaviṃśastomaḥ | bṛhadvairāje pibatāṃ somyaṃ madhvadhvaryurdadhatī yajñapatāviti pṛṣṭhahome viśeṣaḥ | vairājasya virāṭ chandaḥ | pibā somamindra mandantu tvetyādi vidhāyakabrāhmaṇam | vasanto hiṃkāra iti bhaktyupāsanam | prākstomayogād airaṃ vai bṛhadityādīnāṃ daśavyāhṛtīnāṃ dhyānam | araṇībhyāṃ darbhadvayena copākṛte stotre tadaraṇīdvayaṃ darbhadvayaṃ cādāyodgātā svasya dakṣiṇa ūrau kāṣṭhaśakalaṃ darbhadvayaṃ cānūrū nidhāya śakalasyopari tiraścīmaraṇiṃ tasyā upari uttarāmaraṇiṃ nidhāya triḥpradakṣiṇamabhimanthet | gāyatraṃ chando'nuprajāyasva | traiṣṭubhaṃ chando'nuprajāyasva | jāgataṃ chando'nuprajāyasveti mantrairaraṇyoḥ saṃdhānam | pāṇibhyāmālabhya tejo'si tejo mayi dhehi iti pāṇibhyām upajighret | athānyena mathyamāne stomaṃ yuktvā dundubhighoṣaṃ kārayanta ekaviṃśasyaikaṃ paryāyaṃ bṛhatā stutvā mathitamagnimanyena jvālayanto vairājena madhyamaṃ stuyuḥ | punardundubhighoṣaṃ kārayantaḥ uttamaṃ paryāyaṃ bṛhatā samāpayeyuḥ |
yajamānaṃ vācayitvā punarūrjeti sparītyantena nirmathitamagnimabhimantrayeta | tasminnadhvaryuṇāhavanīye prahṛte preddho agna iti virājā svāhāntayā juhuyāt | svāheti ca vāmadevyaṃ maitrāvaruṇasāma | tasya mahānāmnyo garbhaḥ | caturviṃśaḥ stomaḥ | mahānāmnīnāṃ śakvarī chandaḥ | indraḥ prajāpatimupādhāvaditi vidhāyakabrāhmaṇam | pṛthivī hiṃkāra iti bhaktyupāsanam | prākstomayogāt gāvo aśvā iti dhyānam | śaivālamiśritābhiradbhirdarbhadvayena copākṛte stotre darbhadvayaṃ grahītvāpaḥ samīpe nidhāya vāmadevyenaikaṃ paryāyaṃ samāpya śakvarībhirmadhyamaṃ paryāyaṃ stuyuḥ | tatra yo yaḥ sāmāṅgaṃ brūyāt sa udaghoṣaṃ janayet | prathama eva prastāve kuśāvidhānam | dvitīyādiṣu prastāveṣu noṃkāreṇodgīthādānam | uttamābhyāsādanantaram āivā ityādi purīṣapadaiḥ stavanam | evaṃ madhyamaṃ paryāyaṃ samāpya punarvāmadevyena tṛtīyaṃ paryāyaṃ mtuyuḥ | yajamānavācanaṃ kṛtvā tadudakamāstāve'nādhṛṣṭāsīti videyetyantena ninayet | jānunoḥ śaivālānupaharet | athopariṣṭājjapaḥ | śyaitaṃ brahmasāma | tasya vairūpaṃ garbhaḥ | catuścatvāriṃśaḥ stomaḥ | yadyāva indra te śatam iti śatavatyo bhavanti ityādi vairūpasya brāhmaṇam | abhrāṇi saṃplavanta ityabhidhyānam | śyaitavairūpe sāmanī upadhāvāmi | abhi pra vo yadyāvaṛcāvupadhāvāmi | prajāpativiśvarūpāvṛṣī upadhāvāmi | catuścatvāriṃśastomamupadhāvāmītyupasaraṇe viśeṣaḥ | darbhadvayena dhavitreṇa ca stotra upākṛte tad dhavitraṃ yajamānaparicārakahasteṣu nidhāya śyetenaikaṃ paryāyaṃ samāpya dhavitreṇopavījyamānā vairūpeṇa madhyamaṃ paryāyaṃ stuvīran | punaśca śyaitenottamaṃ paryāyaṃ vācayitvā yajamānaṃ dhavitreṇa vāyumutpādya taṃ vāta āvātviti tṛcenānumantrayet | kāleyamacchāvakasāma | tasya revatyo garbhaḥ | aṣṭācatvāriṃśat stomaḥ | ṣoḍaśabhyo hiṃkaroti pathyā viṣṭutiḥ | revatīnāṃ gāyatrī chandaḥ | yātayāmānyanyāni chandāṃsi ityādi brāhmaṇam | kāleyenaikaṃ paryāyaṃ samāpya revatībhirmadhyamaṃ paryāyaṃ stuvīran | tāsāṃ stotre dhenūrdakṣiṇato vatsāṃścottarataḥ kṛtvā dhenūnāṃ vāśitaṃ japeyuḥ | punaḥ kāleyenottame paryāye stute dhenūrvatsāṃśca pūrveṇa sadaḥ saṃmṛjya paścicamenāgnīdhrīyamudīcīriti kṣipeyuḥ | yajamānaṃ vācayitvā gonāmabhiranumantrayeta | havye kāmya ityādinā vośīyetyantena adbhirūpākṛtaṃ cet tadā tā āpo hi ṣṭhā iti tṛcenāstāve ninayet | athārbhavastriṇavaḥ | pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatrasaṃhita surūpāṇi | pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye | purojitī ti śyāvāśva āndhīgave | pari pra dhanvetyakṣarapaṅktiṣu svarnidhanaṃ sauhaviṣam | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | tasya tvamagne yajñānām iti bṛhadgarbhaḥ | trayastriṃśaḥ stomaḥ ekādaśabhyo hiṃkarotītyādibhirviṣṭutibhiḥ | vṛhato gāyatrī chandaḥ | daśavyāhṛtīnāṃ dhyānam | dundubhighoṣaśca pūrvavat | nājāigyūṣā iti ṣūṣūkrātāvityevātra vaktavyam | stotrottamāyāṃ pratya
varohasya vidhānāt | atra pṛṣṭhāgniṣṭomavidhānayordvayorapi rathantarāvyavahitena bṛhatā stavanājjāmitvaṃ prāptamapi abhi pravatā śyaitena parihṛtam | tathā ca jāmikaraṇānītyadhikṛtya nidānamkaṇvarathantaramabhivahati | rathantaranidhanāni bṛhatsaṃnipāteṣu iti | athokthāni | ehyūṣu iti sākamaśvam | vayamu tveiti saubharam | adhāhīndra iti nārmedham | iti tāni ca kramāt triṇavaikaviṃśasaptadaśastomāni | teṣāṃ garbhā na santi | viśvajiti teṣāmevokthatvāt| anye tu ṣāṣṭikānyukthānyutheṣviti pakṣāntaroktānatra garbhānāviḥkurvanti | tadyathāsākamaśvasya gūrdo garbhaḥ | saubharasya bhadram | nārmedhasyodvaṃśaputra iti | garbhāṇāṃ dvipadā chandaḥ | garbhavidhānamuktam | so'yaṃ pakṣadvayasaṃkaro nāsmabhyaṃ rocate | atha ṣoḍaśyādi saṃdhistotrāntam | tatra rātrau panyaṃ panyaṃ śrautakakṣam | daivodāsam hotṛṣāma nityam | kautsasya loke āṣṭādaṃṣṭram | ā tvā viśantvindava ityetāsviti viśeṣaḥ |
catvāryatiriktastotrāṇi brāhmaṇena klṛptāni | tatra ca saṃdhicamasabhakṣaṇānantaraṃ niṣkramaṇādi yajamānopahavāntaṃ kṛtvā svāsu jarābodhīyena trivṛtā stuyuḥ | agnirgāyatryagnirityṛṣyādayaḥ | parivartinī viṣṭutiḥ | gāyatracchandasa hati bhakṣaṇam | gāyatracchandasātiriktastotreṣviti gautama iti vacanāt | tato niṣkramaṇādyupahavāntaṃ kṛtvā svāsu satrāsāhīyena pañcadaśena stuyuḥ | indro gāyatrīndraḥ | pūrvavat bhakṣayitvā punarniṣkramaṇādyupahavāntaṃ kṛtvā svāsu mārgīyaveṇa saptadaśena stuyuḥ | indro gāyatrī viśve devāḥ | pūrvavat bhakṣaṇam | punarupahavāntaṃ kṛtvā idaṃ viṣṇur iti vāravantīyenaikaviṃśena stuyuḥ | indro gāyatrī viṣṇuḥ | pūrvavadbhakṣaṇam | atha stomavimocanādyuvadasanīyāntam ||
iti bṛhatpṛṣṭho'ptoryāmaḥ ||
rathantarapṛṣṭhe tu samantasya sthāne tasyāmevarci kāleyam | rathantarasya sthāne tāsveva | mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyeti vājijid antyam | pārthasya sthāne svāsvauśanam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca svāsu bṛhacca | kāleyasyarkṣu vāravantīyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | idaṃ viṣṇuriti vāravantīyasya sthāne tasminneva tṛce
pūrvaṃ vāravantīyamūhet | anyat sarvaṃ bṛhatpṛṣṭhāptoryāmavat | jyotiraptoryāme tu jyotiṣṭomānāṃ vairājādayo garbhāḥ pūrvavat | yajñāyajñīyasya tvamagne yajñānāmiti bṛhadgarbhaḥ | saṃdherūrdhvamatiriktastotrāṇi | anyatsarvaṃ jyotiratirātravat | kecit punarjyotiratirātre garbharahite saṃdhistotrānantaramatiriktastotrāṇīcchanti | tat pramāṇaṃ gaveṣaṇīyam | itare tvaptoryāmavikalpāḥ spaṣṭāḥ || 4 || iti aptoryāmaḥ || 3 ||
ṭippaṇī
navasaptadaśaḥ
atha navasaptadaśaḥ | trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśa hoturājyam | saptadaśāni stotrāṇi | ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | sokthaḥ | pañcadaśī rātriḥ | trivṛt saṃdhiḥ | navasaptadaśenātirātreṇa ityanuvākenoktaḥ | navasaptadaśāni stotrāṇīti maitrāvaruṇājyaprabhṛtyārbhavāntāni navastotrāṇi saptadaśānītyarthaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ
navasaptadaśasyāmahīyavāduttaraṃ pavasva madhumattama iti sākamaśvam | abhi somāsa āyava iti janitraṃ rauravayaudhājaye | naudhasasyarkṣu janitram | pari pra dhanve ti sapham ||1|| samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomenātirātreṇāṣoḍaśikena || 2 ||
iti | upadavipaveti bahiṣpavamānam | agnaā nomitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasaiti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | pavasva madhumattama iti sākamaśvamekasyām | kakubyatra mādhyaṃdina ekarcastasyāmiti vacanāt | abhi somāsa āyava iti janitra mekasyām | ekamekarcaṃ kurvan prathamāyāmāditaḥ kuryāt ityārabhya mādhyaṃdine cedeko dvau vā bṛhatyāmeveti vacanāt | pūrvaṃ janitrametat | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | naudhasasyarkṣu janitramuttaram | svāsu kāleyammiti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhaye ti gāyatrasaṃhite | pari pra dhanvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale ekarce | purojitī va iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni | ṣoḍaśī na kartavyaḥ | āhīnikī rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ || 2 ||
iti navasaptadaśaḥ || 4 ||
viṣuvān atirātraḥ
atha viṣuvān | trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśānyājyāni | pañcadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | saptadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni | saptadaśa ārbhavaḥ | ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | sattadaśānyukthāni | pañcadaśī rātristrivṛtsaṃdhiḥ | viṣuvatātirātreṇa ityanuvākenoktaḥ
tasya klṛpti -- 
yā jyotiṣṭomasya sā viṣuvataḥ | tasya saptadaśānyukthāni aṣoḍaśiko'tirātraḥ || 1 ||
iti | tasya sarvaṃ jyotiratirātravat | ukthāni saptadaśāni | ṣoḍaśī nāsti iti viśeṣaḥ || 1 ||
iti viṣuvān atirātraḥ || 5 ||
goāyuṣī atirātre
pañcadaśaṃ bahiṣpavamānam ityanuvākābhyāṃ goāyuṣī klṛpte vihite | tayoḥ kalpaḥ
klṛpte goāyuṣī || 1 ||
iti | gorvārkajambhasya loka ityādinā ca āyuṣo vārkajambhasya loka ityādinā klṛpte | tayorāhīnikī rātriḥ || 1 ||
iti goāyuṣī atirātre || 6 ||
viśvajidabhijitau
trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśaṃ hoturājyam ityaṣṭamenānuvākena abhijidatirātra uktaḥ| navamena viśvajit | tayoḥ kalpaḥ -- 
klṛptau viśvajidabhijitau ||1||
iti | abhi pra gopatiṃ girāityabhijitaḥ ityādinā viśvajitaḥ pavasvendramacchetyādinā ca klṛptau| tayorāhīnikī rātriḥ || 1||
iti viśvajidabhijitau || 7 ||
ekastomāścatvāraḥ
athaikastomāścatvāraḥ | trivṛtātirātreṇa ityanuvākena prathamaḥ uktaḥ | pañcadaśenātirātreṇeti dvitīyaḥ | saptadaśenātirātreṇeti tṛtīyaḥ | ekaviṃśenātirātriṇeti caturthaḥ ||
iti ekastomāścatvāraḥ ||
trivṛdatirātraḥ
tatra prathamasya kalpaḥ -- 
yā bṛhaspatisavasya saikastomānāṃ prathamasya | tasyaiva jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān || 1||
iti | agniṣṭomāntaṃ bṛhaspatisavavat | sākamaśvaprabhṛti jyotiṣṭomavat| sarvāṇi stotrāṇi trivṛnti | ṣoḍaśī tu ṣoḍaśastrivṛdvā | tadvakṣyati -- ṣoḍaśāḥ ṣoḍaśino'nubrāhmaṇaṃ ve ti ||1||
iti trivṛdatirātraḥ || 8||
pañcadaśo'tirātraḥ
dvitīyamekastomamāha
gorājyabahiṣpavamānam | indrastomasya mādhyaṃdinaḥ | punānaḥ soma dhāraya iti bṛhatī| svāsu śyaitam || 1||
samānamitaraṃ prathamena sāhasreṇa || 2 ||
tasya jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān ||3||
iti | pavasva vāco agriyodavidyutatyā rucā pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma
dyumām̐ asi pavamānasya te kave 
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agni dūtam vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni | uccā te iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | punānaḥ so iti samantaṃ
ca yaudhājayaṃ ca| vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhaye ti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye | purojitī vo iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam | saphaśrudhyaśyāvāśvānyekarceṣu | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaprabhṛti jyotiṣṭomavat | āhīnikī rātriḥ | sarvaḥ pañcadaśī| ṣoḍaśī ṣoḍaśaḥ pañcadaśo vā | saṃdhestu pañcadaśapūraṇārthamāvāpo'bhyāso vā kāryaḥ| taduktam -- athoktissaṃdhāvāvāpo'bhyāso veti| tatrābhyāsapakṣe pañcabhyo hiṃkarotīti viṣṭutiḥ || 13||
iti pañcadaśo'tirātraḥ || 9 ||
saptadaśo'tirātraḥ
tṛtīyamekastomamāha
tīvrasuta ājyabahiṣpavamānam | mādhyaṃdinaśca ||1||
samānamitaraṃ prathamenaikastomena || 2 ||
iti | upāsmaidavidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavas taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam |
agnaā nomitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | uccā te iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| abhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāramādyāyām | maidhātithaṃ tisṛṣu | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | auśanamantyam |
pṛṣṭhādisaṃdhistotrāntaṃ jyotiṣṭomavat | sarvaṃ saptadaśastomamiti viśeṣaḥ | ṣoḍaśo vā| ṣoḍaśī āhīnikī rātriḥ || 1.2 ||
iti saptadaśo'tirātraḥ || 10 ||
ekaviṃśo'tirātraḥ
caturthamekastomamāha -- 
pratīcīnastomasya bahiṣpavamānam | abhito gāyatrī | jyotiṣa uttarasya bṛhatī ||1||
samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenaikastomena ||2||
iti | jyotiṣa uttarasyeti pakṣiṇa uttarasyetyarthaḥ | atrottarasyeti viśeṣaṇena sāhasrāṇāṃ prathamasya vyāvṛttiḥ | pavasva vācopavasvendo davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asivṛṣā hyasi bhānunā pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtammitraṃ vayamindramidgā caturṛcam | indre agnetyājyāni | uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ satrāsāhīyam | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye | purojitī vo śyāvāśvāndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīyādi saṃdhistotrāntaṃ jyotiṣṭomavat | āhīnikī rātriḥ | sarvamekaviṃśam | ṣoḍaśo vā ṣoḍaśī veti viśeṣaḥ || 12 || ekastomeṣu ṣoḍaśīnāṃ stomaklṛptimāha -- 
ṣoḍaśaḥ ṣoḍaśino'nubrāhmaṇaṃ vā || 3 ||
iti | atra sūtram -- anubrāhmaṇaṃ veti | prakṛtyanvayādekaviṃśāhastomaḥ | saṃdhikārādiprakṛtistomānvayādekaviṃśaḥ ṣoḍaśina iti pūrvaḥ pakṣaḥ | ahastomastviti siddhāntaḥ | tataścāyaṃ kalpasyārthaḥ ekastomānāṃ ṣoḍaśinaḥ ṣoḍaśa stomā vā syuḥ |
api vā tasya tasyāhnastrivṛtātirātreṇetyādinā tena brāhmaṇena yaḥ stomo vihitaḥ sa stomaḥ syāditi | tathā caivodāhṛtam iti trayodaśātirātra uktaḥ ||3||
iti ekaviṃśo'tirātraḥ || 11 ||
ekastomāḥ samāptāḥ ||
dvirātrāstrayaḥ
aṅgirasadvirātraḥ
atha dvirātrāstrayaḥ| jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomaḥ pūrvamahar ityanuvākenāṅgirāsadvirātra uktaḥ| tasya kalpaḥ -- 
ye vyuṣṭidvirātrasyāhanī te āṅgirasadvirātrasyottarādahna ṛṣabhajarābodhīye uddharati | samantadairghaśravase antarātharvaṇam | saṃhitāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyam | viśoviśīyasya loke śrudhyam || 1 ||
iti | prathamasyāhnaḥ upadavipaveti bahiṣpavamānam |
agnaā nomitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | punānaḥ so rauravayaudhājaye | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhaye ti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣkṛtaṃ ca | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir ityekarcayoḥ saphapauṣkale | purojitītyaudalāndhīgave | kāva mantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | jyotiṣṭomastomāḥ | dadhibhakṣaṇāntamahaḥ ||
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ -- pavasva vāco agriyaḥ eṣa devo amartyaḥ eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam iti bahiṣpavamānam | tena sadasi stavanam | ahīnabahiṣpavamānaiḥ sadasi stuvīran prathamādahro'nyatreti vacanāt | prāk dhiṣṇyaviharaṇānniṣkramya tṛṇaprāsanaprabhṛti samāpayeyuḥ| agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni | asya pratneti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | parīto ṣi iti samantamādyāyām | ātharvaṇadairghaśravase tṛcayoḥ | utsedho'dhyāsyāyām | ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyam |
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca vaikhānasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatrasaṃhitajarābodhīyāni | pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣeti niṣedhaḥ śyāvāśvaṃ krauñcam | dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko 'ntyaḥ| yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni | gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | āhīnikī rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ ||
atha stomāḥ -- caturviśaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśanyājyāni | saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | ekaviṃśāni pṛṣṭhāni | triṇava ārbhavaḥ | trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | pratyavarohīṇyukthāni | triṇavaṃ prathamam | athaikaviṃśam | atha saptadaśam | ekaviṃśaḥ ṣoḍaśī pañcadaśī rātristrivṛt saṃdhiriti || 1 ||
iti āṅgirasadvirātraḥ || 12 ||
caitrarathaḥ
atha yasya jyotirukthyaḥ ityanuvākena caitraratha uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
yāṅgirasasya dvirātrasya pūrvā sā caitrarathasya | tasyāyuṣa ukthāni || 1 ||
pūrveti pūrvasyāhnaḥ klṛptirityarthaḥ | pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇamudvam̐śīyamityukthānyekaviṃśāni ||1||
[iti pūrvamahaḥ ]
dvitīyamaharāha -- 
pavastra vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ ā te dakṣaṃ mayo bhuvam || 2||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
śrudhyasya loke jarābodhīyam | nārmedhādrātrimupayanti| atha yadeva dvidivasasyottamamahastadetat || 3||
iti | pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ ā te dakṣaṃ mayo bhuvam iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni | asya pratnāmanu dyutam iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca| parīto ṣi samantaṃ prathamāyām | dairghaśravasaṃ tisṛṣu | aiḍamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu | triṇidhanamāyasyamadhyāsyāyām | ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca vaikhānasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mada iti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyajarābodhīye | ayaṃ pūṣeti śyāvāśvakrauñce | dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharaṃ nārmedha mityukthāni | ṣoḍaśī nāsti | nārmedhādrātrimupayantīti vacanāt | āhīnikī rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ | āyuratirātra uttaramiti brāhmaṇādāyūrātrasya stomāḥ ||3||
iti caitrarathaḥ || 13 ||
kāpivanaḥ
atha yasya pañcadaśo'gniṣṭomaḥ ityanuvākena kāpivanadvirātra uktaḥ| tasya kalpaḥ 
ye caitrarathasyāhanī te kāpivanasya | trivṛtpañcadaśo'gniṣṭomaḥ
pūrvamaharāyuratirātra uttaram ||1||
iti| prathamasyāhnaḥ sarvaṃ caitrarathavat | vyatyāsena trivṛtpañcadaśau
stomau| ārbhave'ntyavarjamekarcāḥ | agniṣṭomasāma pañcadaśamiti tasmādviśeṣaḥ | uttarasyāhnaḥ sarvaṃ caitrarathavadeva || 1 ||
iti kāpivanaḥ || 14 ||
dvirātrāstrayaḥ samāptāḥ ||
trirātrāḥ ṣaṭ
gargatrirātraḥ prathamamahaḥ
atha trirātrāḥ ṣaṭ | trivṛtprātaḥsavanam ityādibhiḥ pañcabhiranuvākaiḥ gargatrirātra uktaḥ | tasya prathamamaharāha -- 
ābhiplavikasya prathamasyāhnaḥ ājyabahiṣpavamānam | mādhyaṃdinaśca ||1|| ityādinā| upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavamānasya te vayam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnaā nomitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ cāśvaṃ ca| pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam | yaudhājayamekākṣaranidhanamahīnakāṇḍe paṭhitam ||1||
gargatrirātraḥ rathantaraṃ svāraṃ ca sauparṇam | śagdhyūṣu śacīpata iti abhīvartaḥ | svāsu kāleyam || 2||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayā iti gāyatraṃ svāśirāṃ cārko'yā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir iti saphākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasaḥ ityaudalaṃ cartanidhanamājyadoham | abhi priyāṇi iti vaikhānasam antyam || 3 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ]
mādhyaṃdine savane vihṛtaṃ hoturdhiṣṇīyamagniṃ dhārayitvā tamājyadohastavanakāle jvalayeyuḥ | taduktaṃ brāhmaṇe -- ājyadohānāmagnimupanidhāya stuvata ityādinā | saphākṣāre ekarce || 3 ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 4 ||
trivṛtprātaḥsavanam | pañcadaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | saptadaśaṃ tṛtīyaṃ savanam ||4||
iti prathamamahaḥ || 16 ||
dvitīyamahaḥ
dvitīyamaharāha -- 
upoṣu jātamapturam davidyutatyā rucā pavasvendo vṛṣā suto rājāmedhābhirīyate iṣe pavasva dhāraya || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
kayā te agne aṅgiraḥ purūruṇā ciddhyasti tamindraṃ vājayāmasi iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ || 2||
ityājyāni ||
asya pratnāmanudyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cābhisomāsa āyavaḥ iti gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ cāyaṃ soma iti nidhanamājyadoham antyam || 3 ||
iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
gautamaṃ prathamāyām | pra hinvāna iti dvihiṃkāramadhyāsyāyām | prātaḥsavane vihṛtasya hoturdhiṣṇyasya dhāraṇam | tasyājyadohastavanakāle prajvalanam || 3||
svāsu vāmadevyam | moṣu tvā vāghataśca na iti gāyatrapārśvam | astāvi manma pūrvyam iti saṃtani | śrayantīyasyarkṣu saṃkṛti ||4||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
santanina uttarayoḥ stotrīyayoḥ ādāveva kuśāvidhānam | aprastāvāsvādāviti vacanāt || 4 ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārko'bhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ sakhāya ā niṣīdata iti vācaḥ sāma śauktaṃ ca | paryūṣu ityāndhīgavam | indrāya soma pavate iti yajñāyajñīyam | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||6||
svāsvāyuṣa ukthāni || 7||
pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇam udvaṃśīyamityukthāni | pañcadaśaṃ prātaḥsavanam | saptadaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | ekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ soktham || 7 ||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ || 17 ||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
tṛtīyamaharāha -- 
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ eṣa devo amartyaḥ eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam || 1||
hotā devo amartyo mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe mahām̐ indro ya ojasāindre agnā namo bṛhad ||2||
ityājyāni ||
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣūktaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ carṣabhaśca pāvamānaḥ | pratnaṃ pīyūṣaṃ pūrvyaṃ yadukthyam ityutsedhaḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam ityābhīṣavam ātharvaṇaṃ pṛśni | tisro vācaḥ iti triṇidhanamājyadoham antyam || 3 ||
iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ|| ]
pūrvavadagnimupanidhāyājyadohastavanam ||3||
bṛhacca satrāsāhīyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca | 4||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca| sa sunve yo vasūnām prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti dīrghaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti niṣedhaśca śyāvāśvaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca | pari pra dhanva iti vājadāvaryo'yā rucā iti nityavatsāḥ | dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ || 4||
ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsu || 5||
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān ||6||
āhīnikī rātriḥ| ekaviṃśaṃ prātaḥsavanam | triṇavaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | trayastriṃśa ārbhavaḥ | catustriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | ekaviṃśānyukthāni saṣoḍaśikāni | ṣoḍaśaṃ prathamaṃ rātriṣāma | pañcadaśī rātriḥ| trivṛtsaṃdhiriti | stomāścatustriṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ | ekādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti | sa tisṛbhiḥ sa saptabhiḥ sa ekayā | ekādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa saptabhiḥ | dvādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti so'ṣṭābhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiriti | uktaṃ ca | dāśarātrikebhyaḥ ekayā jyāyastvityādi | pūrvamahardvayaṃ dadhibhakṣāntam | uttamamaharudavasānīyāntam ||
vāgvai śabalītyanuvākena śabalīhoma uktaḥ | sa ca sūtre vyākhyātaḥ| sahasrapoṣakāmaḥ śabalīhomaṃ kurvītetyādinā || 6||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ || 18||
gargatrirātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
aśvatrirātraḥ
prathamamahaḥ
catuṣṭomo'gniṣṭoma ekaviṃśa ukthaḥ sarvastomo'tirātra ityanuvākenāśvamedhatrirātra uktaḥ | rājā pratijño'śvamedhena yajeta | brahmodanabhojanakāle brahmodanamudgātā bhuñjīta | yajamānācca sahasraṃ pratigṛhṇīyāt | sāvitreṣṭisaṃsthāsu saṃvatsaramaharaharhotari pāriplavamācakṣāṇe hiraṇyakaśipunyāsīta | saṃvatsarādūrdhvam aśvaṃ badhvā yajamāne dīkṣite tadāsanaṃ svīkuryāt | sarveṣāṃ cāhnāṃ cātvāle bahiṣpavamānena stuvīran | tatra prathamamaharāha -- 
pavasva vājasātaye iti tisro'nuṣṭubhaścatasro gāyatrīḥ karoti || 1|| pajrayaudhājaye antarā gauṅgavam |
audalājyadohe gautamam || 2 ||
samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya ||3||
iti | pavasva vājasātayom | devebhyo madhumattarom | vatsaṃ jātaṃ na mātarom | pṛthivīṃ cātijabhriṣom iti bahiṣpavamānam|
agnaā nomitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni| pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ cāśvaṃ ca| pra soma devavītaya iti pajram gauṅgavaṃ yaudhājayamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | auśanamantyam | trīnyatarasyāṃ
saṃbhaveyuriti vacanāt | bṛhatyāmekarcakaraṇam | rathantaraṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇam | śagdhyūṣu śacīpata ityabhīvartaḥ | svāsu kāleyamiti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayati gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir iti ekarcayoḥ saphākṣāre pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudalaṃ gautamaṃ cartanidhanaṃ cājyadoham | abhi priyāṇīti vaikhānasamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | prathamacatuṣṭomavat stomaklṛptiḥ | viṣṭutayaḥ caturviṃśasya tvekasminneva tṛce catvāraḥ ṣaṭkāḥ| paryāyād dvikaviṣṭāvā iti viśeṣaḥ | athavaivaṃ viṣṭutiḥ -- ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā | ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa dvābhyām | ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti sa dvābhyāṃ sa dvābhyāṃ sa dvābhyām | ṣaḍbhyo hiṃkaroti sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiriti | taduktam -- upottamo vā dvikaviṣṭāvo'śvamedhe'ṣṭādaśāditare iti | pañcadaśaparyāyau pūrvāvekaviṃśasyottarāviti vā paryāyaklṛptiḥ | aśvamedhenāvabhṛthatvamahnāṃ kecidadhvaryava āmananti | tatra sūtram -- aśvatrirātre nānāvabhṛthānyahāni | teṣāṃ prathamasya sanādagna ityavabhṛthasāma | yadvā u viśpatiriti dvitīyasya | tyagnāiriti veti | itarathā dadhibhakṣaṇāntaṃ dvayam | tasmin pakṣe ahīnanyāyena bahiṣpavamānena stavanam ||3||
iti prathamamahaḥ || 16 ||
dvitīyamahaḥ
dvitīyamaharāha -- 
upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ upoṣu jātamapturaṃ davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo rājāmedhābhirīyate vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi iṣe pavasva dhāraya || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
abhi somāsa āyava iti gautamam antarikṣaṃ dairghaśravasam | ta ekarcāḥ | santani tisṛṣu | paurumadgaṃ gāyatrapārśvaṃ maidhātithaṃ ta ekarcāḥ | dvihiṃkāraṃ tisṛṣu | mahānāmnyaśca vāmadevyaṃ ca pārthuraśmaṃ ca śrāvantīyasyarkṣu saṃkṛti || 2 ||
samānamitaraṃ madhyamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya ||3||
iti | asminnahani aśvasya vāladhimālabhya bahiṣpavamānaṃ sarpeyuḥ |
athavā adhvaryumeva pucchamālabhate | tamitare yathākāmamanvālabheran | sarpatsu udgātāraṃ yajamāno brūyāt | udgātarupa tvā vṛṇe śatena
gavāṃ niṣkeṇa cāśvo mamodgāsyatīti| asyārthaḥ | udgātastvāṃ
vihāyāśvamudgātāraṃ vṛṇe śatena gavāṃ niṣkeṇa cālaṃkāreṇa | aśvo mamodgāsyatīti | aśvamāstāvamākramayya vaḍavāṃ darśayeyuḥ| tāṃ yadāśvo'bhikrandet | atha yajamāna udgātāraṃ brūyāt | udgātarupa tvā vṛṇe śatena caiva niṣkeṇa ca | tvameva mamodgāsyasīti | udgātre gavāṃ śataṃ niṣkaṃ ca dadyāt | udañcamaśvamutkramayya āstāve yo ma ātmetyupaveśānantaramudgātā aśvavratena stuyāt abhi vājī viśvarūpa iti tṛcasthena | uttarayorapi stotrīyayorhauhoītyādi stotraṃ pratipadamāharet | aśva striṣṭubaśva ityṛṣyādayaḥ| bhaktivibhāgena prayojanam | ekakartṛkatvāt ||
atha bahiṣpavamānena stuvīran | upāsmai gāyatā nara ityādīṣe pavasva dhārayetyantaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | hiraṇyakaśipunyāsīna udgātā bahiṣpavamānena stutvā sa eva taddharedajinam | ekakartṛtvāt ||
athābhimedhanam | yā patnīnāṃ priyatamā yajamānasyetyādinā vihitam | svāhākṛtibhiścarita ityādinā brahmodyavadanaṃ ca| tato dhiṣṇyopasthānādi kāryam | kayā te agne aṅgiraḥ purūruṇā ciddhyasti tamindrā vājayāmasi iyaṃ vāmasya manmana ityājyāni | asya pratneti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| abhi somāsa āyava iti gautamamantarikṣama dairghaśravasamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | saṃtani tisṛṣu | paurumadgagāyatrapārśvamaidhātithamiti sāmatṛcaḥ| dvihiṃkāraṃ tisṛṣu|
ayaṃ soma itīnidhanamājyadohamantyam | mahānāmnyaśca | vāmadevyaṃ ca pārthuraśmaṃ ca śrāyantīyasyarkṣu saṃkṛtīti pṛṣṭhāni | mahānāmnīnāṃ śakvarī chandaḥ | pārthuraśmasya paṅktiḥ | yaste mada iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ | abhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaḥ sāma śauktaṃ ca| paryūṣu iti āndhīgavam | indrāya soma pātave iti yajñāyajñīyam | pavitraṃ te ityariṣṭamantyam | svāsu viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇam udvaṃśīyamityukthāni | sarvamekaviṃśam || 3||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ || 20 ||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
tṛtīyamaharāha -- 
pavasva vāco agriya pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ pavamānasya ta kave ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
gāyatrāduttare catvāra ekarccāḥ | uttamaṃ ca bārhatam | anuṣṭubhi traya ekarccāḥ | uttamaṃ tṛce || 2 ||
samānamitaraṃ uttamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya || 3 ||
iti | hotā devo amartyo mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe mahām̐ indrāya ojasā indre agnā namo bṛhad ityājyāni ||
uccā te iti gāyatraṃ tisṛṣu | goṣūktaṃ jarābodhīyamṛṣabhaḥ pavamāna iti sāmatṛcaḥ | pratnaṃ pīyūṣam ityutsedha ekasyām | parīto ṣiñcateti ābhīśavaṃ tisṛṣu | ātharvaṇaṃ tisṛṣu | pṛśni ekasyām | tisro vāca iti triṇidhanamājyadohamantyam | bṛhacca satrāsāhīyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | sa sunve yo vasūnām prāṇā śiśumahīnām iti dīrghaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā iti niṣedhaḥ śyāvāśvaṃ krauñcamiti sāmatṛcaḥ| yajñāyajñīyaṃ tisṛṣu| pari pra dhanveti vājadāvaryaḥ ayā ruceti nityavatsāḥ | dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rkontyaḥ | vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni| gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | āhinīkī rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ | sarvastomo'tirātravat stomāḥ | trivṛdbahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśānyājyāni | saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ| ekaviṃśāni pṛṣṭhāni | triṇava ārbhavaḥ | trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ| pratyavarohīṇyukthāni | triṇavaṃ prathamam | dve ekaviṃśe saṣoḍaśike| pañcadaśī rātriḥ| trivṛtsaṃdhiriti | ahāvo hāvā ityavabhṛthasāma | aśvamedhavikalpāḥ sūtrata evāvadhāryāḥ ||3||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ || 21 ||
aśvatrirātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
baidatrirātraḥ
trayastrivṛto'tirātrāḥ sarve ṣoḍaśimantaḥ ityanuvākena baidatrirātra uktaḥ| ādyantayorahnoścātvāle madhyamasya bahiṣpavamānastavanam | athavā prathamasya cātvāle parayoḥ sadasyeva | taduktam baidatrirātre satraṃ nyāyaṃ gautamo'hīnaṃ nyāyaṃ dhānaṃjayya iti ||
prathamamahaḥ
tatra prathamasyāhnaḥ kalpaḥ -- 
pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatramekasyām | āśvamekasyām | jarābodhīyamekasyām | pra soma devavītaye iti gauṅgavamekasyām | pajramekasyām | yaudhājayamekasyām | auśanam aupagavam āṣṭādaṃṣṭram | pratyasmai pipīṣata iti nānadaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma ||1||
rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ | samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya || 2 ||
iti | upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavamānasya te vayam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam|
agna ā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatram āśvaṃ jarābodhīyamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | pra soma devavītaye iti gauṅgavaṃ pajraṃ yaudhājayamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇam | śagdhyūṣu śacīpata ityabhīvartaḥ | svāsu kāleyamiti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato haririti saphākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti audalaṃ cartanidhanaṃ cājyadoham | abhi priyāṇi iti vaikhānasamantyaṃ tisṛṣu | itarāṇyādyāsu yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | preṣṭhaṃ va ityauśanam | tvaṃ na indretyaupagavam | indraṃ viśvetyāṣṭādaṃṣṭrayoruttaramityukthāni | uttaramabhiplavavaidatrirātrayorityupagranthavacanāt uttaramityuktam | pratyasmai pipīṣata iti nānadaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma| rātrirāhīnikī| sarvaṃ trivṛt ||2||
iti prathamamahaḥ || 22 ||
dvitīyamahaḥ
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ
upoṣu jātamapturam davidyutatyā rucā iṣe pavasva dhārayā ||
| iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
paryūṣu iti śyāvāśvam | indrāya somapātava iti āndhīgavam | pavitraṃ ta iti yajñāyajñīyamantyam | udvaṃśīyasya loke tairaścyam | udvaṃśīyaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ || 1||
samānamitaraṃ madhyamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya || 2 ||
iti | upoṣu jātamapturaṃ davidyutatyā rucā iṣe pavastra dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam | kayā te agne aṅgiraḥ purūruṇā ciddhayasti tamindraṃ vājayāmasi iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni | asya pratneti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| abhi somāsa āyavaḥ iti gautamaṃ cāntarikṣa ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ ca | ayaṃ soma itīnidhanamājyadohamantyam | antyavarjamekarcāḥ| tatra gautamāntarikṣadairghaśravasāni sāmatṛcaḥ | dvihiṃkāramadhyāsyāyām | adhyāsyāyāṃ cetyadhikṛtya | caturo nānāstotrīyāsviti vacanāt | svāsu vāmadevyam | moṣu tvā vāghataścana iti gāyatrapārśvam | astāvi manma pūrvam iti saṃtani | śrāyantīyasyarkṣu saṃkṛtīti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ | abhi dyumna sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaḥsāma | śauktaṃ ca| paryūṣviti āndhīgavam | indrāya soma pātave iti yajñāyajñīyam | pavitraṃ ta iti ariṣṭamantyam | antyavarjamekarcāḥ| svāsu viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma| pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | udvaṃśīyaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | rātrirāhīnikī saṃdhiḥ | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 2||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ || 23 ||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
uddharati jarābodhīyaṃ satobṛhatī dvipadāticchandasaśca ||2||
pratilomaṃ bārhatāni |3||
ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti dīrghatamaso'rkaḥ | krauñcaṃ ca | dhartā divaḥ iti yajñāyajñīyam antyam ||4|
samānamitaramuttamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya ||5||
iti | pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam iti bahiṣpavamānam | hotā devo amartyomitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe mahā indro ya ojasā indre agnā namo bṛhadityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca goṣūktaṃ ca ṛṣabhaḥ pavamānaḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti pṛśni 
ātharvaṇamābhīśavam | tisro vāca iti triṇidhanamājyadohamantyam | antyavyatiriktā ekarcāḥ | tatra gāyatrī bṛhatyau sāmatṛcau | bṛhacca satrāsāhīyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatrahāviṣmate | sa sunve yo vasūnām prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti dīrghaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti dīrghatamaso'rkaḥ krauñcaṃ ca| dhartā divaḥ iti yajñāyajñīyamantyam | antyameva tṛce | vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni | gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ| sarvaṃ trivṛt | prathamaṃ rātriṣāma | ṣoḍaśaṃ vā| tat baidottame'pyācāryā iti vacanāt || 5 ||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ|| 24||
baidatrirātraḥ samāptaḥ||
chandomapavamānatrirātraḥ
prathamamahaḥ
caturviṃśāḥ pavamānāḥ ityanuvākena chandomapavamāna uktaḥ| tasya prathamasyāhnaḥ kalpaḥ -- 
sāṃvatsarikasya viṣuvato bahiṣpavamānam | ubhau paryāsau | aśvāduttare | somasāma ca rohitakūlīyaṃ ca | pavasvendramaccheti saphākṣāre ||1||
samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnāśvatrirātrasya || 2 ||
iti| sāṃvatsarikasyeti viṣuvadatirātravyāvṛttyartham | upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ babhrave nu svatavase pra svānāso rathā iva pavamānasya te vayam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agna ā yāhi vītaya ityājyāni | pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ cāśvaṃ ca somasāma ca rohitakūlīyaṃ ca | pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ ca gauṅgavaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇam śagdhyūṣvityabhīvartaḥ | svāsu kāleyamiti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramaccheti saphākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasaḥ iti audalaṃ ca gautamaṃ cartanidhanamājyadoham | abhi priyāṇīti vaikhānasamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | caturviṃśāḥ pavamānāḥ| trivṛntyājyāni |
pañcadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni | saptadaśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | nātra stomānāṃ catuṣparyāyatvam || 2||
iti prathamamahaḥ ||25 ||
dvitīyamahaḥ
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ -- 
vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā ete somā abhi priyam somaḥ punāno arṣati utte śuṣmāsa īrate iti purastāt paryāsasyāharati | āmahīyavāduttarāṇyāśu bhārgavaprabhṛtīni ṣaṭ | dvihiṃkārāduttarāṇyacchidraṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca pauruhanmanam | agneścārkāduttarāṇi surūpaprabhṛtāni catvāri | yajñāyajñīyāt pūrvāṇyāsitaṃ ca kautsaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca rayiṣṭham || 1||
samānamitaraṃ madhyamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya || 2 ||
iti | upoṣu jātamapturam davidyutatyā rucā pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ rājā medhābhirīyate vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣāhyasi bhānunā ete somā abhi priyam somaḥ punāno arṣati utte śuṣmāsa īrate iṣe pavasva dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam | kayā te agne aṅgiraḥ purūruṇā ciddhayasti tamindraṃ vājayāmasi iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni | asya pratneti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cāśubhārgavaṃ ca mārgīyavaṃ ca saumitraṃ caiṭataṃ ca sākamaśvaṃ ca vilambasauparṇaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyava iti gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca | dvihiṃkāraṃ cācchindraṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca | ayaṃ soma iti īnidhanamājyadohamantyam | gautamamādyāyām | pauruhanmanamadhyāsyāyām | svāsu vāmadevyam | moṣu tvā vāghataścaneti gāyatrapārśvam | astāvi manma pūrvyam iti santani | śrāyantīyasyarkṣu saṃkṛtīti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ surūpaṃ ca bhāsaṃ ca kākṣīvataṃ ca gāyatrīsāmāsitaṃ ca| abhidyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ sakhāya ā niṣīdata iti vācaḥsāma śauktaṃ caikarccayoḥ | paryūṣvityāndhīgavam | indrāya soma pātave iti āsitaṃ ca kautsaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca rayiṣṭhaṃ ca yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca| pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam | svāsu viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni | catvāriṃśāḥ pavamānāḥ | pañcadaśānyājyāni | saptadaśāni pṛṣṭhāni | ekaviṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ sokthaḥ iti stomāḥ ||2||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ ||26 ||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ kalpaḥ -- 
aṣṭarcāduttarānnavamādahnaścaturaḥ ṣaḍṛcānupaharati | ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam iti paryāsaḥ | ṛṣabhāt pavamānāduttarāṇi surūpaṃ cādārasṛcceḍānāṃ ca saṃkṣāraḥ | svāraṃ ca saindhukṣitam | pṛśnina uttarāṇyarkapuṣpam kaulmalabarhiṣam devasthānam | hāviṣmatāduttarāṇi śāmmadaprabhṛtīni catvāri | yajñāyajñīyāduttaraṃ yadvāhiṣṭhīyam ||1||
samānamitaramuttamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya ||2||
iti | pavasva vāco agriyaḥ eṣa devo amartyaḥ eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā eṣa u sya vṛṣā rathaḥ eṣa vājī hito nṛbhiḥ eṣa kavirabhiṣṭutaḥ sa sutaḥ pītaye vṛṣā ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
hotā devo amartyo mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe mahām̐ indro ya ojasā indre agnā namo bṛhadityājyāni ||
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣūktaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca ṛṣabhaśca pavamānaḥ surūpaṃ cādārasṛcceḍānāṃ saṃkṣāraṃ ca saindhukṣitam | pratnaṃ pīyūṣamityutsedhaḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti ābhīśavaṃ ū0 cātharvaṇaṃ ca pṛśni cārkapuṣpaṃ ca kaulmalabarhiṣaṃ ca devasthānaṃ ca | tisro vāca iti triṇidhanamājyadohamantyam ||
bṛhacca satrāsāhīyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca śāmmadaṃ ca
dāvasunidhanaṃ ca pratīcīneḍaṃ ca kāśītaṃ hāviṣkṛtaṃ ca |
sa sunve yo vasūnām prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti dīrghaśrudhye| ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti niṣedhaśca śyāvāśvaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca | pari pra dhanveti vājadāvaryaḥ | ayā ruceti nityavatsāḥ | dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rkontyaḥ ||
vāravantīyamagniṣṭomasāma | jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān | aṣṭācatvāriṃśāḥ pavamānāḥ | ekaviṃśānyājyāni | triṇavāni pṛṣṭhāni | trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | ekaviṃśānyukthāni saṣoḍaśikāni | pañcadaśī rātriḥ trivṛtsaṃdhiḥ || 2||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ || 27 ||
chandomapavamānatrirātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
antarvasutrirātraḥ
prathamottame ahanī
trivṛtprātaḥsavanam ityanuvākenāntarvasutrirātra uktaḥ| tatra kalpaḥ -- 
ye gargatrirātrasya prathamottame ahanī te antarvasoḥ || 1 ||
iti | uttame'hani prathamaṃ rātriṣāma | pañcadaśamevetyahardvayamuktam ||1||
iti prathamottame ahanī || 28||
dvitīyamahaḥ
madhyamamaharāha -- 
upoṣu jātamapturaṃ davidyutatyā rucā pavasvendo vṛṣā suto rājāmedhābhirīyate ete asṛgramindavo vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā iṣe pavasva dhārayā || 1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
atha yadeva chandomapavamānasya madhyamamahastadetat | tasya tṛcaklṛpta ārbhavaḥ ||2||
iti | ārbhave vācaḥsāma śauktaṃ ca| tatraikarcau | iha tṛcāvityarthaḥ| upoṣu jātamapturamityādi iṣe pavasva dhārayetyantaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | kayā te agne aṅgiraḥ purūruṇā ciddhayasti tamindraṃ vājayāmasi iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni | asya pratnāmanudyutamiti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cāśubhārgavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ ca saumitraṃ caiṭataṃ ca sākamaśvaṃ ca vilambasauparṇaṃ ca| abhi somāsa āyava iti gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca dairdhaśravasaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ cācchidraṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca | ayaṃ soma iti īnidhanamājyadohamantyam | gautamamādyāyām | pauruhanmanamadhyāsyāyām | svāsu vāmadevyam | moṣu tveti gāyatrapārśvam | astāvi manma pūrvyam iti saṃtani | śrayantīyasyarkṣu saṃkṛtīti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ surūpaṃ ca bhāsaṃ ca kākṣīvataṃ ca gāyatrī sāmāsitaṃ ca | abhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaḥsāma śauktaṃ ca | paryūṣvi tyāndhīgavam | indrāya soma pātave ityāsitaṃ ca kautsaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca rayiṣṭhaṃ ca yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca| pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam ||
svāsu viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni | caturviṃśaṃ prātaḥsavanam| catuścatvāriṃśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam| aṣṭācatvāriṃśaṃ tṛtīyaṃ savanam | sokthamiti stomāḥ || 2||
iti madhyamamahaḥ || 26 ||
uktaṃ tṛtīyamahaḥ |
antarvasutrirātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
parākatrirātraḥ
prathamamahaḥ
trivṛtprātaḥsavanam ityanuvākena parākatrirātra uktaḥ| tasya prathamasyāhnaḥ klṛptimāha
yāntarvasoḥ prathamā sā parākasya ||1||
iti ||1||
iti prathamamahaḥ || 30||
dvitīyamahaḥ
atha dvitīyasyāhnaḥ -- 
vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā iti purastātparyāsasyāharati | āmahīyavāduttare āśu ca bhārgavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ cāgneścārkāduttare surūpabhāse | yajñāyajñīyātpūrve āsitakautse || 1||
samānamitaraṃ madhyamenāhnā gargatrirātrasya ||2||
iti | upoṣu jātamapturam davidyutatyā rucā pavasvendo vṛṣā suto rājāmedhābhirīyate vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā iṣe pavasva dhāraye ti bahiṣpavamānam ||
kayā te agne purūruṇā ciddhayasti tamindraṃ vājayāmasi iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni ||
asya pratnāmanudyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cāśubhārgavaṃ ca mārgīyavaṃ ca| abhi somāsa āyava iti gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ ca | ayaṃ soma iti īnidhanamājyadohamantyam ||
svāsu vāmadevyam | moṣu vāghataścaneti gāyatrapārśvam | astāvi manma pūrvyam iti santani | śrāyantīyasyarkṣu saṃkṛtīti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇyaḥ iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ surūpaṃ ca| abhi dyumnaṃ sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaścasāma śauktaṃ ca | paryūṣvityāndhīgavam | indrāya soma pātave iti āsitaṃ ca kautsaṃ ca yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam ||2||
viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma | svāsu pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni ||2||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ || 31||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ -- 
vyuṣṭeruttarasyāhno bahiṣpavamānam | atha yadeva chandomapavamānasyottamamahastadetat | tasya śaśakarṇaklṛptā bṛhatī || 1 ||
iti | sādhyāsyāyāṃ cedeko'dhyāsyāyāmevāntato dvau tasyāṃ caivāditaśca sā śaśakarṇaklṛpteti śaśakarṇaklṛptirvyākhyātā ||
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ eṣa devo amartyaḥ eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
anyatsarvaṃ chandomapavamānasyottamenāhnā samam | ābhīśavaṃ prathamāyām | śrīṇanta iti adhyāsyāyāṃ devasthānamiti viśeṣaḥ ||1||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ || 32||
parākatrirātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
iti śrīvāmanāryasutavaradarājaviracitāyāṃ ārṣeyakalpavyākhyāyāmahīneṣu prathamo'dhyāya āditaḥ ṣaṣṭhaḥ ||6||
<7>
upāsmai gāyatā naro babhrave nu svatavase pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇaya āśurarṣa bṛhanmate pavamānasya te kave 'gna ā yāhi vītaya ityājyāni pra somāso vipaścita iti gayatraṃ cāśvaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ cāśu bhārgavaṃ pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ gauṅgavaṃ yaudhājayamauśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatra sam̐hite pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudalaṃ gautamamākūpāramabhi priyāṇīti dvīḍaḥ padastobho'ntyo yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma 71
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunā somaḥ punāno arṣati pavamānasya te vayamagniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyāni vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ cājigaṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇaṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayetyāyasye antarā dairghaśravasaṃ vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ca yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāṃ cārkastvam̐ hyaṅga daivya pavasva devavītaya iti śaṅkusujñāne ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga ityāsitaṃ dve krauñce vṛṣā matīṇāṃ pavata iti caturiḍaḥ padastobho'ntyo yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāmau śanam̐ saubharamāṣṭādam̐ṣṭram 72
davidyutatyā rucai te asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate 'sṛkṣata pra vājina ete somā abhi priyamiṣe pavasva dhārayāgnināgniḥ samidhyata ityājyānyuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhamaiḍam̐ sauparṇamabhi somāsa āyava iti gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ ca tisro vāca ityaṅgirasām̐ saṃkrośo'ntyo 'bhi tvā vṛṣabhā suta iti rathaṃtaraṃ kastamindra tvāvasaviti vāmadevyam̐ śrāyantīyaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścarka ā sotā pari ṣiñcata sakhāya ā ni ṣīdateti vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ ca sutāso madhumattamā iti tvāṣṭrīsāmanī svāre antarāndhīgavaṃ pavitraṃ ta iti ṣaḍiḍaḥ padastobho'ntyo yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāmā yuṣa ukthāni 73
pavamāno ajījanadeṣa devo amartya eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam̐ hotā devo amartyo yadadya sūra udite mahām̐ indro ya ojaseyaṃ vāmasya manmano 'sya pratnāmanu dyutamiti gāyatramāmahīyavam̐ satrāsāhīyaṃ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti pṛśni cātharvaṇaṃ cābhīśavaṃ cotsedhaścāyam̐ soma itīhavadvāsiṣṭhamantyaṃ yajjāyathā apūrvyeti bṛhadedu madhormadiṃtaramiti vāmadevyaṃ traiśokaṃ ca samantaṃ ca pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣūktaṃ ca sa sunve yo vasūnāṃ prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti dīrgha vāravantīye purojitī vo andhasa iti niṣedhaśca śyāvāśvaṃ ca pari pra dhanveti dīrghatamaso'rko dhartā diva ityaṣṭeḍaḥ padastobho'ntyo yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma sākamaśvaṃ traikakubhaṃ nārmedhaṃ gaurīvitam̐ ṣoḍaśisāma rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ 74
upo ṣu jātamapturamityanurūpo naudhasaṃ brahmasāma sujñānasya loke pauskalaṃ pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudala gautame samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnābhiplavikena 751
ajigakrauñce uddharati sve uṣṇikkakubhau svāsu yāmam̐ samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā caturvīrasyā 752
asṛkṣata pra vājina ityuddharatyagnerarkāduttaramaiḍam̐ saindhukṣitamabhyāsavatastvāṣṭrīsāmno loke triṇidhanaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāma svāsvariṣṭam̐ samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnā caturvīrasya 753
vāravantīyasya loke śrudhyaṃ vipariharatyānuṣṭubhe dvipadāsu vāravantīyaṃ dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ samānamitaramuttamenāhnā caturvīrasya 754
yā jāmadagnyasya prathamā sā sam̐sarpasya 761
navarcasauparṇe uddharati parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti bārhadukthaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ cā yāsye samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā jāmadagnyasya 762
dvādaśāhikasya tṛtīyasyāhna ājyabahiṣpavamānaṃ gāyatrī ca samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnā jāmadagnyasya 763
uddharati paryāsaṃ vrātyā gāyatrī punānaḥ soma dhārayeti bṛhatī gauṣūktāduttaraṃ vāravantīyaṃ purojitī vo andhasa iti niṣedhaḥ śyāvāśvaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāmā harati dvipadāticchandasaḥ samānamitaramuttamenāhnā jāmadagnyasya 764
pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatramekasyāmāśvamekasyāṃ jarābodhīyamekasyāṃ pra soma devavītaya iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnā sam̐sarpasya 771
punānaḥ soma dhārayetyāyasye samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā sam̐sarpasya 772
saindhukṣitāndhīgave uddharati samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnā sam̐sarpasya 773
jarābodhīyavāravantīye uddharati dvipadāticchandasaśca parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti pṛśni ābhīśavamātharvaṇaṃ jyotiṣṭomyanuṣṭup samānamitaramuttamenāhnā sam̐sarpasya 774
vaiśvāmitro dvyaho jarābodhīyasya loke somasāma svāśirāmarkasya maukṣam̐ saubharasyaupagavaṃ 781
dvādaśāhikaṃ tṛtīyamaharvairūpasya loke rathaṃtaraṃ pāṣṭhauhasyāgnerarko gaurīvitasya tvāṣṭrīsāma tasyaitasya tryahasyābhyāsaṅgaḥ svadiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatra sām̐hite tṛcayorgautamaṃ tṛca āsitaṃ tṛce vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ tṛcayoḥ 782
pavamāno ajījanatpunāno akramīdabhi pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇaya āśurarṣa bṛhanmate hinvanti sūramusrayo'gnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanaditi hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇi pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gayatramadārasṛtsatrāsāhīyaṃ tavāham̐ soma rāraṇetyāṣṭādam̐ṣṭramābhīśavam̐ svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasamu hu vā asyeti vāsiṣṭhamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ca pari priyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatram̐ svāśirāmarko bṛhadbhāradvājaṃ tvam̐ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punāna ūrmiṇeti bṛhatka krośe purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāmā tharvaṇaṃ pro ayāsīditi lauśamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma saindhukṣitam̐ saubharamudvam̐śīyam 79
pavamānasya viśvavidasṛkṣata pra vājina eṣa devo amartya eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyāte dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam̐ hotā devo amartyaḥ purūruṇā ciddhyastyuttiṣṭhannojasā saha yajñasya hi stha ṛtvijāsya pratnāmanu dyutamiti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca rṣabhaśca pavamānaḥ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti vāmraṃ ca mānavaṃ cā nūpaṃ co tsedhaścā yam̐ soma itī havadvāsiṣṭhamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca vaiyaśvaṃ ca pari svāno giristhā iti gāyatraṃ gauṣūktaṃ vāravantīyam̐ sa sunve yo vasūnāṃ prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti dīrgha śrudhye somāḥ pavanta indava iti śyāvāśvā ndhigave niṣedhaḥ pari pra dhanveti vājadāvaryo 'yā ruceti nityavatsā dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyo yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma cāturvīro'tiratraḥ ṣoḍaśimān 710
eṣa eva caturaho yo devānāṃ pañcarātrasyānabhyāsaktaḥ punarjarābodhīyam̐ svaṃ lokameti caturthādahna uttaram̐ ṣaḍṛcam̐ satrāsāhīyamābhīśavaṃ bṛhadbhāradvājamātharvaṇaṃ tānyuddharati 7111
pavamānasya viśvavid yatsoma citramukthyamasṛkṣata pravājina utte śuṣmāsa irata ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvamindragni yuvāmima ityaindrāgnamṛṣabhajarābodhīye uddharatyutsedhasya loke'gnestriṇidhanamasāvyam̐śurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣuktaṃ ca pavasva vājasātaya iti śyāvāśvā ndhīgave uddharati dvipadāticchandasaḥ samānamitaramuttamenāhnā devānāṃ pañcarātrasya 7112
jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo gaurukthyo mahāvrataṃ gaurukthya āyuratirātra ekāhakḷptāḥ 7113
pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā samūḍhastveva tasyo pāsmai gāyatā nara upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ pavamānasya te kava iti prathamasyāhno bahiṣpavamānamaudalamāsitaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāmā vikḷptaṃ caturthamahastvāṣṭrīsāma krauñcamete svarāḥ pṛṣṭhyasya gaurīvitaloka udvam̐śaputrādrātrimupayantī ti yadi vyūḍhaḥ 7121
atha yadi samūḍha etadeva prathamasyāhno bahiṣpavamānamete svarā dvitīyasyāhno vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ pañcamasyāhnaḥ pārthasya loka ākūpāraṃ yannavame'hani sāṃvatsarikaḥ samūḍha uttarasya tryahasyāvikḷptā bṛhatyaḥ sarvasya ṣaḍahasyai 7122
eṣa eva tryaho ya ṛtūnāṃ yathā vyūḍhe tṛtīyasyāhno vairūpasya loke rathaṃtaram̐ sutāso madhumattamā iti trīṇi tvāṣṭrīsūmānyaiḍaṃ madhye svāre abhito bṛhattisṛṣu jyotirgaurāyuratirātra ekāhakḷptāḥ 7123
eṣa eva pañcāho ya ṛtūnām̐ samūḍho'bhyāsakto bṛhadrathaṃtarapṛṣṭhaḥ prathamasyāhnaḥ svādiṣṭhārbhavīyā dvitīyasyāhno vāsiṣṭhamantyam̐ svasthāne pārthaṃ caturthasyāhna aurṇāyavasyopariṣṭādbṛhadbhāradvājamāndhīgavasya śuddhāśuddhīyamuddharati ṣoḍaśinaṃ pañcamasyāhnaḥ saṃtanina uparistādaiḍaṃ ca saindhukṣitaṃ gausūktaṃ ca 7124
viśvajidatirātraḥ 7125
eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnām̐ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā samūḍhastvevaikāhikaṃ vrataṃ tasya jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān 7131
eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnām̐ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā samūḍhastveva saptadaśaṃ vrataṃ tasyopāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavasva vāco agriyo davidyutatyā rucotte śuṣmāsa īrate pavamānasya te kava iti bahiṣpavamānamuttare gāyatryā uddharati pūrve anuṣṭubhaḥ samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa vratena tasya stomānvayīnyukthānyekavim̐śaḥ ṣoḍaśi 7132
eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnām̐ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā samūḍhastveva chandomapavamānaṃ vrataṃ tasya pavamānasya te vayamiti caturṛcasya loke tṛcaṃ vipariharati satrāsāhīyajarābodhīye āśu ca bhārgavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ ca saumitraṃ caiṭataṃ ca yaudhājayāduttarāṇi bārhadukthaṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca svāśirāmarkāduttarāṇi śāṃmadaprabhṛtīni catvāryāndhīgavāduttarānyākūpāraṃ ca sādhraṃ ca svāsu dharma samānamitaraṃ prathamena vratena saptarātrikeṇa 7133
eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnām̐ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā samūḍhastveva tasyābhyāsaktaḥ pañcāho yathā pṛṣṭhyāvalambe caturthasyāhna ekavim̐śaḥ ṣoḍaśī pañcamasyāhnaḥ pārthasya loka ākūpāraṃ vātreyaṃ ve ti yadi samūḍho 'tha yadi vyūḍhaścaturthasyāhnaḥ śuddhāśuddhīyasya loka ākūpāraṃ pañcamasyāhnaḥ pārthasyātreyaṃ pārthaṃ vaiva chandomavato vratasya bahiṣpavamānam̐ saptadaśaṃ vratam̐ samānamitaraṃ 7134
jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo gaurukthya āyurukthyo'bhijidagniṣṭomo viśvajidagniṣṭomaḥ sarvajidagniṣṭomaḥ sarvastomo'tirātra ekāhakḷptāḥ 7135
yā devānāṃ pañcarātrasya prathamā sā janakasaptarātrasyānabhyāsaktā 7141
stotrīyānurūpau paryāso vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatram̐ hāviṣmatam ājigaṃ ta ekarcāḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu maukṣasya loke svāśirāmarka aupagavasya saubharam̐ samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā devānāṃ pañcarātrasya 7142
stotrīyānurūpau paryāsa uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ kṣullakavaiṣṭambham̐ svāram̐ sauparṇaṃ ta ekarcā abhi somāsa āyava ityantarikṣaṃ tisṛṣu mahāvaiṣṭambhasya loke śrāyantīyam̐ samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnā devānāṃ pañcarātrasya 7143

iti dvitīyamahaḥ || 6 ||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
athottarasyāhnaścaturviṃśa bahiṣpavamānam | pañcadaśāni trīṇyājyāni | saptadaśamacchāvākasya | ekaviṃśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | saptadaśe dve pṛṣṭhe | ekaviṃśe dve | ekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyaṃ savanaṃ soktham iti ||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ kalpaḥ
asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ ityuddharati | agnerarkāduttaram aiḍaṃ saindhukṣitam | abhyāsavatastvāṣṭrīsāmno loke | triṇidhanaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāma | svāsvariṣṭam || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnā caturvīrasya || 2 ||
iti | davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate ete somā abhi priyam iṣe pavasva dhārayenati bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnināmitraṃ huveindreṇa tāhuveityājyāni || uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ kṣullakavaiṣṭambham aiḍaṃ sauparṇam | abhi somāsa iti gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ ca | tisro vāca iti aṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ ||
abhi tvā vṛṣabhā suta iti rathantaraṃ | kastamindra tvā vasava iti vāmadevyaṃ śrāyantīyaṃ ca rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ aiḍaṃ saindhukṣitam | ā sotā pari ṣiñcatā sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaḥsāma śauktaṃ ca | sutāso madhumattama iti svārantriṇidhane tvāṣṭrīsāmanī antarāndhīgavam | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam | atra svāraṃ tvāṣṭrīsāma dvayoḥ pūrvam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni ||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ ekaviṃśaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | trīṇi cājyāni | pañcadaśamacchāvākasya | caturviśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | ekaviṃśāni pṛṣṭhāni | triṇavaṃ tṛtīyasavanam | dve cokthe | ekaviṃśamacchāvākasyeti stomavidhiḥ || 2 ||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ || 7 ||
caturthamahaḥ
caturthasyāhnaḥ kalpaḥ
vāravantīyasya loke śrudhyam vipariharatyānuṣṭubhe | dvipadāsu vāravantīyam dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ || 1 ||
samānamitaramuttamenāhnā caturvīrasya || 2 ||
iti | pavamāno ajījanat eṣa devo amartya eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā ā te dakṣaṃ mayo bhuvam iti bahiṣpavamānam || hotā devo amartyo yadadya sūra udite mahām̐ indro ya ojasā iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni ||
asya pratnāmanudyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca satrāsāhīyam | parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti pṛśni cātharvaṇaṃ cābhīśavaṃ cotsedhaśca | ayaṃ soma iti ihavadvāsiṣṭhamantyam ||
yajjāyathā apūrvyeti bṛhat | edu madhormadintaramiti vāmadevyaṃ traiśokaṃ samantaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣūktaṃ ca | sa sunve yo vasūnāṃ prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti dīrghavāravantīye | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvaṃ ca niṣedhaśca | pari pra dhanveti dīrghatamaso'rkaḥ | dhartā divaḥ iti aṣṭeḍapadastobho'ntyaḥ ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
sākamaśvaṃ traikakubhaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni ||
gaurīvitaṃṣoḍaśisāma | rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ ||
caturthasyāhnaḥ caturviṃśā pavamānāḥ | pañcadaśaṃ hoturājyam | saptadaśāni trīṇi | ekaviṃśāni pṛṣṭhāni | trayastriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ | ekaviṃśānyukthāni ṣoḍaśikāni | pañcadaśī rātriḥ | trivṛtsaṃdhiḥ | iti stomavidhiḥ ||
iti caturthamahaḥ || 8 ||
iti jāmadagnyaścatūrātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
vāsiṣṭhaścatūrātraḥsaṃsarpo vā
trivṛtprātaḥsavanam ityanuvākena vāsiṣṭhaścatūrātra uktaḥ | sa eva saṃsarpa ityucyate ||
prathamamahaḥ
tasya prathamasyāhnaḥ klṛptimāha
yā jāmadagnyasya prathamā sā saṃsarpasya || 1 ||
iti | trivṛtprātaḥsavanam | pañcadaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | saptadaśaṃ tṛtīyaṃ savanam | iti stomaklṛptiḥ | atiriktāstṛcāḥ |
saptadaśatvāt | pariśiṣṭaṃ jāmadagnyasya prathamenāhnā samam || 1 ||
iti prathamamahaḥ || 9 ||
dvitīyamahaḥ
navarcasauparṇe uddharati | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam bārhadukthaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ cāyāsye || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā jāmadagnyasya || 2 ||
iti | pavasva vāco pavasvendo vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi pavamānasya te vayamiti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahemitraṃ vayamindramidgāthinaindre agnetyājyāni ||
vṛṣā pavasva dhārayenati gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti bārhadukthaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ cāyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti
pārthamantyam | śaśakarṇaklṛptā bṛhatī ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramaccheti śaṅkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti āsitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ cottamam | vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavate iti yāmamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
auśanaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | pañcadaśaṃ prātaḥsavanam | saptadaśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | ekaviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ soktham || 2 ||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ || 10 ||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ kalpaḥ
dvādaśāhikasya tṛtīyasyāhṇa ājyabahiṣpavamānam | gāyatrī ca || 1 || samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnā jāmadagnyasya || 2 ||
iti | davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam iṣe pavasva dhāraya iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnināmitraṃ huveindreṇatā huve ityājyāni ||
uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyavaḥ iti gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ ca | tisro vāca udīrata ityaṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ ||
abhi tvā vṛṣabhā suta iti rathantaram | kastamindra tvā vasavā iti vāmadevyaṃ śrāyantīyaṃ rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni || tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ | aiḍaṃ saindhukṣitam | ā sotā pari ṣiñcatasakhāya ā niṣīdata iti vācaḥsāma śauktaṃ ca | sutāso madhumattamā iti svārādyaṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāmāndhīgavaṃ ca triṇidhanaṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāma | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni ||
saptadaśaṃ prātaḥsavanam | ekaviṃśaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | triṇavaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ soktham || 2 ||
caturthamahaḥ
atha caturthasyāhnaḥ
uddharati paryāsam | vrātyā gāyatrī | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti bṛhatī | gauṣūktāduttaraṃ vāravantīyam | purojitī vo andhasa iti niṣedhaśca śyāvāśvaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāmāharati dvipadāticchandasaḥ || 1 ||
samānamitaramuttamenāhnā jāmadagnyasya || 2 ||
iti | pavamāno ajījanat eṣa devo amartyaḥ eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
hotā devo amartyo yadadya sūra udite mahām̐ indro ya ojasā iyaṃ vāmasya manmana ityājyāni ||
asya pratneti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca satrāsāhīyaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhāraye ti pṛśni cātharvaṇaṃ cābhīśavaṃ cotsedhaśca | ayaṃ soma iti ihavadvāsiṣṭhamantyam || yajjāyathā apūrvyeti bṛhat | edu madhormadintaram iti vāmadevyaṃ traiśokaṃ ca samantaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ gauṣūktaṃ vāravantīyam | sa sunveyo vasūnāṃ prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti dīrghaśrudhye | purojitī vo andhasa iti niṣedhaśca śyāvāśvaṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāma yadūrdhveḍam | pari pra dhanve ti vājadāvaryaḥ | ayā ruce ti nityavatsāḥ | dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
sākamaśvaṃ traikakubhaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni ||
gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ | ekaviṃśaṃ prātaḥsavanam | triṇavaṃ mādhyaṃdinaṃ savanam | trayastriṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanam | pratyavarohīṇyukthāni | triṇavaṃ prathamam | dve ekaviṃśe saṣoḍaśike | pañcadaśī rātriḥ | trivṛtsaṃdhiriti | atra sūtramsaṃsarpasya caturthe'hani ityādi || 2 ||
iti caturthamahaḥ || 12 ||
iti vāsiṣṭhaścatūrātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
vaiśvāmitraścatūrātraḥ
trivṛdagniṣṭomaḥ | pañcadaśa ukthaḥ | saptadaśa ukthaḥ | ekaviṃśo'tirātro viśvāmitrasya saṃjayaḥ ityanuvākena
viśvāmitraścatūrātra uktaḥ ||
prathamamahaḥ
tasya prathamamaharāha
pra somāso vipaścita gāyatramekasyām | āśvamekasyām jarābodhīyamekasyām | pra soma devavītaye iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu || 1 || samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnā saṃsarpasya || 2 ||
iti | upāsmai upoṣu jātam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnaā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni || pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatramāśvaṃ jarābodhīyamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | pra soma devavītaya iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanamantyam ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥpavate haryato harir iti saphapauṣkale | pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudalagautame | kāvamantyam | kāvavarjamekarcāḥ ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
sarvaṃ trivṛt || 2 ||
iti prathamamahaḥ || 13 ||
dvitīyamahaḥ
dvitīyamaharāha -- 
punānaḥ soma dhāraya ityāyāsye || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnā saṃsarpasya || 2 ||
iti | pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasipavamānasya te vayamiti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni ||
vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayenati āyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramacche ti śaṅkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti āsitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca | vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyam | śaṅkusujñānāsitānyekarcāni ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
auśanaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam || 2 ||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ | | 14 ||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
tṛtīyamaharāha
saindhukṣitāndhīgave uddharati || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnā saṃsarpasya || 2 ||
iti | davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam eṣa pavasva dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam |
agnināgniḥ samidhyate ityājyāni ||
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyava iti gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ ca | tisro vāca ityaṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ | gautamamādyāyām | dvihiṃkāramadhyāsyāyām ||
abhi tvā vṛṣabhā suta iti rathantaram | kastamindra tvā vasaviti vāmadevyaṃ śrāyantīyaṃ rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ | ā sotā pari ṣiñcata sakhāya o niṣīdateti vācaḥsāma śauktaṃ caikarcayoḥ | sutāso madhumattamā iti svāramādyaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāma triṇidhanaṃ ca | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
pramam̐hiṣṭhīya hārivarṇamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni | sarvaṃ saptadaśam || 2 ||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ || 15 ||
caturthamahaḥ
caturthamaharāha
jarābodhīyavāravantīye uddharati | dvipadāticchandasaśca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti pṛśni ābhīśavam ātharvaṇaṃ jyotiṣṭomyanuṣṭup || 1 ||
samānamitaramuttamenāhnā saṃsarpasya || 2 ||
iti | pavamāno ajījanat eṣa devo amartyaḥ eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyeti bahiṣpavamānam ||
hotā devo amartyo yadadya sūra udite maho indro ya ojasā iyaṃ vāmasya manmana ityājyāni ||
asya pratnāmanudyutamiti gāyatramāmahīyavaṃ satrāsāhīyam | parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti pṛśnyābhīśavamātharvaṇam | ayaṃ soma iti ihavadvāsiṣṭhamantyam | |
yajjāyathā apūrvyehi bṛhat | edu madhormadintaramiti vāmadevyaṃ traiśokaṃ ca samantaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ gauṣūktaṃ ca | sa sunve yo vasūnām prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti dīrghaśrudhye| purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||
sākamaśvaṃ traikakubhaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni ||
gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ | sarvamekaviṃśam | ekastomasya saṃdherāvāpo'bhyāso bā || 2 ||
iti caturthamahaḥ || 16 ||
iti jāhnavaḥ vaiśvāmitro vā catūrātra samāptaḥ ||
iti catūrātrāḥ ||
atha pañcarātrāḥ
devapañcarātraḥ -- abhyāsaṅgyaḥ
atha pañcarātrāstrayaḥ | dve trivṛtī savane ityanuvākena devānāṃ pañcarātra uktaḥ ||
prathamadvitīye ahanī
vaiśvamitro dvyahaḥ | jarābodhīyasya loke somasāma | svāśirāmarkasya maukṣam saubharasyaupagavam || 1 ||
iti prathamadvitīye ahanī || 17 ||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
dvādaśāhikaṃ tṛtīyamaha | vairūpasya loke rathantaram | pāṣṭhauhasyāgnerarka | gaurīvitasya trivṛtsāma || 1 ||
tasyaitasya tryahasyābhyāsaṅgaḥ | svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite tṛcayoḥ | gautamaṃ tṛce āsitaṃ tṛce vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ tṛcayoḥ ||2 ||
tasya trayāṇāmahnāṃ klṛptimāhavaiśvāmitro dvyaha iti khaṇḍena | vaiśvāmitro dvyahaḥ dve ahanī ukte | jarābodhīyasya loke somasāmeti tayoḥ prathamasya viśeṣaḥ | svāśirāmarkasya maukṣaṃ saubharasyaupagavamiti dvitīyasya | dvādaśāhikaṃ tṛtīyamaharuktamvairūpasya loke rathantaraṃ pāṣṭhauhasyāgnerarko gaurīvitasya tvāṣṭrīsāmeti | tasya viśeṣaḥtasyaitasya tryahasyābhyāsaṅga iti | pūrvasyāhnastārtīyasavanīko yaḥ stomaḥ sa uttarasya | ādyayoḥ savanayoryadanuṣajyate so'bhyāsaṅgaḥ | yadyogādayaṃ pañcarātro'bhyāsaṅgya ityucyate | sa ca yadyapi pañcānāmapyahnāmasti tathāpyanabhyāsaktasya tryahasyātideśādviśeṣato vidhīyate | svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite tṛcayoḥ gautamaṃ tṛce iti punaḥ prathamasyāhno viśeṣaḥ | āsitaṃ tṛca iti dvitīyasya | vācaḥsāma śauktaṃ ca tṛcayoriti tṛtīyasya | tatra prathamasya klṛptiḥupāsmai upoṣu pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam |
agnaā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatramāśvaṃ somasāmeti tṛcaḥ| pra soma devavītaye iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣvauśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato haririti saphapauṣkale | pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti audalagautame | kāvamantyam | saphapauṣkalaudalānyekarcāni| yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | dve trivṛtī savane | pañcadaśamekamiti stomavidhiḥ ||
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ -- pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasi pavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam |
