agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyāni| vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ hāviṣmataṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti āyāsye vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramaccheti śaṅkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣetyāsitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca| vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyam | kakubuṣṇihāvekarcau | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | preṣṭhaṃ vaḥ ityauśanam | tvaṃ na indra ityaupagavam | āṣṭādaṃṣṭraṃ pūrvam ityukthāni| ve pañcadaśe savane saptadaśamekam ||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ -- davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavaḥ rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam iṣe pavasva dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam | agnināmitrahuindreṇa tā huve ityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca| abhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgaṃ prathamāyām | gautamaṃ tisṛṣu | āṣkāraṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām | tisro vāca iti aṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ ca rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ | ā sotā pari ṣiñcata sakhāya ā niṣīdata iti vācaḥsāma śauktaṃ ca| sutāso madhumattamā iti svāraṃtriṇidhane tvāṣṭrīsāmanī | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam | svāraṃ tvāṣṭrīsāma yad dvayoḥ pūrvam | ādyaṃ tu tṛtīye svārayośca pūrvamuttaram ityupagranthavacanāt | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | dve saptadaśe savane | ekaviṃśamekam ||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ || 18||
caturthamahaḥ
caturthasyāhnaḥ klṛptimāha -- 
pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ āśurarṣa bṛhanmate hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat iti hoturājyam |
svānyuttarāṇi ||2||
ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇa indro dadhīco asthabhir iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni ||2||
pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatram | adārasṛt satrāsāhīyam | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇetyāṣṭādaṃṣṭram ābhīśavaṃ svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasam | uhuvā asyeti vāsiṣṭham antyam || 3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ca ||4||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pari priyā divaḥ kavir iti gāyatraṃ svāśirāmarko bṛhadbhāradvājam | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punānaḥ ūrmiṇeti bṛhatkakrośe | purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāmātharvaṇam | pro ayāsīd iti lauśam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||6||
saindhukṣitaṃ saubharam udvaṃśīyam || 7 ||
ityukthāni ||
dve ekaviṃśe savane | triṇavamekam ||7||
iti caturthamahaḥ || 19 ||
pañcamamahaḥ
pañcamasyāhnaḥ -- 
pavamānasya viśvavid asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ eṣa devo amartyaḥ eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
hotā devo amartyaḥ purūruṇā ciddhayasti uttiṣṭhannojasā saha yajñasya hi stha ṛtvijā || 2 ||
ityājyāni ||
asya pratnāmanudyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ carṣabhaśca pavamānaḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti vāmraṃ ca mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ cotsedhaśca | ayaṃ soma iti ihavadvāsiṣṭham antyam ||3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ || ]
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca vaiyaśvaṃ ca || 3 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ gauṣūktaṃ vāravantīyaṃ | sa sunve yo vasūnāṃ prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti dīrghaśrudhye | somāḥ pavanta indavaḥ iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave niṣedhaḥ | pari pra dhanveti vājadāvaryo ayā ruceti nityavatsāḥ | dhartā divaḥ iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ || 4 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 5 ||
caturvīro'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān || 6 ||
iti | sākamaśvaṃ traikakubhaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni | gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | āhīnikī rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ| dve triṇave savane| trayastriṃśamekam | pratyavarohīṇyukthāni | triṇavaṃ prathamam | dve ekaviṃśe saṣoḍaśike | pañcadaśī rātriḥ trivṛtsaṃdhiḥ | iti stomāḥ || 6||
iti pañcamamahaḥ ||20||
iti devapañcarātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
pañcaśāradīyaḥ
trivṛdagniṣṭomaḥ pañcadaśa ukthaḥ saptadaśa ukthaḥ pañcadaśa ukthaḥ saptadaśo'tirātraḥ ityanuvākena pañcaśāradīya uktaḥ | tasya sūtram -- pañcaśāradīye paśubandhairyajete
tyādi|
stomaklṛptimāha 
eṣa eva caturaho yo devānāṃ pañcarātrasyānabhyāsaktaḥ || 1||
iti | tatra prathamasyāhno viśeṣamāha -- 
punarjarābodhīyaṃ svaṃ lokameti ||2||
iti | devānāṃ pañcarātre vaiśvāmitrātideśena prāptaṃ yajjarābodhīyamuddhṛtaṃ jarābodhīyasya loke somasāmeti vacanena tadatra punaḥ svameva sthānamāgacchatītyarthaḥ || 2||
caturthasya viśeṣamāha -- 
caturthādahna uttaraṃ ṣaḍṛcaṃ satrāsāhīyamābhīśavaṃ bṛhadbhāradvājamātharvaṇam |
tānyuddharati || 3||
uttaraṃ ṣaḍṛcamiti prayadgāva āśurarṣetyanayoḥ ṣaḍṛcayoruttaramityarthaḥ ||
tatreyaṃ prathamasya klṛptiḥ -- upāsmai upoṣu pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agna āyāhi ityājyāni | pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatramāśvaṃ jarābodhīyamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | pra soma devavītaya iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥpavate haryato harir iti saphapauṣkale | pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudalagautame | kāvamantyam | kāvavyatiriktā ekarcāḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvaṃ trivṛt ||
iti prathamamahaḥ ||
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ -- pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasipavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtammitraṃ vayamindramidgāthinaḥindre agnetyājyāni | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatrahāviṣmate | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti āyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramaccheti śaṅkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti āsitaṃ kautsam caiḍaṃ ca | vṛṣā matīnāmiti yāmamantyam | śaṅkusujñānāsitānyekarcāni ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | auśanamaupagavamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni| sarvaṃ pañcadaśam ||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ ||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ -- davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavorājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratamiṣe pavasva dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam | agnināmitraṃ huveindreṇa tā huve ityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ kṣullakavaiṣṭambham ca| abhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgamādyāyām | gautamāntarikṣe tṛcayoḥ| āṣkāraṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām | tisro vāca iti aṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca vayaṃ gha tveti mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ ca | taraṇirit siṣāsati iti rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ | ā sotā pari ṣiñcata iti sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ ca ekarcayoḥ| sutāso madhumattamā iti tvāṣṭrīsāmanī svārādyatriṇidhane | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | sarvaṃ saptadaśam ||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ||
caturthasyāhnaḥ pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi prayadgāvo na bhūrṇayo hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā indro dadhīco asthabhir iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni | pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛcca | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇetyāṣṭādaṃṣṭraṃ ca svaḥpṛṣṭhaṃ cāṅgirasam | uhuvāha asyeti vāsiṣṭhamantyam| bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ceti pṛṣṭhāni | pari priyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatraṃ svāśirāmarkaḥ | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punānaḥ ūrmiṇeti bṛhatkakrośe purojitī vo nānadaṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāma ca yadūrve paḍam | pro ayāsīditi lauśamantyam | bṛhatkakrośanānadānyekarcāni | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saindhukṣitaṃ saubharamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam ||3||
iti caturthamahaḥ || 21 ||
pañcamamahaḥ
pañcamasyāhnaḥ kalpaḥ -- 
pavamānasya viśvavid yat soma citramukthyam asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ | utte śuṣmāsa īrate ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
indrāgnī yuvāmima ityaindrāgnam | ṛṣabhajarābodhīye uddharati | utsedhasya loke'gnestriṇidhanam asāvyaṃśurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣūktaṃ ca| pavasva vājasātaya iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | uddharati dvipadāticchandasaḥ ||1||
samānamitaram uttamenāhnā devānāṃ pañcarātrasya ||2||
iti | ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvamityantaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | hotā devo amartyaḥ purūruṇā ciddhyasti uttiṣṭhannojasā saha indrāgnī yuvāmime ityājyāni| asya pratneti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti vāmramādyāyām | mānavānūpe tṛcayoḥ | agnestriṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām | ayaṃ soma itīhavadvāsiṣṭhamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca vaiyaśvaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni| asāvyaṃśurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca goṣūktaṃ ca| sa sunve yo vasūnām prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti dīrghaśrudhye | pavasva vājasātaya iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | dhartā divaḥ iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ | kakubiṣṇihāvekarcau | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ traikakubhaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni | gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ| sarvaṃ saptadaśam | ekastomanyāyena saṃdherāvāpo'bhyāso vā ||2||
iti pañcamamahaḥ || 22||
iti pañcaśāradīyaḥ samāptaḥ ||
vratamadhyaḥ pañcarātraḥ
jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo gaurukthyo mahāvrataṃ gaurukthya āyuratirātraḥ ityanuvākena vratavatpañcarātra uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo gaurukthyo mahāvrataṃ gaurukthya āyuratirātraḥ ||1||
ekāhaklṛptāḥ ||2||
iti | klṛpto jyotiṣṭomo gaurvārkajambhasya loka iti khaṇḍenaikāhaprakaraṇoktairjyotiṣṭomādibhirete klṛptāḥ| jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomaḥ prathamamahaḥ| gaurukthyo dvitīyam | mahāvrataṃ tṛtīyam | gaurukthyaścaturtham | āyuratirātraḥ pañcamam | tasyāhinikī rātriḥ | prathamādahno'nyatra sadasi bahiṣpavamānastavanam | prāk dhiṣṇyaviharaṇānniṣkramya tṛṇaprāsanaprabhṛtisamāpanam | dadhibhakṣāntāni catvāryahāni | udavasānīyāntaṃ pañcamam || iti || 2 ||
iti vratamadhyaḥ pañcarātraḥ || 23 ||
ṛtūnāṃ ṣaḍahaḥ
atha ṣaḍahāstrayaḥ| pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ | ṛtavo na pratyatiṣṭhan ityanuvākena ṛtuṣaḍaha uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ 
pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā ||1||
iti | brāhmaṇena daśarātre pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaho vyūḍha uktaḥ | ayaṃ cāvacchinnasamūḍhaḥ syāditi keṣāṃcinmatam | vyūḍha ityanyeṣām || 1 ||
svamatamāha -- 
samūḍhastveva ||2||
iti | asmin pakṣe bahuprabalopapattisadbhāvāditi bhāvaḥ | tathā
ca nidānam -- pṛṣṭhyamavacchinnaṃ vyūhetsamūhediti vyūḍhaḥ syāditi śāṇḍilyāyana ityādi | tatra ca svamata eva vaktavye vikalpo'yaṃ nyāso vyūhapakṣasyāpyācāryamatatvānna heyatvamiti pradarśanārtham ||2||
ata evāha -- 
tasyopāsmai gāyatā nara upoṣu jātamapturam pavamānasya te kave iti prathamasyāhno bahiṣpavamānam || 2||
audalam āsitaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāma | aviklṛptaṃ caturthamahastvāṣṭrīsāma krauñcam ete svarāḥ pṛṣṭhayasya gaurīvitaloke udvam̐śaputrādrātrimupayantīti yadi vyūḍhaḥ || 3|| iti | navāhayogābhāvāt prathame'hani pratnavat bahiṣpavamānasya nivṛttiḥ | ete svarā iti etāni svārāṇi sāmānītyarthaḥ | ete svarā iti vakṣyamāṇena vacanenaiteṣāmeva grahaṇārthamuktamidam | anvahaṃ gaurīvitasya satrapramuktatvāt iha nānāḥ svarā vihitāḥ | caturthaṃ tvaharavikṛtam | tatra hi ṣoḍaśitvena gaurīvitasya vidhānāt asaṃcārāyāpūrvameva svaro vyadhāyi ||
tatra prathamasyāhnaḥ klṛptiḥ -- upāsmai upoṣu pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnaā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | pra somāso vipaścitaḥ iti gāyatramāśvaṃ somasāmeti sāmatṛcaḥ | pra soma devavītaya iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣvauśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni| pṛṣṭhe rathamativaheyurityādinoktāḥ pṛṣṭhadharmāḥ bhavanti | pra somāso madacyuta iti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir iti saphākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti audalagautame | kāvamantyam | antyavarjamekarcāḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvaṃ trivṛt | sarvatra pathyā viṣṭutayaḥ||
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ -- pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasipavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtaṃmitraṃ
vayam indramidgācaturṛcam | indre agnetyājyāni | caturṛce antyāyā uddhāraḥ | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ ca| punānaḥ soma dhārayeti āyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti vāsiṣṭhamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mada iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca |
pavasvendramaccheti śaṅku sujñāne ekarcayoḥ | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga ityāsitamādyāyām | krauñcaṃ tisṛṣu| vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvamāmahīyavamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam ||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ -- davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavorājā medhābhirīyatetaṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratamiṣe pavasva dhāraya iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnināmitraṃhu indreṇa tā huve ityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgamādyāyām | gautamāntarikṣe tṛcayoḥ| āṣkāraṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām | tisro vāca iti aṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ | vairūpaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca pṛṣṭhāni | tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca pāṣṭhauhaṃ ca | ā sotā pari ṣiñcateti sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ caikarcayoḥ | sutāso madhumattamā iti tvāṣṭrīsāmanī svārādyatriṇidhane | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni| sarvaṃ saptadaśam ||
caturthasyāhnaḥ -- prata āśvinīḥ pavamāno ajījanat prayadgāvo na ṣaḍṛcam | āśuḥ ṣaḍṛcam | hinvantīti bahiṣpavamānam | janasyaayaṃ vāmindro dadhīiyaṃ vāmasyetyājyāni | pavasva dakṣa sādhana iti gāyatraṃ cātharvaṇaṃ ca nidhanakāmaṃ ca | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇetyāṣṭādaṃṣṭam ābhīśavaṃ svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasam | punāno akramīdabhīti satrāsāhīyamantyam | vairājaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ceti pṛṣṭhāni | pari priyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca | tvaṃ hyaṅgasomaḥ punāna ūrmiṇeti bṛhatkātīṣādīye | purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadāndhīgave | somaḥ pavate janitā matīnām iti vātsapramantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saindhukṣitaṃ saubharaṃ vasiṣṭhasya priyamityukthāni | gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | sarvamekaviṃśam ||
pañcamasyāhnaḥ -- govitpavasva pavamānasya viśvavit pra somāso adhanviṣuḥ saptarcam | pra kaviḥ saptarcam | yavaṃ yavaṃ caturṛcam| yāste iti bahiṣpavamānam | tavaśripurūruṇottiṣṭhannindrāgnīyuvāmityājyāni |
arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatraṃ ca yaṇvaṃ ca śākalavārśe | soma uṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhiriti mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ ca vāmraṃ cāgnestriṇidhanam | yatsoma citramukthyamiti śaiśavamantyam| mahānāmnyaśca vāmadevyaṃ ca bārhadgiraṃ ca rāyovājīyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | asāvyaṃśurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca santani ca | abhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnā miti cyāvanakrośe | pavasva vājasātaya iti tvāṣṭrīsāma ca yatsābhyāsam | ṛṣabhaśca śākvaraḥ | pārthaṃ cāṣṭeḍaṃ ca padastobhaṃ ca| indurvājī pavate gonyoghā iti dāśaspatyamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saṃjayaṃ ca saumitraṃ ca mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ cetyukthāni ||
sarvaṃ triṇavam ||
ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ -- jyotiryajñasya asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ pavasva devavīratidaśarcam | sanādaśarcam | taratsamacaturṛcam | ete somā iti bahiṣpavamānam | imampratibhindhiyajñasye tyājyāni | indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ ca iṣovṛdhīyaṃ ca gāyatrīkrauñcaṃ ca vājadāvaryaśca revatyaśca | mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyeti aukṣṇorandhre ca svāraiḍe | vājajicca varuṇasāma ca goṣṭhaṃ ca | etamu tyaṃ dakṣa kṣipa itīhavadvāmadevyamantyam | revatīṣu vāravantīyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca ṛṣabhaśca raivataḥśyenaśceti pṛṣṭhāni | parisvāno gāyatraṃ ca trīṇi vaidanvatāni | sa sunve yo vasūnāṃ taṃ vaḥ sakhāyo madāyeti dīrghaṃ ca kārṇaśravasaṃ ca | somāḥ pavanta indava iti svāraṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca madhuścunnidhanaṃ ca krauñce ca vāṅnidhanaiḍe | ayā pavasveti śnauṣṭamantyam | somāḥ pavauho iti svāraṃ krauñcam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | gūrdaṃ ca bhadraṃ codvaṃśaputraścetyukthāni | sarvaṃ trayastriṃśam ||
āhīnikī rātriḥ pañcadaśinī| trivṛtsaṃdhiḥ | ṣoḍaśi nāsti | udvaṃśaputrādrātrimupayantīti vacanāt ||
iti vyūḍhaḥ samāptaḥ||
samūḍhaḥ
samūḍhasya klṛptimāha -- 
atha yadi samūḍhaḥ | etadeva prathamasyāhno bahiṣpavamānam | ete svarāḥ || 1 || dvitīyasyāhno vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | pañcamasyāhnaḥ pārthasya loka ākūpāraṃ yannavame'hani || 2 ||
sāṃvatsarikaḥ samūḍhaḥ uttarasyāviklṛptā bṛhatyaḥ sarvasya ṣaḍahasya ||3||
iti | samūḍhasya prathamastryahaḥ sarvo vyūḍhavat | dvitīye'hani vṛṣā śoṇa iti vāsiṣṭhasya sthāne pārthamiti viśeṣaḥ ||
caturthasyāhnaḥ -- pavamāno ajījanadityanuvākokto gavāmayanikaḥ samūḍhaḥ uttarasya tryahasya kartavyam | abhīvartakāleyānukalpanasya gavāmayanaprayuktatvāt asya sarvasya ṣaḍahasya dāśarātrikya eva mādhyaṃdinabṛhatyaḥ | tatra caturthasyāhnaḥ klṛptiḥ | pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ āśurarṣa bṛhanmate hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇa indro dadhīco asthabhir iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni | pavasva dakṣasādhanaḥ iti gāyatraṃ cātharvaṇaṃ ca nidhanakāmaṃ ca | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇetyāṣṭādaṃṣṭram ābhīśavaṃ svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasam | somaḥ pavate janitā matīnāmiti janitramantyam | vairūpaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ceti pṛṣṭhāni | pari priyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatramaurṇāyavaṃ ca | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punānaḥ ūrmiṇeti bṛhatkātīṣādīye | purojitī vo andhasaḥ iti nānadāndhīgave| pra ta āśvinīriti lauśamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saindhukṣitaṃ saubharaṃ vasiṣṭhasya priyamityukthāni| gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | sarvamekaviṃśam ||
pañcamasyāhnaḥ -- pavamānasya viśvavit yatsoma citramuktham pra somāso adhanviṣuḥ pra kavirdevavītaye yavaṃ yavanno andhayāste dhārā madhuścuta iti bahiṣpavamānam | agne stomaṃ manāmahe purūruṇā ciddhayasti uttiṣṭhannojasā saha indrāgnī yuvāmime ityājyāni | arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatraṃ ca yaṇvaṃ ca śākalaṃ ca vārśaṃ ca| somā uṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhiriti mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ ca vāmraṃ cāgnestriṇidhanam | indurvājīti sampāntyamantyam | mahānāmnyaśca vāmadevyaṃ ca bārhadgiraṃ ca rāyovājīyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | asāvyaṃśurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca saṃtani ca| abhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti cyāvanakrośe | pavasva vājasātaye iti tvāṣṭrīsāma ca ṛṣabhaśca śākvaraḥ ākūpāraṃ. cāṣṭeḍaṃ ca padastobhaḥ | govitpavasveti dvayabhyāghātaṃ lauśamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saṃjayaṃ ca saumitraṃ ca mahāvaiśvamitraṃ cetyukthāni | sarvaṃ triṇavam ||
ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ -- asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavasva devavīrati sanā ca soma jeṣi ca taratsamandī dhāvati ete somā asṛkṣateti bahiṣpavamānam | yamagne pṛtsumartyam prati vāṃ sūra udite bhindhi viśvā abhi dviṣaḥ yajñasya hi stha ṛtvijetyājyāni| indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ ceṣovṛdhīyaṃ ca gāyatrīkrauñcaṃ ca vājadāvaryaśca revatyaśca | mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaukṣṇorandhre vājajicca varuṇasāma cāṅgirasāṃ goṣṭhaḥ | ayā pavasvetīhavadvāsiṣṭhamantyam | revatīṣu vāravantīyaṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca ṛṣabhaśca śākvaraḥ śyenaśceti pṛṣṭhāni | pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca trīṇi ca vaidanvatāni | sa sunve yo vasūnām taṃ vaḥ sakhāyo madāyeti dīrghaṃ ca kārṇaśravasaṃ ca| somāḥ pavante iti krauñcaṃ ca trayāṇāmādyām | madhuścinnidhanaṃ ca krauñce ca vāṅnidhanaiḍe | jyotiryajñasya marutāṃ dhenvantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | gūrdaṃ bhadrodvaṃśaputra ukthāni| sarvaṃ trayastriṃśam | udvaṃśaputrādrātrimupayantītyetadatrāpi samam | ṛtuṣaḍahe kathaṃ rātrirityārabhya kartavyeti gautamaḥ ityaviśeṣeṇa nidānavacanāt | tatra rātrirāhīnikī | pañcadaśī saṃdhiḥ ||
itiḥ samūḍhaḥ||
iti ṛtūnāṃ ṣaḍahaḥ || 24 ||
āyuṣkāmaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ
trivṛdagniṣṭomaḥ pañcadaśa ukthyaḥ saptadaśa ukthyo jyotirgaurāyuratirātra ityanuvākenāyuṣkāmaḥ ṣaḍaha uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
eṣa eva tryaho ya ṛtūnāṃ yathā vyūḍhe || 1 ||
tṛtīyasyāhno vairūpasya loke rathantaram | sutāso madhumattama iti trīṇi tvāṣṭrīsāmānyaiḍaṃ madhye svāre abhito bṛhat tisṛṣu || 2||
jyotirgaurāyuratirātraḥ | ekāhaklṛptāḥ || 3||
iti | asya prathamastryahaḥ ṛtuṣaḍahavat | dvitīyasyāhno mādhyaṃdināntyaṃ vāsiṣṭham | yathā vyūḍha iti viśeṣavacanāt | tṛtīyasyāhno vairūpasya loke rathantaram | ārbhavastu tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca pāṣṭhauhaṃ ca | ā sotā pari ṣiñcata sakhāya ā niṣīdatevācaśca sāma śauktaṃ caikarcayoḥ | sutāso madhumattamā iti tvāṣṭrīsāma svārayorādyamādyāyām | aiḍaṃ madhyamāyām svārayoruttaramuttamāyām | bṛhattisṛṣu | pavitraṃ ta iti ariṣṭamantyamiti ||
jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomaścaturthamahaḥ | atra tṛtīyacaturthayorahnorapi rathantarapṛṣṭhatvena jāmitā syāditi tatparihārārthaṃ tṛtīyasyārbhave bṛhadanukalpanam | gaurukthyaḥ pañcamamahaḥ | āyuratirātraḥ ṣaṣṭham | jyotirādayaḥ ekāhaklṛptāḥ || 3||
iti āyuṣkāmaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ ||25||
pṛṣṭhyāvalambaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ
abhyāsaṅgyaḥ pañcāho viśvajidatirātraḥ ityanuvākena pṛṣṭhāvalambaḥ ṣaḍaha uktaḥ| tasya kalpaḥ -- 
eṣa eva pañcāho ya ṛtūnāṃ samūḍho'bhyāsakto bṛhadrathantarapṛṣṭhaḥ || 1||
iti| ṛtuṣaḍahavat samūḍho devapañcarātravat abhyāsakto bṛhadrathantarapṛṣṭhaḥ pañcāhaḥ ||1||
prathamasyāhnaḥ svādiṣṭhārbhavīyā || 2 ||
dvitīyasyāhno vāsiṣṭhamantyam | svasthāne pārtham ||3||
caturthasyāhnaḥ aurṇāyavasyopariṣṭāt bṛhadbhāradvājam āndhīgavasya śuddhāśuddhīyam uddharati ṣoḍaśinam || 4 ||
pañcamasyāhnaḥ santanina upariṣṭād aiḍaṃ ca saindhukṣitaṃ gauṣūktaṃ ca || 5 ||
iti | upāsmai gāyatā nara upoṣu jātamāturam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agna āyāhi vītaye ityājyāni | pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatramāśvaṃ somasāmeti sāmatṛcaḥ | pra soma devavītaya iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva pavate haryato haririti saphākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudalagautame | kāvamantyam | saphākṣāraudalānyekarcāni | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | dve trivṛtī savane | pañcadaśamekam ||
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ -- pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasipavamānasya te vayamiti bahiṣpavamānam| agniṃ dūtamityājyāni | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ cāyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti ihavadvāsiṣṭhamantyam | ṣaṣṭhe'hni vāsiṣṭhasyāprayogāt saṃcārāprasaṅgāt ihavadvāsiṣṭhasya pañcame ca pārthasya daśarātravat sthitiḥ | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | pavasvendramaccheti śaṅkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣetyāsitakrauñce | vṛṣā matīnā
miti yāmamantyam | śaṅkusujñāne ekarcayoḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvamāmahīyavamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | dve pañcadaśe savane | saptadaśamekam ||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ -- davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam iṣe pavasva dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam | agnināgniḥ samidhyate ityājyāni| uccā te gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgamādyāyām | gautamāntarikṣe tṛcayoḥ | āṣkāraṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām | tisro vāca iti aṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ |
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ ca rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca pāṣṭhauhaṃ ca | ā sotā pari ṣiścata sakhāya ā niṣīdata iti vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ ca | sutāso madhumattamā iti tvāṣṭrīsāmanī svāratriṇidhane | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | dve saptadaśe savane | ekaviṃśamekam ||
caturthasyāhnaḥ -- pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi prayadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ āśurarṣa bṛhanmate hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇaindro dadhīco asthabhir iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni | pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatraṃ cātharvaṇaṃ ca nidhanakāmaṃ ca| tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇetyāṣṭādaṃṣṭramābhīśavaṃ svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasam | somaḥ pavate janitā matīnām iti janitramantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ceti pṛṣṭhāni | pari priyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca bṛhadbhāradvājaṃ ca| tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punāna ūrmiṇeti vṛhatkātīṣādīye | purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadaṃ cāndhīgavaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca | pra ta āśvinīr iti lauśamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saindhukṣitaṃ saubharaṃ vāsiṣṭhasya priya mityukthāni | uddharati ṣoḍaśinaṃ vairājāpāyāt | tathā ca sūtram -- virāḍapāye cāpaitīti | nidānaṃ ca -- virājāmanvapāyaṃ svarājo vapeyuriti | dve ekaviṃśe savane| triṇavamekam ||
pañcamasyāhnaḥ -- pavamānasya viśvavit yatsoma citramukthyam pra somāso adhanviṣuḥ pra kavirdevavītaye yavaṃ yavaṃ no andhasā yāste dhārā madhuścutaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | agne stomaṃ manāmahe purūruṇā ciddhayastiuttiṣṭhannojasā sahaindrāgnī yuvām ityājyāni | arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatraṃ ca yaṇvaṃ ca śākalaṃ ca vārśaṃ ca | soma uṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhiḥ iti mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ ca | agnestriṇidhanam | indurvājīti saṃpāntyamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca bārhadgiraṃ ca rāyovājīyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | asāvyaṃśurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca saṃtani caiḍaṃ ca saindhukṣitaṃ gauṣūktaṃ ca | abhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti cyāvanakrośe pavasva vājasātaye iti tvāṣṭrīsāma ca ṛṣabhaśca śākvaraḥ | pārthaṃcāṣṭeḍaṃ ca padastobhaḥ | govitpavasveti dvayabhyāghātaṃ lauśamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saṃjayaṃ ca saumitraṃ ca mahāvaiśvāmitraṃ cetyukthāni | dve triṇave savane | trayastriṃśamekam | savanaśeṣatvādukthāni trayastriṃśānyeva syuḥ iti sūtram ||25 ||
ṣaṣṭhamaharāha -- 
viśvajidatirātraḥ || 6||
iti | sa ca viśvajitaḥ pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye yadyatirātra ityādinā klṛptaḥ || 6||
iti pṛṣṭhyāvalambaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ || 26 ||
saptarātrāḥ
saptarṣisaptarātraḥ
atha saptarātrāḥ sapta | pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaho mahāvratamatirātra ityanuvākena saptarṣisaptarātra uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnāṃ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā ||1||
samūḍhastveva ||2||
vyākhyātametat || 12||
aikāhikaṃ vratam | tasya jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān ||3||
iti| vratasyāno viśvāsu havyamiti śyaitaṃ brahmasāma | gaurīvitasya loke audalam | samānamitaraṃ sāṃvatsariketyaikāhikaṃ vratamuktaṃ maśakena | tasyāgniṣṭomasāmānte sākamaśvaṃ saubharaṃ nārmedhamityukthānītyadhikṛtya sūtram -- saptarātrāṇāṃ prathamasyātirātre pañcaviṃśānyahastomatvādekaviṃśānītyanujyotiṣṭomapradeśāditi | tasmādekaviṃśānyevokthāni | gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma ekaviṃśam | āhīnikī rātriḥ pañcadaśī saṃdhistrivṛt ||3||
iti saptarṣisaptarātraḥ ||27||
prājāpatyaḥ saptarātraḥ
pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ saptadaśaṃ mahāvratamatirātraḥ | tasya trivṛcchiraḥ pañcadaśau pakṣau saptadaśa ātmaikaviṃśaṃ puccham ityanuvākena prājāpatyaḥ saptarātra uktaḥ ||
tasya kalpaḥ -- 
eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnāṃ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā ||1||
samūḍhastveva ||2||
saptadaśaṃ vratam | tasyopāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavasva vāco agriyo davidyutatyā rucā utte śuṣmāsa īrate pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||1||
spaṣṭamidam || 1||
uttare gāyatryā uddharati pūrve anuṣṭubhaḥ | samānamitaraṃ pūrveṇa vratena ||2||
tasya stomānvayīnyukthāni | ekaviṃśaḥ ṣoḍaśī ||3||
iti | saptadaśānyājyāni | asya pratnāmanudyutam iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ prathamāyām | dairghaśravasaṃ tisṛṣu | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyam iti | saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ| vrataṃ hotuḥ pṛṣṭham | tasya trivṛcchiraḥ| pañcadaśau pakṣau | saptadaśa ātmā | ekaviṃśaṃ pucchamiti viśeṣaḥ| itarāṇi pṛṣṭhāni saptadaśāni| pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye ekarcayoḥ | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam iti | saptadaśa ārbhavaḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | ilāndaṃ vā| ilāndapakṣe śvāvāśvasthāne yajñāyajñīyam | ukthādi saṃdhistotrāntaṃ pūrvamahāvratavat | tasya stomānvayīnyukthāni| ahastomaṃ saptadaśam | prakṛtistomaṃ caikaviṃśam | vratasya vā prakṛtistomaṃ pañcaviṃśam | atra tu ahastomamanvīyurukthānītyarthaḥ | tathā coktaṃ stomānvayīnīti saptadaśāni syuradhikārādityādi||3||
iti prājāpatyaḥ saptarātraḥ || 28||
paśukāmasya saptarātraḥ
pṛṣṭhayaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ chandomapavamānaṃ mahāvratamatirātraḥ ityanuvākena paśukāmaḥ saptarātra uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnāṃ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā ||1||
samūḍhastveva || 2||
chandomapavamānaṃ vratam | tasya pavamānasya te vayam iti caturṛcasya loke tṛcam ||3||
vipariharati satrāsāhīyajarābodhīye | āśu ca bhārgavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ ca saumitraṃ caiṭataṃ ca yaudhājayāduttarāṇi | bārhadukthaṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca | svāśirāmarkāduttarāṇi śāmmadaprabhṛtīni catvāri | āndhīgavāduttarāṇyākūpāraṃ ca sādhraṃ ca svāsu dharma ||4||
samānamitaraṃ prathamena vratena sāptarātrikeṇa || 5 || iti | upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ upoṣu jātamapturaṃ pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavaḥ pavamānasya te vayam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | caturviṃśam | pañcadaśānyājyāni| asya pratnāmanudyutamiti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca satrāsāhīyaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca āśubhārgavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ saumitraṃ caiṭataṃ ca | parīto ṣiñcateti samantaṃ dairghaśravasaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca | ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyamiti | catuścatvāriṃśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ | samantaṃ prathamāyām | pauruhanmanamadhyāsyāyām | vratādīni pṛṣṭhāni prakṛtivat| parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ śāmmadaṃ ca dāvasunidhanaṃ ca pratīcīneḍaṃ kāśītaṃ ca hāviṣkṛtaṃ | pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye | purojitī vo andhasa ityaudalaṃ ca madhuścinnidhanaṃ ca śvāvāśvaṃ cāndhīgavaṃ cākūpāraṃ ca sādhraṃ ca svāsu dharma sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam ityaṣṭācatvāriṃśa ārbhavaḥ | agniṣṭomasāmādi saṃdhistotrāntaṃ prathamavratavat || 5 ||
iti paśukāmaḥ saptarātraḥ|| 29 ||
kṣullakajāmadagnyaḥ saptarātraḥ
abhyāsaṃgyaḥ pañcāho'tha trayastriṃśamahastasya catutriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ saptadaśaṃ mahāvratamatirātrastasya caturviṃśaṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ trivṛcchiraḥ pañcadaśau pakṣau saptadaśa ātmaikaviṃśaṃ puccham ityanuvākena kṣullakajāmadagnyākhyaḥ saptarātra uktaḥ| tasya kalpaḥ -- 
eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnāṃ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā || 1 ||
samūḍhastveva || 2||
tasyābhyāsaktaḥ pañcāho yathā pṛṣṭhyāvalambe | caturthasyāhnaḥ ekaviṃśaḥ ṣoḍaśī | pañcamasyāhnaḥ pārthasya loke ākūpāraṃ vātreyaṃ veti yadi samūḍhaḥ || 3||
atha yadi vyūḍhaścaturthasyāhnaḥ śuddhāśuddhīyasya loka ākūpāram | pañcamasyāhnaḥ pārthasyātreyaṃ pārthaṃ vaiva | chandomavato vratasya bahiṣpavamānam | saptadaśaṃ vratam |
samānamitaram ||4||
iti | ṛtuṣaḍahavannānāpṛṣṭhaḥ pṛṣṭhyāvalambavadabhyāsaktaḥ pañcāhaḥ |
caturthasyāhnaḥ indra juṣasveti gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāmaikaviṃśam | vairājaprayuktaḥ ṣoḍaśī| tatra samūḍhapakṣe pañcamasyāhnaḥ pārthasya loke ākūpāraṃ vātreyaṃ veti vikalpaḥ| vyūḍhapakṣe caturthasyāhnaḥ | śuddhāśuddhīyasya ākūpāram pañcamasyāhnaḥ |
pārthasyātreyaṃ pārthameva | pṛṣṭhyāvalambādviśeṣaḥ | ṣaṣṭhamahastrayastriṃśaṃ ṛtuṣaḍahavadvaṃśaputrāntaṃ catustriṃśo'gniṣṭomaḥ| ekādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti |
sa tissṛbhiḥ sa saptabhiḥ sa ekayā | ekādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti | sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa saptabhiḥ | dvādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti so'ṣṭābhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiriti | catustriṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ | dāśarātrikebhya ekayā jyāyaḥsvityādivacanāt | saptadaśaṃ mahāvratam atirātrastasya caturviṃśaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | trivṛcchiraḥ | pañcadaśau pakṣau | saptadaśa ātmā | ekaviṃśaṃ puccham iti saptamamaharuktam | tasya chandomavato vratasya bahiṣpavamānam | upāsmai upoṣu jātamapturaṃ pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā sutodavidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavaḥ pavamānasya te vayam pavamānasya te kave iti caturviṃśam | itarat sarvaṃ saptadaśa vratātirātravat ||4||
iti kṣullakajāmadagnyaḥ saptarātraḥ || 30||
indrasaptarātraḥ
jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo gaurukthya ityādyanuvākena indrasaptarātra uktaḥ ||
tasya kalpaḥ -- 
jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo gaurukthya āyurukthyo'bhijidagniṣṭomo viśva
jidagniṣṭomaḥ sarvajidagniṣṭomaḥ sarvastomo'tirātraḥ | ekāhaklṛptāḥ ||1||
iti| sarvajiditi mahāvratamucyate | ekāhagrahaṇaṃ sāṃvatsarikavyāvṛttyartham || 1||
iti indrasaptarātraḥ|| 31 ||
janakasaptarātraḥ
catvāri trivṛntyahānyagniṣṭomamukhāni | viśvajinmahāvrataṃ jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ityanuvākena janakasaptarātra uktaḥ | agniṣṭomamukhānīti caturṣu prathamamaharagniṣṭomasaṃstham | itarāṇyukthasaṃsthānītyarthaḥ| stotraklṛptimāha
yā devānāṃ pañcarātrasya prathamā sā janakasaptarātrasyānabhyāsaktā ||7.14.1|| iti | upāsmai gāyatā nara upoṣu jātamapturam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnaā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatramāśvaṃ somasāmeti sāmatṛcaḥ| pra soma devavītaya iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir iti saphapauṣkale | pra sunvānāyāndhasaḥ ityaudalagautame | kāvamantyam | kāvameva tṛce | itarāṇyādyāsu| yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 1||
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ hāviṣmatam ājigaṃ ta ekarcāḥ | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu | maukṣasya loke svāśirāmarkaḥ | aupagavasya saubharam ||2||
samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā devānāṃ pañcarātrasya ||3||
iti| pavasva vāco pavasvendo pavamānasya te vayamiti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtamityājyāni | vṛṣā pavasveti gāyatraṃ hāviṣmatamājigamiti sāmatṛcaḥ| punānaḥ soma dhārayeti triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | pavasvendramaccheti śaṅkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga ityāsitakrauñce | vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavate iti yāmamantyam | yāmameva tṛce | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | auśanaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni| sarvaṃ trivṛt ||3||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ | uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatraṃ kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ svāraṃ sauparṇaṃ ta ekarcāḥ | abhi somāsa āyavaḥ ityantarikṣaṃ tisṛṣu | mahāvaiṣṭambhasya loke śrāyantīyam ||1||
samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnā devānāṃ pañcarātrasya ||4||
iti | davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavaḥ iṣe pavasva dhāraya iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnināgniḥ samidhyata ityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ svāraṃ sauparṇamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | abhi somāsa āyava ityantarikṣaṃ tisṛṣu | tisro vāca ityaṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ | ā sotā pari ṣiñcatasakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaśca sāma śauktam | sutāso madhumattamā iti tvāṣṭrīsāmanī svāraṃtriṇidhane | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam | antyameva tṛce| yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 4 ||
caturthasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ | pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatramadārasṛt satrāsāhīyam ta ekarcāḥ | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇeti svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasaṃ tisṛṣu | svāśirāmarkasya loke gauṣūktam | lauśasya dīrghatamaso'rkaḥ | saubharasya traikakubham || 5 ||
samānamitaraṃ caturthenāhnā pañcaśāradīyasya ||6||
iti | pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇaindro dadhīco asthabhiḥiyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni| pavasva dakṣasādhanaḥ iti gāyatramadārasṛt sahatrāsāhīyamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇeti svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasaṃ tisṛṣu| uhu vā asyeti vāsiṣṭhamantyam| bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca | traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ceti pṛṣṭhāni | pari priyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣūktaṃ ca | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivyasomaḥ punāna ūrmiṇā iti bṛhatkakrośe | purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāma ca | dhartā divaḥ iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ | antyavarjamekarcāḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saindhukṣitaṃ traikakubhamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni | sarvaṃ trivṛt ||6||
viśvajinmahāvrataṃ jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān | ekāhaklṛptāḥ ||7||
iti | ekāhiko viśvajit pañcamamahaḥ | mahāvrataṃ jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān saptamamahaḥ | tasyāhīnikī rātriḥ ||7||
iti janakasaptarātraḥ ||32||
pṛṣṭhyastomaḥ saptarātraḥ
pṛṣṭhyaḥ stomaḥ ṣaḍaho viśvajidatirātraḥ ityanuvākena pṛṣṭhyastomaḥ saptarātra uktaḥ| tasya kalpaḥ 
eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnāṃ samūḍho'nabhyāsakto bṛhadrathantarapṛṣṭhyaḥ | caturthasyāhnaḥ pratyasmai pipīṣita iti gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma | ṣaṣṭhasyāhno revatīnāṃ loke dārḍhacyutam | vipariharatyaukṣṇorandhre
viśvajidatirātraḥ || 1 ||
iti | ṣaṭpṛṣṭhasaṃyogābhāvādrevatīnāmuddhāraḥ | aukṣṇorandhrayorvyatyāso bṛhatyā iḍārambhaṇārthaḥ| pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍahe tu revatīnāmaiḍokṣṇorandhrasya ca saṃnipāte jāmiprasaṅgādiḍārambhaṇatvamupekṣitam ||
viśvajidatirātraḥ saptamamahaḥ||1||
iti pṛṣṭhyastomaḥ saptarātraḥ samāptaḥ || 33 ||
aṣṭarātraḥ
pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaho mahāvrataṃ jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ityanuvākena aṣṭarātra uktaḥ| tasya kalpaḥ -- 
eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnāṃ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā ||1||
samūḍhastveva || 2||
aikāhikaṃ vratam | jyotiṣṭomo'tirātro'ṣoḍaśikaḥ ||3||
iti | ṛtuṣaḍavat | samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā nānāpṛṣṭhaḥ | prathamaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ | ṣaṣṭhe'hani rātriḥ | aikāhikaṃ vratam | saptamamahaḥ | aṣoḍaśiko jyotiṣṭomo'tirātro'ṣṭamamahaḥ | tasyāhīnikī rātriḥ ||3||
iti aṣṭarātraḥ ||34||
devānāṃ navarātraḥ
atha dvau navarātrau| tayoḥ prathamaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaho jyotirgaurāyuratirātraḥ ityanuvākenoktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ -- eṣa eva ṣaḍaho ya ṛtūnāṃ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā ||1||
samūḍhastveva ||2||
jyotirgaurāyuratirātraḥ | ekāhaklṛptāḥ || 3||
iti| ṛtuṣaḍahavannānāpṛṣṭhaḥ prathamaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ | jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomaḥ | saptamamahaḥ | gaurukthyo'ṣṭamaḥ | āyuratirātro navamam | ete jyotirādaya ekāhaklṛptāḥ ||3||
iti devānāṃ navarātraḥ || 35 ||
paśukāmasya navarātraḥ
jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo gaurukthya āyurukthyo'bhyāsaṅgyaḥ pañcāho viśvajidatirātraḥ ityanuvākena dvitīyo navarātra uktaḥ| tasya kalpaḥ -- 
jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo gaurukthya āyurukthyaḥ| tasyāyuṣaḥ purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvamekasyām | āndhīgavamekasyām | ākūpāramekasyām | bṛhattisṛṣu | bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambham ||1||
pṛṣṭhyāvalambasya pañcāho viśvajidatirātro viśvajidatirātraḥ || 2 ||
iti | jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomaḥ prathamamahaḥ | gaurukthyo dvitīyam | āyurukthyastṛtīyam | tasyāyuṣaḥ | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvamāndhīgavamākūpāramiti sāmatṛcaḥ| bṛhat tisṛṣu| bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambhamiti viśeṣaḥ | aikāhikā ete| pṛṣṭhyāvalambasya pañcāhaḥ | eṣa eva paccāho ya ṛtūnāmityādinoktaḥ | navamamaharviśvajidatirātraḥ ||2||
iti paśukāmasya navarātraḥ ||36||
iti śrīvāmanāryasutavaradarājaviracitāyām ārṣeyakalpavyākhyāyāṃ saptamo'dhyāyaḥ ||7||
<8>
agna āyūm̐ṣi pavasa ityanurūpaḥ śagdhyū ṣu śacīpata ityabhīvarto brahmasāma pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudaladaivodase abhi priyāṇīti yajñāyajñīyamantyaṃ namaste agna ojasa iti jarābodhīyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsu vā samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnā janakasya saptarātrasya 811
vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yajñāyajñīyamantyam̐ saubharamagniṣṭomasāma svāsu samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā devānāṃ pañcarātrasya 812
davidyutatyā rucai te asṛgramindava iṣe pavasva dhārayāgnināgniḥ samidhyata ityājyānyuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatramekasyāṃ vairūpamekasyām̐ saumitramekasyāmā soma svāno adribhirityantarikṣaṃ tisṛṣu tisro vāca ityaṅgirasām̐ saṃkrośo'ntyo rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ cāsotā pariṣiñcata sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ ca sutāso madhumattamā iti tvāṣṭrīsāma cāndhīgavaṃ ca pavitraṃ ta iti yajñāyajñīyamantyam̐ śrudhyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsu 82
pavamāno ajījanatpunāno akramīdabhi rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhrataṃ hinvanti sūramusrayo 'gnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanaditi hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇi pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatraṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāma ca yadīṅkhayantīrityabhi somāsa āyava ityāṣṭādam̐ṣṭraṃ cātharvaṇaṃ cābhīśavaṃ ca svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasamu hu vā asyeti vāsiṣṭhamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca vayamenamidā hya iti vāsiṣṭham̐ svāsu pṛśni pari priyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca tvam̐ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punāna ūrmiṇeti bṛhatka krośe purojitī vo andhasa iti yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca kṣullakakāleyaṃ ca pra ta āśvinīḥ pavamāna dhenava iti lauśamantyaṃ viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsu 83
pavamānasya viśvavidyatsoma citramukthyaṃ pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇaya āśurarṣa bṛhanmate yāste dhārā madhuścuto'gne stomaṃ manāmaha iti hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇi arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatram̐ yaṇvam̐ śākalam̐ soma u ṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhiriti vāmramanūpamagnestriṇidhanamabhi tripṛṣṭhamiti saṃpāntyā rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cendramiddevatātaye tvamindra yaśā asīti yauktasrucaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ cāsāvyam̐śurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣūktaṃ cābhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti cyāvana vārśe pavasva vājasātaya iti yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca rayiṣṭhaṃ ca govitpavasveti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśamantyamagniṃ nara iti bṛhadāgneyamagniṣṭomasāma tṛtīyasyāhna ukthāni 84
asṛkṣata pra vājina etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipo babhrave nu svatavasa iti ṣaḍṛcasya tṛcamutte śuṣmāsa īrata ete somā asṛkṣata yamagne pṛtsu martyamiti hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇi pavamānasya jighnata iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛcca parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti śauktaṃ ca yaśaścābhīśavaṃ ca pratodaśca sākamukṣa itīhavadvāsiṣṭhamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca pṛṣṭhaṃ ca tvam̐ somāsi dhārayuriti gayatraṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca sa sunve yo vasūnāṃ taṃ vaḥ sakhāyo madāyeti dīrghaṃ ca kārṇaśravasaṃ ca somāḥ pavanta indava iti krauñce trirasmai sapta dhenavo duduhrira iti marutāṃ dhenvantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma 85
ye somāsaḥ parāvatyamitrahā vicarṣaṇiḥ pra svānāso rathā ivāsṛgramindavaḥ pathā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam̐ hotā devo amartyaḥ pra vo mitrāya gāyatendrā yāhi citrabhānavindrāgnī rocanā divo 'dhvaryo adribhiḥ sutamiti gāyatraṃ ca mārgīyavaṃ caiḍaṃ ca sauparṇa m̐! rohitakūlīyaṃ ca tavāham̐ soma rāraṇeti pauruhanmanaṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca gauṅgavaṃ ca dvinidhanaṃ cāyasyaṃ pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa iti vārāhamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca vāśaṃ ca vaiyaśvaṃ ca pavasva deva āyuṣagiti gāyatram̐ svāśirāṃ cārka aiḍam̐ saindhukṣitameṣa sya dhārayā suto gomanna indra aśvavaditi śārkara traite purojitī vo andhasa iti krauñcaṃ gautamaṃ yajñāyajñīyaṃ pro ayāsīdityaiḍaṃ yajñasārathyantyamā te agna idhīmahīti sraugmatamagniṣṭomasāma 86
pavamāna asṛkṣatāpaghnanpavate mṛdhaḥ pavasva devavīrati sanā ca soma jeṣi ca taratsa mandī dhāvatyayā vītī pari srava viśvebhiragne agnibhiryadasya sūra udite mahām̐ indro ya ojasā tośā vṛtrahaṇā huva indrāyendo marutvata iti dārḍhacyutapañcamāni mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyeti goṣṭhapañcamāni hā u hu vā i śiśumiti vāsiṣṭhamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca vaikhānasaṃ ca pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca trīṇi ca vaidanvatāni tvam̐ hyaṅga daivya pavasva devavītaya iti sauparṇaṃ ca vaiśvamanasaṃ ca pari tyam̐ haryatam̐ harimityākūpāraṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāma ca yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca yadvāhiṣṭhīyaṃ ca dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyo 'gniṃ vo devamagnibhiḥ sajoṣā iti rāyovājīyamagniṣṭomasāma saindhukṣitaṃ traikakubhamudvam̐śīyam 87
upo ṣu jātamapturameṣa devo amartya eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyaiṣa kavirabhiṣṭuta upa tvā raṇvasaṃdṛśamā no mitrāvaruṇā tamindraṃ vājayāmasīndrāgnī ā gatam̐ sutamuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cā jigaṃ cābhīkaṃ ca punānaḥ soma dhārayeti paurumadgaṃ cāṣkāraṇidhanaṃ ca kāṇvaṃ maidhātithaṃ co tsedhaścau śanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naipātithaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ cā gneścārko bhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśa indramaccha sutā ima ityaiśiraṃ ca rohitakūlīyaṃ cābhī no vājasātamamiti śyāvāśvaṃ ca niṣedhaśca sādhraṃ ca yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca kāvamantyamimam̐ stomamarhate jātavedasa iti samantamagniṣṭomasāma 881
viśvajidatirātraḥ 882
yā bṛhaspatisavasya sā kusurubindasya prathamā tasyai januṣaikarcau 891
hāviṣmatasya loke yauktāśvam̐ svāśirāmarkasya hāviṣmatam̐ śaṅkusujñānayoḥ satrāsāhīyaśrudhye uddharatyukthāni samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā janakasaptarātrasya 892
yā pūrvā sottarā 89 3
pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīya śrudhye vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma sākamaśvam̐ saubharaāṣṭādam̐ṣṭramityukthāni samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā trikakubhaḥ 894
ete asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyata iti purastātparyāsasya tṛce januṣaikarcayoḥ saphapauṣkale tṛtīyasyāhna ukthāni samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena 895
yā pūrvā sottarā 896
tīvrasuta ājyabahiṣpavamānaṃ madhyaṃdinaśca sam̐hitasya loke'gnerrakaḥ samānamitarametasyaiva rāthaṃtareṇa pañcadaśena 897
dvitīyasya svarasāmna ājyabahiṣpavamānaṃ bṛhatī ca pavamānasya te vayamiti paryāso maukṣasya loke svāśirāmarkaḥ samānamitarametasyaiva bārhatena pañcadaśena 89 8
yā pūrvā sottarā tasyāgnerarkasya loke sam̐hitaṃ kāvasya dīrghatamaso'rka udvam̐śiyāntānyukthāni 899
ekavim̐śo'tirātro ya ekastomānāmuttamastasya stomānvayī ṣoḍaśī 8911
pṛṣṭhyāvalambasya pañcāhaḥ prathamasyāhnaḥ pratnavadbahiṣpavamānampañcamasyāhno 'sṛkṣatetyanurūpo 8101
ye somāsaḥ parāvatīti pratipadṛtāvāṇaṃ vaiśvānaramiti hoturājyaṃ kaṇvarathaṃtarasya loke somasāma pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa iti vārāhamantyamatreyam̐ svaraḥ pro ayāsīdityaiḍaṃ yajñasārathyantyam̐ samānamitaram̐ saptamenāhnā dvādaśāhasya 8102
hinvanti sūramusraya iti pratipadviśvebhiragne agnibhiriti hoturājyamu hu vā i śiśumiti vāsiṣṭhamantyam̐ kautsam̐ svaro hā u dhartetyaiḍam̐ śārgamantyam̐ samānamitaramaṣṭamenāhnā dvādaśāhasyo 8103
upoṣu jātamapturamiti pratipadupa tvā raṇvasaṃdṛśamiti hoturājyam̐ hā u hu vā akrāniti vāsiṣṭhamantyaṃ pari tyaṃ haryatam̐ harimiti gaurīvitaloka āsitamekasyāṃ dīrghatamaso'rka ekasyāmākūpāramekasyāṃ yatsvayonyasāvi somo aruṣo vṛṣā harirityaiḍaṃ yāmam̐ samānamitaraṃ navamenāhnā dvādaśāhasya 8104
antarotsedhaniṣedhau maidhātithaā jāgṛvirityauśanamantyam̐ svāsu rathaṃtaravāmadevye pari pra dhanveti vājajitsūryavatīṣu kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma samānamitaraṃ daśamenāhnā dvādaśāhasya 8105
viśvajidatirātraḥ 8106
triṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo'yameva tasya savanabhāja stomāḥ samāvadbhājo vā 8111
jyotirukthyastasya sāhasraṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ yathā prathamasya sam̐hitasya loke maukṣaṃ nārmedhasyāṣṭādam̐ṣṭramuddharati rātrim̐ samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenaikastomena 8112
yā pūrvā sottarā 8113
gaurukthyo 8114
abhijidagniṣṭomastasyābhijitaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayetyabhīvartastisṛṣu rauravamekasyāṃ kaṇvarathaṃtaramekasyāṃ maidhātithamekasyāṃ yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu yadyu vai kaṇvarathaṃtaraṃ tṛcasthaṃ cikīrṣedabhīvartakaṇvarathaṃtare anyonyasya sthānaṃ vyatiharetkaṇvarathaṃtarayaudhājaye vā samānamitaraṃ 8115
yā pūrvā sottarā 8116
viśvajidagniṣṭoma āyurukthyo viśvajidagniṣṭomaḥ sarvastomo'tirātra ekāhakḷptāḥ 8117
pṛṣṭhyāvalambasya pañcāhaḥ pṛṣṭhyastomasya ṣaṣṭhamaharvaidanvatebhya uttarampayaḥ ṣaṭtrim̐śaṃ tṛtīyasavanam̐ sokthaṃ trayaśchandomā evameva samūḍhāḥ punaḥ kaṇvarathaṃtaram̐ svaṃ lokameti catuṣṭomayoḥ pūrvo viśvajidatirātro viśvajidatirātraḥ 8118
vivṛti
aṣṭamo'dhyāyaḥ
trikakubdaśarātraḥ
atha daśarātrāścatvāraḥ | trivṛdagniṣṭomaḥ pañcadaśa ukthyaḥ trivṛdagniṣṭomaḥ saptadaśo'gniṣṭoma ekaviṃśa ukthyaḥ | saptadaśo'gniṣṭomastriṇavo'gniṣṭomastrayastriṃśa ukthyastriṇavo'gniṣṭomo viśvajidatirātraḥ ityanuvākena trikakubdaśarātra uktaḥ | tatra prathamasyāhnaḥ kalpaḥ -- 
agna āyūṃṣi pavase ityanurūpaḥ | śagdhyūṣu śacīpate ityabhīvarto brahmasāma | pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudaladaivodāse | abhi priyāṇīti yajñāyajñīyam antyam || 1 ||
namaste agna ojasa iti jarābodhīyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsu vā || 2||
samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnā janakasya saptarātrasya ||3||
iti | upāsmai gāyatā nara agna āyūṃṣi pavase pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agna ā yāhi vītaye ityājyāni | pra somāso vipaścitaḥ iti gāyatramāśvaṃ somasāmeti sāmatṛcaḥ | pra soma devavītaye iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | śagdhyūṣu śacīpate ityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleyamiti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato haririti saphapauṣkale | pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudaladaivodāse | abhi priyāṇīti yajñāyajñīyamantyam | antyamarjamekarccāḥ | namaste agna ojasaḥ iti jarābodhīyamagniṣṭomasāma| svāsu vā | tasya gāyatrī chandaḥ| sarvaṃ trivṛt || 3||
iti prathamamahaḥ ||1||
dvitīyamahaḥ
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ kalpaḥ -- 
vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavate iti yajñāyajñīyamantyam | saubharam agniṣṭomasāma svāsu ||1||
samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā davonāṃ pañcarātrasya || 2||
iti | pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasipavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni | vṛṣā pavastra dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayetyāyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramaccheti śaṅkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti āsitakrauñce | vṛṣā matīnāmiti yajñāyajñīyamantyam| śaṅkusujñānāsītānyekarccāni | saubharamagniṣṭoma svāsu | tasya kakupchandaḥ | auśanamaupagavamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam || 2 ||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ ||2||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ -- 
davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavaḥ iṣe pavasva dhāraya agnināgniḥ samidhyate ityājyāni || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatramekasyām vairūpamekasyām | saumitramekasyām | ā soma svāno adribhir ityantarikṣaṃ tisṛṣu | tisro vāca ityaṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ || 2||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca ||3||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ cā sotā pari ṣiñcata sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ ca | sutāso madhumattamā iti tvāṣṭrīsāma cāndhīgavaṃ ca | pavitraṃ ta iti yajñāyajñīyam antyam || 4 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
antyavyatiriktā ekarcāḥ || 4||
śrudhyam agniṣṭomasāma svāsu ||5||
tasyoṣṇikchandaḥ| sarvaṃ trivṛt || 5 ||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ ||3||
caturthamahaḥ
caturthasyāhnaḥ -- 
pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi rājāmedhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat iti hoturājyam | svānyuttarāṇi ||2||
ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇāindro dadhīco asthabhiḥiyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni ||2||
pavasva dakṣasādhanaḥ gāyatraṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāma ca | yadīṅkhantīr ityabhi somāsa āyava ityāṣṭādaṃṣṭraṃ cārthavaṇaṃ cābhīśavaṃ ca svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasam | uhuvā vāsiṣṭham antyam ||3||
[iti mādhyaṃ dinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
āṣṭādaṃṣṭraṃ prathamāyām | svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasamadhyāsyāyām ||3||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca vayamenamidāhya iti vāsiṣṭham | svāsu pṛśni ||4||
iti pṛṣṭhāni||
pari priyā divaḥ kavir iti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punāna ūrmiṇeti bṛhatkakrośe | purojitī vo andhasa iti yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca kṣullakakāleyaṃ ca| pra ta āśvinīr iti lauśam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
bṛhatkakrośe ekarcayoḥ || 5 ||
viśoviśīyamagniṣṭomasāma svāsu ||6||
tasyānuṣṭupchandaḥ | sarvaṃ saptadaśam ||6||
iti caturthamahaḥ || 4 ||
pañcamamahaḥ
pañcamasyāhnaḥ -- 
pavamānasya viśvavid yatsoma citramukthyam pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ āśurarṣa bṛhanmate yāste dhārā madhuścutaḥ ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agne stomaṃ manāmahe iti hoturājyam | svānyuttarāṇi || 2|| purūruṇā ciddhayastiuttiṣṭhannojasā sahaindrāgnī yuvāmime ityājyāni ||2||
arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatraṃ yaṇvaṃ śākalam | soma uṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhir iti vāmram ānūpam agnestriṇidhanam | abhi tripṛṣṭham iti sampāntyā ||3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ cendramiddevatātaye tvamindrayaśā asīti yauktasrucaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ ca || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
asāvyaṃśurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca goṣūktaṃ cābhidyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti cyāvanavārśe | pavasva vājasātaye iti yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca rayiṣṭhaṃ ca | govitpavasveti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
agniṃ nara iti bṛhadāgneyamagniṣṭomasāma ||6|| tasya virāṭ chandaḥ || 6||
tṛtīyasyāhna ukthāni || 7||
pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | sarvamekaviṃśam || 7 ||
iti pañcamamahaḥ || 5||
ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ
ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ -- 
asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ babhrave nu svatavase iti ṣaḍṛcasya tṛcam | utte śuṣmāsa īrate ete somā asṛkṣata || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
yamagne pṛtsu martyam iti hoturājyam | svānyuttarāṇi ||2||
prati vāṃ sūra uditebhindhi viśvā apadviṣaḥyajñasya hi stha ṛtvijetyājyāni ||2||
pavamānasya jighnataḥ iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛcca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti śauktaṃ ca yaśaścābhīśavaṃ ca pratodaśca | sākamukṣa itīhavadvāsiṣṭham antyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
śauktaṃ prathamāyām | pratodo'dhyāsyāyām || 3 ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrayantīyaṃ ca pṛṣṭhaṃ ca ||4||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
tvaṃ somasi dhārayur iti gāyatraṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca | sa sunve yo vasūnām taṃ vaḥ sakhāyo madāyeti dīrghaṃ ca kārṇaśravasaṃ ca | somāḥ pavanta indavaḥ iti krauñce | trirasmai sapta dhenavo duduhire iti marutāṃ dhenu antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
dīrghakārṇaśravase ekarce ||5||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | 6 ||
sarvaṃ saptadaśam ||6||
iti ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ || 6||
saptamamahaḥ
saptamasyāhnaḥ -- 
ye somāsaḥ parāvati amitrahā vicarṣaṇiḥ pra svānāso rathā iva asṛgramindavaḥ pathā ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
babhrave nu svatavasa iti ṣaḍṛcasya pūrvastṛcaḥ pūrvasminnahani viniyuktaḥ| uttama uttarasminnahani viniyujyate amitrahāvicarṣaṇiriti | tathā ca nidānam -- babhrave nu svatavase iti ṣaḍṛcaḥ ṣaṣṭhasaptamayorviharati santānārtha ityādi ||1||
hotā devo amartyaḥ pra vo mitrāya gāyata indrāyāhi citrabhānav indrāgnī rocanā divaḥ ||2||
ityājyāni ||
adhvaryo adribhiḥ sutam iti gāyatraṃ ca mārgīyavaṃ caiḍaṃ ca sauparṇam rohitakūlīyaṃ ca| tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇeti pauruhanmanaṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca gauṅgavaṃ ca dvinidhanamāyāsyam | pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa iti vārāham antyam ||3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca vāśaṃ ca vaiyaśvaṃ ca ||4||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pavasva deva āyuṣag iti gāyatraṃ svāśirāmarkaḥ aiḍaṃ saindhukṣitam | eṣa sya dhārayā suto gomanna indra aśvavad iti śārkaratraite | purojitī vo andhasa iti krauñcaṃ gautamaṃ yajñāyajñīyam | pro ayāsīd iti aiḍaṃ yajñasārathi antyam ||5||
[iti ārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
ā te agna idhīmahīti sraugmatamagniṣṭomasāma ||6|| tasya paṅktiśchandaḥ| sarvaṃ triṇavam || 6 ||
iti saptamamahaḥ ||7||
aṣṭamamahaḥ
aṣṭamasyāhnaḥ -- 
pavamānā asṛkṣata apaghnan pavate mṛdhaḥ pavasva devavīrati sanā ca deva jeṣi ca tarat sa mandī dhāvati ayā vītī parisrava ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
viśvebhiragne agnibhir yadadya sūra udite mahām̐ indro ya ojasā tośā vṛtrahaṇā huve ||2||
ityājyāni ||
indrāyendo marutvata iti dārḍhacyutapañcamāni ||
gāyatraṃ ceṣovṛdhīyaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca vājadāvaryaśca dārḍhacyutam |
mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyeti goṣṭhapañcamāni || aukṣṇorandhre ca vājajit varuṇasāma goṣṭhaṃ ca ||
hā uhuvāi śiśumiti vāsiṣṭham antyam ||3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca vaikhānasaṃ ca ||4||
| iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca trīṇi ca vaidanvatāni tvam̐ hyaṅga daivya pavasva devavītaya sauparṇaṃ ca vaiśvamanasaṃ ca | pari tyaṃ haryataṃ harim iti ākūpāram ca tvāṣṭrīsāma ca yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca yadvāhiṣṭhīyaṃ ca | dhartā diva iti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ || 5 ||
[iti ārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
agniṃ vo devamagnibhiḥ sajoṣā iti rāyovājīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||6||
saindhukṣitaṃ traikakubham udvaṃśīyam ||7||
ityukthāni||
sarvaṃ trayastriṃśam || 7 ||
iti aṣṭamamahaḥ || 8||
navamamahaḥ
navamasyāhnaḥ -- 
upoṣu jātamapturam eṣa devo amartyaḥ eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā eṣa kavirabhiṣṭutaḥ ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
upa tvā raṇvasaṃdṛśam ā no mitrāvaruṇā tamindraṃ vājayāmasi indrāgnī āgataṃ sutam || 2 ||
ityājyāni ||
uccā te jātamandhasa gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cājigaṃ cābhīkaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti paurumadgaṃ cākṣāraṇidhanaṃ ca kāṇvaṃ maidhātithaṃ cotsedhaś cauśanam antyam ||3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naipātithaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca ||4||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārko'bhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ indramacchā sutā ime ityaiṣiraṃ ca rohitakūlīyaṃ cābhī no vājasātamam iti śyāvāśvaṃ ca niṣedhaś ca sādhraṃ ca yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca kāvam antyam ||5||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ||]
imaṃ stomamarhate jātavedase iti samantamagniṣṭomasāma ||6||
tasya jagatī chandaḥ| sarvaṃ triṇavam ||6||
iti navamamahaḥ ||6||
daśamamahaḥ
daśamamaharāha -- 
viśvajidatirātraḥ ||1||
iti ||1||
iti daśamamahaḥ|| 10||
iti trikakubdaśarātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
kusurubindadaśarātraḥ
prathamamahaḥ
trayastrivṛto'gniṣṭomāstrayaḥ pañcadaśa ukthyāstrayaḥ saptadaśa ukthyā ekaviṃśo'tirātraḥ ityanuvākena kusurubindadaśarātra uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ -- yā bṛhaspatisavasya sā kusurubindasya prathamā | tasyai januṣaikarcau ||1|| upadavipaveti bahiṣpavamānam | agnaā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyamiti sāmatṛcaḥ| punānaḥ soma dhāraye ti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir iti saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | kāvamantyam | antyavarjamekarcāḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvaṃ trivṛt ||
iti prathamamahaḥ || 11||
dvitīyamahaḥ
dvitīyamaharāha -- 
hāviṣmatasya loke yauktāśvam svāśirāmarkasya hāviṣmatam śaṅkusujñānayoḥ satrāsāhīyaśrudhye | uddharatyukthāni || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā janakasaptarātrasya || 2 ||
iti | pavasva vāco pavasvendo pavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam |
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyāni | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti āyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti āsitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca| vṛṣāmatīnāmiti yāmamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma| sarvaṃ trivṛt ||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ || 12||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
tṛtīyamaharāha -- 
yā pūrvā sottarā ||1||
yā prathamasyāhnaḥ klṛptiḥ saiva tṛtīyasyetyarthaḥ ||1||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ || 13 ||
caturthamahaḥ
caturthamaharāha -- 
pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye | vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmam antyam || yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharam āṣṭādaṃṣṭram ityukthāni ||1||
samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā trikakubhaḥ || 2 ||
iti | pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasipavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtamityājyāni | vṛṣā pavasveti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti āyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇyaiti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramacche ti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye ekarccayoḥ | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti āsitamekasyām | krauñcaṃ tisṛṣu | vṛṣā matīnāmiti yāmamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma| sākamaśvaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam ||
iti caturthamahaḥ ||14||
pañcamamahaḥ
pañcamamaharāha -- 
ete asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate iti purastāt paryāsasya tṛce | januṣaikarcayoḥ saphapauṣkale | tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ ukthāni ||1||
samānamitaraṃ jyotiṣṭomena ||2||
iti | upāsmaidavidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavorājā medhābhirīyate pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agna ā yāhi vītaye ityājyāni | uccā te gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti rauravayaudhājaye | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni| svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato hāririti saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave
| kāvamantyam | saphapauṣkalaśyāvāśvānyekarceṣu | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam ||
iti pañcamamahaḥ ||15||
ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ
caturthasyāhnaḥ klṛptiḥ ṣaṣṭhasyātidiśati -- 
yā pūrvā sottarā ||1||
iti|
iti ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ ||16||
saptamamahaḥ
saptamamaharāha -- 
tīvrasuta ājya bahiṣpavamānaṃ mādhyaṃdinaśca | saṃhitasya loke'gnerarkaḥ ||1||
samānamitarametasyaiva rathantareṇa pañcadaśena ||2||
iti | upāsmai gāyatā naro davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavas taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam |
agnaā no mitrātyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| abhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam| dvihiṃkāramādyāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale ekarcayoḥ | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ svāraṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | sarvaṃ saptadaśam ||
iti saptamamahaḥ || 17 ||
aṣṭamamahaḥ
aṣṭamamaharāha -- 
dvitīyasya svarasāmna ājyabahiṣpavamānaṃ bṛhatī ca | pavamānasya te vayam iti paryāsaḥ | maukṣasya loke svāśirāmarkaḥ ||1||
samānamitarametasyaiva bārhatena pañcadaśena ||2||
iti | pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi utte śuṣmāsa īrate pavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtamityājyāni| vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca| parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ cāyāsyaṃ ca| vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | kakubuṣṇihāvekarcau | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ saptadaśam ||
iti aṣṭamamahaḥ ||18||
navamamahaḥ
yā saptamasyāhnaḥ klṛptiḥ saiva navamasyetyāha -- 
yā pūrvā sottarā ||1||
iti | tatra viśeṣam āha -- 
tasyāgnerarkasya loke saṃhitam kāvasya dīrghatamaso'rkaḥ | udvaṃśīyāntānyukthāni || 2 ||
svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale ekarcayoḥ | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvā ndhīgave | kāvasyarkṣu dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyaḥ ityārbhavaḥ| pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | itarat saptamenāhnā samam ||2||
iti navamamahaḥ | 16||
daśamamahaḥ
daśamamaharāha -- 
ekaviṃśo'tirātraḥ | ya ekastomānāmuttamastasya stomānvayī
ṣoḍaśī || 1 ||
iti | atra nidānam -- tasya stomānvayī ṣoḍaśītyavicāramekaviṃśa iti ||1||
iti daśamamahaḥ || 20 ||
iti kusurubindadaśarātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
chandomavān daśarātraḥ
[prathamādipañcamāntānyahāni]
abhyāsaṅgyaḥ pañcāhaścatvāraśchandomā viśvajidatirātraḥ ityanuvākena chandomavān daśarātra uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ
pṛṣṭhyāvalambyasya pañcāhaḥ | prathamasyāhnaḥ pratnavad bahiṣpavamānam || 1 |
pañcamasyāhno'sṛkṣatepyanurūpaḥ || 2 ||
iti | atra viśvajitā chandomānāṃ pratyūhitatvāt ṣaṣṭhasyāhno lopānnavāhayogābhāve'pi daśamasya vidyamānatvāttadarthaṃ pratnavato navarcasya vidhānārthaṃ ṣāṣṭhikaścānurūpo daśamārthameva pañcame vidhīyate | uktaṃ hi -- daśamasya bahiṣpavamānam | dvitīyaprabhṛtīnāṃ pañcānāmahnāmanurūpā. | prathamācca bahiṣpavamānam | tannavarcamityādi ||
prathamasyāhnaḥ asya pratnānavarcaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | agnaā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatramāśvaṃ somasāmeti sāmatṛcaḥ | pra soma devavītaye iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatra saṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato hari riti saphākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti audalagautame | kāvamantyam | saphākṣāraudalānyekarcāni | yajñāyajñīya magniṣṭomasāma | dve trivṛtī savane | pañcadaśamekam ||
dvitīyasyāhnaḥpavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā hyasipavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtaṃmitraṃ huve indramidgāthinaindra agnetyājyāni | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeiti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti āyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti ihavadvāsiṣṭhamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | pavasvendramaccheti ekarcayoḥ śaṅku sujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti āsitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca | vṛṣā matīnāmiti yāmamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvamāmahīyavamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | dve pañcadaśe savane | saptadaśamekam ||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥdavidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavorājā medhābhirīyatetaṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratamiṣe pavasva dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam | agnināmitraṃ huveindreṇatā huve ityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgamādyāyām | gautamāntarikṣe tṛcayoḥ | āṣkāraṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām | tisro vāca iti aṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ ca rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca pāṣṭhauhaṃ ca | ā sotā pari ṣiñcata sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ ca | sutāso madhumattamā iti tvāṣṭrīsāmanī svārādyatriṇidhane pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | dve saptadaśe savane | ekaviṃśamekam ||
caturthasyāhnaḥpavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi prayadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ āyurarṣa bṛhanmate hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat | ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇāindro dadhīco asthabhiriyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni | pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatraṃ cātharvaṇaṃ ca nidhanakāmaṃ ca | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇetyāṣṭādaṃṣṭramābhīśavaṃ svaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasam | somaḥ pavate janitā matīnāmiti janitramantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ceti pṛṣṭhāni | paripriyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca | bṛhacca bhāradvājaṃ ca | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punāna ūrmiṇeti vṛhatkātīṣādīye | purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadaṃ cāndhīgavaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca | pra ta āśvinīriti lauśamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saindhukṣitaṃ saubharaṃ vasiṣṭhasya priya mityukthāni | ṣoḍaśina uddhāraḥ | dve ekaviṃśe savane | triṇavamekam ||
pañcamasyāhnaḥpavamānasya viśvavid asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ pra somāso adhanviṣuḥ pra kavirdevavītaye yavaṃ yavaṃ no andhasā yāste dhārā madhuścutaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | agne stomaṃ manāmahe purūruṇāciddhyastiuttiṣṭhannojasā sahaindrāgnī yuvām ityājyāni | arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatraṃ ca yaṇvaṃ ca śākalaṃ ca vārśaṃ ca | soma uṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhi riti mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ ca vāmraṃ cāgnestriṇidhanam | indurvājīti sampāntyamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca bārhadgiraṃ ca rāyovājīyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | asāvyaṃśurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca saṃtani ca aiḍaṃ ca saindhukṣitaṃ gauṣūktaṃ ca | abhidyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti cyāvanakrośe | pavasva vājasātaye iti tvāṣṭrīsāma ca yatsābhyāsam | ṛṣabhaśca śākvaraḥ pārthaṃ cāṣṭeḍaṃ ca padastobhaḥ | govitpavasveti dvayabhyāghātaṃ lauśa mantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | saṃjayaṃ ca saumitraṃ ca vaiśvāmitraṃ cetyukthāni | dve triṇave savane | trayastriṃśamekam || 2 || iti pañcamamahaḥ || 21 ||
ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ
atha catvāraśchandomāḥ | daśarātre saptamādīni catvāryahāni | tānyatra ṣaṣṭhādīni | teṣāṃ prathamasya samūḍhaklṛptimāhaye somāsaḥ parāvatīti pratipat | ṛtāvanaṃ vaiśvānaram iti hoturājyam | kaṇvarathantarasya loke somasāma pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa iti vārāhamantyam | ātreyaṃ svaraḥ | pro ayāsīd iti aiḍaṃ yajñasārathi antyam || 1 ||
samānamitaraṃ saptamenāhnā dvādaśāhasya || 2 ||
iti | ye somāsaḥ parāvati pra svānāso rathā iva asṛgramindavaḥ pathā ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam iti bahiṣpavamānam | ṛtāvanaṃ vaiśvānaraṃ | pra vo mitrāya gāyataindrāyāhi citrabhānotamīḍiṣva yo arciṣa tyājyāni | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayenati gāyatraṃ ca santani ca svāraṃ sauparṇaṃ ca rohitakūlīyaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti somasāma ca gauṅgavaṃ ca dvinidhanamāyāsyam | pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa iti vārāhamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ cābhinidhanaṃ kāṇvaṃ ca vaikhānasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ maukṣamadhyardheḍaṃ somasāmeti sāmatṛcaḥ | agnerarkastisṛṣu | eṣa sya dhārayā sutasakhāya ā niṣīdateti śārkaplavau | purojitī vo andhasa iti ātreyaṃ ca kārtayaśaṃ ca | pra vājyakṣā iti sauhaviṣam | pro ayāsīdityaiḍaṃ yajñasārathyantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | vātsaṃ ca sauśravasaṃ ca vīṅkaṃ cetyukthāni | sarvaṃ caturviṃśam || 2 || iti ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ || 22 | |
saptamamahaḥ
dvitīyasya chandomasya samūḍhaklṛptimāha
hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ iti pratipat | viśvebhiragneragnibhir iti hoturājyam | uhuvāi śiśumiti vasiṣṭham antyam || 1|| kautsaṃ svāro hāu dharte tyaiḍaṃ śārṅgam antyam || 2 ||
samānamitaramaṣṭamenāhnā dvādaśāhasya || 3 ||
iti | hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ ete somā abhi priyam somaḥ punāno arṣati somā asṛgramindavaḥ utte śuṣmāsa īrate ayāvītī parisrava apaghnan pavate mṛdhaḥ ayā pavasva dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam | viśvebhiragne agnibhir mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe tamindraṃ vājayāmasi indre agnā namo bṛhat ityājyāni | adhvaryo adribhiḥ sutam iti gāyatraṃ ca vairūpaṃ crāśubhārgavaṃ ca mārgīyavaṃ ca saumitraṃ caiṭataṃ ca sākamaśvaṃ ca vilambasauparṇaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyavaḥ iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca gāyatrapārśvaṃ ca pauruhanmana ca dvaigataṃ ca hārāyaṇaṃ cācchidraṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ ca | hāi uhu vāi śiśumiti vāsiṣṭhamantyam | dvihiṃkāraṃ prathamāyām | bārhadukthamadhyāsyāyām | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naipātithaṃ ca vaiyaśvaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | pavasva devaḥ āyuṣagiti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ surūpaṃ bhāsaṃ ca kākṣīvataṃ ca gāyatrasāmāsitaṃ ca | abhidyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti aiṣiratraite | abhī no vājasātamam iti svāraṃ ca kautsamaiḍaṃ ca kautsaṃ vā | śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca rayiṣṭhaṃ ca audalaṃ ca | pavasva soma mahāntsamudra iti dharma | hāu dhartetyaiḍaṃ śārṅgamantyam | aiṣiratraite ekarce | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | gāyatrauśanaṃ sāṃvartaṃ māruta mityukthāni | sarvaṃ catuścatvāriṃśam | tatra samīṣantīprayogāvuktau || 3 ||
iti saptamamahaḥ || 23 ||
aṣṭamamahaḥ
tṛtīyasya chandomasya samūḍhaklṛptimāhaupoṣu jātamapturam iti pratipat | upa tvā raṇvasaṃdṛśam iti hoturājyam | hāu hu vā akrān iti vāsiṣṭham antyam | pari tyaṃ haryataṃ harim iti gaurīvitaloke āsitamekasyām| dīrghatamaso'rka ekasyām | ākūpāramekasyām yat svayoniṃ | asāvi somo aruṣo vṛṣā harir iti aiḍaṃ yāmam || 1 || samānamitaraṃ navamenāhnā dvādaśāhasya | | 2 ||
iti | upoṣu jātamapturam eṣa devo amartya eṣa dhiyā yātvyaṇyā eṣa u sya vṛṣā rathaḥ eṣa vājī hito nṛbhir eṣa kavirabhiṣṭuta sa sutaḥ pītaye vṛṣā pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | upa tvā raṇvasadṛśaṃ mitraṃ huve pūtadakṣam mahām̐ indro ya ojasā tā huve yayoridam ityājyāni | pavamānasya jighnataḥ iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛcca surūpa ca hariśrīnidhanaṃ ca saindhukṣitaṃ ca bābhravaṃ ceḍānāṃ saṃkṣāramṛṛṣabhaśca pavamānaḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti pṛṣṭhaṃ tisṛṣu | kaulmalabarhiṣamarkapuṣpaṃ dairghaśravasamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | devasthānaṃ tisṛṣu | saṃkṛtiṃ tṛce | vaiyaśvamekasyām | bhargastisṛṣu | ābhīśavamekasyām | yaśastisṛṣu | ' vāsiṣṭhamadhyāsyāyām | hāu uhu vā akrāniti| vāsiṣṭhamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca samantaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | tvaṃ somāsi dhārayuriti gāyatraṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca śāmmadaṃ ca dāvasunidhanaṃ ca pratīcīneḍaṃ ca kāśītaṃ ca hāviṣkṛtaṃ ca | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya pavasva devavītaye iti sauparṇaṃ ca vaiśvamanasaṃ ca |
parityaṃ haryataṃ harimici āsitottaraṃ dīghatamaso'rkaḥ svayonyākūpāramiti sāmatṛcaḥ | nihavaṃ tisṛṣu | yadvāhiṣṭhīya cāsitaṃ ca sādhraṃ cākūpāraṃ ca | pavasva soma mahe dakṣāyeti vidharma | asāvi somo aruṣo vṛṣā harir ityaiḍaṃ yāmamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | aidhmavāhaṃ traikakubhamudvaṃśīyamityukthāni | sarvamaṣṭācatvāriṃśam || 2 || ityaṣṭamamahaḥ || 24 ||
navamamahaḥ
caturthasya chandomasya klṛptimāha
antarotsedhaniṣedhau maidhātitham | ā jāgṛvir ityauśanam antyam | svāsu rathantaram vāmadevye | pari pra dhanveti vājajit | sūryavatīṣu kāvam antyam || 1 || yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 2 || samānamitaraṃ daśamenāhnā dvādaśāhasya || 3 || iti | asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ pavamānasya viśvajit pavamāno ajījanat ete asṛgramindavaḥ pavasvedo vṛṣā sutaḥ asya pratnā navarcam iti bahiṣpavamānam |
suṣamiddho na āvaha yadadya sūra udite u tvā undantu somā indrāgnī āgataṃ sutam ityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cājigaṃ cābhīkaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhāraye tyutsedhaśca maidhātithaṃ ca niṣedhaṃ cājāgṛvirityauśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | udu tye madhumattamā ityabhīvartaḥ svāsu kāleya miti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti sapharohitakūlīye | paryūṣviti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | pari pra dhanveti vājajit | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam | sarvaṃ caturviśam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | trayastriṃśam | atra daśamadharmā mānasaṃ ca kartavyāḥ | saha dharmaiḥ sarvatra pṛṣṭaḥ syāddaśamavrate ceti vacanāt | daśamasyāhno'naṅgameke mānasaṃ bruvate ityupakramyāgantu syādadhikṛtyāmnānāditi vacanācca || 3 ||
iti navamamahaḥ || 25 ||
daśamamahaḥ
daśamamaharāha
viśvajidatirātra || 1 ||
iti || 1 ||
iti daśamamahaḥ || 26 ||
iti chandomavān daśarātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
devapūrdaśarātraḥ
prathamamahaḥ
triṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo jyotirukthyastriṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo gaurukthyo'bhijiragniṣṭomo gaurukthyo viśvajidagniṣṭoma āyurukthyo viśvajidagniṣṭomasya sarvastomo'tirātraḥ ityanuvākena devapūrdaśarātra uktaḥ | tasya kalpaḥ
triṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo'yameva || 1 || ayamiti sarvaprakṛtibhūto jyotiṣṭomaḥ parāmṛśyate || 1 ||
tasya triṣṭomatvaṃ yathā syāt tathā darśayatitasya savanabhāja stomāḥ samāvadbhājo vā || 2 ||
trivṛtpañcadaśasaptadaśāstrayastomā yathākramaṃ trīṇi savanāni bhajanta iti savanabhājaḥ | catvāri stotrāṇi trivṛnti | catvāri pañcadaśāni catvāri saptadaśānītyevaṃ samāni stotrāṇi bhajanta iti samāvadbhājo vā syurityarthaḥ | samāvacchabdaḥ samānāparaparyāyaiti prathamamaharuktam ||2||
iti prathamamahaḥ ||27||
dvitīyamahaḥ
dvitīyamaharāha -- 
jyotirukthyaḥ | tasya sāhasraṃ bahiṣpavamānaṃ yathā prathamasya | saṃhitasya loke maukṣam nārmedhasyāṣṭādaṃṣṭram | uddharati rātrim || 1||
samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenaikastomena || 2 ||
iti | pavasva vāco agriyo davidyutatyā rucā pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam| agniṃ dūtaṃmitraṃ vayamindramidgāthinoindre agnetyājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ ca | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye | purojitī vo andhasa iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam | saphaśrudhye ekarcayoḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma| sākamaśvaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭra mityukthāni | jyotiriti samākhyayā jyotiṣṭomā stomāḥ | ukthya iti vacanādrātreḥ ṣoḍaśinaścoddhāraḥ | atra nidānam -- ukthyasaṃsthaṃ jyotiṣaṃ bṛhatpṛṣṭhaṃ bārhataṃ tantramityādi ||2||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ || 28||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
yā prathamasya klṛptiḥ saiva tṛtīyasyetyāha -- 
yā pūrvā sottarā ||1||
iti ||1||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ ||29||
caturthamahaḥ
caturthamaharāha -- 
gaurukthyaḥ || 1||
iti | aikāhiko'yam || 1||
iti caturthamahaḥ || 30||
pañcamamahaḥ
pañcamamaharāha -- 
abhijidagniṣṭomaḥ | tasyābhijitaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti abhīvartastisṛṣu | rauravamekasyām | kaṇvarathantaramekasyām | maidhātithamekasyām | yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | yadyu vai kaṇvarathantaraṃ tṛcasthaṃ cikīrṣedabhīvartakaṇvarathantare anyonyasya sthānaṃ vyatiharet kaṇvarathantarayaudhājaye vā ||1||
iti | punānaḥ soma dhārayetyabhīvarto rauravayaudhājaye iti pūrvamuktam | iha tu rauravasthāne rauravaṃ kaṇvarathantaraṃ maidhātithamiti sāmatṛcaḥ kāryaḥ | yadi tu kaṇvarathantarasya rathantaraparokṣatvenāpacitatvāt tṛce karaṇamiṣṭaṃ tadā punānaḥ soma dhārayeti kaṇvarathantaraṃ tisṛṣu | rauravamabhīvarto maidhātithamiti sāmatṛco yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣviti abhīvartastisṛṣu rauravaṃ yaudhājayaṃ maidhātithamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | kaṇvarathantaraṃ tisṛṣviti vā || 1|| samānamitaram || 2||
itarat sarvamaikāhikenābhijitā samam | atra gaurabhijitordvayorapi bṛhatpṛṣṭhatvāt jāmitāmāhurityabhijitormadhyaṃdine kaṇvarathantaraṃ kalpitam| tathā ca nidānam -- kaṇvarathantaramabhiyanti rathantaranidhanānīti bṛhatsaṃnipāteṣuajāmikaraṇānīti || 2 ||
iti pañcamamahaḥ||
ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ
ṣaṣṭhamaharāha -- 
yā pūrvā sottarā ||1||
iti| gaurukthya ekāhaḥ klṛpta ityarthaḥ || 1 ||
iti ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ || 32||
saptamādidaśamāntānyahāni
saptamādīni catvāryahāni -- 
viśvajidagniṣṭoma āyurukthyo viśvajidagniṣṭomaḥ sarvastomo'tirātraḥ |
ekāhaklṛptāḥ || 1||
iti || 1||
iti saptamādīnyahāni || 33 ||
iti devapūrdaśarātraḥ samāptaḥ ||
pauṇḍarīka ekādaśarātraḥ
abhyāsaṅgyaḥ ṣaḍahastrayaśchandomāścatuṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo viśvajidatirātra ityanuvākena pauṇḍarīka ekādaśarātra uktaḥ |
tasya dvādaśāhopasatkatvapakṣe dvādaśāhe sutyāmityādi dvādaśāhavat |
ṣaḍupasatkatvapakṣe ṣaḍaha sutyāmityādi | sutyāsu yajñasārathigānam | lokadvāraṃ subrahmaṇyā ca prakṛtivadeva | stomaklṛptimāha -- 
prathamādipañcamāntānyahāni pṛṣṭhayāvalambasya pañcāhaḥ || 1 ||
iti | eṣa eva pañcāho ya ṛtūnāmityādinā pṛṣṭhyāvalambasya pañcāha uktaḥ | tasya prathamasya klṛptiḥupāsmai gāyatā naraḥ upoṣu jātamapturam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | viśvarūpā jyotirgānayordhūrgānānāṃ ca nivṛttiḥ | ahiṃkāravisṛṣṭahiṃkārabhakārāḥ pravartante | agnaā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ prathamāyām | āśvaṃ dvitīyāyām | somasāma tṛtīyāyām | pra soma devavītaye iti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | asmin ṣaḍahe bṛhadrathantarayostotre pṛṣṭhye rathamativaheyur ityādinoktau rathadundubhighoṣau na kāryau | rathantarādiṣaṭpṛṣṭhasaṃprayoge vihitatvādrathaghoṣādīnām |
taduktamupagranthekathaṃ viprayuktānāmiti kriyerannityeke | saṃprayuktānāmāmnānāt | kathaṃ viprayuktānāṃ pratiyāmetyathāpi bṛhadrathantarayoḥ saṃprayuktayoreva tatpṛṣṭhe rathavyativartanaṃ ca dundubhyāhananaṃ ca bhavati nāsaṃprayuktayoriti | ayaṃ ca ṣaḍaho vṛhadrathantara0 pṛṣṭha iti ṣaṭpṛṣṭhasaṃprayogo'tra nāsti ||
athārbhavaḥ | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato haririti ekarcayoḥ saphākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasa ityaudalaṃ prathamāyām | gautamaṃ tisṛṣu | kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | dve trivṛtī savane | pañcadaśamekam | prāgudayanīyādatirātrāddadhibhakṣāntāni sarvāṇyahāni | aharanteṣu vasatīvarīpariharaṇakāle śvaḥsutyāpravacanīsanāmagrahā subrahmaṇyā kāryā | hāriyojanātipraiṣakāle'pi śvaḥsutyāpravacanīṃ subrahmaṇyāṃ kecidicchanti | tatsūtrakārasya nābhimatamiti pūrvameva pratipāditam || dvitīyasyāhno yajñasārathyādi subrahmaṇyāntaṃ pūrvavat | pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasipavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam | dvitīyādīnāmahnāṃ bahiṣpavamānaiḥ sadasi stuvīran | prākdhiṣṇyaviharaṇānniṣkramya tṛṇaprāsanaprabhṛti cātvāle samāpayeyuḥ | agniṃ dūtaṃmitraṃ vayamindramidgācaturcam | indre agnetyājyāni | caturṛce'ntyāmuddharet | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ yauktāśvaṃ ca yat pūrvam | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti aiḍaṃ triṇidhanamāyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti vāsiṣṭhamantyam | pārthamantyaṃ kecidicchanti | tadārṣeyakalpasya neṣṭam | dvitīyasyāhno vāsiṣṭhamantyamiti pṛṣṭhyāvalambe vacanāt asya ca tatprakṛtikalpatvāt | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | pavasvendramacchetyekarcayoḥ śaṅkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti āsitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca yaduttaram | vṛṣā matīnāmiti yāmamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | ehyūṣviti sākamaśvam | evāhī tyāmahīyavam | indraṃ viśvā ityāṣṭādaṃṣṭram ityukthāni | dve pañcadaśe savane | saptadaśamekam | stomavimocanādidadhibhakṣāntaṃ subrahmaṇyā ca pūrvavat ||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ -- davidyutatyā rucā ete asagramindavo rājā medhābhirīyatetaṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam iṣe pavasva dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam | agnināmitraṃ huveindreṇatā huve ityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyavaḥ iti paurumadgaṃ prathamāyām | gautamaṃ tisṛṣu | antarikṣaṃ tisṛṣu | pra hinvāna ityadhyāsyāyām āṣkāraṇidhanaṃ | tisro vāca iti aṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | vayaṃ ghatveti mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ ca | taraṇirit siṣāsatīti rauravaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | tisro vāca udīrate iti gāyatraṃ ca pāṣṭhauhaṃ ca | ā sotā pari ṣiñcata sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ ca | sutāso madhumattamā iti tvāṣṭrīsāmanī svārādyatriṇidhane | pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | dve saptadaśe savane | ekaviṃśamekam | stomavimocanādi pūrvavat ||
caturthasyāhnaḥpavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ āśurarṣa bṛhanmate hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇaindro dadhīca asthabhir iyaṃ vāmasya manmanaḥ ityājyāni | pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatraṃ cātharvaṇaṃ ca nidhanakāmaṃ ca | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇetyāṣṭādaṃṣṭramābhīśavamsvaḥpṛṣṭhamāṅgirasam | somaḥ pavate janitā matīnāmiti janitramantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ceti pṛṣṭhāni | pari priyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca bṛhadbhāradvājaṃ ca | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punānaḥ ūrmiṇeti bṛhatkātīṣādīye | purojitī vo andhasa iti nānadaṃ cāndhīgavaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca yatpadanidhanam | pra ta āśvinīriti lauśamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | agniṃ va iti saindhukṣitam | vayamu tveti saubharam | imamindreti vasiṣṭhasya priyam | ityukthāni | uddharati ṣoḍaśinam | sūtre'pi tathaiva siddhāntitampauṇḍarīkacaturthe ṣoḍaśī ṣaṣṭhābhāvādityādinā | dve ekaviṃśe savane | triṇavamekam ||
pañcamamyāhnaḥpavamānasya viśvavid yatsoma citramukthyam pra somāso adhanviṣuḥ pra kavirdevavītaye yavaṃ yavaṃ no andhasā yāste dhārā madhuścutaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | agne stomaṃ manāmahe purūruṇā ciddhyasti uttiṣṭhannojasā saha indrāgnī yuvām ityājyāni | arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatraṃ ca yaṇvaṃ ca śākalaṃ ca vārśaṃ ca | soma uṣvāṇeti mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ vāmramagnestriṇidhanaṃ ca | indurvājīti saṃpāntyamiti mādhyaṃdinaḥ | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | indro madāyati bārhadgiram | svādorityeti rāyovājīyamiti pṛṣṭhāni | asāvyaṃśurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca santani caiḍaṃ ca saindhukṣitaṃ gauṣūktaṃ cābhidyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnāmiti cyāvanakrośe | pavasva vājasātaye iti tvāṣṭrīsāma ca yatsvārayoruttaramṛṣabhaśca śākvaraḥ pārthaṃ cāṣṭeḍaśca padastobhaḥ | govitpavasveti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | ā te agna idhīmahīti saṃjayam | indrāya sāma gāyateti saumitram | asāvi soma indra ta iti mahāvaiśvāmitramityukthāni | dve triṇave savane | trayastriṃśamekam | | 1 || iti prathamādipañcamāntānyahani ||
ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ
pṛṣṭhyastomasya ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ | vaidanvatebhya uttaraṃ paya | ṣaḍviṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ soktham | | 2 ||
iti ṣaṣṭhamaharuktam | tasya dve trayastriṃśe savane | ṣaṭatriṃśamekamiti stomaklṛptiḥ | abhyāsaṅgyaḥ ṣaḍaha ityabhyāsaṅgavidhānāt ṣaṭtriṃśaṃ tṛtīyasavanaṃ sokthamiti kalpavacanācca |
