ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ -- asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavasva devavīrati sanā ca soma jeṣi ca taratsamandī dhāvati ete somā asṛkṣateti bahiṣpavamānam | yamagne pṛtsu martyam prati vāṃ sūra udite bhindhi viśvā apa dviṣaḥ yajñasya hi stha ṛtvijaḥ ityājyāni | indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ ceṣovṛdhīyaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca vājadāvaryaśca dārḍhyacyutaṃ | mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍasvāre aukṣṇorandhre | vājajidvaruṇasāma | goṣṭhaṃ ca | ayā pavā pavatveti ihavadvāsiṣṭhamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca | surūpakṛtnumūtaye ityṛṣabhaṃ ca raivatam | ubhe yadindra rodasīti araṇyegeyaṃ ca śyenamiti pṛṣṭhāni | parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca vaidanvatāni trīṇi tṛtīyādyacaturthāntāni | payaśca | sa sunve yo vasūnām taṃ vai sakhāyo madāyeti dīrghaṃ ca kārṇaśravasaṃ ca | somāḥ pavanta indavaḥ iti krauñcaṃ ca yattrayāṇāmādyam | madhuścunnidhanaṃ ca krauñce vāṅnidhanaiḍe | jyotiryajñasyeti marutāṃ dhenvantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | dvādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti sa tisṛbhiḥ so'ṣṭābhiḥ sa ekayā | dvādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ so'ṣṭābhiḥ | dvādaśabhyo hiṃkaroti so'ṣṭābhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiriti ṣaṭtriṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ | triṇavādyāvatsamā iti vacanāt | agne tvaṃ na iti gūrdam | imānukamiti bhadram | pra va indretyudvaṃśaputra ityukthāni | stomavimocanādi pūrvavat | asminnahani saṃsthite prāguttarasyāhnaḥ upakaraṇāt bahubhāṣaṇaṃ svādhyāyādhyayanaṃ ca varjayeyuḥ | sarpirmadhubhyāmṛtvijo bhojayediti ṣaḍahaḥ saṃpūrṇaḥ || 2 ||
iti ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ ||
saptamāṣṭamanavamānyahāni
trayaśchandomā evameva samūḍhāḥ | punaḥ kaṇvarathantaraṃ svaṃ lokameti || 3 ||
atha chandomāstraya evameva samūḍhā iti | yathā chandomavati daśarātre tathetyarthaḥ | punaḥ kaṇvarathantaraṃ svalokametīti dvādaśāhe ṣaṣṭhasaptamayorahnorbārhatayorjāmiprasaṅgāt saptamasya mādhyaṃdine kaṇvarathantaraṃ kalpitam | yathā ca śrutiḥ | jāmi dvādaśāhasyāstīti smāhogradevo rājani bārhataṃ saptamamityādi | chandomavaddaśarātre ṣaṣṭhasya lopājjāmyabhāvādityārambhaṇād bṛhatī yathā syāditi kaṇvarathantarasya loke somasāmetyuktam | iha tu ṣaṣṭhasya sadbhāvājjāmitā syāditi tannivṛttyarthaṃ svasthāne kaṇvarathantaraṃ kāryamityarthaḥ | tatra saptamasyāhno
bahiṣpavamānam ye somāsaḥ parāvati hinvānāso rathā iva asṛgramendavaḥ pathā ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam iti ṛtāvanaṃ vaiśvānaram | pra vo mitrāya gāyata indrāyāhi citrabhāno tamīḍiṣva yo arciṣetyājyāni | aṣṭābhyo hiṃkarotīti viṣṭutiḥ | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca saṃtani caiḍaṃ ca sauparṇaṃ rohitakūlīyam | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti kaṇvarathantaraṃ ca gauṅgavaṃ ca dvinidhanaṃ cāyāsyam | pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa iti vārāhamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca | vayaṃ gha tveti abhinidhanaṃ kāṇvam | na kiṣṭamiti vaikhānasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ tisṛṣu | agnerarko'dhyardheḍaṃ somasāma maukṣaṃmiti sāmatṛcaḥ | api vā gāyatraṃ maukṣaṃ somasāmeti sāmatṛcaḥ | agnerarkastisṛṣu | eṣa sya dhārayā sutaḥ sakhāya ā niṣīdateti śārkaraplavau | purojitī vo andhasa iti ātreyaṃ ca kārtayaśaṃ ca | pra vājyakṣā iti sauhaviṣam | pro ayāsīdityaiḍaṃ yajñasārathyantam | yajñāyajñīya magniṣṭomasāma | ā te vatsa iti vātsam | tvaṃ na indretyaupagavam | yadindracitra ma iha neti vīṅkamityukthāni | sarvaṃ caturviṃśam ||
aṣṭamasyāhnaḥhinvanti sūramusrayaḥ ete somā abhi priyam somaḥ punāno arṣati somā asṛgramindavaḥ utte śuṣmāsa īrate ayāvītī parisrava apaghnan pavate mṛdhaḥ ayā pavasva dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam | viśvebhiragne agnibhiḥ mitraṃ vayaṃ havāmahe tamindraṃ vājayāmasi indre agnā namo bṛhad ityājyāni | adhvaryo adribhiḥ sutam iti gāyatraṃ ca vairūpaṃ cāśurbhāgavaṃ ca mārgīyavaṃ ca saumitraṃ caiṭataṃ ca sākamaśvaṃ ca vilambasauparṇaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca gāyatrapārśvaṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca hārāyaṇaṃ cācchindraṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ ca | uhu vāi śiśumiti vāsipṭhamantyam | dvihiṃkāraṃ prathamāyām | bārhadukthamadhyāsyāyām | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca | yadindra prāgudagiti naipātithaṃ ca | ubhayaṃ śṛṇavaccaneti vaiyaśva ceti pṛṣṭhāni | pavasva deva āyuṣagiti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ surūpaṃ ca bhāsaṃ ca kākṣīvataṃ ca gāyatrīsāmāsitaṃ ca | abhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnām ityaiṣiratraite ekarce | abhī no vājasātamamiti svāraiḍakautse śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca rayiṣṭhaudale ca | pavasva soma mahāntsamudra iti dharma | hāu dharteti aiḍaṃ śārṅgamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | preṣṭhaṃ va ityauśanam | endra no gadhi priyeti sāṃvartam | purāṃ bhinduryuvā kaviriti mārutamityukthāni | sarvaṃ catuścatvāriṃśastomam | tasya catuścatvāriṃśastomasya tisro viṣṭutayaḥ āmnātāḥ | tāsu ājyānāṃ prathamā | pṛṣṭhānāṃ dvitīyā | agniṣṭomasāmnāṃ ca tṛtīyetyekaḥ pakṣaḥ | pakṣāntaramapi dvādaśāhe darśitam ||
navamasyāhnaḥupoṣu jātamapturam eṣa devo amartyaḥ eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā eṣa u sya vṛṣā ratha eṣa vājī hito nṛbhiḥ eṣa kavirabhiṣṭutaḥ sa sutaḥ pītaye pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | upa tvā raṇva saṃdṛśaṃ mitraṃ huve pūtadakṣaṃ mahāṃ indro ya ojasā tā huve yayoridam ityājyāni | pavamānasya jighnata iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛcca surūpaṃ ca hariśrīnidhanaṃ ca saindhukṣitaṃ ca | gatanidhanaṃ bābhravaṃ caiḍānāṃ saṃkṣāra ṛṣabhaśca pavamānaḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam miti pṛṣṭhaṃ tisṛṣu |
kaulmalabarhiṣaṃ prathamāyām | arkapuṣpaṃ dvitīyāyām | dairghaśravasaṃ tṛtīyāyām | devasthānaṃ tisṛṣu | saṃkṛti tisṛṣu | vaiyaśvaṃ prathamāyām | bhargastisṛṣu | ābhīśavaṃ prathamāyām | yaśastisṛṣu | vāsiṣṭhamadhyāsyāyām | hāi uhu vāi akrāniti vāsiṣṭhamantyam | tasya bṛhatyāmaraṇyegeyāni tṛceṣvityādivacanādevaṃ klṛptiḥ | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca | yata indra bhayāmaha iti samantaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | tvaṃ somāsi dhārayuriti gāyatraṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca śāmmadaṃ ca yaddāvasunidhanaṃ ca pratīcīneḍaṃ ca kāśītaṃ hāviṣkṛtaṃ ca | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya pavasva devavītaye iti sauparṇaṃ ca vaiśvamanasaṃ ca | pari tyaṃ haryataṃ harimityāsitottaraṃ prathamāyām | dīrghatamasorko dvitīyāyām | ākūpāraṃ tṛtīyāyām yatsvayoni | nihavayadvāhiṣṭhīyāsitādyasādhrākūpārāṇi tṛceṣu | pavasva somamahe dakṣāyeti vidharma | asāvi somo aruṣo vṛṣā hari riti aiḍaṃ yāmamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | ā ghā ya ityaidhmavāham |
ya eka iditi traikakubham | gāyanti tveti udvaṃśīyamityukthāni | sarvamaṣṭācatvāriṃśam | aṣṭācatvāriṃśasyottarayā maitrāvaruṇasyaivājyaṃ vidadhyāt | prathamayaivāntyāni stotrāṇi | 3 ||
iti saptamaṣṭamanavamānyahāni || 36 ||
daśamamahaḥ
catuṣṭomayoḥ pūrvaḥ || 1 ||
catuṣṭomayoḥ pūrvaṃ daśamamahaḥ | tasya klṛptiḥayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhagaḥ iti tisro'nuṣṭubhaḥ | catasro gāyatrīḥ karoti | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhagom iti triṣa pādeṣu retasyā | vyākhyadrodasī ubhomiti dvitīyā stotrīyā | somāsaḥ kaṇva te pathomiti tṛtīyā | pavamāna śravā dhiyomiti rathantaravarṇā iti catasṛbhiḥ bahiṣpavamānam | agnaā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnī tyājyāni | teṣāmaṣṭistomaścatuḥparyāyaḥ kartavyaḥ | dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti sa prathamayā | dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti sa prathamayā | dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti sa madhyamayā | dvābhyāṃ hiṃkaroti sa uttamayeti viṣṭutiḥ | catuṣṭomayoḥ catuṣparyāyāḥ | tatra catuṣkaṃ dvau prathamāyāṃ paryāyau syātām ityārabhya tathāṣṭini dvikāstutasya paryāyā iti vacanāt | tatra prathamadvitīyau tṛcabhāgasthānayorvidhāya dvitīyasya tṛcabhāgasthānasyottarataḥ pratyagagreṇa kuśādvayena tṛtīyaṃ paryāyaṃ vidadhyāt | tasyottaratastṛcabhāgasthāne caturtham | atha mādhyaṃdinaḥ -- uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti rauravaṃ maidhātithaṃ yaudhājayamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | pra tu dravetyauśanamantyamiti dvādaśaḥ | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca śrāyantīyaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ṣoḍaśāni | ṣoḍaśastomasya viṣṭutiḥ | catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā sa ekayā | catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa ekayā sa dvābhyāṃ sa ekayā | catasṛbhyo hiṃkaroti sa ekayā sa ekayā sa ekayā sa dvābhyāmiti | ṣoḍaśe catvāro dvādaśaparyāyāsteṣāṃ madhyamo sadṛśāviti vacanāt | uttarottarāḥ paryāyāḥ | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale ekarcayoḥ | purojitī vo andhasaḥ iti śyāvāśvāndhīgave | yajñāyajñīyaṃ ca kāvamantyamiti viṃśatyārbhavaḥ | yajñā eṣa taṃ te śrudhī havamiti vāravantīyaṃ caturṣu tṛceṣu ṣaḍbhiḥ ṣaḍbhiḥ stotrīyābhiḥ | caturviṃśamagniṣṭomasāma | caturviṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ ṣadbhyo hiṃkaroti sa dvābhyāṃ sa dvyābhyāṃ sa dvābhyāmiti prathamaḥ paryāyaḥ | evamuttare trayaḥ paryāyāḥ | caturviṃśe catvāraḥ ṣaṭkāḥ paryāyāḥ ityādivacanāt | asminnahani daśamadharmā mānasasaṃyuktebhyo'nye kartavyā iti nidāne pratipāditam | ke punardaśamadharmāḥ | ṛcaṃ sāma dadhikrāvṇa ityanayoruttamapādābhyāsaḥ | āyurme prāṇā iti mantre punarasmāsu dadhmasītyabhyāsaḥ | anye'pi dharmā apratigrahāmetyasminnahani anuṣṭubhaṃ mātrāṃ kṛtvāpakṣīrannityādinoktāḥ mānasasaṃyuktebhyo'nye draṣṭavyāḥ || 1 ||
iti daśamamahaḥ || 37 ||
ekādaśamahaḥ
viśvajidatirātro viśvajidatirātra || 1 ||
ekādaśamaharviśvajidatirātraḥ | tasya trivṛd bahiṣpavamānam | upa tvā jāmayo giro janīyanto nvagravaḥ uta naḥ priyāḥ priyāsu tatsaviturvareṇyam somānāṃ svaraṇaṃ agna āyūṃṣi pavase pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | uttama eva tṛce | itare ekarcāḥ yathāliṅgaṃ devatābhedaḥ | suṣamiddho na āvaha tā naḥ śaktaṃ pārthivasya yuñjanti bradhnamaruṣaṃ carantam tamīḍiṣva yo arciṣetyājyāni | suṣamiccaturṛce | yathāgotramuddhāraḥ | pañcadaśaṃ hoturājyam | saptadaśaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya | ekaviṃśaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ | pañcadaśamacchāvākasya | asya pratneti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca| parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti kāleyaṃ prathamāyām | rathantaraṃ tisṛṣu | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | ayaṃ soma iti pārthamantyamiti saptadaśo mādhyaṃdinaḥ | rathantarastutikāle pṛṣṭhye rathamativaheyur ityādinokto rathaghoṣaḥ kartavyaḥ | ṣaṭpṛṣṭha prayogasadbhāvāt | viśvajiti vairājadharmān kuryāditareṣvapi pṛṣṭheṣviti ācāryamatiriti vacanācca | vairājaṃ ca mahānāmnyaśca vairūpaṃ ca revatyaśceti pṛṣṭhāni | ekaviṃśaṃ hotuḥ pṛṣṭham | triṇavaṃ maitrāvaruṇasya | saptadaśaṃ brāhmaṇācchaṃsinaḥ | ekaviṃśamacchāvākasya | vairājasya stotra upākṛta ityādinā vairājasya dharmā uktāḥ | apassāvakā upa nidhāyetyādinā mahānāmnīnām | upa vājayamānā ityādinā vairūpasya | vāravantīyasya stotre dhenuḥ ityādinā revatīnām | parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphaśrudhye | paryūṣviti vāmadevyam | purojitī vo andhasa iti āndhīgavaṃ yajñāyajñīye | pari pra dhanvāsu dvipadāsu vāravantīyam | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyamiti triṇava ārbhavaḥ | tvamagne yajñānāṃ hoteti vṛhadagniṣṭomasāma trayastriṃśam | bṛhataḥ stotre dundubhimāhanyuḥ | sākamaśvaṃ saubharaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni | triṇavaṃ prathamam | dve ekaviṃśe | indra juṣasveti gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāmaikaviṃśam | āhīnikī rātriḥ pañcadaśī | saṃdhistrivṛt | stomavimocanādyuda vasanīyāntam | āharantikī subrahmaṇyātra na kartavyā uttarārthā''harantikīti vacanāt ||
athāpastambena pauṇḍarīkasya prakārāntareṇāhaḥkalṛptiruktāabhyāsaṅgyaḥ pañcāhaścatvāraśchandomā mahāvrataṃ viśvajit sarvapṛṣṭho'tirātra iti | atra mahāvratavyatiriktaṃ yathā chandomavaddaśarātre tathā kartavyam | mahāvrataṃ tu aikāhikam | atra syurmānasā dharmāḥ | tathā dāśamikāḥ iti kecidācakṣate | tatra nirṇayaṃ brūmaha vayam --

vadanti sāṃvargajitā gautamā iti sūtrataḥ || 1 ||
sāṃvargajitapakṣeṇa vratānte mānasaṃ bhavet |
ye'nye dāśamikā dharmāḥ kāryāḥ pūrvatra te'hani || 2 ||
pakṣāntareṇa pūrve hi dharmāḥ kāryāḥ samānasāḥ |
tatsarparājñādanyeṣāṃ dharmāṇāṃ karaṇaṃ vrate || 3 ||

parasmin pauṇḍarīke tu na dharmā nāpi mānasam || 4 ||
iti | atha vyūḍho navarātro mahāvrataṃ vaiśvānaraḥ ityāśvalāyanoktaḥ pauṇḍarīkaḥ | tatra vyūḍho navarātro vyūḍhadvādaśāhavat kartavyaḥ | viśeṣastūcyate | audalamāsitaṃ tvāṣṭrīsāmāvikṛtaṃ caturthamahastvāṣṭrīsāma krauñcamete svarāḥ pṛṣṭhyasya gaurīvitaloke | ātreyaṃ saptamasyāhnaḥ svāraṃ kautsamaṣṭamasya | navamasyāhraḥpari tyaṃ haryataṃ harimiti gaurīvitaloke | āsitamekasyām | dīrghatamaso'rkaḥ ekasyām | ākūpāramekasyām yatsvayoni kartavyam | evaṃ mādhyaṃdināntyaṃ sāmarājameva niyatam | na tu dīrghatamaso'rkaḥ | bṛhatyāṃ cāsau nānukalpanīyo'saṃcārāya | mahāvratamaikāhikaṃ vaiśvānaraśabdena jyotiṣṭomo'tirātra ucyate | tasyāhīnikī rātriḥ || 4 ||

sam̐sadamatirātraḥ pṛṣṭhyastomaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ prathamasyāhna eṣapratnānurūpo gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhyanvahamavikḷptaṃ caturthamahaḥ pañcamasyahna ākūpārasya loke tvāṣṭrīsāma yaddvyanutodaṃ 935
pavasvendo vṛṣā suta ete asṛgramindava uta no goṣaṇiṃ dhiyam̐ śaśamānasya vā nara upa naḥ sūnavo giraḥ pra svānaso rathā ivāsṛgramindavaḥ pathā pavamānasya te vayaṃ pavamānasya te kave svānyājyāni pavamānasya jighnata iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cai ḍaṃ ca sauparṇa m̐! rohitakūlīyaṃ ca saṃtani punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca kanvarathaṃtaraṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ ca yaudhājayam̐ svamantyam̐ svāni pṛṣṭhāni pavamāno ajījanaditi gāyatra sam̐hite jarābodhīyaṃ ca sauśravasaṃ ca sve uṣṇikkakubhāvabhī no vājasātamamiti gaurīvitaṃ ca kārtayaśaṃ ca śyāvāśvā ndhīgave añjata iti kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma 94
upāsmai gāyatā nara upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ babhrave nu svatavasa ete somā abhi priyam̐ somaḥ punāno arṣati pavamānasya te kave 'gna ā yahi vītaya ityājyāni pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ cāśvaṃ ca somasāma cāśu ca bhārgavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca hārāyaṇaṃ ca yaudhājayamauśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayati gāyatra sam̐hite kākṣīvataṃ ca bhāsaṃ ca pavasvendramaccheti saphākṣāre pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca padanidhanaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyam audala gautame kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma 95
eṣa u sya vṛṣā ratha eṣa vāji hito nṛbhiriti purastātparyāsasya ṣaḍṛce yauktāśvāduttare surūpaṃ carṣabhaśca pāvamāna āyāsyābhyāṃ pūrve pṛṣṭhakaulmalabarhiṣe vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyameṣa sya dhārayā suta pavasva devavītaya iti śaṅkusujñāne gaurīvitāduttare yadvāhiṣṭhīyā site samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā dvādaśāhasya 961

kḷptamā nānābrahmasāmabhyaḥ 968
nānābrahmasāmnāmatirātrastrayaḥ pañcāhāḥ pañcāhakḷptā nānābrahmasāmānaḥ prathamasya pañcāhasya yathālokaṃ bṛhadrathaṃtare naudhasam̐ śyaitaṃ mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ 18
traiśokamubhayam̐ śṛṇavacca na iti vāśametāni brahmasāmāni dvitīyasya pañcāhasyānyonyasya lokaṃ bṛhadrathaṃtare tvamindra pratūrtiṣvityabhīvartastvāmidā hyo nara iti mānavaṃ yatpṛṣṭhye chandasyaṃ pañcame'hani ka īṃ veda sute sacetyāṣkāraṇidhanaṃ kāṇvam̐ svāsu yauktasrucam̐ svāsu śrāyantīyametāni brahmasāmāni tṛtīyasya pañcāhasya yathālokaṃ bṛhadrathaṃtare śagdhyū ṣu śacipata iti mānavaṃ yacchandasyaṃ nityaṃ pañcame'hani vayamenamidā hya iti vāsiṣṭham ā tvā sahasramā śatamiti bhāradvājaṃ yo rājā carṣaṇīnāmiti janitraṃ tvamindra yaśa asītīndrasya yaśa etāni brahmasāmāni 971
viśvajidatirātrastasya gaurīvitasya loke nānadamagnirmūrdhā divaḥ kakuditi satrāsāhīyaṃ traikakubhaṃ nārmedhaṃ gaurīvitam̐ ṣoḍaśisāma rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ 972
athaiṣa eva pañcāha āvṛtta āvṛttasya pañcāhasyānyonyasya lokaṃ bṛhadrathaṃtare pibā sutasya rasina iti jamadagnerabhīvarto yadindra prāgapāgudagiti naipātithamā mandrairindra haribhirityabhinidhanaṃ kāṇvaṃ yathā gauro apākṛtamiti gautamasya manājyaṃ tvamaṅga pra śam̐siṣamiti paurumīḍhametāni brahmasāmāni 973
