uttamasya trayastrim̐śadrātrasyātirātrastrayaḥ pañcāhāḥ pañcāhakḷptāḥ śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmāno yathālokaṃbṛhadrathaṃtarā yathā vā nānābrahmasāmasu kḷpto viśvajidyo nānābrahmasāmasvathaita eva pañcāhā āvṛttā āvṛttānāṃ pañcāhānāṃ prathamasya pañcāhasya prathamasyāhno'bhi somāsa āyava iti mānavamekasyāṃ tasyāmevānūpaṃ bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambhamekasyāṃ maidhātithamekasyāṃ vāmraṃ tisṛṣvagnestriṇidhanamadhyāsyāyāṃ samānamitaraṃ 976
kḷptamā vidhṛtibhyaḥ 977
vidhṛtīnāṃ jyotiṣṭomo'tirātro'ṣoḍaśikaḥ kautsasya loka udvam̐śīyam ā tvā viśantvindava ityetāsu jarābodha tad viviḍḍhi prati ṣyā sūnarī janyeṣo uṣā apūrvyetyetāsu gāyatrīṣu jarābodhīyam̐ saṃdhiṣāmā 981
atha yadeva kusurubindasya rāthaṃtaraṃ trivṛdahastadetattasya gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi yadbārhataṃ trivṛdahastaduttaraṃ pavamānasya te kava iti paryāso yauktāśvasya loke hāviṣmataṃ mādhucchandasasya kāleyam̐ hāviṣmatasya maukṣaṃ gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi sākamaśvam̐ saubharamāṣṭādam̐ṣṭramityukthāni samānamitaraṃ yā pūrvā sottarā tasyāḥ pramam̐hiṣṭhīyam̐ hārivarṇamudvam̐śīyaṃ tairaścyaṃ vetyukthāni 982
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān kautsasya loka udvam̐śīyamā tvā viśantvindava ityetāsvagne vājasya gomata uṣastaccitramābharāśvina vartirasmadetyetāsūṣṇikṣu śrudhyam̐ saṃdhiṣāmā 983
atha yadeva kusurubindasya rāthaṃtaraṃ pañcadaśamahastadetat tasya gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi yadbārhataṃ pañcadaśaṃ taduttaraṃ pavamānasya te kava iti paryāso mādhucchandasasya loke kāleyaṃ gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhyetābhyāmeva viparyāsamā daśamādahno daśamasyāhna āṣṭādam̐ṣṭrasya loke nārmedhaṃ pratyasmai pipīṣata iti nānadam̐ ṣoḍaśisāma 984
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimānavikḷpto 985
atha yadeva kusurubindasya rāthaṃtaram̐ saptadaśamahastadetattasya maidhātithasya loke bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambham̐ rauravasya maidhātithamagnerarkasya sam̐hitaṃ gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi yadbārhatam̐ saptadaśaṃ taduttaraṃ pavamānasya te kava iti paryāso mādhucchandasasya loke kāleyam̐ svāśirāmarkasya maukṣaṃ gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhyetābhyāmeva viparyāsamā dvādaśādahna uddharatyuttarebhyo vārkajambhaṃ yathālokaṃ maidhātitharaurave dvādaśasyāhna udvam̐śīyāntānyukthāni 986
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān kautsasya loka udvam̐śīyamā tvā viśantvindava ityetātvagniṃ taṃ manye yo vasurmahe no adya bodhaya prati priyatamam̐ rathamityetāsu paṅktiṣu rāyovājīyam̐ saṃdhiṣāma 987
pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā vyūḍhastveva 991
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimānauśanasyarkṣu vaiśvajyotiṣamantyaṃ yatpra gāyatetyabodhyagniḥ samidhā janānāmidam̐ śreṣṭhaṃ jyotiṣāṃ jyotirāgādabhātyagniruṣasāmanīkamityetāsu triṣṭupsvauśanam̐ saṃdhiṣāma 992
babhrave nu svatavasa iti purastātparyasasya ṣaḍṛcamāmahīyavāduttaramāśu bhārgavam̐ rauravayaudhājaye antarā maidhātitham̐ svāsu saphapauṣkale samānamitarametasyaiva rāthaṃtareṇa pañcadaśena pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇaya iti purastātparyāsasya ṣaḍṛcam̐ hāviṣmatāduttaram̐ svāram̐ sauparṇam āyāsye antarā dairghaśravasaṃ tvam̐ hyaṅga daivya pavasva devavītaya iti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye samānamitarametasyaiva barhātena pañcadaśenaitābhyāmeva viparyāsamā dvādaśādahno navamasyāhnaḥ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārko daśamasyāhno yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāṃ carka ekādaśasyābhi priyāṇīti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyo dvādaśasyāhno 'ntarotsedhaniṣedhau dairghaśravasaṃ tasyaivāhna udvaṃśīyāntānyukthāni 993
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātro'ṣoḍaśikaḥ kāvasyarkṣu vāsiṣṭhamantyaṃ yadeṣa prakośa iti janasya gopā ajaniṣṭa jāgṛviretā u tyā uṣasaḥ ketumakratābodhyagnirjma udeti sūrya ityetāsu jagatīṣu kāvam̐ saṃdhiṣāma 994
yamātirātrāsu goṣṭomasya dairghaśravasasya loke kaṇvarathaṃtaraṃ bārhadukthasya dairghaśravasaṃ gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhyasodaśiko'tirātraḥ āyuṣṭomasya maidhātithasya loke bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambham̐ rauravasya maidhātithaṃ gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi hārivarṇasyarkṣu saubharamaṣoḍaśiko'tirātro 'bhijito yathā pūrdaśarātra evaṃ bṛhatī gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi hārivarṇasyarkṣu saubharam̐ ṣoḍaśimānatirātraḥ kḷpto viśvajitkḷptaḥ sarvastomo navasaptadaśasyābhi somāsa āyava iti janitraṃ tisṛṣu rauravamekasyāṃ bṛhannidhanamvārkajambhamekasyāṃ maidhātithamekasyāṃ yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyāṃ gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhyāṣoḍaśiko'tirātraḥ 9101
kḷptā āñjanābhyañjanāḥ 9102
saṃvatsarakḷptāḥ saṃvatsarasaṃmitā ekaṣaṣṭirātraṃ ca 9103
vidhṛtibhiḥ kḷptāḥ savituḥ kakubho rāthaṃtareṇāhnā trivṛtaḥ pratipadyate bārhatena pañcadaśān rāthaṃtareṇa saptadaśān bārhatenaikavim̐śān yatrāgniṣṭomo bārhatamaharyauktāśvaṃ madhyaṃdine hāviṣmatamārbhava uddharatyarkaṃ yo vidhṛtiṣu samānamitaraṃ 9104
kḷptamā madhyepṛṣṭhyādā madhyepṛṣṭhyāt 9105
vivṛti
navamo'dhyāyaḥ
satrāṇi
dvādaśarātraṃ satram
atha trayoviṃśamukhairadhyāyairbrāhmaṇe tribhiḥ |
satrāṇyuktāni tānyatra kalpānte navamādibhiḥ ||
sūtrakāreṇa dīkṣākrayaprasavotthānāni sarvasatreṣu pūrvapakṣe upapādayeyur ityādinā teṣāṃ dīkṣākālādikamuktam | nidānaṃ tu athātaḥ satrāṇi | teṣāṃ dvādaśāhaḥ prathamaḥ ityādi | tatra dvādaśāhasya brāhmaṇoktāṃ klṛptimupajīvya viśeṣamāha -- 
dvādaśāhasya ṣoḍaśimantāvatirātrau || 1||
iti | sa ca vyūḍhasamūḍharūpatvena dvividhaḥ san satrāhīnātmakatvena punardvaividhyāccaturvidha ityuktam | tatra vyūḍhaḥ satrātmakaḥ purastāduktaḥ | ahīnātmakastu gaurīvitasvaro nānāsvaro veti pakṣadvayam | saṃbhave gaurīvitasvarasya sāmakalpe viśeṣo nāstīti karmakalpake viśeṣaḥ prāgevoktaḥ| nānāsvarasya tu pauṇḍarīkavyūḍhanavarātravat svaraklṛptiḥ| ahīnanyāyādanyat sarvaṃ satrabhūtavyūḍhadvādaśāhavat| samūḍhaśca dvādaśāho na kalpakāreṇokta iti nyāyena kalpanīyaḥ | tatra pṛṣṭhyaṣaḍahasya samūḍhaklṛptiḥ gavāmayanoktā | chandomānāṃ chandomavaddaśarātroktā| prathamasyāhno'sya pratnā
navarcameva bahiṣpavamānam | navāhayogasya vidyamānatvāt | dvitīyasyāhno vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthaṃ mādhyaṃdināntyam | tṛtīye'hni na viśeṣaḥ | caturthasyāhnaḥ pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi iti stotrīyānurūpau| agnirvṛtrāṇi iti hoturājyam | somaḥ pavata iti janitraṃ mādhyaṃdināntyam | pra ta āśvinīriti lauśamārbhavāntyam | pañcamasyāhnaḥ pavamānasya viśvajit yatsoma citramukthyam iti stotrīyānurūpau | agne stomaṃ manāmahe iti hoturājyam | indurvājīti sampāntyāntyaṃ mādhyaṃdinasya | pārthasya loke tvāṣṭrīsāma yadyanutodam | govitpavasveti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśamārbhavāntyam | ṣaṣṭhasyāhno'sṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipa iti stotrīyānurūpau | yamagne pṛtsu martyamiti hoturājyam | ayā pavā pavasvainā vasūnītīhavadvāsiṣṭhaṃ mādhyaṃdināntyam | jyotiryajñasyeti marutāṃ dhenvārbhavāntyam | saptamasyāhno ye somāsaḥ parāvatī ti pratipat | ṛtāvanaṃ vaiśvānaramiti hoturājyam | pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa iti pārthaṃ mādhyaṃdināntyam | pro ayāsīditi yajñasārathyārbhavāntyam | kaṇvarathantaraṃ svasthānasthameva | somasāmakalpanasya jāmiprayuktatvāt iha ca jāmisadbhāvāt | svarāntaklṛptiścāhīnaprayuktetyātreyaṃ nātra
satrāṇidvādaśarātram 
kāryam | aṣṭamasyāhno hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ iti pratipat | viśvebhiragne agnibhiriti hoturājyam | uhu vāi śiśumiti vāsiṣṭhaṃ mādhyaṃdināntyam | hāu dharteti śārṅgamārbhavāntyam | navamasyāhnaḥ| upoṣu jātamapturam iti pratipat | upa tvā raṇvasadṛśamiti hoturājyam | pṛṣṭhasyottare dīrghatamaso'rko māṇḍavaṃ caiḍamiti sāmatṛcaḥ kāryaḥ| sāmarājāntyapakṣe'pi dīrghatamaso'rko'syānugrahāya sa uktaḥ | hāu uhu vāi akrāniti vāsiṣṭhamantyam | asāvi somo aruṣo vṛṣā harirityaiḍaṃ yāmamārbhavāntyam| daśamasyāhnaḥ utsedhaniṣedhayormadhye maidhātitham | ājāgṛvirityauśanaṃ mādhyaṃdināntyam | svāsu rathantaravāmadevye | paryūṣviti gaurīvitamekasyām | śyāvāśvamekasyām | āndhīgavaṃ tisṛṣu | ityanuṣṭubhaḥ klṛptiḥ | tathaiva hi chandomavaddaśarātrasya daśamamatidiśya tasya sadṛśaprayuktaṃ gaurīvitaṃ na kalpanīyaṃ iti vakṣyati | kecit ājāgṛviriti gaurīvitaṃ mādhyaṃdināntyamicchanto'nuṣṭubhi na vikāramicchanti | tadayuktam | mādhyaṃdine gaurīvitakalpasyāpyūhaprayuktatvāt samūhaprayuktasyauśanasya lopaprasaṅgācca | tathā ca dvādaśāhe nidānam -- yajñāyajñīyagaurīvitarathantaravāmadevyānyevaṃyuktāni sāmavyūha ityācakṣate iti chandomavaddaśarātre ca samāpadyante | daśame'hani sāmāni samūhediti | tasmādanuṣṭubho yathoktā klṛptiḥ kartavyā| pari pra dhanveti sauhaviṣasthāne vājajit | sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | itaratsarvaṃ satrabhūtavyūḍhadvādaśāhavat iti satrabhūtaḥ samūḍha uktaḥ | ahīnabhūtasya samūḍhasya pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaha ṛtuṣaḍahavat kāryaḥ | prathamasyāhno'sya pratnānavarcameva bahiṣpavamānam | chandomāstu yathā chandomavaddaśarātre tathaiva vikṛtāḥ kartavyā iti dvādaśāhavidhā uktāḥ ||
iti dvādaśarātraṃ satraṃ samāptam||1||
trayodaśa rātre dve satre
tatra prathamam
atha trayodaśarātre dve satre | atirātraḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ sarvastomo'tirātraścatvāraśchandomā atirātra ityanuvākena pūrvaṃ trayodaśarātramuktam | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
satrāṇicaturdaśarātrāṇi
trayodaśarātre prathamasya chandomasya kaṇvarathantarasya loke somasāma punānaḥ soma dhārayetyetat || 1 ||
iti | ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ prathamasya chandomasya sarvastomo'tirātreṇa vyavadhānāt jāmyabhāvāt somasāma vihitam | anyat sarvaṃ satrabhūtavyūḍhadvādaśāhavat | sarvastomo'tirātrastvahīnoktaḥ | tasya sātrikī rātriḥ satreṣu prāyaṇīyodayanīyayoratirātrayoryatra ṣoḍaśī nādiśyate na ca pratiṣidhyate tatra tasya prakṛtivadyathākāmī| yatra tu vidhirniṣedho vā vidyate tatra tau niyamārthāviti sūtre nirṇayaḥ ||1||
iti prathamaṃ trayodaśarātram || 2 ||
dvitīyaṃ trayodaśarātram
atirātro dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśceti dvitīyaṃ trayodaśarātramuktam | tasya sarvaṃ satrabhūtavyūḍhadvādaśāhavat| viśeṣamāha -- 
sāṃvatsarikaṃ vrataṃ satreṣu ||1||
iti || 1||
iti dvitīyaṃ trayodaśarātram ||3||
caturdaśarātrāṇi
atha caturdaśarātrāṇi trīṇi | atirātraḥ pṛṣṭhayaḥṣaḍahaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahastrayastriṃśārambhaṇe'tirātraḥ ityanuvākena prathamaṃ caturdaśarātram | pṛṣṭhyāvanulomapratilomau samūḍhau gavāmayanavat | tatra prathamasya pṛṣṭhasya viśeṣamāha -- 
yatrātirātrāt pṛṣṭhyamupayanti prathamasyāho'sya pratnānurūpo yatra vā trikakudrukebhyaḥ || 2 ||
iti | anurūpo bahiṣpavamānasya dvitīyastṛcaḥ | upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ asya pratnāmanudyutam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānamityarthaḥ| atirātrasya pṛṣṭhyastrikadrukādyayoścāhnoḥ rāthantaratvāt ajāmyarthaṃ pratnavatyāḥ asyapratnāyāḥ kalpanam | pūrvasmin trayodaśarātre navāhayogasadbhāvādasya pratnānavarcaṃ sthitameveti siddhamajāmitvam | tathā ca sūtram -- pratnāvaccādyabahiṣpavamānaṃ pareṇa tvetadasya pratnānurūpa ityāha dṛṣṭaṃ ca daśamārthamapyavacchede | navāhayogācceti | atra trayastriṃśayorahnoḥ saṃnipāte'pyajāmitvaṃ nidāne pratipāditam | atha khalvatra trayastriṃśe ahanī saṃnipatataḥ kenājāmītyahna eva sāmānyādapi vā kaṇvarathantaramanukalpayet | pūrvasyāhna ārbhava uttarasya vā madhyaṃdine iti |
iti prathamaṃ caturdaśarātram || 4 ||
dvitīyaṃ caturdaśarātram
atirātro jyotirgaurāyustryahaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaha āyurgaurjyotiratirātraḥ iti dvitīyaṃ caturdaśarātramuktam | tatra kalpaḥ 
satrāṇi -- caturdaśarātrāṇi yatra jyotirgaurāyustryaha aikāhikāste gaurīvitasvarāḥ || 1 ||
iti | atra tryahagrahaṇaṃ jyotirgaurāyustryaha ityetenaiva saṃniveśena yatra jyotirādayo vidhīyante tatraivāsya vacanasya pravṛttiryathā syāt | yatra tu jyotirgaurāyurgaurāyuḥ pañcāha ityādi prakaraṇāntareṇa saṃniveśastatra mā bhūdityevamartham ||1||
ata evāyurgaurjyotiratirātra iti codanāyāṃ syādityāha -- 
ete evāvṛttā yatrāyurgaurjyotiratirātraḥ || 2 ||
iti| atrāpyatirātragrahaṇaṃ saṃniveśavivakṣāpratipādanārtham | atra yadyapi atirātrādrāthantarādrāthantaraṃ jyotirupayanti tathāpi jyotiṣaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti pratnavatī bṛhatītyajāmi | pṛṣṭhayaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ samūḍho gavāmayanavat | tasya prathamasyāhno'sya pratnāmanudyutamityanurūpaḥ | yatra vā trikadrukebhya ityuktatvāt || 2||
iti dvitīyaṃ caturdaśarātram ||5||
tṛtīyaṃ caturdaśarātram
atirātro gauścāyuśca dve ahanī dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātra iti tṛtīyaṃ caturdaśarātramuktam | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
ete eva goāyuṣī rātrisatreṣvanyatra yamātirātrābhyaśca
saṃvatsarasaṃmitābhyaśca || 1 ||
iti | ete eveti aikāhike gaurīvitasvare ityarthaḥ| trayodaśarātraprabhṛtiśatarātraparyantāni satrāṇi rātrisatrāṇītyucyante | rātriśabdena coditatvāt | anyat sarvaṃ vyūḍhadvādaśāhavat || 1||
iti tṛtīyaṃ caturdaśarātram || 6||
caturdaśarātrāṇi samāptāni |
pañcadaśarātrāṇi
atha pañcadaśarātrāṇi catvāri | atirātraḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍaho mahāvrataṃ pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍahastrayastriṃśārambhaṇo'tirātraḥ iti prathamaṃ pañcadaśarātram | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
klṛptaṃ prathamaṃ pañcadaśarātram ||1||
iti | pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍahāvanulomapratilomau samūḍhau gavāmayanavat | prathamasya ṣaḍahasya prathamasyāhno'sya pratnāmanurūpaḥ | sāṃvatsarikaṃ vratam ||1||
iti prathamaṃ pañcadaśarātram || 7 ||
dvitīyaṃ pañcadaśarātram
atirātrastrivṛdagniṣṭomo jyotirgaurāyustryahaḥ | pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍaha āyurgaurjyotiratirātraḥ iti dvitīyaṃ pañca
satrāṇipañcadaśarātrāṇi daśarātram | ūrdhvamatirātrādāgneyyekā dvitīye pañcadaśarātre dve veti subrahmaṇyāyāṃ viśeṣa uktaḥ| tasya kalpaḥ -- 
dvitīyasya pañcadaśarātrasyātirātraḥ trivṛdagniṣṭudagniṣṭomo yasya vāyavyāsvagniṣṭomasāma | tasyāgniḥ pratnena janmaneti hoturājyam |
gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi || 1||
iti | rāthantarayoratirātrāgniṣṭutorajāmyarthamagniḥ pratnenetyetadvihitam | trikadrukā anulomā āvṛttāścaikāhikā gaurīvitasvarāḥ| pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ samūḍhaḥ prathamasyāhno'sya pratnāmanudyutamiti anurūpaḥ || 1||
iti dvitīyaṃ pañcadaśarātram || 8||
tṛtīyaṃ pañcadaśarātram
trivṛdagniṣṭomo jyotirgaurāyustryaho dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ ityanuvākena tṛtīyaṃ pañcadaśarātramuktam | trikadrukebhyaḥ prāk sarvā eva subrahmaṇyā āgneyyaḥ| sarvāḥ prāk trikadrukebhyastṛtīye iti vacanāt | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
eṣa evottarāsu | tasya svānyājyāni || 1 ||
uttarāsu rātriṣu tṛtīyeyaṃ pañcadaśarātre pūrvokta eva trivṛdagniṣṭudagniṣṭomaḥ | tasya agna āyāhītyādīnyevājyāni| atirātrasyādyasyābhāvāt | jāmyabhāvādityarthaḥ ||1||
iti tṛtīyaṃ pañcadaśarātram || 9 ||
caturthaṃ pañcadaśarātram
klṛptamā saptadaśarātrāt || 1||
iti | jyotirgaurāyuraikāhikā gaurīvitasvarāḥ | vyūḍho dvādaśāho'tirātrikaḥ | atirātro jyotirgaurāyustryaho dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātra iti caturthaṃ pañcadaśarātraṃ | vyūḍhadvādaśāhavat | jyotirgaurāyurityaikāhikā gaurīvitasvarāḥ || 1 ||
iti caturthaṃ pañcadaśarātram || 10||
ṣoḍaśarātram
etā eva samahāvratāḥ ityanuvākena ṣoḍaśarātramuktam | atirātro jyotirgaurāyustryaho dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśceti ṣoḍaśarātram | sāṃvatsarikavratamiti viśeṣaḥ pūrvasmātsatrāt ||
iti ṣoḍaśarātram ||
saptadaśarātram
atirātro jyotirgaurāyurgaurāyuḥ pañcāho dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ iti saptadaśarātramuktam | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
saptadaśarātre catvāryābhiplavikānyahāni śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmāni pauṣkala śrudhyauṣṇihāni | prathamasyāhna eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ ||1||
satrāṇi -- saptadaśarātram iti| jyotirādipaccāho gavāmayanikābhiplavapañcāhavat kartavyaḥ | tatrāditaścaturṇāmahnāmayaṃ viśeṣa uktaḥ | tadyathā prathamasyāhnaḥ -- eṣa pratnena janmanati tṛco'nurūpaḥ| atra nidānam -- prathamasyāhna eṣa pratnānurūpa ityajāmyartho vyāvṛttaye pṛṣṭhyābhiplavayoriti | naudhasaṃ brahmasāma | pauṣkalamuṣṇihi| dvitīyasyāhnaḥ -- śyaitaṃ brahmasāma| śrudhyamuṣṇihi | tṛtīyasyāhno naudhasaṃ brahmasāma| pauṣkalamuṣṇihi | caturthasyāhnaḥ śyaitaṃ brahmasāma| śrudhyamauṣṇihamiti| sarvatra śyaitanaudhase svāsveva | svāsu tvāvartanīti vacanāt ||1||
pañcamasyāhno viśeṣamāha -- 
pañcamasyāhno dve savane | ṣaṣṭhasya tṛtīyasavanam | tasya naudhasaṃ brahmasāma | śrudhyamuṣṇihi | yajiṣṭhaṃ tvā vavṛmahe ityaidhmavāhaṃ traikakubhaṃ nārmedham | aidhmavāham sauśravasaṃ mārutam iti vā ||2||
ityukthāni ||
āyuṣo dve savane | jyotiṣastṛtīyasavanaṃ pañcamasyāhnaḥ | tasya naudhasaṃ brahmasāma | śrudhyamuṣṇihi | yajiṣṭhaṃ tvā vavṛmaha ityaidhmavāhaṃ traikakubhaṃ nārmedhamityukthāni | aidhmavāhaṃ sauśravasaṃ mārutamiti vā | atra nidānam -- pañcame'hani ṣaṣṭhasyāhnastṛtīyasavanaṃ pratyāharati | ṣaḍahāvilopāya | sakala ? sa tveva stomo yaḥ pañcamasya sā saṃstheti | evaṃ pañcāha uktaḥ | anyatsarvaṃ vyūḍhadvādaśāhavat | atirātrau saṣoḍaśikāvaṣoḍaśikau vā || 2 ||
iti saptadaśarātraṃ samāptam || 11||
aṣṭādaśarātram
atirātro'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ ityaṣṭādaśarātram uktam 
yatrātirātrād abhiplavamupayanti prathamasyāhna eṣa pratnānurūpo yatra
vā trikadrukebhyaḥ | yatrābhiplavo rātrisatreṣu śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāma 
pauṣkala śrudhyauṣṇiho'nyatra saṃvatsarasaṃmitābhyaḥ klṛptamāviṃśatirātrāt ||1||
iti abhiplavasya prathamasyāhnaḥ eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ | naudhasaśyaite brahmasāmanī vyatyāsam | pauṣkalaśrudhye cauṣṇihe | anyatsarvaṃ yathā gavāmayane ||1||
iti aṣṭādaśarātram || 12||
ekonaviṃśarātram
etā eva samahāvratā etābhirvai vāyuḥ ityanuvākenaikonaviṃśatirātramuktam | atirātro'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho
satrāṇi -- viṃśatirātram
dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraścetyahaḥklṛptiḥ | sāṃvatsarikaṃ vrataṃ pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ ||
iti ekonaviṃśarātram ||13 ||
viṃśatirātram
atirātro'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho'bhijicca viśvajicca dve ahanī dvādaśāhasya dvādaśāhānyatirātraḥ iti viṃśatirātram | uktāvatirātrau | abhiplavaśyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmapauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ prathamasyāhnaḥ | eṣa pratnānurūpa iti viśeṣaḥ ||
viṃśatirātre sāṃvatsarikau viśvajidabhijitau abhitaḥ | yathā pūrdaśarātra
evaṃ bṛhatī || 1||
iti | pūrdaśarātre hyuktaṃ tasyābhijitaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayetyabhīvartastisṛṣu | rauravamekasyām | kaṇvarathantaramekasyām | maidhātithamekasyām | yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣvityādi | atrābhiplavikasya ṣaṣṭhasyāhno'bhijitaśca bārhatatvājjāmitā mā bhūdityabhijito madhyaṃdine kaṇvarathantarasyānukalpanam | gavāmayanikamitaratsarvam || 1||
iti viṃśatirātram ||14||
ekaviṃśatirātram
prathamam
atirātro'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho'tirātro'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho'tirātraḥ iti pūrvamekaviṃśatirātramuktam | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
pūrvasyaikaviṃśatirātrasyāṣoḍaśikāvatirātrāvabhitaḥ | ṣoḍaśimān madhye | sarve vā ṣoḍaśikāḥ | sarve tveva ṣoḍaśimantaḥ ||1||
iti viśeṣa uktaḥ | abhiplavāḥ śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānaḥ pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ prathamayorabhiplavayoḥ| prathamasyāhnaḥ eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ | uttamo'bhiplavo'nuloma iti keṣāṃcinmatam | pratiloma ityanyeṣām | taduktam -- pūrvasminnekaviṃśatirātre'bhiplavaṃ tṛtīyamanulomaṃ śaucivṛkṣiḥ | na hyāvṛttirvijñāyate| pratilomaṃ śāṇḍilyaḥ| dvāvabhiplavāviti vacanāt ||1||
iti prathamamekaviṃśatirātram || 15 ||
uttaramekaviṃśatirātram
atirātraḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍahastrayaḥ svarasāmāno divākīrtyamahastrayaḥ
svarasāmānaḥ pṛṣṭhayaḥṣaḍahastrayastriṃśārambhaṇo'tirātra 
iti uttaramekaviṃśatirātram| tasya kalpaḥ
uttarasyaikaviṃśatirātrasya saṃvatsaraklṛptānyahāni ||1||
satrāṇi -- dvāviṃśatirātram tatra viśeṣamāha -- 
anuklṛptābhīvartakāleyaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ || 2 ||
iti | pṛṣṭhyasya sarvā bṛhatyo dvādaśāhavat kāryāḥ | na tu saṃvatsaravadityarthaḥ || 2|| svarasāmnāṃ viśeṣamāha
śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānaḥ svarasāmānaḥ paupkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ || 3 ||
iti ||3||
viṣuvato viśeṣamāha -- 
viṣuvataḥ sāmatṛcasya loke ābhīśavam | śrāyanta iva sūryam iti vikarṇam || 4||
samānamitaram || 5 ||
iti ||5||
iti uttaramekaviṃśatirātram || 16 ||
ekaviṃśatirātre samāpte||
dvāviṃśatirātram
atirātro jyotirgaurāyustryaho'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca iti dvāviṃśatirātram | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
klṛptamā saṃsadbhyaḥ || 1||
iti | atirātrau sātrikau | trikadrukā aikāhikā gaurīvitasvarā abhiplavaḥ śyaitanaudhasaṃ brahmasāma | pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ | prathamasyāhnaḥ eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ| yatrātirātrādabhiplavamupayanti | prathamasyāhna eṣa pratnānurūpo yatra vā trikadrukebhya ityuktatvāt | anyat sarvaṃ sāṃvatsarikam || 1||
iti dvāviṃśatirātram || 17 ||
trayoviṃśatirātram
atirātro jyotirgaurāyurgaurāyuḥ pañcāho'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ iti trayoviṃśatirātram | saptadaśarātraḥ pañcāhavat pañcāhaḥ kartavyaḥ | itarat sarvaṃ pūrvavat kartavyam ||
iti trayoviṃśatirātram ||
caturviṃśatirātraṃ pūrvaṃ saṃsadāmayanākhyam
prathamādisaptamāntānyahāni
atha de caturviṃśatirātre | atirātraḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ stomaḥ ṣaḍahastrayastriṃśamaharaniruktamupahavyasya tantre klṛptaṃ tasya kaṇvarathantaraṃ mādhyaṃdine'tha trayastriṃśaṃ niruktaṃ triṇavaṃ dve ekaviṃśe triṇavaṃ trayastriṃśamaharniruktaṃ trayastriṃśamaharaniruktaṃ pṛṣṭhyaḥ stomaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ pratyaṅ trivṛdaharaniruktaṃ jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ityanuvākena saṃsadākhyaṃ caturviṃśatirātramuktam | atra kalpaḥ -- 
saṃsadāmatirātraḥ pṛṣṭhyastoma ṣaḍahaḥ || 1 ||
iti | saṃsadāmiti bahuvacanaṃ rātrīṇāṃ viśeṣyatvāt | atirātraḥ prathamamahaḥ| atha pṛṣṭhyaḥ stomaḥ ṣaḍaho'nahīnoktaḥ ||1||
tasyātra viśeṣamāha -- 
prathamasyāhna eṣa pratnānurūpo gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhyanvaham | aviklṛptaṃ
satrāṇi -- caturviṃśatirātre aṣṭamamahaḥ caturthamahaḥ | pañcamasyāhna ākūpārasya loke tvāṣṭrīsāma yad dvyanutodam ||2|| iti | aviklṛptaṃ caturthamahariti gaurīvitaṃ nānadasthāne na kāryamityarthaḥ | trayastriṃśamaharaniruktamupahavyasya tantre klṛptam | tasya kaṇvarathantaraṃ madhyaṃdina ityuktam || 2 ||
iti prathamādisaptamāntānyahāni ||18||
aṣṭamamahaḥ
aṣṭamamaharāha -- 
pavasvendo vṛṣā suta ete asṛgramindava uta no goṣaṇiṃ dhiyam śaśamānasya vā naraḥ upa naḥ sūnavo giraḥ pra svānāso rathā iva asṛgramindavaḥ pathā pavamānasya te vayam pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
svānyājyāni ||2||
iti | upahavyasya tantre klṛptamiti vacanāt svānīti upahavyasaṃbandhīnītyarthaḥ | hotā devo amartyaḥ pra vāṃ mahidyavī ayamu te samatasi gāva upavadāvaṭa ityājyāni ||2||
pavamānasya jighnataḥ iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ caiḍaṃ ca sauparṇaṃ rohitakūlīyaṃ ca santani | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca kaṇvarathantaraṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ svam antyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ || ]
sākamukṣa iti pārthaṃ mādhyaṃdināntyam || 3||
svāni pṛṣṭhāni ||4||
mā bhema mā śramiṣmeti bṛhat | svāsu vāmadevyam | imā u tvā purūvaso yasyāyaṃ viśva ārya iti śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||4||
pavamāno ajījanat iti gāyatrasaṃhite jarābodhīyaṃ ca sauśravasaṃ ca | sve uṣṇikkakubhau | abhī no vājasātamam iti gaurīvitaṃ ca kārtayaśaṃ ca śyāvāśvāndhīgave | añjata iti kāvam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||6||
sarvāṇi stotrāṇi trayastriṃśāni | atropahavyasya saptadaśatvādasya cāhnastrayastriṃśatvādaupahavyeṣu pavamāneṣu yathāsthānamāvāpaḥ kṛtaḥ |
satrāṇi -- caturviṃśatirātre navamamahaḥ anyatsarvamupahavyavadeva klṛptam | viṣṭutinetrāṇyāvarttīni bhavantīti ||6||
iti caturviṃśatirātre aṣṭamamahaḥ || 16 ||
caturviṃśatirātre navamamahaḥ
atha trayastriṃśamaharniruktamāha -- 
upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ upoṣu jātamapturam babhrave nu svatavase ete somā abhi priyam somaḥ punāno arṣati pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna āyāhītyājyāni || 2 ||
pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ cāśvaṃ ca somasāma cāśu ca bhārgavam mārgīyavam | pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ hārāyaṇaṃ ca yaudhājayam auśanam antyam || 3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ || ]
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite kākṣīvataṃ ca bhāsaṃ ca | pavasvendramaccheti saphākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca padanidhanaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyam audalagautame kāvam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ || ]
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 6 ||
sarvaṃ trayastriṃśam || 6||
iti caturviṃśatirātre navamamahaḥ || 20||
caturviṃśatirātre daśamamahaḥ
triṇavamaharāha 
eṣa u sya vṛṣā ratha eṣa vājī hito nṛbhir iti purastāt paryāsasya ṣaḍṛce yauktāśvāduttare | surūpaṃ carṣabhaśca pāvamānaḥ āyāsyābhyāṃ pūrve pṛṣṭhakaulmalabarhiṣe | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārtham antyam || 1||
eṣa sya dhārayā sutaḥ pavasva devavītaye iti śaṅkusujñāne gaurīvitāduttare | yadvāhiṣṭhīyāsite ||2||
satrāṇi -- caturviṃśatirātre dvādaśamahaḥ samānamitaraṃ dvitīyenāhnā dvādaśāhasya || 3 ||
iti || 3||
iti daśamamahaḥ || 21 ||
ekādaśamahaḥ
atha dve ekaviṃśe ahanī| tayorādyamāha 
utte śuṣmāso asthur iti purastātparyāsasya caturṛca | athaitadeva tṛtīyamahastṛcaklṛptamekaviṃśam || 1||
iti| etadeveti | dvādaśāhīyamityarthaḥ| dvādaśāhikasya tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ saptadaśatvāt tasyaikaviṃśatvasaṃpādanārthamuktaṃ tṛcaklṛptam | tataśca bṛhatyāṃ paurumadgādīni catvāryapi sāmāni tṛca eva kāryāṇi | adhyāsyā nu lupyante| tathā ca satreṣvāṣkāranidhanaṃ kāṇvamabhisomatṛce'dhīmahe | evamārbhave vācaśca sāma śauktaṃ ca tṛca eva kārye || 1 ||
iti ekādaśamahaḥ || 22 ||
dvādaśādicaturdaśāntānyahāni
athottaramekaviṃśamaharārabhya trayāṇāmahnāṃ klṛptimāha -- 
prajñāta uttarastryahaḥ || 1 ||
iti| ekaviṃśaṃ triṇavaṃ trayastriṃśamiti tryahaḥ prajñātaḥ dvādaśīyaḥ prasiddha eva | tatra na kaścidviśeṣa ityarthaḥ| so'yamanantaroktaḥ ṣaḍaho viśālaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ityucyate | tathā ca nidānam -- sa eṣa āvṛttastomaḥ pūrvastryaho'nupūrvastoma uttara etaṃ viśālaḥ pṛṣṭhya ityācakṣate iti | caturdaśāhāni klṛptāni | trayastriṃśamaharaniruktamityuktam || 1 ||
dvādaśādicaturdaśāntānyahāni ||23||
pañcadaśamahaḥ
pañcadaśamaharāha -- 
athaitadeva trayastriṃśamaharaniruktamāharati || 1 ||
etadeveti pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ ityādinānantaramevāṣṭamatvena klṛptamityarthaḥ | tasya viśeṣamāha -- 
tasya kaṇvarathantarasya loke vāśaṃ pārthasyauśanaṃ bṛhato rathantaraṃ śyaitasya naudhasaṃ
śrudhyasya pauṣkalam || 2 ||
iti || 2 ||
iti pañcadaśamahaḥ || 24 ||
ṣoḍaśādiekaviṃśāntānyahāni
pṛṣṭhyaḥ stomaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ pratyaṅiṅatyuktaṃ ṣaḍahamāha -- 
pṛṣṭhayaḥ stomaḥ ṣaḍaha ityāvṛttaḥ || 1 ||
iti || 1 ||
iti ṣoḍaśādiekaviṃśāntānyahāni || 25 ||
dvāviṃśamahaḥ
dvāviṃśamaharāha -- 
trivṛdaharaniruktam | tasya pavasvendo vṛṣā suta uta no goṣaṇiṃ dhiyam śaśamānasya vā naraḥ upa naḥ sūnavo giraḥ pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
satrāṇi -- caturviṃśatirātraṃ pūrvam
pavamānasya jighnataḥ iti gāyatramekasyām | āmahīyavamekasyām aiḍaṃ sauparṇamekasyām | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | śyāvāśvasya loke gaurīvitam || 1 ||
samānamitaramupahavyena ||2||
sarvaṃ trivṛt ||3||
iti | bahiṣpavamāne prathamāntyau tṛcau | ekarcā itare | itarayoḥ pavamānayorantyameva tṛce || 3 ||
iti dvāviṃśamahaḥ || 26 ||
trayoviṃśamahaḥ
trayoviṃśamaharāha -- 
jyotiṣṭomo'gniṣṭomastasyānuṣṭub yatharṣabhasya || 1 ||
iti | ṛṣabhasya kratoryathānuṣṭupsu purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitamekasyām| āndhīgavamekasyām | śyāvāśvamekasyām | bṛhat tisṛṣviti | tathāsyāpītyarthaḥ || 1||
iti trayoviṃśamahaḥ || 27 ||
caturviṃśamahaḥ
atrānuṣṭubhi bṛhataḥ karaṇamuttarasyāhno jyotirātratvāt ajāmyartham | tadevāha -- 
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ | samānamitaram || 1 ||
iti || 1 ||
iti caturviṃśamahaḥ || 28 ||
iti caturviśatirātraṃ pūrvaṃ samāptam ||
uttaraṃ caturviṃśatirātram
pañcaviṃśati rātrādidvātriṃśadrātrāntāni satrāṇi
atirātro dvāvabhiplavau ṣaḍahau dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ iti dvitīyaṃ caturviṃśatirātram ||
klṛptamā nānābrahmasāmabhyaḥ ||1||
iti | abhiplavau śyaitanaudhasau brahmasāmānau pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihau | prathamasyābhiplavasya prathamasyāhnaḥ eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ | atra sūtram -- saṃvatsarādeśādrātrisatreṣu tathaivābhiplavānāmabhyāsaḥ syāditi gautamaḥ iti | tasyārthaḥ -- gavāmayane'bhiplavasyādiṣṭatvādrātrisatreṣvabhiplavānāṃ tatprakṛtikatvāt gavāmayanābhyāsavadabhyāsa iti | tataśca yathāsaṃbhavaṃ dvitīyādiṣvabhiplaveṣu dvitīye'hni madhyaṃdine hāviṣmatasya sthāne yauktāśvamuttaraṃ | bhargayaśasorviṣuvatke satre sūtroktā vyavasthā | anyatra vikalpa eva | ārbhave maukṣasya sthāne hāviṣmatam | caturthe vīṅkaśuddhāśuddhīye api bhargayaśobhyāṃ vyākhyāte | pañcame'hani yaṇvasthāne dvitīyādyabhiplavakrameṇāpatyaṃ santani śākvaravarṇaṃ ca | tatra yadā grāmegeyaṃ santani syāttadā mānavāt pūrvaṃ vāmraṃ kāryamajāmyartham | ṣaṣṭhe'hani pṛṣyāthānantarye satyāśvasūktam | triṇi dhanāyāsyayoḥ sthāne yathākramamiṣo
satrāṇi -- caturviṃśatirātramuttaram vṛdhīyasamante iti viśeṣaḥ | daśāhātirātrāvuktau ||
etā eva samahāvratāḥ iti pañcaviṃśatirātram | sāṃvatsarikaṃ vrataṃ pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ | tasya tadeva sthānaṃ yatsaṃvatsare ||
atirātro gauścāyuśca dve ahanī dvāvabhiplavau ṣaḍahau dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ iti ṣaḍviṃśatirātram | goāyuṣī aikāhike gaurīvitasvare | abhiplavau śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānau pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihau | dvādaśāho vyūḍhaḥ | uktāvatirātrau ||
atirātro jyotirgaurāyustryahau dvāvabhiplavau ṣaḍahau dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātra iti saptaviṃśatirātram | jyotirādaya aikāhikā gaurīvitasvarāḥ | pūrvavadabhiplavau | prathamasyābhiplavasya prathamasyāhnaḥ eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ| itaraṃ vyūḍhadvādaśāhavat ||
etā eva samahāvratāḥ ityaṣṭāviṃśatirātramudayanīyātirātrātpūrvaṃ sāṃvatsarikaṃ vratam | pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ ||
atirātro jyotirgaurāyurgaurāyu pañcāho dvāvabhiplavau ṣaḍahau dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ iti ekānnatriṃśadrātram | saptadaśarātrasadabhiplavaṃ cāha -- abhiplavau pūrvavat | eṣa pratnā na kartavyā| itare vyūḍhadvādaśāhavat ||
atirātrastrayo'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātra iti triṃśadrātram | abhiplavāḥ śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānaḥ pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ| prathamasyābhiplavasya prathamasyāhna eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ | itaraduktam ||
etā eva samahāvratāḥ iti ekatriṃśadrātram | sāṃvatsarikaṃ vrataṃ pūrvasmādviśeṣaḥ ||
atirātro gauścāyuśca dve ahanī trayo'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ iti dvātriṃśadrātram | goāyuṣī aikāhike gaurīvitasvare | abhiplavāḥ śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānaḥ pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ| itare vyūḍhadvādaśāhavat || 1 ||
iti pañcaviṃśatirātrādidvātriṃśadrātrāntāni satrāṇi || 29 ||
iti uttaraṃ caturviṃśatirātram ||
trayastriṃśadrātraṃ prathamam
prathamādiṣoḍaśāntānyahāni
atha trayastriṃśadātrāṇi trīṇi| atirātrastrayaḥ pañcāhāḥ | viśvajidatirātra ekaḥ pañcāho dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ iti prathamaṃ trayastriṃśadrātram | etā rātrayo nānābrahmasāmāna ityucyante | pañcāhānāṃ nānābrahmasāmakatvāt | tadāha
nānābrahmasāmnāmatirātrastrayaḥ pañcāhāḥ pañcāhaklṛptāḥ ||1||
iti | nānābrahmasāmāna iti atirātrānantaraṃ trayaḥ pañcāhāḥ saptadaśarātrasya pañcāhena klṛptāḥ || 1||
satrāṇi -- trayastriṃśadrātraṃ prathamam tatra viśeṣaḥ
nānābrahmasāmānaḥ || 2||
iti || 2||
prathamasya pañcāhasya yathālokaṃ bṛhadrathantare || 3 ||
iti | yathālokaṃ yathāsthānam | etaccottara [tra] viparyasya vakṣyamāṇatvāt iha tatheti vispaṣṭapradarśanārthamuktam ||3||
naudhasaṃ śyaitaṃ mahāviṣṭambhaṃ traiśokam | ubhayaṃ śṛṇavacca na iti vāśam | etāni brahmasāmāni ||4||
iti | prathamasya pañcāhasya naudhasādīni kramāt brahmasāmāni || 4 ||
dvitīyasya pañcāhasyānyonyasya lokaṃ bṛhadrathantare | tvamindra pratūrtiṣu iti abhīvartaḥ | tvāmidāhyo nara iti mānavaṃ yat pṛṣṭhye | chandasyaṃ pañcame'hani | ka īṃ veda sute sacetyāṣkāraṇidhanaṃ kāṇvam | svāsu yauktasrucam | svāsu śrāyantīyam | etāni brahmasāmāni ||5||
iti | anyonyasya lokamiti āpadyate iti śeṣaḥ | śrūyate hi yadrathantarasya lokaṃ bṛhadāpadyate bṛhato rathantaramiti | atra bṛhadrathantarayoranyonyalokāpādanamajāmyartham | yatpṛṣṭhya iti pṛṣṭhye ṣaḍahe yadvihitaṃ taduttaraṃ mānavamityarthaḥ| nanu yadyatra dvitīye'hani uttaraṃ mānavaṃ brahmasāmatvena vidhīyate tarhi pañcame'hani pavamāne'bhisomāyāṃ mānavasya saṃcāraprasaṅga ityata uktam | chandasyaṃ pañcame'hanīti | pūrvamānavamityarthaḥ | jyotiṣṭomātirātrayoryāni sāmāni viniyuktāni tato'nyāni chandasānīti chandasyānīti cocyante | tathā ca prāyaṇīye caturviṃśe nidānam -- atha khalu ya ūrdhvaṃ jyotiṣṭomadaśāhābhyāṃ yajñakratavaḥ saptabhiḥ paryāyaisteṣu jyotiṣṭomānāṃ ca dāśarātrikāṇāṃ ca dravyāṇāmāgamo bhavati | stomānvitāni sthānānvitāni pṛṣṭhānvitāni tantrānvitāni bhāgānvitāni saṃsthānvitāni āśīḥsaṃprayāṇīti rūpācchāndasānyāyanti iti | trikakubdaśarātre ca nānāmadhyenidhanāni na dāśarātrikāṇi | madhyenidhanānyanubhavanti | ataśchandasyānyupayuṅkta iti ||5||
tṛtīyasya pañcāhasya yathālokaṃ bṛhadrathantare | śagdhyūṣu śacīpate iti mānavaṃ yacchandasyaṃ nityaṃ pañcame'hani vayamenamidāhya iti vāsiṣṭham ā tvā sahasramā śatam iti bhāradvājam | yo rājā carṣaṇīnām iti janitram | tvamindra yaśā asi indrasya yaśaḥ etāni brahmasāmāni ||6||
iti | nityaṃ pañcame'hanīti dvitīye'hani chandasyamānavasya vidhānāt | saṃcārāprasaṅgāt pañcame'hani nityatayā vihitamuttarameva mānavaṃ sthitamityarthaḥ ||6||
iti prathamādiṣoḍaśāntānyahāni || 30 ||
satrāṇi -- trayastriṃśadrātraṃ prathamam saptadaśamahaḥ
viśvajidatirātraḥ| tasya gaurīvitasya loke nānadam | agnirmūrdhā divaḥ kakud iti satrāsāhīyam traikakubhaṃ nārmedham gaurīvitaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma 5 || 1||
rātriḥ saṃdhiḥ || 1 ||
iti | sāṃvatsarikasya viśvajito viśeṣakathanaṃ etadaharviṣuvatsthānīyam | atra tṛtīyapañcāhāntyasya rāthantarasyāhno vaiśvajite pavamāne bṛhatyāṃ sthitena rathantareṇa saṃnipātāt prasaktaṃ jāmitvaṃ tatraiva sthitayā asya pratneti pratnavatyā gāyatryā parihṛtam ||1||
iti saptadaśamahaḥ ||31|
aṣṭādaśādidvāviṃśāntānyahāni
athaiṣa eva pañcāha āvṛttaḥ || 1 ||
pañcamamaharārabhya prātilomyena kārya ityarthaḥ | asya cāvṛttatvamupapāditaṃ nidāne -- uttamaḥ pañcāha āvṛtto bhavatītyanāvṛtta iti vyākhyātam | pūrvasminnekaviṃśatirātra iti ||1||
āvṛttasya pañcāhasyānyonyasya lokaṃ bṛhadrathantare | pibā sutasya rasina iti jamadagnerabhīvarto yadindra prāgapāgudag iti naipātitham | āmandrairindra haribhira iti abhinidhanaṃ kāṇvam yathā gauro apākṛtam iti gautamasya manādyam | tvamaṅga praśaṃsiṣam 
iti paurumīḍham | etāni brahmasāmāni ||2||
iti | atra pañcamasyāhnaḥ prathamasya sato rathantarasya sthāne bṛhat | caturthasyāhno dvitīyasya sato bṛhataḥ sthāne rathantaramityeva vyatyāsaḥ | 2||
iti aṣṭādaśādidvāviṃśāntānyahāni | 32||
trayoviṃśāditrayastriṃśāntānyahāni
dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ || 1 ||
iti | vyūḍhadvādaśāhavadityarthaḥ || 1||
iti trayoviṃśāditrayastriṃśāntānyahāni || 33 ||
iti trayastriṃśadrātraṃ prathamamaṃ samāptam ||
trayastriṃśadrātraṃ dvitīyam
atirātro'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho'tirātro'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho'tirātro'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca iti madhyamaṃ trayastriṃśadrātram | abhiplavāḥ śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānaḥ pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ | sarveṣāṃ prathamasyāhnaḥ eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ ||
madhyamasya trayastriṃśadātrasya sarve ṣoḍaśimanto'tirātrāḥ || 1 ||
iti viśeṣaḥ || 1||
iti dvitīyaṃ trayastriṃśadrātram || 34||
satrāṇi -- tṛtīyaṃ trayastriṃśadrātram trayastriṃśadrātraṃ tṛtīyam
atirātrastrayaḥ pañcāhā viśvajidatirātrastrayaḥ pañcāhā atirātraḥ iti uttamaṃ trayastriṃśadrātram | tasya kalpaḥ -- 
uttamasya trayastriṃśadrātrasyātirātraḥ | trayaḥ pañcāhāḥ pañcāhaklṛptāḥ ||1||
iti | tasya viśeṣamāha
śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmāno yathālokaṃ bṛhadrathantarāḥ || 2 ||
iti | atra nānābrahmasāmatvasya śravaṇāditi bhāvaḥ ||
pakṣāntaramāha -- 
yathā vā nānābrahmasāmasu ||3||
iti | prathamatrayastriṃśadātrokta evāyaṃ pañcāha iti yasya buddhistanmateneti bhāvaḥ| tathā ca nidānam -- yasya tu syāt sa evāyaṃ pañcāha iti | tathaiva tasya bṛhadrathantare tathā brahmasāmānīti ||3||
klṛpto viśvajidyo nānābrahmasāmasu || 4 ||
iti | atrāpi saptadaśamaho viṣuvatsthānīyam ||4||
athaita eva pañcāhā āvṛttāḥ || 5 ||
iti | tatra paurvapakṣikāḥ pañcāhā yadi yathā lokabṛhadrathantarāstata
āvṛttā api tathā ayathā lokabṛhadrathantarāste'ta ete'pi tathaiveti vyavasthā || 5 ||
āvṛttānāṃ pañcāhānāṃ prathamasya pañcāhasya prathamasyāhno'bhi somāsa āyavaḥ iti mānavamekasyām | tasyāmevānūpam | bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambhamekasyām | maidhātithamekasyām | vāmraṃ tisṛṣu | agnestriṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām || 6||
samānamitaram || 7||
iti | atra vaiśvajitasya rāthantarasya saṃdheḥ pūrvasmin pakṣasi pañcāhānāṃ pañcamenottarasmin prathamatāṃ gatena rāthantareṇāhnāṃ saṃnipāte jāmi syāditi tannivṛttyarthaṃ bṛhannidhanasya vārkajambhasya kalpanamiti sthite nidhanavato mānavasya purastādupariṣṭācca tannidhanavatastasya karaṇaṃ jāmiprasaṅgādevāyuktamiti svārādānūpādūrdhvaṃ tat kalpitam | stomopapattyarthaṃ caiṣāṃ maidhātithasya cāgantorekarcakaraṇam | caturṇāṃ prathamāyāṃ dvāvitarau nānottarayoriti nyāyānmānavamekasyāṃ tasyāmevānūpamityādikramo jñātavyaḥ | vāmrāgneye yathāpūrvameva
tṛcādhyāsyayoḥ sthite | nanu pañcāhasyābhiplavaprakṛtikatvāt saṃvatsarādeśādrātrisatreṣu ityuktena nyāyena paurvapakṣikasya tṛtīyasya pañcāhasya pañcame'hani santaninaḥ karaṇāt āvṛttaprathamabhūte'sminnahani saṃtanimānavayornidhanavatoḥ saṃnipātāt jāmi syāt | taduktam -- āvṛttānāṃ pañcāhānāṃ prathamasya pañcāhasya prathamasyāhno
satrāṇi -- catustriṃśadrātram mānavādirbṛhatī tajjāmi syāt saṃtanineti | na cedatra vaktuṃ śakyaṃ pañcame'hani yadahargrāmageyaṃ saṃtani syāt | pūrvaṃ vāmraṃ syāditi | tathā hi -- mānavāt vāmramānavānūpavārkajambheṣu pūrvavadekarceṣu kṛteṣu maidhātithaṃ tṛce kartavyaṃ syāt | na cāgantunastṛce karaṇaṃ kvacidapi dṛṣṭam | evaṃ tarhi vaikṛtatvāt pañcāhānāṃ na teṣu saṃvatsaravadabhyāsaḥ pravartate | api tu prathamasyaivādhikāreṇa abhiplavāṃstu na prasṛjet | saṃvatsaravadabhyāsaḥ | taduktam -- vaikṛtatvāttu nivṛttiḥ | pañcāheṣu abhiplavān na prasṛjediti | atra ca yathālokaṃ bṛhadrathantaratve dvayorapi pakṣasoḥ pratipañcāhamādyantarayorapi pakṣasoḥ pratipañcāhamādyantayorahnoḥ rāthantaratvāt jāmi syāditi| tannivṛttaye pūrvasmin pakṣasi prathamasyāhna eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ kāryaḥ| uttarasmin pratipañcāhamāvṛttaprathame'hani pūrvavadvārkajambhaṃ kāryam | taduktam -- eṣa pratnāvārkajambhe vā pratipañcāhaṃ yathā lokabṛhadrathantarāścedeṣa pratnā pūrvapakṣasi vārkajambham uttara iti || 7 ||
iti trayastriṃśadrātraṃ tṛtīyam ||35 ||
catustriṃśadrātraṃ
atirātro jyotirgaurāyustryahastrayo'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca iti catustriṃśadrātram ||
klṛptamā vidhṛtibhyaḥ || 1||
iti | atirātrau sātrikau trikadrukā aikāhikā gaurīvitasvarāḥ | abhiplavāḥ śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānaḥ pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ | prathamasyābhiplavasya prathamasyāhna eṣa pratnānurūpa iti viśeṣaḥ | itaratsarvaṃ sāṃvatsarikam || 1||
iti catustriṃśadrātram || 36||
pañcatriṃśadrātram
atirātro jyotirgaurāyurgaurāyuḥ pañcāhastrayo'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātra ityanuvākena pañcatriṃśadrātraṃ | saptadaśarātravat | abhiplavaḥ pañcāhaḥ pūrvavad | eṣa pratnā na kartavyā| itaraduktam |
iti pañcatriṃśadrātram ||
ṣaṭtriṃśadrātram
atirātraścatvāro'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātra ityanuvākena ṣaṭtriṃśadrātramuktam | prathamasyābhiplavasya
prathamasyāhnaḥ eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ| abhiplavāḥ śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānaḥ pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ| anyat sāṃvatsarikam ||
iti ṣaṭtriṃśadrātram ||
satrāṇi -- catvāriṃśadrātram saptatriṃśadrātram
etā eva samahāvratāḥ iti saptatriṃśadrātram | sāṃvatsarikaṃ vrataṃ pūrvasmātsatrādviśeṣaḥ ||
iti saptatriṃśadrātram ||
aṣṭātriṃśadrātram
atirātro gauścāyuśca dve ahanī catvāro'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ ityaṣṭātriṃśadrātramuktam | goāyuṣī aikāhike gaurīvitasvare | abhiplavāḥ śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānaḥ pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ | vyūḍho dvādaśāhaḥ | atirātrau sātrikau ||
iti aṣṭācatvāriṃśadrātram ||
ekānnacatvāriṃśadrātram
atirātro jyotirgaurāyustryahaścatvāro'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ ityekānnacatvāriṃśadrātram | tasya sarvamuktam ||
iti ekānnacatvāriṃśadrātram ||
catvāriṃśadrātram
etā eva samahāvratā iti catvāriṃśadrātram| sāṃvatsarikaṃ vratam | upāntyamahaḥ | anyat sarva pūrvavat ||
iti catvāriṃśadrātram ||
ekānnapañcāśadrātraṃ
prathamam
[prāyaṇīyo'tirātraḥ ]
athaikānnapañcāśadrātrāṇi sapta| atirātrastrīṇi trivṛntyahānyagniṣṭomamukhānyatirātro daśa pañcadaśā ukthyāḥ ṣoḍaśimaddaśamamaharatirātro dvādaśa saptadaśā ukthyāḥ atirātraḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaho'tirātro dvādaśaikaviṃśā ukthyā atirātraḥ iti vidhṛtisaṃjñamekānnatriṃśadrātram | tasya prāyaṇīyātirātre viśeṣamāha -- 
vidhṛtīnāṃ jyotiṣṭomo'tirātro'ṣoḍaśikaḥ kautsasya loka udvaṃśīyam ā tvā viśantvindavaḥ ityetāsu | jarābodha tadviviḍhḍhi pratiṣyā sūnarī jani eṣo uṣā apūrvyetyetāsu gāyatrīṣu jarābodhīyaṃ saṃdhiṣāma || 1 ||
iti | vidhṛtīnāmiti bahuvacanaṃ saṃsadādivadrātribahutvāpekṣam | atra nidānam aiḍaṃ cetsaṃdhiṣāma syāt kautsasya loka udvaṃśīyameteneḍākāmaśca sidhyatyajāmikalpaśca iti | tadyadetāsu ā tvā viśantvindava iti etā rātrervikṛtau dṛṣṭā iti | nityāsu vetyasya sthāne'dhyardheḍāyā vidhānādiḍākāmaḥ
satrāṇi -- trivṛnti ahāni sidhyati svāratvādajāmikalpaśca | indra suteṣvityetāsvevodvaṃśīyaṃ kāryamityanye | yadāha -- nityāsu veti vicārayantyarthakāritamudvaṃśīyaṃ sa kimartho nityāścāvapediti | tathā ca sūtreṣu -- ā tvendra suteṣu iti dvayorapi tṛcayorudvaṃśīyamadhīmahe | gāyatrīṣu jarābodhīyamityādibrāhmaṇānusāreṇa gāyatrīṣu saṃdhiṣāma vihitam | tanmūlatvasūcanārthameva kalpe gāyatrīṣviti chandaḥkīrtanam | evamuṣṇikṣu śrudhyamityādāvapi draṣṭavyam || 1 ||
iti prāyaṇīyo'tirātraḥ || 38 ||
trivṛnti ahāni | teṣāṃ prathamam
athāgniṣṭomasaṃsthamekaṃ dve ukthasaṃsthe iti trīṇi trivṛntyahāni | teṣāṃ prathamamāha -- 
atha yadeva kusurubindasya rāthantaraṃ trivṛdahastadetat | tasya
gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi || 1 ||
iti | upadavipaveti bahiṣpavamānam | agnaā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatramāmahīyavaṃ jarābodhīya miti sāmatṛcaḥ | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | auśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni| svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir iti saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitāndhīgave | kāvamantyam | antyameva tṛce | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 1||
dvitīyaṃ trivṛdaharāha
yad bārhataṃ trivṛdahastaduttaram ||2||
iti | kusurubindasyetyeva || 2 ||
tatra viśeṣamāha -- 
pavamānasya te kave iti paryāsaḥ | yauktāśvasya loke hāviṣmatam mādhucchandasasya kāleyam hāviṣmatasya maukṣam | gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi | sākamaśvam saubharam āṣṭādaṃṣṭram ityukthāni || 3 ||
samānamitaram || 4 ||
iti | pavasva vāco pavasvendo pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtamityājyāni | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ hāviṣmatamājigamiti
satrāṇi -- atirātraḥ sāmatṛcaḥ | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitakrauñce | vṛṣā matīnāmiti yāmamantyam | yāmavarjamekarcāḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma| sākamaśvaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni || 4 ||
prathamasya trivṛto yā klṛptiḥ saiva tṛtīyasyeti atidiśam̐statra viśeṣamāha -- 
yā pūrvā sottarā | tasyāḥ pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇam udvaṃśīyaṃ tairaścyam ityukthāni ||5||
iti trivṛnti ahāni || 38 ||
atirātraḥ
trivadbhya uttaramatirātramāha -- 
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān | kautsasya loka udvaṃśīyam ā tvā viśantvindavaḥ ityetāsu | agne vājasya gomataḥ uṣastaccitramābhara aśvinā vartirasmad ityetāsūṣṇikṣu śrudhyaṃ saṃdhiṣāma || iti ||1||
iti atirātraḥ || 39||
daśa pañcadaśāḥ ukthyāḥ
pañcadaśānāṃ daśānāṃ prathamamāha -- 
atha yadeva kusurubindasya rāthantaraṃ pañcadaśamahastadetat | tasya
gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi || 1||
iti | upāsmai gāyatā narodavidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavorājā medhābhirīyate pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agna ā no mitrāyāhīndrāgnītyājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti rauravayaudhājaye | auśanamantyam| rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | ayā pavasva devayuḥ pavate haryato harir iti saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo andhasa
iti gaurīvitāndhīgave | kāvamantyam | uṣṇikkakubhau gaurīvitaṃ caikarcāḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścya mityukthāni | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam || 1||
satrāṇi -- pañcadaśā ukthyāḥ dvitīyaṃ pañcadaśamāha yad bārhataṃ pañcadaśaṃ taduttaram | pavamānasya te kave iti paryāso mādhucchandasasya loke kāleyam | gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi || 2 ||
iti | pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasi pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtamityājyāni | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti āyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramokṣe | pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca | vṛṣā matīnāmiti yāmamantyam | kakubuṣṇihau gaurīvitaṃ caikarcāḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam || 2|| uttarāṇi pañcadaśānyāha -- 
etābhyāmeva viparyāsamādaśamādahnaḥ ||3||
iti | rāthantareṇa pañcadaśena tṛtīyam, bārhatena caturtham ityevaṃ vyatyāsena daśamaṃ pañcadaśena samam | yāvat klṛptiḥ || 3||
tatra daśamasya viśeṣamāha -- 
daśamasyāhnaḥ āṣṭādaṃṣṭrasya loke nārmedham | pratyasmai pipīṣata iti nānadaṃ ṣoḍaśisāma ||4||
iti | ṣoḍaśisaṃsthametadahaḥ | ukthyatvaṃ vidhāya ṣoḍaśimaddaśama miti vacanāt | anuṣṭupsu nānadamityetadasminnahani ||4||
iti daśa pañcadaśā ukthyāḥ || 40 ||
uttaraḥ atirātraḥ
pañcadaśebhya uttaramatirātramāha -- 
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimānaviklṛptaḥ || 1 ||
iti| atra saṃdhiṣāmābhipretya bṛhatīṣu rathantaramiti śrutāvuktam ||
iti uttaraḥ atirātraḥ ||41||
dvādaśa saptadaśā ukthyāḥ
saptadaśānāṃ prathamamaharāha -- 
atha yadeva kusurubindasya rāthantaraṃ saptadaśamahastadetat | tasya maidhātithasya loke bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambham | rauravasya maidhātitham | agnerarkasya saṃhitam | gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi || 1 ||
satrāṇi -- saptadaśā ukthyāḥ iti | upāsmai gāyatā naro davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavaḥ taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam |
agna ā yāhi vītaye ityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca vārkajambhaṃ yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam | dvihiṃkāramādyāyām | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca yad bṛhannidhanaṃ maidhātithaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitāndhīgave | kāvamantyam | saphapauṣkale ekarcayoḥ | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścyamityukthāni | sarvaṃ saptadaśam | asyāhno rāthantarasya pūrveṇa jyotiṣṭomātirātreṇa rathantarasaṃdhinā saṃnipātājjāmitā mā bhūditi madhyaṃdine vārkajambhaṃ kalpitam | tadvaśācca rauravasya maidhātitham | trivṛtpañcadaśayostu rauravayoḥ pūrvasmin jyotiṣṭomātirātre saubharamukthānāṃ brahmasāmetyajāmi || 1 ||
dvitīyaṃ saptadaśamāha -- 
yad bārhataṃ saptadaśaṃ taduttaram | pavamānasya te kave iti paryāsaḥ | mādhucchandasasya loke kāleyaṃ svāśirāmarkasya maukṣam | gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi ||2||
iti | pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā sutovṛṣā soma utte śuṣmāsa īrate pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtamityājyāni | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca| parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ cāyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | śaśakarṇaklṛptā bṛhatī| bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇyam iti gāyatramaukṣe | pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca| vṛṣā matīnāmiti yāmamantyam | kakubuṣṇihāvekarcau | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ saptadaśam || 2 ||
parāṇi saptadaśānyāha -- 
etābhyāmeva viparyāsamādvādaśādahnaḥ uddharatyuttarebhyo vārkajambham | yathālokaṃ maidhātitharaurave ||3||
satrāṇi -- pṛṣṭhayaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ iti | tṛtīyādiṣu saptadaśeṣu pūrvasyāhno bārhatatvājjāmyabhāvādvārkajambhamuddhāryam | tataśca yathāsthānaṃ maidhātitharaurave ityarthaḥ| tatreyaṃ bṛhatyāṃ klṛptiḥ| abhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca rauravayaudhājaye iti | itarat samānam || 3 ||
dvādaśasyāhno viśeṣamāha -- 
dvādaśasyāhna udvaṃśīyāntānyukthāni || 4 ||
iti || 4||
iti dvādaśa saptadaśā ukthyāḥ ||42||
atirātraḥ
saptadaśebhyaḥ paramatirātramāha -- 
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān | kautsasya loka udvaṃśīyam | ā tvā viśantvindavaḥ ityetāsvagniṃ taṃ manye yo vasurmaheno adya bodhaya prati priyatamaṃ ratham 
ityetāsu paṅktiṣu rāyovājīyaṃ saṃdhiṣāma || 1 ||
iti| tadevamekānnatriṃśadahānyukthāni ||1||
iti atirātraḥ ||43||
pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ
pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ samūḍho vā vyūḍho vā | samūḍhastveva ||
iti | vyākhyāto'yaṃ grantha ṛtuṣaḍahe| prathamasyāhno'sya pratnānurūpa iti kecit | neti vayam | pūrvasminnatirātre kriyamāṇena saubhareṇaivājāmitāyāḥ siddhatvāt || 1||
iti pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ ||44||
atirātraḥ
pṛṣṭhyāt paramatirātramāha -- 
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātraḥ ṣoḍaśimān | auśanasyarkṣu vaiśvajyotiṣamantyaṃ yatpragāyateti | abodhyagniḥ samidhā janānām idaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ jyotiṣāṃ jyotirāgād abhītyagniruṣasāmanīkam ityetāsu triṣṭupsvauśanaṃ saṃdhiṣāma ||1|| iti| auśanasyāsaṃcārārthaṃ tatsthāne vaiśvajyotiṣaṃ kalpitam ||1||
iti atirātraḥ ||45||
dvādaśa ekaviṃśā ukthyāḥ
ekaviṃśānāṃ dvādaśānāmādyamāha -- 
babhrave nu svatavase iti purastāt paryāsasya ṣaḍṛcam | āmahīyavāduttaramāśubhārgavam | rauravayaudhājaye antarā maidhātitham svāsu saphapauṣkale || 1||
samānamitarametasyaiva rāthantareṇa pañcadaśena ||2||
iti | upāsmai gāyatā narodavidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavorājā medhābhirīyate babhrave nu svatavase pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agna ā yāhi vītaye ityājyāni | uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ
satrāṇi -- ekaviṃśā ukthyāḥ  cāśubhārgavaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhāraye ti rauravaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam | rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramacche ti saphapauṣkale | purojitī va iti gaurīvitāndhīgave | kāvamantyam | yajñāyajñīya magniṣṭomasāma | pramam̐hiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścya mityukthāni | sarvamekaviṃśam || 2 ||
dvitīyamekaviṃśamāha -- 
prayadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ iti purastāt paryāsasya ṣaḍṛcam | hāviṣmatāduttaraṃ svāraṃ sauparṇam āyāsye antarā dairghaśravasam | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya pavasva devavītaye iti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye ||3||
samānamitarametasyaiva bārhatena pañcadaśena || 4 ||
iti| pavasva vācopavasvendovṛṣā somaḥvṛṣā hyasi prayadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | agniṃ dūtamityājyāni | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇam | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti aiḍaṃ cāyāsyaṃ dairghaśravasaṃ ca triṇidhanaṃ cāyāsyam | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam | bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya pavasva deva vītaye iti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca | vṛṣā matīnāmiti yāmamantyam | yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ saptadaśam || 4 ||
tṛtīyādīnyekaviṃśānyāha -- 
etābhyāmeva vipāryāsamā dvādaśādahnaḥ || 5 ||
iti | tatra navamādīnāṃ viśeṣamāha -- 
navamasyāhnaḥ svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārko daśamasyāhno yaste mado vareṇyaḥ iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ ekādaśasyābhi priyāṇīti dīrghatamaso'rko'ntyo dvādaśasyāhno'ntarotsedhaniṣedhau dairghaśravasam || 6||
tasyaivāhna udvaṃśīyāntānyukthāni || 7 ||
satrāṇiekānnapañcāśadrātraṃ dvitīyam iti | navame'hani saṃhitasya sthāne'gnerarkaḥ | daśame'hani maukṣasya svāśirāmarkaḥ | ekādaśe'hani kāvasya dīrghatamaso'rkaḥ | dvādaśe'hani āyāsyayoḥ sthāne utsedhaniṣedhau āṣṭādaṃṣṭrasyo dvaṃśīyam | samānamitarat || 7||
iti dvādaśa ekaviṃśā ukthyāḥ || 46||
atirātraḥ
udayanīyātirātramāha -- 
jyotiṣṭomo'tirātro'ṣoḍaśikaḥ | kāvasyarkṣu vāsiṣṭham antyam yadeṣa prakośa iti | janasya gopā ajaniṣṭa jāgṛvir etā u tasyā uṣasaḥ ketumakrata abodhyagnirjma udeti sūrya ityetāsu jagatīṣu kāvaṃ saṃdhiṣāma || 1||
iti | atrāpi kāvasya saṃcāro mā bhūditi tatsthāne vāsiṣṭhaklṛptiḥ || 47||
ekānnapañcāśadrātraṃ dvitīyam (yamātirātrāḥ) atirātrau dvāvabhiplavau ṣaḍahau gauścāyuścātirātrau dvāvabhiplavau | ṣaḍahāvabhijicca viśvajiccātirātrāveko'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ sarvastomaśca nava saptadaśaścātirātrau dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca iti yamātirātrā uktāḥ | prāyaṇīyodayanīyāvatirātrau ṣoḍaśimantāvaṣoḍaśikau vā | prathamasyābhiplavasyāhna eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ | tatra goāyuṣī aikāhike | ete eva goāyuṣī rātrasatreṣvanyatra yamātirātrābhyaśca sāṃvatsarasaṃmitābhyaścetyuktatvāt ||
tatra goṣṭomasya viśeṣamāha -- 
yamātirātrāsu goṣṭomasya | dīrghaśravasasya loke kaṇvarathantaram bārhadukthasya dairghaśravasam | gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi aṣoḍaśiko'tirātraḥ || 1 ||
iti | ābhiplavikasya ṣaṣṭhasyāhno gośca bārhatatvādajāmyarthaṃ kaṇvarathantaraklṛptiḥ || 1 ||
āyuṣṭomasya viśeṣamāha -- 
āyuṣṭomasya maidhātithasya loke bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambhaṃ rauravasya maidhātithaṃ gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi | hārivarṇasyarkṣu saubharam aṣoḍaśiko'tirātraḥ ||2||
iti | goṣṭomasaṃbandhinaḥ saṃdherāyuṣaśca rāthantaratvād ajāmyarthaṃ
vārkajambham āyuṣyeva ca pṛṣṭhyasaṃdhyo rathantarayorajāmyarthaṃ hārivarṇasya sthāne tasyarkṣu saubharaṃ vihitam | śrūyate hi yadi rathantarasāmnā saubharaṃ kuryādajāmitā iti || 2||
satrāṇiekānnapañcāśadrātraṃ dvitīyam abhijito'tirātrasya viśeṣamāha -- 
abhijito yathā pūrdaśarātra evaṃ bṛhatī gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi hārivarṇasyarkṣu saubharam | ṣoḍaśimānatirātraḥ || 3 ||
iti | atrāpi kaṇvarathantarasaubharayorajāmyarthaṃ klṛptiḥ || 3 || viśvajitsarvastomayoḥ klṛptimāha -- 
klṛpto viśvajit || 4||
klṛptaḥ sarvastomaḥ || 5 ||
iti | ekāhaprakaraṇe klṛptāvetau | tayoḥ sātrikī rātriḥ || 5 || navasaptadaśasyātirātrasya viśeṣamāha -- 
navasaptadaśasyābhi somāsa āyavaḥ iti janitraṃ tisṛṣu | rauravamekasyām bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambhamekasyām | maidhātithamekasyām yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyām | gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi | aṣoḍaśiko'tirātraḥ || 6 ||
iti | atrāpyajāmyarthaṃ bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambhaṃ tadvaśenāguntuno maidhātithasya kalpanaṃ sāmatṛcakaraṇaṃ ca| uktamanyat ||6||
iti ekānnapañcāśadrātraṃ dvitīyaṃ (yamātirātrāḥ) samāptam ||48||
āñjanābhyajanāḥ
tṛtīyamekānnapañcāśadrātram
atirātraścatvāro'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahāḥ sarvastomo'tirātro dvāvabhiplavau ṣaḍahau dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ ityañjanābhyañjanā uktāḥ | āñjanābhyañjanāsu gārhapatya upasatsu ityādi sūtram ||
klṛptā āñjanābhyañjanāḥ || 1||
iti | prathamasyābhiplavasya prathamasyāhnaḥ eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ | uttamābhiplavau pratilomau | etenokte vidhṛtyādīnāṃ tṛtīyaṣaṣṭhe iti vacanāt | sarve'bhiplavāḥ śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānaḥ pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ| prajñātamitaratsarvam || 1 ||
iti āñjanābhyañjanāḥ ||
tṛtīyamekānnapañcāśadātram samāptam || 49||
saṃvatsarasaṃmitāḥ
caturthamekānnapañcāśadrātramekaṣaṣṭirātraṃ ca
atirātraścaturviṃśaṃ prāyaṇīyamahastrayo'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahāḥ abhijitttrayaḥ svarasāmāno divākīrtyamahastrayaḥ svarasāmāno viśvajideko'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ āyuśca gauśca dve ahanī dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca saṃvatsarasaṃmitāḥ uktāḥ ||
saṃvatsaraklṛptāḥ saṃvatsarasaṃmitāḥ ekaṣaṣṭirātraṃ ca || 1 ||
iti | gavāmayanena klṛptā ityarthaḥ| ataścābhiplavāḥ abhīvartabrahmasāmānaḥ sujñānauṣṇihāḥ| uktaṃ ca -- śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmapauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇiho anyatra saṃvatsarasaṃmitābhya iti |
satrāṇi -- ekānnapañcāśadrātraṃ pañcamam goāyuṣī ca sāṃvatsarike | uktaṃ ca -- ete eva goāyuṣī rātrisatreṣvanyatra yamātirātrābhyaśca saṃvatsarasaṃmitābhyaśca iti | tṛtīye'bhiplave iṣovṛdhīyasaṃmate kuryānna vā kuryāditi vicāre kuryādeveti siddhānto nidāne sthāpitaḥ | yastu viśvajitaḥ paro'bhiplava āvṛttastasya prathame'hani yaṇvāpatyasaṃtaniśākvaravarṇānām , katamat kuryāditi vicāre yaṇvaṃ tu syāditi nirṇayaḥ sūtroktaḥ || 1 ||
iti saṃvatsarasaṃmitāḥ samāptāḥ ||
caturthamekānnapañcāśadrātramekaṣaṣṭirātraṃ ca samāptam || 50 ||
pañcamamekānnapañcāśadrātram
savituḥ kakubho vā
atirātro nava trivṛntyahānyagniṣṭomamukhaḥ ṣaḍaho'tha yāni trīṇyahāni agniṣṭomāvabhitaḥ ukthyaṃ madhyato navapañcadaśānyahānyagniṣṭomamukhaḥ ṣaḍaho'tha yāni trīṇyahānyagniṣṭomāvabhita ukthyaṃ madhyato nava saptadaśānyahāni agniṣṭomamukhaḥ ṣaḍaho'tha yāni trīṇyahānyagniṣṭomāvabhita ukthyaṃ madhyato navaikaviṃśānyagniṣṭomamukhaḥ ṣaḍaho'tha yāni trīṇyahānyagniṣṭomāvabhita ukthyaṃ madhyato dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca iti savituḥ kakubha uktāḥ | tatra prāyaṇīyādatirātrāt parāṇi nava trivṛntyahāni | teṣāmādyamagniṣṭomasaṃstham | pañcokthāni | avaśiṣṭe tryahe prathamottame ahanī agniṣṭomasaṃsthe | madhyamamukthyam | evaṃ pañcadaśānyapi navāhāni draṣṭavyāni | stotraklṛptimāha -- 
vidhṛtibhiḥ klṛptāḥ savituḥ kakubhaḥ ||1||
iti | tatra viśeṣamāha -- 
rāthanteraṇāhnā trivṛtaḥ pratipadyate | bārhatena pañcadaśān | rāthantareṇa saptadaśān | bārhatenaikaviṃśān || 2 ||
iti| pratipadyate arabhata ityarthaḥ | etaduktaṃ bhavati| vidhṛtiṣu trivṛdādīnāṃ sarveṣāṃ rāthantarairahobhirārambhaḥ | iha tu na tathā | kintu vaidhṛtaṃ yadrāthantaraṃ trivṛdahastadatra trivṛtaṃ prathamaṃ bārhataṃ dvitīyamityevaṃ vyatyāsena navamāntaṃ kuryāt | navamamaho rāthantaraṃ bhavati | tataścājāmyarthaṃ vaidhṛtaṃ yad yad bāhataṃ pañcadaśaṃ tadatra pañcadaśānāṃ prathamaṃ rāthantaraṃ dvitīyamityevaṃ vyatyāsena navapañcadaśāni kuryāt | bārhataṃ navamaṃ bhavati | atha vaidhṛtena rāthantareṇa saptadaśena saptadaśānārabhya vyatyāsamā navamāt kuryāt | bārhatenaikaviṃśenaikaviṃśāniti || 2 ||
yatrāgniṣṭomo bārhatamaharyauktāśvaṃ madhyaṃdine hāviṣmatamārbhavaḥ || 3||
iti | uktena prakāreṇa rāthantarabārhatayorahnorvyatyāsena kriyamāṇayoryatrāgniṣṭomasaṃsthaṃ bārhatamaharbhavati | yathā pañcadaśaikaviṃśeṣu ca prathamasaptamanavamāni | tatra teṣāṃ madhyaṃdine pavamāne hāviṣmatasya sthāne yauktāśvaṃ kāryam |
satrāṇi -- ekānnapañcāśadrātre ṣaṣṭhasaptame ārbhave maukṣasya sthāne hāviṣmatam | tathā ca kusurubindadaśarātre nidānam bārhateṣvahaḥsu yauktāśvahāviṣmate agniṣṭomeṣu hāviṣmatamaukṣa uktheṣu | maukṣa ukthasāmeti || 3 ||
uddharatyarkaṃ yo vidhṛtiṣu || 4 ||
iti | vidhṛtiṣu ekaviṃśānāṃ navamādyahaḥsu trayo'rkāḥ vihitāḥ | tatra daśamaikādaśayoratrābhāvāt dvarkau luptau | yo'pi navame'gnerarko vihitaḥ svādiṣṭhayeti gāyatramagneścārkaḥ iti tamuddhṛtya saṃhitaṃ tasya sthāne kuryāt | daśarātre'rkāṇāṃ kārtsnyena | kariṣyamāṇatvādityarthaḥ| tathā ca nidānam -- uddharatyarkaṃ yo vidhṛtiṣu daśarātre hyarkān kariṣyan bhavatīti ||4||
samānamitaram ||5||
iti | vidhṛtibhiriti śeṣaḥ || 5 ||
iti pañcamamekānnapañcāśadrātram (savituḥ kakubho vā) samāptam ||51||
ṣaṣṭhasaptame ekānnapañcāśadrātre
atirātraścatvāro'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā mahāvrataṃ dvāvabhiplavau ṣaḍahau dvādaśāhasya daśāhānyatirātraḥ iti ṣaṣṭhamekānnapañcāśadrātramuktam |
klṛptamā madhyepṛṣṭhyād ā madhyepṛṣṭhyād ||1||
iti | āvṛttāvuttamābhiplavau | prajñātamitarat ||
atirātraḥ ṣaḍabhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca iti saptamamekānnapañcāśadrātrasya sarvamuktam||1||
iti ṣaṣṭhasaptame ekānnapañcāśadrātre samāpte || 52 ||
ekaṣaṣṭirātram
atirātraścaturviṃśaṃ prāyaṇīyamahastrayo'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahāḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaho'bhijit trayaḥ svarasāmāno divākīrtyamahastrayaḥ svarasāmāno viśvajit pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahastrayastriṃśārambhaṇa eko'bhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaha āyuśca gauśca dve ahanī dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca iti ekaṣaṣṭirātram| tasya gavāmayanavadahāni kartavyāni| saṃvatsaraklṛptāḥ saṃvatsarasaṃmitā ekaṣaṣṭirātraṃ ca ityuktatvāt | āvṛtte'bhiplave ca svayamevaitenoktam | ekaṣaṣṭirātram iti vacanāt ||
iti ekaṣaṣṭirātram ||
śatarātram
atirātro jyotirgaurāyustryahaścaturdaśābhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahā dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca 
satrāṇi -- saṃvatsararātram
iti śatarātramuktam | prathamasyābhiplavasya prathamasyāhnaḥ eṣa pratnānurūpaḥ| abhiplavāḥ śyaitanaudhasabrahmasāmānaḥ pauṣkalaśrudhyauṣṇihāḥ | sāṃvatsarikaṃ vratam | siddhamitarat ||
iti śatarātram ||
saṃvatsararātram
atha saṃvatsarasatrāṇi ṣaṭ | atirātraścaturviṃśaṃ prāyaṇīyamahar ityārabhya saṃvatsarabrāhmaṇaṃ brāhmaṇam ityantena gavāmayanamuktam | tasya klṛptiḥ pūrvamevoktā ||
iti saṃvatsararātraṃ samāptam ||
iti śrīvāmanāryasutavaradarājaviracitāyāmārṣeyakalpavyākhyāyāṃ satreṣu prathamo'dhyāya ādito navamaḥ samāptaḥ ||9||
<10>
madhyepṛṣṭhyasya kḷpto'tirātraḥ kḷptaṃ caturvim̐śamaharābhiplavikasya prathamasyāhnaḥ pra somaso vipaścita iti gāyatramāśvamāśu bhārgavaṃ pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ gauṅgavaṃ yaudhājayaṃ ta ekarcāḥ samānamitaraṃ trivṛddvitīyasyāhnaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti mādhucchandasamekasyāṃ bharga ekasyāmaiḍamāyāsyamekasyāṃ triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu samānamitaraṃ pañcadaśaṃ tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ sa na indrāya yajyava iti gāyatraṃ ca vairūpaṃ ca samānamitaraṃ trivṛccaturthasyāhnastavāham̐ soma rāraṇeti pṛśnyekasyāmātharvaṇamekasyāmābhīśavamekasyāṃ yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu samānamitaraṃ pañcadaśaṃ pañcamasyāhno'psā indrāya vāyava iti gāyatraṃ ca mārgīyavaṃ ca vāmreṇa bṛhatīmārabhate samānamitaraṃ trivṛt ṣaṣṭhasyāhna eṣa vājī hito nṛbhiriti purastātparyāsasya ṣaḍṛcam̐ samanamitaraṃ pañcadaśaṃ kḷptamā bṛhaspatistomāt tasyā bhīvarto brahmasāma sujñānamuṣṇihi gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi samānamitaraṃ kḷptamendrastomāttasye ndrakratau śyaitaṃ gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi samānamitaraṃ kḷptamā vyūḍhebhyo'gniṣṭomebhyasteṣāṃ kḷptaṃ prathamamaharyathā dvādaśāham 101
dvitīyasyāhna stotrīyānurūpau paryāso vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ yauktāśvam̐ svāram̐ sauparṇaṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti āyāsye antarā dairghaśravasaṃ ta ekarcāḥ samānamitaraṃ trivṛttṛtīyasyāhna strotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ sa na indrāya yajyava iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca samānamitaraṃ trivṛccaturthasyāhna stotrīyānurūpau paryāso nānadasya loke gaurīvitam̐ samānamitaraṃ trivṛtpañcamasyāhna stotrīyānurūpau paryāso 'rṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatramekasyāṃ yaṇvaṃ tisṛṣu soma u ṣvānaḥ sotṛbhirityānūpamekasyāmagnestriṇidhanaṃ viṣṭārapaṅktau bārhadgirasyarkṣu saṃtanyasā0vyam̐śurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣūktaṃ ca pavasva vājasātaya iti gaurīvitaṃ carṣabhaśca śākvaraḥ samānamitaraṃ trivṛt ṣaṣṭhasyāhna stotrīyānurūpau paryāsa indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatramekasyām̐ revatyastisṛṣu mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍamaukṣṇora0ndhramekasyaṃ goṣṭho viṣṭārapaṅktau pari svāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca vaidanvataṃ ca yannidhanavat somāḥ pavanta indava iti gaurīvitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca yatsvayoni samānamitaraṃ trivṛt 102
saptamasyāhna stotrīyo navarcasya tṛco'nurūpaḥ paryāso vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatramekasyāṃ mārgīyavamekasyāmaiḍam̐ sauparṇamekasyāṃ kāṇvasyarkṣu saṃtani yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārka uddharati dvipadāḥ aṣṭamasyāhna stotrīyo navarcasya tṛco'nurūpaḥ paryāsastava tya indo andhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca vilambasauparṇaṃ cābhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca gāyatrapārśvaṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ cācchidraṃ ca pavasva deva āyuṣagiti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāṃ cārko bhī no vājasātamamiti gaurīvitaṃ ca triṇidhanaṃ ca tvāṣṭrīṣāmoddharati dvipadā hinvanti sūramusraya itīḍānām̐ saṃkṣāro'ntyaḥ samānamitaraṃ trivṛnnavamasyāhna stotrīyo daśarcasya tṛco'nurūpaḥ paryāsaḥ pavamāno rathītama iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛcca parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti pṛṣṭhaṃ ca kaulmalabarhiṣaṃ ca devasthānaṃ ca dīrghatamasaścārkaḥ sāmarājamantyaṃ tvam̐ somāsi dhārayuriti gāyatraṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca pari tyam̐ haryatam̐ harimiti gaurīvitaṃ ca saṃkṛti co ddharati dvipadā upo ṣu jātamapturamiti pratīcīneḍaṃ kāśītamantyam̐ samānamitaraṃ trivṛt 103
daśamasyāhnaḥ pañcānāmahnāmanurūpāstraya ekarcā dvau tṛcāviti bahiṣpavamānamuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatramāmahīyavamābhīkaṃ punānaḥ soma dhārayetyantarotsedhaniṣedhau gaurīvitaṃ ta ekarcā ā jāgṛviriti yajñāyajñīyamantyam uddharati dvipadāḥ samānamitaraṃ trivṛdudbhicca valabhicca dve ahanī tasyodbhido maidhātithasya loke bṛhannidhanamvārkajambham̐ rauravasya maidhātithamubhayorgaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi samānamitaraṃ kḷptamāvṛttebhyo'bhiplavebhya āvṛttānāmabhiplavānāṃ prathamasyābhiplavasya prathamasyāhno mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍamaukṣṇorandhramekasyāṃ kaṇvarathaṃtaramekasyāṃ varuṇasāmaikasyāṃ triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu samānamitaramuddharatyuttarebhyaḥ kaṇvarathaṃtaravaruṇasāmanī tṛcakḷptā bṛhatī sāṃvatsarike goāyuṣī tayoraikāhike bṛhatyau 104
asya pratnāmanu dyutamete somā abhi priyam̐ somaḥ punāno arṣati somā asṛgramindavaḥ pavasva vṛṣṭimā su naḥ pra te dharā asaścataḥ pavamānasya te kave 'gna ā yāhi vītaya ityājyāni pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ cāśvaṃ ca somasāma cāāśu ca bhārgavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ cābhīkaṃ cai ḍaṃ ca sauparṇa m̐! rohitakūlīyaṃ ca pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca hārāyaṇaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ ca gauṅgavaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatra sam̐hite śaiśavaṃ ca bhāsaṃ ca kākṣīvataṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca pavasvendramaccheti saphākṣāre pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca vaiśvāmitraṃ cāsāvi soma indra ta iti yasya yoniḥ padanidhanaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyam̐ svāraṃ ca kautsamaurukṣayam adardaratsamasṛja iti etayoruttaramaudala gautame kāvamantyaṃ yajñayajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma 105
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunaiṣa devo amartya iti daśarcasya tṛcamuddharatyuttamameṣa u sya vṛṣā ratha eṣa vājī hito nṛbhireṣa kavirabhiṣṭuta utte śuṣmāso asthuḥ pavamānasya te vayamagniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyāni vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ ca surūpaṃ ca hariśrīnidhanaṃ cājigaṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇaṃ chandasyaṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ carṣabhaśca pāvamānaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca pṛṣṭhaṃ ca yaśaśca kaulmalabarhiṣamāyāsye vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ca yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca śāṃmadaṃ ca dāvasunidhanaṃ cāsitaṃ cāśvasūktaṃ caiṣa sya dhārayā sutaḥ pavasva devavītaya iti śaṅku sujñāne ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitaṃ caiḍaṃ ca kautsaṃ bhargaścāsitaṃ ca yadvāhiṣṭhīyaṃ ca krauñce eteṣāmeva prathamottame vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthāni 106
davidyutatyā rucaite asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyāsṛgramindavaḥ pathā pavamānā asṛkṣatā paghnanpavate mṛdha iṣe pavasva dhārayā gnināgniḥ samidhyata ityājyānyuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca saumitraṃ cai ṭataṃ ca sākamaśvaṃ ca vilambasauparṇaṃ cābhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgaṃ ca sadoviśīyaṃ ca gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ cācchidraṃ ca maidhātithaṃ cāṣkāraṇidhanaṃ ca kāṇvaṃ tisro vāca ityaṅgirasām̐ saṃkrośo'ntyo rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca pāṣṭhauhamaiḍam̐ saindhukṣitamauśana vairūpe sve uṣṇikkakubhau sutāso madhumattamā iti gaurīvitaṃ ca sādhraṃ ca śyāvāśvaṃ ca trīṇi ca tvāṣṭrīsāmānyaidam̐ svāraṃ triṇidhanaṃ pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthāni 107
pavamāno ajījanatpunāno akramīdabhi pra yadgāvo na bhūrnaya āśurarṣa bṛhanmate babhrave nu svatavase pra svānāso rathā iva hinvanti sūramusrayo 'gnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanaditi hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇi pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatraṃ cātharvaṇaṃ ca nidhanakāmamadārasṛtsatrāsāhīyaṃ tavāham̐ soma rāraṇetyāṣṭādam̐ṣṭraṃ cārkapuṣpaṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ ca māṇḍavaṃ ca vidāvasviti yasya nidhanamābhīśavaṃ ca svaḥpṛṣṭhaṃ cāṅgirasam̐ somaḥ pavate janitā matīnāmiti janitramantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traikrośaṃ ca pṛśni ca pari priyā divaḥ kaviriti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca bṛhacca bhāradvājaṃ mārgīyavaṃ ca yadīnidhanam̐ sve uṣṇikkakubhau purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurivitaṃ ca vārkajambhaṃ yadīnidhanam̐ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca yadiḍābhiraiḍamākūparaṃ ca yannavame'hanyāndhīgavaṃ pra ta āśvinīḥ pavamāna dhenava iti lauśamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthānyuddharati ṣoḍaśinam 108
utte śuṣmāsa īrata iti purastātparyāsasya pañcarcam agne stomaṃ manāmaha iti hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇi svā gāyatrī somā u ṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhiriti mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ ca vaiṣṇave ca tavāham̐ soma rāraṇeeteṣāṃ prathame yauktasrucaṃ vāmramagnestriṇidhanamindurvājī pavate gonyoghā iti saṃpāntyā rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca bārhadgiraṃ ca rāyovājīyaṃ cāsāvyam̐śurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca saṃtani cādhyardheḍaṃ ca somasāma gauṣūktaṃ ca sve uṣṇikkakubau pavasva vājasātaya iti gaurīvitaṃ carṣabhaśca śākvarastvāṣṭrīsāma ca yaddvyanutodaṃ trāsadasyavamaṣṭeḍaḥ padastobho govitpavasveti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthāni pavasva devavīratīti daśarcasya tṛcamuddharatyuttamamaiḍamaukṣṇora0ndhramuddharati svāraṃ vaidanvatamuddharati gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi atha yadeva pṛṣṭhyastomasya ṣaṣṭhamahastadetattṛcakḷptaṃ trim̐śam 109
ye somāsaḥ parāvati pra svānāso rathā ivāsṛgramindavaḥ pathā yavaṃyavaṃ no andhasāte dakṣaṃ mayobhuvamṛtāvānaṃ vaiśvānaramiti hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇi svā gāyatrī punānaḥ soma dhārayeti somasāma ca kaṇvarathaṃtaraṃ ca paurumadgaṃ ca gaugavaṃ ca dvinidhanaṃ cāyāsyaṃ pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa iti vārāhamantyam̐ svāni pṛṣṭhāni yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ tisṛṣvagnerarka ekasyāṃ jarābodhīyamekasyāṃ dakṣaṇidhanamekasyām̐ sve uṣṇikkakubhau purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca kārtayaśaṃ cātreyaṃ ca gautamaṃ ca dvipadāsu sauhaviṣampro ayāsīdityaiḍaṃ yajñasārathyantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthāni 1010
hinvanti sūramusraya ete somā abhi priyam̐ somaḥ punāno arṣatyutte śuṣmāso asthurayā vītī pari srava viśvebhiragne agnibhiriti hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇyadhvaryo adribhiḥ sutamiti gāyatraṃ ca vairūpaṃ cāśu ca bhārgavaṃ vilambasauparṇaṃ cābhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca gāyatrapārśvaṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca hārāyaṇaṃ cācchidramu hu vā i śiśumiti vāsiṣṭhamantyam̐ svāni pṛṣṭhāni pavasva deva āyuṣagiti gāyatraṃ tisṛṣu svāśirāmarka ekasyāṃ kākṣīvatamekasyāṃ bhāsamekasyām̐ sve uṣṇikkakubhāvabhī no vājasātamamiti gaurīvitaṃ caiḍaṃ ca kautsamaudalaṃ ca rayiṣṭhaṃ ca svāsu dharma hā u dhartetyaiḍam̐ śārgamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthāni 1011
upo ṣu jātamapturameṣa devo amartyaḥ sa sutaḥ pītave vṛṣāyā pavasva dhārayā pavamānasya te kava upa tvā raṇvasaṃdṛśamiti hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇi pavamānasya jighnata iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛcceḍānāṃ ca saṃkṣāra ṛṣabhaśca pāvamānaḥ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti devasthānaṃ ca saṃkṛti ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca dīrghatamasaścārko hā u hu vā akrāniti vāsiṣṭhamantyam̐ svāni pṛṣṭhāni tvam̐ somāsi dhārayuriti gāyatraṃ tisṛṣvāśvasūktamekasyāṃ pratīcīneḍaṃ kāśitamekasyām̐ hāviṣkṛtamekasyām̐ sve uṣṇikkakubhau pari tyam̐ haryatam̐ harimiti gaurīvitaṃ ca vāṅnidhanaṃ ca krauñcam̐ sādhram̐ svāsu vidharmāsāvi somo aruṣo vṛṣā harirityaiḍaṃ yāmamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthānyatha yadeva chandomavato daśarātrasya navamamahastadatra daśamaṃ tasya paryū ṣviti gaurīvitamekasyāmauṣṇihamokonidhanamekasyām̐ śyāvāśvamekasyāmāndhīgavaṃ tisṛṣu samānamitaraṃ mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca 1012
