madhyepṛṣṭhyamayanam
ādityānāmayanāparākhyam
atirātraścaturviṃśam | prāyaṇīyamahardvau trivṛtpañcadaśau ṣaḍahau pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍahau dvau trivṛtpañcadaśau ṣaḍahau sa māsaḥ sa dvitīyaḥ sa tṛtīyaḥ sa caturthaḥ sa pañcamastrayastrivṛtpañcadaśāḥ ṣaḍahāḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍahastrivṛd bṛhaspatistomastrayaḥ svarasāmāno divākīrtyamahastrayaḥ svarasāmānaḥ pañcadaśa indrastoma ukthyaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍahastrayastriṃśārambhaṇaḥ ekaḥ pañcadaśastrivṛt ṣaḍaho dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni vyūḍhā agniṣṭomāstrivṛta udbhicca balabhicca dve ahanī dvau pañcadaśatrivṛtau ṣaḍahau pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍaho dvau pañcadaśatrivṛtau ṣaḍahau sa māsaḥ sa dvitīyaḥ sa tṛtīyaḥ sa caturthaḥ pañcadaśatrivṛtṣaḍahaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍahaḥ pañcadaśatrivṛt ṣaḍaho gauścāyuśca dve ahanī chandomadaśāho'ṣṭācatvāriṃśaṃ prathamamaharatha catuścatvāriṃśaṃ catvāriṃśaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśaṃ dvātriṃśaṃ trim̐śaṃ dve aṣṭāvim̐śe pañcavim̐śaṃ caturvim̐śaṃ mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca | ādityānāṃ madhyepṛṣṭhyam ityanuvākena ādityānāmayanamuktam | tadeva madhyepaṣṭhyamityucyate | pratimāsaṃ madhyepṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍaho'treti kṛtvā ||
madhyepṛṣṭhyasya klṛpto'tirātraḥ | klṛptaṃ caturvim̐śamahaḥ ||1||
iti | atirātraḥ prakṛtaḥ| caturviṃśaṃ gavāmayanikam | idamahardvayaṃ ṣaṣṭhamāsapūrakatvena gaṇyate || 1 ||
ābhiplavikasya prathamasyāhnaḥ | pra somāso vipaścitaḥ iti gāyatramāśvam āśubhārgavam | pra soma devavītaye 
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyamayanam
pajraṃ gauṅgavaṃ yaudhājayam | ta ekarcāḥ | samānamitaraṃ trivṛt || 2 ||
iti | gāyatrībṛhatyau sāmatṛce klṛpte | auśanamantyamiti mādhyaṃdinaḥ ārbhavo'ntyavarjamekarcāḥ | samānamitaraṃ prathamenāhnābhiplavikena | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 2 ||
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti mādhucchandasamekasyām bharga ekasyām aiḍamāyāsyamekasyām | triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu | samānamitaraṃ pañcadaśam || 3|| .
iti | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti mādhucchandasaṃ bharga aiḍamāyāsyamiti sāmatṛcam | triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyamiti mādhyaṃdinaḥ | uttareṣvabhiplaveṣu hāviṣmatasya sthāne yauktāśvamuttaram | bhargasthāne yaśaḥ | uttarasmin pakṣasi ārbhave śaṅkusujñāne | gaurīvitānyekarceṣu | itaradgavāmayanasyābhiplavasya dvitīyenāhnā samam | pañcadaśaṃ sarvam || 3 ||
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ sa na indrāya yajyava iti gāyatraṃ ca vairūpaṃ ca || 4 ||
samānamitaraṃ trivṛt || 5 ||
iti | uttarayoḥ pavamānayoḥ antyavarjamekarcāḥ | samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnābhiplavikena | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 5 ||
caturthasyāhnastavāhaṃ soma rāraṇeti pṛśnyekasyām ātharvaṇamekasyām ābhīśavamekasyām yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | samānamitaram | pañcadaśam || 6||
iti | pavasva dakṣasādhana iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛt | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇeti pṛśnyātharvaṇamābhīśavamiti sāmatṛco yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu | uhu vā asyeti vāsiṣṭhamantyam iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ | ārbhave kakubuṣṇihau gaurīvitaṃ caikarcāḥ | samānamitaraṃ caturthenābhiplavikena | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam || 6 ||
paścamasyāhno'psā indrāya vāyava iti gāyatraṃ ca mārgīyavaṃ ca | vāmreṇa bṛhatīmārabhate | samānamitaram | trivṛt || 7 ||
iti | apsā indrāyeti gāyatraṃ ca mārgīyavaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyava iti vāmraṃ ca mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ ca | agneśca triṇidhanam | abhi tripṛṣṭham iti sampāntyeti mādhyaṃdinaḥ| antyavarjamekarcāḥ | yaṇvādīnāṃ saṃtanitvena tṛca eva kartavyatvādekarcakaraṇāyogādihoddhāraḥ| tathā ca santaniprakaraṇe sūtram -- ekarceṣu coddhṛtaṃ kalpe iti| vāmreṇa bṛhatyārambhaṇamajāmyarthamiḍārambhaṇārthaṃ ca | ārbhave'ntyaṃ tṛce abhiplavasya pañcamenāhnā| samānamitarat || 7 ||
satrāṇi -- madhyapṛṣṭhyamayanam
ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ eṣa vājī hato nṛbhir iti purastāt paryāyasya ṣaḍṛcam | samānamitaram | pañcadaśam || 8 ||
iti | asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ eṣa vājī hato nṛbhiḥ pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam| ārbhave kakubuṣṇihau gaurīvitaṃ caikarcāḥ | dvitīye'bhiplave iṣovṛdhīyasamante pṛṣṭhyānantaryāt | samānamitarat ṣaṣṭhenābhiplavikena | sarvaṃ pañcadaśam || 8 |
dvāvabhiplavau pṛṣṭhyaḥṣaḍahaḥ punaśca dvāvabhiplavāviti prathamo māsaḥ | tathānye catvāro māsāḥ| ṣaṣṭhe māse trayo'bhiplavāḥ| pṛṣṭhyaśca pūrvavat |
tadāha -- 
klṛptaṃ bṛhaspatistomāt || 9 ||
iti ||9||
bṛhaspatisavasya viśeṣamāha -- 
tasyābhīvarto brahmasāma | sujñānamuṣṇihi gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi | samānamitaram || 10 ||
iti || 10||
athāvṛttasvarasāmaparyantaṃ gavāmayanavat | tadāha -- 
klṛptamindrastomāt || 11 ||
iti ||11||
indrastomasya viśeṣamāha -- 
tasyendrakratau śyaitaṃ . gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi |
samānamitaram || 12 ||
iti || 12||
atha gavāmayanikaḥ pṛṣṭhyānantaroktayābhiplava āvṛttaḥ | tadāha -- 
klṛptamā vyūḍhebhyo agniṣṭomebhyaḥ || 13 ||
iti| dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni vyūḍhā agniṣṭomāstrivṛta iti śrutyuktā vyūḍhā agniṣṭomā daśa || 13 ||
tān kramādāha -- 
teṣāṃ klṛptaṃ prathamamaharyathā dvādaśāham ||14||
prathamamahardvādaśāhavadavikṛtam || 14 ||
iti prathamamahaḥ || 1||
dvitīyamahaḥ
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāso vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ yauktāśvaṃ svāraṃ sauparṇam | punānaḥ soma dhārayetyāyāsye antarā dairghaśravasam | ta ekarcāḥ | samānamitaram | trivṛt ||1||
iti| pavasva vācopavasvendo pavamānasya te vayam iti bahiṣpavamānam | madhyaṃdine gāyatrībṛhatyau sāmatṛcaklṛpta ārbhave'ntyavarjamekarcāḥ | samānamitarad dvitīyenāhnā dvādaśāhasya | sarvaṃ trivat | sarveṣu ahaḥsūktānāmuddhāraḥ || 1||
iti dvitīyamahaḥ ||2||
tṛtīyamahaḥ
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ sa na indrāya yajyava iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca | samānamitaram | trivṛt || 1||
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyamayanam
iti| davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavaḥ iṣe pavasva dhārayeti bahiṣpavamānam | sa na indrāya yajyava iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca ekarcayoḥ | abhi somāsa āyavaḥ iti paurumadgaṃ ca gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca sāmatṛcaḥ | āṣkāraṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām | tisro vāca iti aṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ | ādyameva tisṛṣu | itarāṇi ādyāsu | samānamitaraṃ tṛtīyenāhnā dvādaśāhasya | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 16 ||
iti tṛtīyamahaḥ ||
caturthamahaḥ
caturthasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāso nānadasya loke gaurīvitam | samānamitaram | trivṛt || 17 ||
iti | pra ta āśvinīḥ pavamāno ajījanat hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam | mādhyaṃdine gāyatrī bṛhatyau sāmatṛcau| antyaṃ tṛce | ārbhave antyavarjamādyāsu | ukthānāṃ ṣoḍaśinaścoddhāraḥ | samānamitaraṃ caturthenāhnā dvādaśāhasya | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 17 ||
iti caturthamahaḥ ||
pañcamamahaḥ
pañcamasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāso'rṣā soma dyumattamaḥ iti gāyatramekasyām | yaṇvaṃ tisṛṣu | soma uṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhiḥ ityānūpamekasyām | agnestriṇidhanaṃ viṣṭārapaṅktau | bārhadgirasyarkṣu saṃtani | asāvyaṃśurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca goṣūktaṃ ca | pavasva vājasātaye iti gaurīvitaṃ ca | ṛṣabhaśca śākvaraḥ || 1||
samānamitaram | trivṛt || 18||
iti | govitpavasva pavamānasya viśvajit yāsta iti bahiṣpavamānam |
asāvyaṃśurbhadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca gauṣaktaṃ ca| abhidyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti cyāvanakrośe | pavasva vājasātaye iti gaurīvitaṃ ca ṛṣabhaśca śākvaraḥ | indurvājīti dāśaspatyamityārbhavaḥ | antyavarjamādyāsu | samānamitaraṃ pañcamenāhnā dvādaśāhasya | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 18||
ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāsa indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatramekasyām | revatyastisṛṣu mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍamaukṣṇorandhramekasyām | goṣṭho viṣṭārapaṅktau parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca vaidanvataṃ ca yannidhanavat | somāḥ pavanta indavaḥ iti gaurīvitaṃ ca
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyamayanam
krauñcaṃ ca yat svayoni || samānamitaram | trivṛt || 16 ||
iti | jyotiryajñasya asṛkṣata ete somā iti bahiṣpavamānam | indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatramekasyām | revatyastisṛṣu | mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyeti aiḍamaukṣṇorandhamekasyām | iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ| goṣṭho viṣṭārapaṅktau | parisvāno giriṣṭhāḥ iti gāyatraṃ ca | vaidanvataṃ ca yannidhanavat | sasunveyo vasūnāmtaṃ naḥ sakhāyo madāyeti dīrghakarṇaśravase somāḥ pavanta indavaḥ iti svayoni ca krauñcam | ayā pavā pavasveti śnauṣṭamantyamityārbhavaḥ | antyavarjamekarcāḥ| samānamitaraṃ ṣaṣṭhenāhnā dvādaśāhasya| sarvaṃ trivṛt || 19 ||
iti ṣaṣṭhamahaḥ ||
saptamamahaḥ
saptamasyāhnaḥ stotrīyo navarcasya tṛco'nurūpaḥ paryāsaḥ | vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatramekasyām | mārgīyavamekasyām aiḍaṃ sauparṇamekasyām kāṇvasyarkṣu saṃtani | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ | uddharati dvipadāḥ | samānamitaram trivṛt ||20||
iti | pra kāvyaṃ prasūnvānāya ā te dakṣam iti trayastṛcā bahiṣpavamānam | vṛṣā pavasva
dhārayeti gāyatraṃ mārgīyavam aiḍaṃ sauparṇamiti sāmatṛcaḥ| punānaḥ soma dhārayeti kaṇvarathantaraṃ gauṅgavamāyāsya miti sāmatṛcaḥ | pro ayāsīditi pravadbhārgavamantyam | iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ| vayaṃ gha tveti santani brahmasāma | yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārkaḥ | eṣa sya dhārayā sutaḥ sakhāya ā niṣīdateti śārkaraplavau purojitīti gaurīvitaṃ ca kārtayaśaṃ ca| ye somāsa iti jarābodhīyam ityārbhavaḥ| antyameva tṛcam | samānamitaraṃ saptamenāhnā dvādaśāhasyeti sarvaṃ trivṛt || 20 ||
iti saptamamahaḥ ||
aṣṭamamahaḥ
aṣṭamasyāhnaḥ stotrīyo navarcasya tṛco'nurūpaḥ paryāsaḥ | tava tya indo andhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca vilambasauparṇaṃ cābhisomāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca gāyatrapārśvaṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ cācchidraṃ ca | pavasva deva āyuṣag iti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarko abhī no vājasātamam iti gaurīvitaṃ ca triṇidhanaṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāma | uddharati dvipadāḥ | hinvanti sūramusraya itīḍānāṃ saṃkṣāro'ntyaḥ | samānamitaram | trivṛt || 21 ||
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyamayanam
iti | śiśuṃ jajñānam ete somā abhipriyam ayā pavasva dhārayeti trayastṛcā bahiṣpavamānam | tava tya indo andhasa iti gāyatraṃ vilambasauparṇaṃ caikarcayoḥ| abhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ gāyatrapārśvaṃ pauruhanmanamiti sāmatṛco'cchidramadhyāsyāyām | dhartā diva iti udvadbhārgavaṃ tisṛṣu | iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ | pavasva deva āyuṣagiti gāyatraṃ ca svāśirāmarkaḥ | abhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnām ityaiṣiratraite abhī no vājasātamamiti gaurīvitaṃ ca triṇidhanaṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāma ca | hinvanti sūramusraya iti iḍānāṃ saṃkṣāro'ntya ityārbhavaḥ| samānamitaramaṣṭamena | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 21 ||
iti aṣṭamamahaḥ ||
navamamahaḥ
navamasyāhnaḥ stotrīyo daśarcasya tṛco'nurūpaḥ paryāsaḥ | pavamāno rathītama iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛcca | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti pṛṣṭhaṃ ca kaulmalabarhiṣaṃ ca devasthānaṃ ca dīrghatamasaścārkaḥ sāmarājam antyam | tvaṃ somāsi dhārayuḥ iti gāyatraṃ cāśvasūktam ca | pari tyaṃ haryataṃ harim iti gaurīvitaṃ ca saṃkṛti ca | uddharati dvipadāḥ | upoṣu jātamapturam iti pratīcīneḍaṃ kāśītam antyam | samānamitaram | trivṛt || 22 ||
iti | akrāntsamudraḥ eṣa devo amartyaḥ pavamānasya te kave iti tṛcatrayaṃ bahiṣpavamānam | pavamāno rathītama iti gāyatramadārasṛccaikarcayoḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti pṛṣṭhaṃ kaulmalabarhiṣaṃ devasthānamiti sāmatṛco'dhyāsyāyām | dīrghatamaso'rkaḥ sāmarājamiti mādhyaṃdinaḥ | tvaṃ somāsi dhārayuriti gāyatraṃ cāśvasūktam | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya pavasva deva vītaya iti sauparṇaṃ ca vaiśvamanasaṃ ca| pari tyaṃ haryataṃ harimiti gaurīvitaṃ ca saṃkṛti ca| upoṣu jātamapturamiti pratīcīneḍaṃ kāśītamantyamityārbhavaḥ | antyavarjamekarcāḥ | samānamitaraṃ navamenāhnā dvādaśāhasya | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 22 ||
iti navamamahaḥ ||
daśamamahaḥ
daśamasyāhnaḥ pañcānāmahnāmanurūpāstraya ekarcā dvau tṛcāviti
bahiṣpavamānam | uccā te jātamandhasa iti
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyamayanam
gāyatramāmahīyavam ābhīkaṃ | punānaḥ soma dhārayetyantarotsedhaniṣedhau gaurīvitam | ta ekarcāḥ | ā jāgṛvir iti yajñāyajñīyamantyam | uddharati dvipadāḥ | samānamitaram | trivṛt || 23||
iti | asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ pavamānasya viśvavit pavamāno ajījanat iti traya ekarcāḥ| ete asṛgramindavaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ iti tṛcau bahiṣpavamānam | mādhyaṃdine gāyatrībṛhatyau sāmatṛcau| ārbhave sauhaviṣasyoddhāraḥ| antyameva tṛce | itarāṇyādyāsu | samānamitaraṃ daśamenāhnā dvādaśāhasya | sarvaṃ trivṛt | daśamadharmā mānasaṃ ca dvādaśāhavat || 23 ||
udbhicca balabhicca dve ahanī | tasyodbhido maidhātithasya loke bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambhaṃ rauravasya maidhātitham ubhayorgaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi samānamitaram || 24||
iti | vyūḍhānāmagniṣṭomānāṃ daśamasyodbhidaśca rāthantaratvādajāmyarthaṃ bṛhannidhanavārkajambhabalabhidantānyaṣṭāviṃśatirahāni saptame māse vratātirātrau triṃśattamau || 24 ||
klṛptamāvṛttebhyo'bhiplavebhyaḥ || 25 ||
iti | abhividhyarthamādatra praśliṣṭaḥ || 25 ||
tatra viśeṣamāha -- 
āvṛttānāmabhiplavānāṃ prathamasyābhiplavasya prathamasyāhno mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍamaukṣṇorandhramekasyām | kaṇvarathantaramekasyām varuṇasāmaikasyām | triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu
| samānamitaram || 26 ||
iti | asyāhno balabhidaśca bārhatatvādajāmyarthaṃ kaṇvarathantaraṃ tadvaśācceha varuṇasāma kalpitam || 26 ||
uddharatyutterabhyaḥ kaṇvarathantaravaruṇasāmāni | tṛcaklṛptā bṛhatī || 27 ||
iti | uttarebhyo'bhiplavebhyaḥ kaṇvarathantaravaruṇasāmāni uddharet | jāmyarthabhāvāt | tataśca mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍaṃ cākṣṇorandhraṃ triṇidhanaṃ cāyāsyamiti tṛcābhyāmeva klṛptā bṛhatītyarthaḥ | tatrābhiplavābhyāṃ pṛṣṭhyena punaścābhiplavābhyāmityāvṛttaiḥ ṣaḍahairaṣṭamo māsaḥ| tathānye trayaḥ| dvādaśe'pi māse'bhiplavaḥ pṛṣṭhyo'bhiplavaḥ ityabhiṣiviṃśatirahāni | viṃśe goāyuṣī || 27||
tayoḥ klṛptimāha -- 
sāṃvatsarike goāyuṣī | tayoraikāhike bṛhatyau || 28 ||
iti | sāṃvatsarebhya āyurgauriti kramaḥ| tatrājāmyarthaṃ vārkajambhakalpanamityuktam | iha tu tayorvyatihārājjāmyabhāvāt bṛhatyāvaikāhikyau |
satrāṇimadhyepṛṣṭhyam
tadyathā gaurvārkajambhasya loke dairghaśravasasya bārhadukthamiti āyuṣo vārkajambhasya loke maidhātithaṃ tasya rauravamiti ca || |
iti prathamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ || 1 |
dvitīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
atha chandomadaśāhaḥ | tasya prathamasyāhnaḥ klṛptimāha -- 
asya pratnāmanudyutam ete somā abhi priyaṃ somaḥ punāno arṣati somā asṛgramindavaḥ pavasva vṛṣṭimāsu naḥ pra te dhārā asaścataḥ pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna ā yāhi vītaye ityājyāni ||2||
pra somāso vipaścitaḥ gāyatraṃ cāśvaṃ ca somasāma cāśubhārgavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ cābhīkaṃ caiḍaṃ ca sauparṇaṃ rohitakūlīyaṃ ca | pra soma devavītaye pajraṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca hārāyaṇaṃ ca dvihiṃkāraṃ ca gauṅgaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanam antyam || 2 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ]
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca || 3 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite śaiśavaṃ ca bhāsaṃ kākṣīvataṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ ca | pavasvendramaccheti saphākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca vaiśvāmitraṃ ca | asāvi soma indra ta iti yasya yoniḥ| padanidhanaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ svāraṃ ca kautsam aurukṣayam | adardarutsamasṛja ityetayoruttaram | audalagautame | kāvam antyam || 4 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 5 ||
sarvamaṣṭācatvāriṃśam || 5 ||
iti dvitīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ || 2||
tṛtīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ -- 
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā
satrāṇimadhyepṛṣṭhyam 
hyasi bhānunā eṣa devo amartya iti daśarcasya tṛcamuddharatyuttamam | eṣa u sya vṛṣā ratha eṣa vājī hito nṛbhir eṣa kavirabhiṣṭutaḥ utte śuṣmāso asthuḥ pavamānasya te vayam ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni ||2|| vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ ca surūpaṃ ca hariśrīnidhanaṃ cājigaṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇaṃ chandasyaṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ carṣabhaśca pavamānaḥ | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca pṛṣṭhaṃ ca yaśaśca kaulmalabarhiṣam āyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārtham antyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ || ]
samantadairghaśravase ādyāyām | anyāni tisṛṣu| mādhyaṃdine cedeko dvau vā bṛhatyāmeveti vacanāt| ekamekarcaṃ kurvan prathamāyāmāditaḥ kuryāt | dvau tasyāmeveti || 3||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ca || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca śāmmadaṃ ca dāvasunidhanaṃ cāsitaṃ cāśvasūktaṃ caiṣa sya dhārayā sutaḥ pavasva deva vītaye śaḍkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhagaḥ iti gaurīvitaṃ caiḍaṃ ca kautsam bhargaścāsitaṃ ca yadvāhiṣṭhīyaṃ ca krauñce | eteṣāmeva prathamottame | vṛṣāmatīnāṃ pavata iti yāmam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ || ]
śaṅkusujñāne ekarcayoḥ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||6||
svānyukthāni || 7 ||
sākamaśvam evā hyasi vīrayurityāmahīyava māṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ catuścatvāriṃśam || 7||
iti tṛtīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||3||
caturthaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
tṛtīyasyāhnaḥ -- 
davidyutatyā rucaite asṛgrami davo rājā medhāmirīyate tantvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā asṛgramindavaḥ pathā pavamānā asṛkṣata apaghnan pavate mṛdha iṣe pavasva dhārayā ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyam 
agnināgniḥ samidhyate ityājyāni || 2||
uccā te jātamandhasaḥ iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca saumitraṃ caiṭataṃ ca sākamaśvaṃ ca vilambasauparṇaṃ ca | abhi somāsa āyavaḥ iti paurumadgaṃ ca sadoviśīyaṃ ca gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ cācchindraṃ ca maidhātithaṃ cāṣkāraṇidhanaṃ ca kāṇvam | tisro vāca ityāṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośo'ntyaḥ ||3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ|| ]
paurumadgaṃ prathamāyām ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca mahāviṣṭambhaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca||4||
iti pṛṣṭhāni |
tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ ca pāṣṭhauham aiḍaṃ saindhukṣitam auśanavairūpe sve uṣṇikkakubhau | ā sotā pari ṣiñcata sakhāya ā niṣīdateti vācaḥsāma śauktaṃ ca | sutāso madhumattamā iti gaurīvitaṃ ca sādhraṃ ca śyāvāśvaṃ ca trīṇi ca tvāṣṭrīsāmāni aiḍaṃ svāraṃ triṇidhanam pavitraṃ ta ityāriṣṭamantyam || 5 ||
gaurīvitamekarce| svāraṃ tvāṣṭrīsāma dvayorādyam || 5 ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 6 ||
svānyukthāni || 7||
pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścya mityukthāni | sarvaṃ catvāriṃśam | gautamaścatvāriṃśam iti sūtram | trayodaśabhyo hiṃkaroti| sa tisṛbhiḥ sa navabhiḥ sa ekayā| trayodaśabhyo hiṃkaroti sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiḥ sa navabhiḥ| trayodaśabhyo hikaroti sa navabhiḥ sa ekayā sa tisṛbhiriti
triṇavādyāvat kalpitā viṣṭutiḥ | naikānnacatvāriṃśena stutvā madhyamāmahiṃkṛtāṃ tṛcasya prastūyāt | sa āskando nāma || 7||
iti caturthaḥ khaṇḍaḥ || 4 ||
pañcamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
caturthasyāhnaḥ -- 
pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ āśurarṣa bṛhanmate babhrave nu svatavase pra svānāso rathā iva 
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyam
hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanat iti hoturājyam | svānyuttarāṇi ||2||
ityājyāni ||
ayaṃ vāmindro dadhīyaṃ vāmasyetyuttarāṇi ājyāni ||2||
pavasva dakṣasādhana gāyatraṃ cātharvaṇaṃ ca nidhanakāmam adārasṛt satrāsāhīyam | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇeti āṣṭādaṃṣṭraṃ cārkapuṣpaṃ ca bārhadukthaṃ ca māṇḍavaṃ ca vidāvasviti yasya nidhanam | ābhīśavaṃ ca svaḥpṛṣṭhaṃ cāṅgirasam | somaḥ pavate janitā matīnām iti janitram antyam ||3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca traiśokaṃ ca pṛśni ca || 4 ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
pari priyā divaḥ kavir iti gāyatraṃ caurṇāyavaṃ ca bṛhacca bhāradvājaṃ mārgīyavaṃ ca yadīnidhanam | sve uṣṇikkakubhau ||
tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya somaḥ punānaḥ ūrmiṇeti bṛhatkātīṣādīye ||
purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca vārkajambhaṃ yadīnidhanaṃ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca yadiḍābhiraiḍam ākūpāraṃ ca yannavame'hanyāndhīgavam | pra ta āśvinīḥ pavamāna dhenavaḥ iti lauśama antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 6 ||
svānyukthāni | uddharati ṣoḍaśinam ||7||
saindhukṣitaṃ saubharaṃ vasiṣṭhasya priyamityukthāni| sarvaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśam || 7 ||
iti pañcamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ || 5 ||
ṣaṣṭhaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
pañcamasyāhnaḥ
utte śuṣmāsa īrate iti purastāt paryāsasya
pañcarcam || 1||
pavamānasya viśvavit yatsoma citramukthyam pra somāso adhanviṣuḥ pra kavirdevavītaye yavaṃ yavaṃ no andhasā utte śuṣmāsa
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyam
īrate yāstedhārā madhuścutaḥ iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agne stomaṃ manāmahe iti hoturājyam |
svānyuttarāṇi ||2||
purūruṇā ciddhyasti uttiṣṭhannojasā saha indrāgnī yuvāmime ityājyāni ||2||
svā gāyatrī ||3||
arṣā soma dyumattama iti gāyatraṃ ca yaṇvaṃ ca śākalaṃ ca vārśaṃ ca ||
soma u ṣvāṇaḥ sotṛbhir iti mānavaṃ cānūpaṃ ca vaiṣṇave ca | tavāhaṃ soma rāraṇetyeteṣāṃ prathame | yauktasrucaṃ vāmram agnestriṇidhanam | indurvājī pavate gonyoghā iti sampāntyā || 4 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
mānavamādyāyām | bṛhatīprathamatvādvaiṣṇavottaraṃ ca ūhapāṭhaḥ || 4 ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca bārhadgiraṃ ca rāyovājīyaṃ ca ||5||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
asāvyaṃśurmadāyeti gāyatraṃ ca saṃtani cādhyardheḍaṃ ca somasāma gauṣūktaṃ ca sve uṣṇikkakubhau | abhi dyumnaṃ bṛhadyaśaḥ prāṇā śiśurmahīnām iti cyāvanakrośe ||
pavasva vājasātaye iti gaurīvitaṃ carṣabhaśca śākvaras tvāṣṭrīsāma ca yad dvyanutodam trāsadasyavam aṣṭeḍaḥ padastobhaḥ | govit pavasveti dvyabhyāghātaṃ lauśam antyam || 6||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
cyāvanakrośe ekarcayoḥ || 6||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma |
svānyukthāni || 7||
saṃjayaṃ saumitraṃ mahāvaiśvamitra mityukthāni | sarvaṃ dvātriṃśam || 7||
ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ
pavasva devavīratīti daśarccasya tṛcamuddharatyuttamam | aiḍamaukṣṇorandhramuddharati | svāraṃ vaidanvatamuddharati | gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi | atha yadeva pṛṣṭhyastomasya ṣaṣṭhamahastadetat tṛcaklṛptaṃ triṃśam || 8||
iti | asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavasva devavīrati | triṃśasya viṣṭutiḥ || 8||
iti ṣaṣṭhaḥ khaṇḍaḥ || 6 ||
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyam
saptamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
saptamasyāhnaḥ -- 
ye somāsaḥ parāvati pra svānāso rathā ivāsṛgramindavaḥ pathā yavaṃ yavaṃ no andhasā ā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam || ṛtāvānaṃ vaiśvānaram iti hoturājyam |
svānyuttarāṇi || 2||
pra vo mitrāyaindrā yāhi citrabhānotamīḍiṣvetyājyāni ||
svā gāyatrī ||3||
vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca santani caiḍaṃ sauparṇaṃ rohitakūlīyaṃ ca ||
punānaḥ soma dhārayeti somasāma ca kaṇvarathantaraṃ ca paurumadgaṃ ca gauṅgavaṃ ca dvinidhanamāyāsyam | pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa 
iti vārāham antyam ||4||
somasāmaikarcce ||4||
svāni pṛṣṭhāni || 5 ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ cābhinidhanaṃ ca kāṇvaṃ vaikhānasaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni || 5 ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ tisṛṣvagnerarka ekasyām jarābodhīyamekasyām | dakṣanidhanamekasyām | sve uṣṇikkakubhau ||
eṣa sya dhārayā sutaḥ sakhāya ā niṣīdateti śārkaraplavau ||
purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca kārtayaśaṃ cātreyaṃ ca gautamaṃ ca dvipadāsu sauha viṣam | pro ayāsīd ityaiḍaṃ yajñasārathyantyam ||6||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
gaurīvitamekasyām ||
yajñāyajñīyam agniṣṭomasāma ||
svānyukthāni ||7||
vātsapraṃ sauśravasaṃ vīṅkamityukthāni| sarvamaṣṭāviṃśam || 7 ||
iti saptamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||
aṣṭamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
aṣṭamasyāhnaḥ -- 
hinvanti sūramusrayaḥ ete somā abhi priyaṃ somaḥ punāno arṣati 
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyam
utte śuṣmāso asthur ayā vītī pari srava ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
viśvebhiragne agnibhir iti hoturājyam | svānyuttarāṇi ||2||
[ityājyāni ||]
āṣṭamikāni ||2||
adhvaryo adribhiḥ sutam iti gāyatraṃ ca vairūpaṃ cāśu ca bhārgavaṃ vilambasauparṇaṃ cābhi somāsa āyava iti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca gāyatrapārśvaṃ ca pauruhanmanaṃ ca hārāyaṇaṃ cācchidraṃ | u hu vā i śiśum iti vāsiṣṭham antyam || 3 ||
acchidramadhyāsyāyām || 3||
svāni pṛṣṭhāni || 4||
āṣṭamikāni ||
pavasva deva āyuṣag iti gāyatraṃ tisṛṣu | svāśirāmarka ekasyām | kakṣīvatamekasyām | bhāsamekasyām | sve uṣṇikkakubhau ||
āṣṭamikyau |
abhī no vājasātamam iti gaurīvitaṃ caiḍaṃ ca kautsam audalaṃ ca rayiṣṭhaṃ ca svāsu dharma | hā u dharte tyaiḍaṃ śārṅgam antyam || 5||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
gaurīvitamekasyām ||6||
yajñāyajñīyam agniṣṭomasāma ||
svānyukthāni || 6 ||
āṣṭamikāni | sarvamaṣṭāviṃśam || 6 ||
iti aṣṭamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||8||
navamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
navamasyāhnaḥ -- 
upo ṣu jātamapturam eṣa devo amartyaḥ sa sutaḥ pītaye vṛṣā ayā pavasva dhārayā pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
upa tvā raṇvasaṃdṛśam iti hoturājyam | svānyuttarāṇi || 2 ||
[ityājyāni ||]
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyam
nāvamikāni || 2||
pavamānasya jighnata iti gāyatraṃ cādārasṛcceḍānāṃ ca saṃkṣāraḥ ṛṣabhaśca pāvamānaḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti devasthānaṃ ca | saṃkṛti ca dairghaśravasaṃ ca dīrghatamasaścārko hā u hu vā akrān iti vāsiṣṭham antyam || 3 || dīrghatamaso'rko'dhyāsyāyām || 3 ||
svāni pṛṣṭhāni ||4||
nāvamikāni ||4||
tvaṃ somāsi dhārayur iti gāyatraṃ tisṛṣvāśvasūktamekasyām | pratīcīneḍaṃ kāśītamekasyām | hāviṣkṛtamekasyām | sve uṣṇikkakubhau ||
nāvamikyau ||
pari tyaṃ haryataṃ harim iti gaurīvitaṃ ca vāṅnidhanaṃ ca krauñcaṃ sādhraṃ svāsu vidharma | asāvi somo aruṣo vṛṣā harir iti aiḍaṃ yāmam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
gaurīvitamekasyām || 5 ||
yajñāyajñīyam agniṣṭomasāma |
svānyukthāni ||6||
nāvamikāni| sarvaṃ pañcadaśam || 6||
daśamasyāhnaḥ -- 
atha yadeva chandomavato daśarātrasya navamamahastadatra daśamam | tasya paryūṣu iti gaurīvitamekasyām | auṣṇihamokonidhanamekasyām śyāvāśvamekasyām | āndhīgavaṃ tisṛṣu ||7||
samānamitaram || 8 ||
iti | asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ pavamānasya viśvavit pavamāno ajījanad ete asṛgramindavaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā sutaḥ asya pratnāmanu dyutam iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
suṣamiddho na ā vaha yadadya sūra udite u tvā mandantu somā indrāgnī ā gataṃ sutam ityājyāni | suṣamiccaturṛce | yathāgotramuddhāraḥ |
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ cājigaṃ cābhīkaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti utsedhaśca maidhātithaṃ ca niṣedhaścājāgṛvirityauśanam antyam ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatra saṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti sapha rohitakūlīye | paryūṣviti
satrāṇi -- madhyepṛṣṭhyam
gaurīvitam adhyāsyāyām | auṣṇihamokonidhanaṃ dvitīyāyām | śyāvāśvaṃ tṛtīyāyām | āndhīgavaṃ tisṛṣu| para pra dhanveti vājajit sūryavatīṣu kāvamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | daśadharma mānasaṃ ca dvādaśāhavat | sarvāṇi stotrāṇi caturviṃśāni | atra kevalamṛkasāmānyeva daśamādatidiśyante| na stomo'pi | tena agniṣṭomamānase api caturviṃśe eveti sūtre nirṇayaḥ ||
mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca | mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca ||
iti sāṃvatsarikaṃ vratam | atirātraḥ prākṛtaḥ ||
iti navamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||
ādityānāmayanaṃ samāptam ||
iti śrīvāmanāryasutavaradarājaviracitāyām ārṣeyakalpavyākhyāyāṃ satreṣu dvitīyo'dhyāyaḥ ādito daśamaḥ ||10||
<11>
purastātpṛṣṭhyasya kḷpto'tirātraḥ kḷptaṃ caturvim̐śamahaḥ kḷptaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ kḷptaṃ prathamamaharabhiplavasya yathā madhyepṛṣṭhye dvitīyasyāhna stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti bṛhatī samānamitaraṃ trivṛtkḷptaṃ tṛtīyamaharyathā madhyepṛṣṭhye caturthasyāhna stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti bṛhatī samānamitaraṃ trivṛtkḷptaṃ pañcamamaharyathā madhyepṛṣṭhye ṣaṣṭhasyāhna paryū ṣviti gaurīvitamekasyāmauṣṇihamokonidhanamekasyām̐ śyāvāśvamekasyāmāndhīgavaṃ tisṛṣu samānamitaraṃ mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca 111
purastātpṛṣṭhyasya kḷpto'tirātraḥ kḷptaṃ caturvi śam̐mahaḥ kḷptaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ kḷptaṃ prathamamaharabhiplavasya yathā madhyepṛṣṭhye dvitīyasyāhna stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti bṛhatī samānamitaraṃ trivṛtkḷptaṃ tṛtīyamaharyathā madhyepṛṣṭhye caturthasyāhna stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti bṛhatī samānamitaraṃ trivṛtkḷptaṃ pañcamamaharyathā madhyepṛṣṭhye ṣaṣṭhasyāhna somasāma cāśu ca bhārgavaṃ pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ gauṅgavaṃ yaudhājayamauśanamantyam̐ rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatra sam̐hite pavasvendramaccheti saphākṣāre pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ gautamaṃ padanidhanaṃ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ kāvamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma 112
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunaiṣa u sya vṛṣā ratha utte śuṣmāso asthurayā pavasva dhārayā pavamānasya te vayamagniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmaha ityājyāni vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ ca surūpaṃ carṣabhaśca pāvamānaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayeti pṛṣṭhaṃ ca yaśaśca kaulmalabarhiṣāyāsye vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyaṃ bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca mādhucchandasaṃ ca yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatram̐ hāviṣmatamāsitameṣa sya dhārayā sutaḥ pavasva devavītaya iti śaṅkusujñāne ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitaṃ ca bhargaścāsitakrauñce vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthāni 113
davidyutatyā rucaite asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratameṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyāpaghnanpavate mṛdha iṣe pavasva dhārayā gnināgniḥ samidhyata ityājyānyuccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca saumitraṃ caiṭataṃ cābhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgaṃ ca gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ cāṣkāraṇidhanaṃ ca kāṇvaṃ tisro vāca ityaṅgirasām̐ saṃkrośo'ntyo rathaṃtaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca mahāvaiṣṭambhaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca tisro vāca udīrata iti gāyatraṃ pāṣṭhauhamaiḍam̐ saindhukṣitam̐ sve uṣṇikkakubhau sutāso madhumattamā iti gaurīvitaṃ ca trīṇi ca tvāṣṭrīsāmānyaiḍaṃ svāraṃ triṇidhanaṃ pavitraṃ ta ityariṣṭamantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthāni 114
babhrave nu svatavasa iti ṣaḍṛcam̐ satrāsāhīyaṃ māṇḍavaṃ mārgīyavam̐ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ tānyuddharatyatha yad eva pūrvasya chandomadaśāhasya caturthamahastadetattṛcakḷptaṃ trim̐śaṃ kḷptaṃ pañcamamaharyathā madhyepṛṣṭhye ṣaṣṭhasyāhno 'yā vītī pari sraveti purastātparyāsasya tṛcamaukṣṇorandhre antarārkapuṣpaṃ yacchandasyaṃ payaścaturthāni vaidanvatāni gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhyatha yadeva pṛṣṭhyastomasya ṣaṣṭhamahastadetattṛcakḷptam̐ ṣaṭtrim̐śam 115
ye somāsaḥ parāvati pra svānāso rathā ivāsṛgramindavaḥ pathā pra yadgāvo na bhūrṇaya āśurarṣa bṛhanmate yavaṃyavaṃ no andhasā te dakṣaṃ mayobhuvamṛtāvānaṃ vaiśvānaramiti hoturājyam̐ svānyuttarāṇi svā gāyatrī tasyāṃ gauṣuktaśākale punānaḥ soma dhārayeti somasāma ca kaṇvarathaṃtaraṃ ca paurumadgaṃ ca gautamaṃ ca vāśaṃ ca gauṅgavaṃ ca dvinidhanaṃ cāyāsyaṃ pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa iti vārāhamantyam̐ svāni pṛṣṭhāni yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārko jarābodhīyaṃ ca dakṣaṇidhanaṃ ca sve uṣṇikkakubhau purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca kārtayaśaṃ ca tvāṣṭrīsāmanī svāraṃ cākāraṇidhanaṃ cātreyaṃ ca gautamaṃ ca dvipadāsu sauhaviṣaṃ pro ayāsīdityaiḍaṃ yajñasārathyantyaṃ yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma svānyukthānyatha ye eva chandomavato daśarātrasya catuścatvārim̐śāṣṭācatvārim̐śe ahanī te ete ubhayorgaurīvitamanuṣṭubhyatha yadeva pūrvasya chandomadaśāhasya daśamamahastadatra daśamammahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca 116
vidhṛtibhiḥ kḷptaṃ dṛtivātavatorāyanamā triṇavebhyastrivṛdbhya ā uddharatyukthāni pañcadaśebhyaḥ ṣoḍaśinam̐ saptadaśebhyo vārkajaṃbhodvam̐śīye ekavim̐śebhyo'rkānutsedhaniṣedhāvudvam̐śīyaṃ tānyuddharati caturthaṃ tṛcaṃ pra svānāso rathā iveti purastātparyāsasya navarcamāmahīyavāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyam̐ rauravayaudhājayābhyāṃ pūrve dvihiṃkāramaidhātithe gaurīvitāduttare śuddhāśuddhīyaśyāvāśve samānamitarametasyaiva rāthaṃtareṇaikavim̐śenāśurarṣa bṛhanmata iti purastātparyāsasya ṣaḍṛcam̐ hāviṣmatāduttaramājigaāyāsyābhyāṃ pūrve samantadairghaśravase gaurīvitāduttare madhuścunnidhanāsite samānamitarametasyaiva bārhatenaikavim̐śenaitābhyāmeva viparyāsaṃ māsamāharati caturthaṃ tṛcamubhau paryāsāvāśubhārgavāduttaraṃ mārgīyavaṃ dvaigataṃ madhye bārhatānām̐ sam̐hitāduttare kākṣīvatabhāse samānamitarametasyaiva rāthaṃtareṇa triṇavenāpaghnanpavate mṛdho 'yā pavasva dhārayeti purastātparyāsasya tṛce svārātsauparṇāduttaram̐ harīśrinidhanamarkapuṣpaṃ madhye bārhatānāṃ maukṣāduttare śāṃmadaṃ ca dāvasunidhanaṃ ca samānamitarametasyaiva bārhatena triṇavenaitābhyāmeva viparyāsamekonatrim̐śatamahāni mahāvrataṃ viśuvānathaita eva māsā āvṛttāstairevāvṛttairuttarānṣaṇmāsām̐steṣāmekānnatrim̐śi prathamo 1171
dṛtivātavatorayanena kḷptaṃ kuṇḍapāyināmayanamā daśarātrātprajñāto daśarātraḥ prajñātaṃ vrataṃ prajñāto'tirātro 1172

ekādaśo'dhyāyaḥ

prathamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
atirātraścaturviṃśaṃ prāyaṇīyamahaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaścatvārastrivṛto'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahāḥ sa māsaḥ sa dvitīyaḥ sa tṛtīyaḥ sa caturthaḥ sa pañcamastrayastrivṛto'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahāḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahastrivṛd bṛhaspatistomastrayaḥ svarasāmāno divākīrtyamahastrayaḥ svarasāmānaḥ pañcadaśa indrastoma ukthyaḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahastrayastriṃśārambhaṇa ekastrivṛdabhiplavaḥ ṣaḍaho dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni vyūḍhā agniṣṭomāstrivṛta udbhicca balamicca dve ahanī catvārastrivṛto'bhiplavāḥ ṣaḍahāḥ pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ sa māsaḥ sa dvitīyaḥ sa tṛtīyaḥ sa caturtho dvau trivṛtābhiplavau ṣaḍahau pṛṣṭhyaḥ ṣaḍahaḥ āyuśca gauśca dve ahanī chandomadaśāhaścaturviṃśaṃ prathamamahardve aṣṭāviṃśe triṃśaṃ dvātriṃśaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśaṃ catvāriṃśaṃ catuścatvāriṃśamaṣṭācatvāriṃśaṃ caturviṃśaṃ mahāvrataṃ cātirātraścāṅgirasāṃ purastāt pṛṣṭhyam ityanuvākenāṅgirasāmayanamuktam | tadeva purastāt pṛṣṭhyamityucyate ||
purastātpṛṣṭhyasya klṛpto'tirātraḥ | klṛptaṃ caturviṃśamahaḥ | klṛptaḥ
pṛṣṭhyaḥ klṛptaṃ prathamamaharabhiplavasya yathā madhyepṛṣṭhye || 1||
atirātraḥ prakṛtivat | caturviṃśaṃ pṛṣṭhyaśca saṃvatsaravat | abhiplavasya prathamamaharmadhyedvipṛṣṭhavat ||1||
satrāṇi -- purastātpṛṣṭhyam 
dvitīyasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam
iti bṛhatī| samānamitaram | trivṛt || 2 ||
iti | parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti mādhucchandasaṃ bhargaḥ aiḍamāyāsyamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | triṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām | bṛhatī | anyatsamaṃ sāṃvatsarikasyābhiplavasya dvitīyenāhnā | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 2||
klṛptaṃ tṛtīyamaharyathā madhyepṛṣṭhye | caturthasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutam iti bṛhatī | samānamitaram | trivṛt ||3||
iti | pavamāno ajījanat punāno akramīdabhi pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam |
uttarayoḥ pavamānayorantyavarjamekarcāḥ | parīto ṣiñcatā sutamiti pṛśnyātharvaṇamābhīśavamiti sāmatṛcaḥ | yaudhājayamadhyāsyāyāmiti bṛhatyāḥ klṛptiḥ| itarat sarvaṃ sāṃvatsarikasyābhiplavasya caturthenāhnā samam | sarvaṃ trivṛt || 3 ||
klṛptaṃ pañcamamaharyathā madhyepṛṣṭhye | ṣaṣṭhasyāhnaḥ stotrīyānurūpau paryāsaḥ | indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatramekasyām | āśvasūktamekasyām vājadāvarya ekasyām | mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍamaukṣṇorandhramekasyām | varuṇasāmaikasyām | triṇidhanamāyāsyamekasyām | samānamitaram | trivṛt || 4||
iti| asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam | mādhyaṃdine gāyatrībṛhatyoḥ yathoktau sāmatṛcau | uttarayoḥ pavamānayorantyameva tṛce| samānamanyat sāṃvatsarikasyābhiplavasya ṣaṣṭhenāhnā ||4||
klṛptamāvṛttebhyo'bhiplavebhyaḥ | āvṛttānāmabhiplavānāṃ prathamasyābhiplavasya prathamasyāhnaḥ indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatramekasyām | tasyāmevāśvasūktam | mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍamaukṣṇorandhramekasyām | tasyāmeva kaṇvarathantaram | varuṇasāmaikasyām | triṇidhanamāyāsyamekasyām ||
samānamitaram || 5 ||
iti | madhyepṛṣṭhavadajāmitvārthamatra bṛhatyāṃ kaṇvarathantaraṃ kalpitam | tadvaśācca stomopapattyarthaṃ gāyatryāṃ vājadāvarya uddhṛtāḥ || 5 ||
uddharatyuttarebhyaḥ kaṇvarathantaram | punarvājadāvaryaḥ
svaṃ lokamāyanti || 6||
uktamabhiplaveṣu jāmyabhāvattebhyaḥ kaṇvarathantaramuddhāryam | tataśca pūrvasmin pakṣasi vājadāvaryaḥ svasthānamevāgacchanti | gāyatramekasyām | āśvasūktamekasyām | vājadāvarya ekasyāmiti || 6 ||
sāṃvatsarike goāyuṣī aviklṛpte || 7 ||
iti | atra hi saṃvatsaravadāyurgauriti kramaḥ | tataścājāmyarthaṃ

iti prathamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ || 2 ||
dvitīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
chandomadaśāhasya prathamamaharāha -- 
asya pratnāmanudyutam ete somā abhi priyam agna āyūṃṣi pavase pavamānasya te kave || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna āyāhi vītaye ityājyāni || 2 ||
pra somāso vipaścita iti gāyatraṃ cāśvaṃ ca somasāma cāśubhārgavam | pra soma devavītaya iti pajraṃ gauṅgavaṃ yaudhājayam auśanam antyam ||3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca ||4||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphākṣāre | pra sunvānāyāndhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ gautamaṃ padanidhanaṃ śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ kāvam antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || 6 ||
sarvaṃ caturviṃśam ||6||
iti dvitīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ || 2||
tṛtīyaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
dvitīyamaharāha -- 
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ pavasvendo vṛṣā suto vṛṣā soma dyumām̐ asi vṛṣā hyasi bhānunaiṣa u sya vṛṣā ratha utte śuṣmāso asthur asyā pavasva dhārayā pavamānasya te vayam ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni ||2|| vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca yauktāśvaṃ ca surūpaṃ carṣabhaśca pavamānaḥ | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti pṛṣṭhaṃ ca yaśaśca kaulmalabarhiṣāyāsye ti | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārtham antyam ||3||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
satrāṇi -- purastātpṛṣṭhyam
pṛṣṭhamādyāyām ||3||

yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ hāviṣmatam āsitam | eṣa sya dhārayā sutaḥ pavasva devavītaye iti śaṅkusujñāne | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhagaḥ iti gaurīvitaṃ ca bhargaś cāsitakrauñce | vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmam antyam || 5 ||

gaurīvitamekasyām | krauñcaṃ ca trayāṇāmuttamam || 5 ||

caturthaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
tṛtīyamaharāha -- 
davidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate taṃ tvā nṛmṇāni bibhratam eṣa dhiyā yātyaṇvyā apaghnan pavate mṛdhe iṣe pavasva dhārayā || 1 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agnināgniḥ samidhyate ityājyāni || 2 || uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ ca kṣullakavaiṣṭambhaṃ ca saumitraṃ caiṭataṃ cābhi somāsa āyava iti paurumadgaṃ ca gautamaṃ cāntarikṣaṃ ca maidhātithaṃ cāṣkāraṇidhanaṃ ca kāṇvam tisro vāca ityaṅgirasāṃ saṃkrośontyaḥ | 3 ||
iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||
āṣkāraṇidhanamadhyāsyāyām || 3||

caturthamaharāha -- 
babhrave nu svatavase iti ṣaḍṛcam | satrāsāhīyaṃ māṇḍavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ śuddhāśudvīyam | tānyudvarati ||1||
atha yadeva pūrvasya chandomadaśāhasya caturthamahastadetat tricaklṛptaṃ triṃśam || 2||
iti | pūrvasya hi caturthaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśam | tatrāsya triṃśatvasiddhyarthaṃ bahiṣpavamānāt babhrave nu svatavase iti ṣaḍṛcam madhyaṃdināt satrāsāhīyamāṇḍave ārbhavānmārgīyavaśuddhāśuddhīye evoddharet | trīṇyāvartīni | śeṣaṃ samānam || 2||
klṛptaṃ pañcamamaharyathā madhyepṛṣṭhye || 3 ||
ṣaṣṭhasyāhno'yāvītī pari sraveti purastāt paryāsasya tṛcam | aukṣṇorandhre antarārkapuppaṃ yacchandasyam | payaścaturthāni vaidanvatāni | gaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi || 4 ||
atha yadeva pṛṣṭhyastomasya ṣaṣṭhamahastadetat tṛcaklṛptam | ṣaṭtriṃśam || 5 ||
iti | asṛkṣata pra vājinaḥ etamu tyaṃ daśa kṣipaḥ pavasva devavīrati sanā ca soma jeṣi ca taratsanmadī dhāvati ayāvīti parisrava ete somā asṛkṣateti bahiṣpavamānam ||
yamagne pṛtsu martya prati vāṃ sūra udite bhindhi viśvā apa dviṣo yajñasya hi stha ṛtvijetyājyāni ||
indrāyendo marutvata iti gāyatraṃ ceṣovṛdhīyaṃ ca gāyatrī krauñcaṃ ca vājadāvaryaśca dārḍhacyutaṃ ca | mṛjyamānaḥ suhastyetyaiḍaṃ caukṣṇoradhra
satrāṇi -- purastātpṛṣṭhyam
muttaraṃ vārkapuṣpaṃ svāramaukṣṇorandhraṃ vājajicca varuṇasāma cāṅgirasāṃ goṣṭho'yā pavā pavasvainā vasūni itīhavadvāsiṣṭhamantyam ||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ carṣabhaśca raivataśyenaśca pṛṣṭhāni ||
parisvāno giriṣṭhā iti gāyatraṃ ca trīṇi ca vaidanvatāni payaśca | sasunve yo vasūnāṃ taṃ vassakhāyo madāyeti dīrgha kārṇaśravasaṃ ca | somāḥ pavanta indava iti gaurīvitaṃ ca madhuścunnidhanaṃ ca krauñce vāṅnidhanaiḍe | jyotiryajñasyeti marutāṃ dhenu antyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | gūrdaṃ bhadramudvaṃśaputra ityukthāni | sarvaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśam || 5 ||
iti pañcamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||5||
ṣaṣṭhaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
saptamamaharāha -- 
ye somāsaḥ parāvati pra svānāso rathā ivāsṛgramindavaḥ pathā prayadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ āśurarṣa bṛhanmate yavaṃ yavaṃ no andhasā ā taṃ dakṣaṃ mayobhuvam ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
ṛtāvanaṃ vaiśvānaram hoturājyam | svānyuttarāṇi ||2|| sāptamikāni ||2||
svā gāyatrī | tasyāṃ gauṣūktaśākale ||
vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca saṃtani caiḍaṃ sauparṇaṃ rohitakūlīyaṃ ca goṣūktaṃ ca śākalaṃ ca ||
punānaḥ soma dhārayeti somasāma ca kaṇvarathantaraṃ ca paurumadgaṃ ca gautamaṃ ca vāśaṃ ca gauṅgavaṃ ca dvinidhanaṃ cāyāsyaṃ pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇa iti vārāham antyam || 3 ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
somasāmaikasyām ||3||
svāni pṛṣṭhāni | 4||
satrāṇi -- purastātpṛṣṭhyam
sāptamikāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ cāgneścārko jarābodhīyaṃ ca dakṣaṇidhanaṃ ca | sve uṣṇikkakubhau || sāptamikyau ||
purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitaṃ ca kārtayaśaṃ ca tvāṣṭrī sāmanī svāraṃ cākāraṇidhanaṃ cātreyaṃ ca gautamaṃ ca dvipadāsu sauhaviṣam | pro ayāsīd ityaiḍaṃ yajñasārathi antyam || 5 ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
gaurīvitamekasyām | svāraṃ tvāṣṭrīsāma dvayoruttaram | pañcame saptame vāhni chandomadaśāhasyetyupagranthavacanāt || 5 ||
yajñāyajñoyamagniṣṭomasāma |
svānyukthāni ||6||
sāptamikāni | sarvaṃ catvāriṃśam || 6||
aṣṭamanavame hi kalpayati -- 
atha ya eva chandomavato daśarātrasya catuścatvāriṃśāṣṭacatvāriṃśe ahanī te ete | ubhayorgaurīvitamanuṣṭubhi ||7||
iti daśamamaharāha -- 
atha yadeva pūrvasya chandomadaśāhasya daśamamahastadatra daśamam ||
iti ||7||
mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca 8||
iti | aṅgirasāmayanaṃ samāptam 8 ||
iti ṣaṣṭhaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||6||
saptamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
dṛtivātavatorayanam
atirātrastribṛtā māsaṃ pañcadaśena māsaṃ saptadaśena māsamekaviṃśena māsaṃ triṇavena māsaṃ trayastriṃśena māsaṃ mahāvrataṃ trayastriṃśena māsaṃ triṇavena māsamekavim̐śena māsam̐saptadaśena māsaṃ pañcadaśena māsaṃ trivṛtā māsamatirātraḥ iti dṛtivātavatorayanamuktam |
vidhṛtibhiḥ klṛptaṃ dṛtivātavatorayanamā triṇavebhyaḥ ||1||
tatra viśeṣamāha
trivṛdbhya uddharatyukthāni| pañcadaśebhyaḥ ṣoḍaśinam | saptadaśebhyo vārkajambhodvam̐śīye | ekavim̐śebhyo'rkānutsedhaniṣedhau udvam̐śīyantāni | uddharati caturthaṃ tṛcam ||2||
satrāṇi -- purastātpṛṣṭhyam
iti | trivṛtaḥ sarve'gniṣṭomāḥ | pañcadaśa ukthyāḥ | nātra daśame ṣoḍaśī| saptadaśānāṃ prathame'hani vārkajambham | dvādaśe'hanyāṣṭādaṃṣṭramevokathyāntam | nodvaṃśīyam | ekaviṃśānāṃ navame'hani saṃhitameva | daśame'hani maukṣameva | na svāśirāmarkaḥ | ekādaśe'hani kāvameva | na dīrghatamaso'rkaḥ | dvādaśe'hani āyāsyayormadhye dairghaśravasam | notsedhaniṣedhayoḥ | tasyaivāhna āṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | nodvaṃśīyāntāni | tatra sarve māsā rāthantarabārhatābhyāṃ vyatyāsāḥ kāryāḥ || 2|| triṇave māse prathamamaharāha -- 
pra svānāso rathā iveti purastāt paryāsatya navarcamāmahīyavāduttaraṃ jarābodhīyam | rauravayodhājayābhyāṃ pūrve | dvihiṃkāramaidhātithe gaurīvitāduttare | śuddhāśuddhīyaśyāvāśve | samānamitaram etesyaiva rāthantareṇaikaviṃśena ||3||
iti | bahippavamāne yaścaturthatṛco rājā bhedhābhirīyate iti tasyoddhāraḥ | rauravayaudhājayābhyāmiti dvayorupādānaṃ tayornairantaryapratipādānārtham | ekaviṃśe hi tayormadhye maidhātithaṃ vihitam | iha tu dve api te nirantare kṛtvā tābhyāṃ dvihiṃkāramaidhātithe kārye ityarthaḥ| tayoḥ klṛptiḥ -- upāsmai gāyatā narodavidyutatyā rucā ete asṛgramindavo babhrave nu svatavase pra svānāso rathā iva pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam || 3||
agna ā yāhi vītaya ityājyāni || 4 ||
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ cāśubhārgavam | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca maidhātithaṃ ca rauravaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam ||
rathantaraṃ ca vāmadevyaṃ ca naudhasaṃ ca kāleyamiti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite | pavasvendramaccheti saphapauṣkale | purojitī vo andhasa 
iti gaurīvitaṃ ca śuddhāśuddhīyaṃ ca śyāvāśvāndhīgave kāvamantyam ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | pramaṃhiṣṭhīyaṃ hārivarṇaṃ tairaścya mityukthāni | sarvaṃ triṇavam ||4||
dvitīyamaharāha -- 
āśurarṣa bṛhanmate iti purastāt paryāsasya ṣaḍṛcam | hāviṣmatāduttaramājigam | āyāsyābhyāṃ pūrve samantadairghaśravase | gaurīvitāduttare madhuścunnidhanāsite | samānamitaram etasyaiva bārhatenaikaviṃśena ||5||
iti| atrājigamāyasyābhyāmiti nairantaryeṇa nirdeśāt tayormadhye dairghaśravasamuddhṛtya tābhyāṃ pūrve samantadairghaśravase kārye | tatraivaṃ
satrāṇi -- dṛtivātavatorayanam
klṛptiḥ -- pavasva vācaḥpavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasi prayadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ aśurarṣa bṛhanmate pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni ||
vṛṣā pavastra dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ cājigaṃ ca sauparṇaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ cāyāsye | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyam |
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ceti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe | tvaṃ hyaṅga daivya pavasva devavītaye iti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitaṃ ca madhuścunnidhanaṃ cāsitaṃ ca krauñcaṃ ca | vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavata iti yāmamantyam || . yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma | sākamaśvaṃ saubharamāṣṭādaṃṣṭramityukthāni | sarvaṃ triṇavam ||
etābhyāmeva viparyāsaṃ māsam || 5 ||
iti | uktābhyāṃ rāthantarabārhatābhyāṃ triṇavābhyāṃ vyatyastābhyāṃ māsaṃ pūrayedityarthaḥ || trayastriṃśaṃ māsamāha -- 
āharati caturthaṃ tṛcam | ubhau paryāsau | āśubhārgavāduttaraṃ mārgīyavam dvaigataṃ madhye bārhatānām | saṃhitāduttare kākṣīvatabhāse |
samānamitarametasyaiva rāthantareṇa triṇavena ||6||
iti | upāsmai gāyatā narodavidyutatyā rucāḥ ete
asṛgramindavo rājā medhābhirīyate babhrave na svatavase pra svānāso rathā iva pavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna āyāhi vītaya ityājyāni ||
uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatraṃ cāmahīyavaṃ ca jarābodhīyaṃ cāśubhārgavaṃ mārgīyavaṃ ca| punānaḥ soma dhārayeti dvihiṃkāraṃ ca
maidhātithaṃ ca dvaigataṃ ca rauravaṃ ca yaudhājayaṃ cauśanamantyam iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatrasaṃhite . kākṣīvataṃ ca bhāsaṃ
satrāṇi -- dṛtivātavatorayanam
cetyārbhavī gāyatrī | itarat sarvamasyaiva rāthantareṇa triṇavena samam | sarvaṃ trayastriṃśam | ityekamahaḥ || 6||
athāparam -- 
apaghnan pavate mṛdho'yā pavasva dhārayeti purastāt paryāsasya tṛce | svārāt sauparṇā duttaraṃ hariśrīnidhanamarkapuṣpaṃ madhye bārhatānām | maukṣāduttare śāmmadaṃ ca dāvasunidhanaṃ ca || samānamitarametasyaiva bārhatena triṇavena ||7||
iti | pavasva vācaḥpavasvendovṛṣā somavṛṣā hyasi prayadgāvo na bhūrṇayaḥ āśurarṣa bṛhanmate apaghnan pavate mṛdho'yā pavasva dhārayāpavamānasya te kave iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agni dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni ||
vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ cājigaṃ ca svāraṃ ca sauparṇaṃ hariśrīnidhanaṃ ca | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantaṃ ca dairghaśravasaṃ cārkapuṣpaṃ cāyāsye vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārthamantyamiti mādhyaṃdinaḥ ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatramaukṣe śāmmadaṃ ca dāvasunidhanaṃ ceti ārbhavīyā gāyatrī | samānamitarametasyaiva bārhatena triṇavena| sarvaṃ trayastriṃśam ||7||
etābhyāmeva viparyāsam ekonatriṃśatamahāni || 8 ||
iti ṣaṇmāsā uktāḥ ||8||
mahāvrataṃ viṣuvān || 9||
viṣuvatsthāne mahāvratamityarthaḥ ||9||
athaita eva māsā āvṛttāḥ| tairāvṛttairuttarān ṣaṇmāsāṃsteṣāmekānnatriṃśī prathamaḥ || 10||
iti | ekānnatriṃśadahāni parimāṇamasyetyekānnatriṃśī māsaḥ | śacśanorḍiniḥ iti ḍiniḥ pratyayaḥ| atra ṣaṣṭhasaptamayormāsayorekānnatriṃśatkathanamatirātryābhyāṃ saha sāṃvatsarikyaṃ mā bhūditi | sūtre tu tayorapi triṃśatvaṃ vikalpenoktam | dṛtivātavatorayane dvāvekonau māsau sunuyuḥ trivṛtāviti gautamastrayastriṃśāviti dhānaṃjayyaḥ ubhau vā triṃśinau syātāṃ yathaitat brāhmaṇam iti || 10 ||
iti dṛtivātavatorayanaṃ samāptam ||
kuṇḍapāyināmayanam
māsaṃ dīkṣitā bhavanti | te māsi somaṃ krīṇanti | teṣāṃ dvādaśopasadaḥ upasadbhiścaritvā somamupanahya māsamagnihotraṃ juhvati | māsaṃ darśapūrṇābhyāṃ yajante | māsaṃ vaiśvadevena | māsaṃ varuṇapraghāsaiḥ |
satrāṇi -- kuṇḍapāyināmayanam
māsaṃ sākamedhaiḥ | māsaṃ śunāsīryeṇa trivṛtā| māsaṃ pañcadaśena | māsaṃ saptadaśena | māsamekaviṃśena| māsaṃ triṇavena | māsamaṣṭādaśatrayastriṃśānyahāni dvādaśāhasya daśāhāni | mahāvrataṃ cātirātraśca iti kuṇḍapāyināmayanam | agnihotrādimāsāḥ ṣaṭ | sutyāmāsāḥ ṣaḍiti saṃvatsarasatramidam | paurṇamāsīprabhṛtayo dīkṣāḥ || kauṇḍapāyinasyetyādi sūtram | tasyopasadbhyo'nantaramupavasathaṃ karma kṛtvātirātramupeyuḥ| satrāntareṣu tathā darśanāditi śāṇḍilyamatam | tatra ca dvādaśāhavat subrahmaṇyā kartavyā | agnihotramāsādiṣvapi pratyahamaharādyantayoḥ subrahmaṇyāhvānaṃ kartavyam | kauṇḍapāyinasārasvatayoriṣṭyayaneṣu sutyāvacchāṇḍilyastathāgnihotramāsa iti vacanāt | tatra ca dvādaśāhaparyanteṣu ahassu sutyāmāgacchetyeva sutyādeśaḥ| na tvetāvadahe sutyāmiti | tata ūrdhvaṃ dvādaśāhavat | taduktaṃ -- kauṇḍapāyinatāpaścitayordvādaśāhe sutyeti viditvā parimāṇāhvānaṃ dhānaṃjayyaḥ sutyāmāgacchetāṃtarāḥ iti | sutyānāṃ tu purastādupavasathaṃ karma kṛtvā syādatirātraścaivaṃ cānyadarśanāditi gautamamatam | tatra śaunāsīryāṣṭādaśaparyantāḥ sarvā eva subrahmaṇyāḥ sutyāmāgaccheti vai kartavyāḥ| brāhmaṇe'nuktatvādatirātro na syāditi dhānaṃjayyamatam | anyattu sūtre evāvadhāryatām ||1||
stotraklṛptimāha -- 
dṛtivātavatorayanena klṛptam | kuṇḍapāyināmayanamā daśarātrāt ||1||
iti| trivṛtā māsaṃ pañcadaśena | māsaṃ saptadaśena | māsamekaviṃśena | māsaṃ triṇavena | māsamaṣṭādaśatrayastriṃśānyahānītyantaṃ klṛptam || 1 ||
prajñāto daśarātraḥ ||2||
dvādaśāhīyaḥ || 2||
prajñātaṃ vratam ||3||
sāṃvatsarikam ||3||
prajñāto'tirātraḥ ||4||
jyotiṣṭomena ||4||
iti kuṇḍapāyināmayanaṃ samāptam ||
tapaścitāmayanam
savatsaraṃ dīkṣitā bhavanti | saṃvatsaramupasadbhiścaranti | saṃvatsaraṃ prasūto bhavati ityanuvākena tapaścitāmayanamuktam | atrāpi vyūḍhavat saṃvatsarasyāntyeṣu dvādaśasūpasaddivaseṣu subrahmaṇyāyāḥ parimāṇāhvānaṃ sutyāmāgacchetītarāḥ | atirātraḥ syādvā na veti pūrvavadvikalpaḥ ||
gavāmayanena klṛptaṃ tapaścitāmayanaṃ jyotiṣṭomena vāviklṛptena | 1 ||
iti | atrāviklṛpteneti gaurīvitasvaranivṛttyartham | tathā ca sūtroktān tāpaścitāmayanavicārānadhikṛtya nidānam -- teṣāṃ
yānyekāhairvartante'gaurīvitāni tāni | yāni nānāhobhiḥ sagaurīvitāni tāni | nānāhaḥsaṃtānārthaṃ gaurīvitamiti | saṃvatsarasatrāṇi ṣaḍuktāni ||1||
iti tapaścitāmayanaṃ samāptam ||
satrāṇi -- agneḥ sahasrasāvyam
dvādaśasaṃvatsaraṃ, ṣaṭtriṃśatsaṃvatsaraṃ śatasaṃvatsaram
trayastrivṛtaḥ saṃvatsarāstrayaḥ saptadaśāstrayaḥ ekaviṃśāḥ ityanuvākena prajāpatervātaśasaṃvatsaramuktam | nava trivṛtaḥ saṃvatsarāḥ nava pañcadaśāḥ navaikaviṃśā iti śāktyānāṃ ṣaṭtriṃśatsaṃvatsaram | pañcaviṃśatistrivṛtaḥ saṃvattarāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ pañcadaśāḥ pañcaviṃśatiḥ saptadaśāḥ pañcaviṃśaterekaviṃśāḥ iti sādhyānāṃ śatasaṃvatsaram ||
dṛtivātavatorayanena klṛptaṃ prajāpaterdvādaśasaṃvatsaraṃ ṣaṭtriṃśatsaṃvatsaraṃ śatasaṃvatsaram || 1||
tatrāgniṣṭomāstrivṛta ukthyāḥ | pañcadaśādayaḥ atirātraviṣūvataśvāvacanānna syuriti śāṇḍilyamatam | itaratsatranyāyenātirātrāvabhitaḥ syātām | madhye viṣūvān | bṛhatpṛṣṭho rathantaramadhyaṃdinaḥ | tasya ca paurvapakṣikaḥ pañcadaśastomaḥ auttarapakṣikaḥ saptadaśaśca vyatyāsaṃ syātāmiti dhānaṃjayyamatam | vikalpāntarāṇi tu sūtra eva draṣṭavyāni || 1||
iti dvādaśasaṃvatsaraṣaṭtriṃśatsaṃvatsaraśatasaṃvatsarāṇi samāptāni ||
agneḥ sahasrasāvyam
atirātraḥ sahasramahānyatirātraḥ ityanuvākena sahasrasāvyamuktam ||
jyotiṣṭomenāviklṛptena klṛptamagneḥ sahasrasāvyam gaurīvitasvareṇa vā ||1||
atra sūtram -- sahasrasāvye sahātirātrābhyāṃ sahasramahānītyācāryāḥ sahasrasāvyam iti hyāhāntareṇeti śaucivṛkṣiḥ sahasramahānītyantareṇātirātrāvāheti || 1||
ityagneḥ sahasrasāvyaṃ samāptam ||
iti saptamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||
aṣṭamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
prathamaṃ sārasvatamayanam
sarasvatyā vinaśane dīkṣante ityanuvākena prathamaṃ sārasvatamuktam | tasya sūtram -- dakṣiṇe tīre sarasvatyā vinaśanasya dīkṣeran sārasvatāyeti ||
prathamasya sārasvatasya ṣoḍaśimantāvatirātrau || 1 ||
iti | dvivacanaṃ vakṣyamāṇodayanīyātirātrābhiprāyam ||
anubrāhmaṇaṃ klṛptamiṣṭyayanam ||
iti | saṃsthite'tirātre sānnāyyena yajante ityuktatvāt te tamāpūryamāṇamamāvāsyena yanti | teṣāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ goṣṭomastomo bhavati | ukthyo bṛhatsāmā | saṃsthite goṣṭome paurṇamāsaṃ nirvapante | te tam apakṣīyamāṇaṃ paurṇamāsena yānti | teṣāmamāvāsyāyām
satrāṇi -- prathamaṃ sārasvatamayanam 
āyuṣṭomastomo bhavati | ukthyo rathantarasāmā iti brāhmaṇena pūrvapakṣāparapakṣayoryathākramaṃ darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmayanaṃ klṛptam | tataḥ pūrṇamāsāmāvāsyābhyāṃ yathākramaṃ goāyuṣī vihite ||2||
tayoḥ klṛptimāha -- 
aikāhike goāyuṣī aviklṛpte ||3||
iti | atha satrasyotthāne catvāri parvāṇi śrūyante | śataṃ gāvaḥ sahasraṃ saṃpeduḥ | śate goṣu ṛṣabhamapyṛjanti | tā yadā sahasraṃ saṃpadyante'thotthānam | yadā sarvajyāniṃ jīyante'thotthānaṃ yadā gṛhapatirmriyate'thotthānam | yadā plakṣaṃ prasravaṇamāgacchantyathotthānam iti ||
tatra śruto'pyudayanīyātirātraḥ satranyāyena kartavya ityabhiprāyāt āha -- 
atirātrādutthānam || 4 || iti | atirātraścāyaṃ catuppadamapi sarvāsu viśeṣāśravaṇāt prāyaṇīyātirātra eva | sūtre tu pratiparvamanye'tirātra ityayamapi pakṣa uktaḥ| sa tu tatraiva draṣṭavyaḥ ||
apṛṣṭhaśamanīyametat satram | agnaye kāmāyeṣṭiḥ pṛṣṭhaśamanārthe naiva bhavatīti atra prāyaṇīyā sutyāmāgacchetītarāḥ| ūrdhvaṃ cātirātrāt yāvat subrahmaṇyāhvānaṃ dvādaśāhavat tata ūrdhvamiṣṭityā(tvā)daharādyantayoḥ subrahmaṇyāhvānam | tatra sutyāyāḥ prāk sutyāmāgacchetyeva sutyādeśaḥ | taduktam -- kauṇḍapāyinasārasvatayoriṣṭyayaneṣu sutyāvacchāṇḍilya iti | sutyāmāgacchetītarāḥ| ūrdhvaṃ cātirātrāt prathamasya sārasvatasya prāk śvaḥsutyāyā iti ca || 4 ||
iti (mitrāvaruṇayoḥ) prathamaṃ sārasvatamayanaṃ samāptam ||
dvitīyaṃ (indrāgnyoḥ) sārasvatamayanam
atirātrastrivṛtpañcadaśamindrāgnyorayanaṃ goāyuṣī indrakukṣī atirātraḥ iti dvitīyaṃ sārasvatamuktam | tasya sūtrametenaivottare vyākhyātāḥ ityādi | atra kalpaḥ -- 
dvitīyasya sārasvatasya ṣoḍaśimantāvatirātrau | dṛtivātavatorayanena klṛpte trivṛtpaccadaśe | agniṣṭomastrivṛdukthyaṃ pañcadaśaṃ rathantaraṃ trivṛd bārhataṃ pañcadaśam | aikāhike goāyuṣī gaurīvitasvare || 1||
iti | pūrvatra darśapūrṇamāsābhyāmivātra yathoktābhyāṃ trivṛtpañcadaśābhyāmahobhyāṃ pakṣadvayamapi vyatyāsam | na tu pūrvapakṣe| trivṛtāparapakṣe pañcadaśeneti | taduktam -- sarvatra tveva trivṛtpañcadaśe vyatyāsaṃ syātāmiti | parvaṇostu goāyuṣī
satrāṇi -- dvitīyaṃ sārasvatamayanam
pūrvavat | uktaṃ ca parvaṇorindrakukṣī iti | atra ca trivṛtpañcadaśābhyāmuktena prakāreṇāyane kriyamāṇe tithivṛddhikṣayābhāve caturdaśyāṃ pañcadaśamaharupetaṃ bhavatītyanupetena trivṛtaivottaraḥ pakṣaḥ upakramyate | vṛddhikṣayasadbhāve caturdaśyāṃ trivṛdaharupetamityanupetena pañcadaśena uttaraḥ pakṣaḥ upakramyate | taduktamya etayoryasminnanupate parvāgacchettenottaraṃ pakṣamupakramateti ||1|| atra codayati -- 
atirātrādrathantarād rāthantaraṃ trivṛdaharupayanti ||2||
iti| tataśca jāmiprasaṅga iti śeṣaḥ || 2 ||
pariharati -- 
rāthantarasyāhnaḥ pratnavatī bṛhatī tenājāmi || 3 ||
iti | trivṛto hi rāthantarasyāhnaḥ punānaḥ soma dhārayati bṛhatī | tatra ca pratnaṃ sadhasthamāsad iti pratnaśabdo vidyata ityajāmitvaṃ siddhamityarthaḥ ||3||
atha tithervṛddhikṣayabhāvena pūrvapakṣe caturdaśyāṃ pañcadaśaṃ bārhatamaharupeyate paurṇamāsyāṃ ca bārhato goṣṭomaḥ | tadā tayoḥ saṃnipāto jāmītyata āha -- 
yatra bārhatādahno gāmupayanti goryathā yamātirātrāsvevaṃ bṛhatī ||4||
iti | yamātirātrāsu goṣṭomasya dairghaśravasasya loke kaṇvarathantaraṃ bārhadukthasya dairghaśravasamiti hyuktam | yadā tu vṛddhikṣayasadbhāvena caturdaśyāṃ trivṛdaharupayanti tadā paurṇamāsyāmupetāt bārhatāt goṣṭomāt paramaparapakṣapratipaditi bārhataṃ pañcadaśamupayantīti jāmitvaṃ syāttat pariharati -- 
yatra gaurbārhatamaharupayanti bārhatasyāhnaḥ punānaḥ somadhārayeti aiḍamāyāsyamekasyām kaṇvarathantaramekasyām dairghaśravasamekakasyām triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu || 5 ||
iti ||
aya yadāparapakṣe mate(tithe?)rvṛddhikṣayābhyāṃ caturdaśyāṃ rathantaraṃ trivṛdupayanti amāvāsyāyāṃ ca rāthantaramāyuṣṭomaṃ tadā jāmi syāt ityatyata āha -- 
yatra rāthantarādahna āyurupayanti āyuṣo yathā yamātirātrāsvevaṃ
bṛddhatī || 6||
iti | yamātirātrāsviti hyuktam | āyuṣṭomasya maidhātithasya loke bṛhannidhanaṃ vārkajambhaṃ rauravasya medhātithamiti || 6 ||
yadā tu vṛddhikṣayābhavāccaturdaśyāṃ pañcadaśamevopeyate'syāmāvāsyāyāmupetādrathantarādāyuṣaḥ paraṃ pūrvapakṣapratipadi rāyantaraṃ trivṛdevopeyate iti jāmi syāttatrāha -- 
yatrāyuṣo rāthantaramaharupayanti rathantarasyāhnaḥ pratnavatī bṛhatī tenājāmi ||7||
iti | gatārthametat || 7 ||
satrāṇi -- tṛtīyaṃ sārasvatamayanam
atrāpi pūrvavadutthānamatirātrādanantaraṃ kāryamityāha -- 
atirātrādutthānam || 8||
iti ||8||
iti dvitīyam (indrāgnyoḥ) sārasvatamayanaṃ samāptam ||
tṛtīyaṃ (aryamṇaḥ) sārasvatamayanam
atirātro jyotirāyustryaho viśvajidabhitāvindrakukṣī atirātraḥ iti tṛtīyaṃ sārasvatamuktam ||
tṛtīyasya sārasvatasya ṣoḍaśimantāvatirātrau | aikāhikāstrikadrukā
viśvajidabhijitau ca gaurīvitasvarāḥ || 1 ||
iti | viśvajidabhijitau ca aikāhikau gaurīvitasvarā jyotirādayaḥ | atra trikadrukā darśapūrṇamāsasthānīyāḥ| paurṇamāsyāmabhijidamāvāsyāyāṃ viśvajit | uktaṃ hi -- parvaṇorindrakukṣī iti | paurṇamāsyāmabhijidatirātravikārāṇāmabhyāsenāyane kriyamāṇe teṣāṃ madhye yasminnanupete parvāgacchettenottaraṃ pakṣamupakrameta | taduktam -- etenoktamuttarasya trikadrukeṣviti ||1|
tatra saha vasato jāmiprasaṅgāt krameṇa pariharati -- 
atirātrādrāthantarādrāthantaraṃ jyotirupayanti jyotiṣaḥ pratnavatī bṛhatī tenājāmi ||2|| iti | paṣṭhametat yatra tvāyuṣo rāthantarādrāthantaraṃ jyotirupayanti tadā jāmitvaṃ pūrvameva parihṛtam | yatrāyuṣo rāthantara upayanti jyotiṣaḥ pratnavatī bṛhatī tenājāmīti || 2 ||
atha yadā titheḥ sāmyaṃ tadā caturdaśyāṃ gaurbhavati paurṇamāsyāmabhijit | ubhāvapi bṛhatpṛṣṭhāviti jāmiprasaṅgādāha -- 
yatra gorabhijitamupayanti abhijito yathā yamātirātrāsvevaṃ bṛhatī || 3||
iti | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti abhīvartastisṛṣu | rauravamekasyām | rathantaramekasyām | maidhātithamekasyām | yaudhājayaṃ tisṛṣu iti pūrdaśarātre'bhijito bṛhatyuktā| saiva yamātirātrāsvatidiśyate | atra ca yathā pūrdaśarātre evaṃ bṛhatīti kartavyau satratvena pratyāsattyā | yathā yamātirātrāsvityuktam || 3||
yatrābhijito jyotirupayanti jyotiṣaḥ pratnavatī bṛhatī tenājāmi || 4||
iti | tithivṛddhau vābhijiccaturdaśyāṃ goṣṭome kṛte paurṇamāsyām abhijit | tatra rāthantarāgniṣṭomasāma | tasmācca paramaparapakṣapratipadi rāthantaraṃ jyotirbhavatīti jāmi prathamāyām || 5 ||
yatra vāyurāyuṣo yathā yamātirātrāsvevaṃ bṛhatī ||5||
iti | yatra vāyurityatrābhijita upayantītyanuṣaṅgaḥ | yatra yathābhijita ūrdhvamāyurupayanti tatretyarthaḥ | tithisāmye hi caturdaśyāṃ
satrāṇidārṣadvataturāyaṇe 
goṣṭome kṛte paurṇamāsyāmabhijidrāthantarāgniṣṭomo bhavati jāmiṃ pratyaparapakṣapratipadi ca rāthantara āyuriti jāmitā syāt || 5 ||
yatra jyotiṣo vāyurarāyuṣo vā viśvajitamupayanti viśvajito'sya pratnā
gāyatrī || 6||
iti | yadaikasmin pakṣe vṛddhirityanayoḥ pūrvasmin viṣaye viśvajito | bṛhadagniṣṭomasāma paraṃ bārhate goṣṭome prakriyamāṇe prasaktāṃ jāmitāṃ pariharati -- 
yatra viśvajito gāmupayanti goryathā yamātirātrāsvevaṃ bṛhatī tenājāmi || 7||
atirātrādutthānam || 8||
iti| atirātrāgatānāmatrāpi pūrvavaccatvāryutthānaparvāṇi || 8 ||
iti tṛtīyaṃ (aryamṇaḥ) sārasvatamayanaṃ samāptam ||
dārṣadvatamayanaṃ, turāyaṇaṃ ca
saṃvatsaraṃ brāhmaṇasya gā rakṣet ityanuvākena dārṣadvatamuktam | āgneyo'ṣṭākapālaḥ ityanuvākena tauramuktam |
upāyaklṛpte dārṣadvatataurai ||1||
upāyo brāhmaṇoktānuṣṭhānaprakāraḥ | tenaiva klṛpte naivānyatarayoḥ kalpe niyamo'stītyarthaḥ | te ca sūtre dārṣadvataurayorvrātānīti spaṣṭamevaṃ vyākhyāte || 1 ||
iti dārṣadvatamayanaṃ turāyaṇaṃ ca samāptam ||
iti aṣṭamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ ||
navamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
sarpasatram
atirātrāvabhito'gniṣṭomā madhye sarvo dvādaśī saṃvatsaro dvādaśo viṣuvān ityanuvākena sarpasatramuktam ||
sarpasatrasya ṣoḍaśimantāvatirātrau || 1 ||
agniṣṭomeṣu prathamamāha -- 
januṣaikarccayoḥ saphapauṣkale śyāvāśvasya loka gaurīvitam | samānanitaraṃ virājadvandvena | sarvo daśī || 2 || iti | athaiṣa virāḍityukto virāḍdvandvena sarvo daśīti sarvo'yamagniṣṭomo daśistoma ityarthaḥ || 2|
dvitīyamagniṣṭomamāha -- 
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ utte śuṣmāso asthuḥ pavamānasya te kave | 3 ||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityājyāni ||4||
satrāṇi -- sarpasatraṃ
vṛṣā pavasva dhārayeti gāyatramekasyām | yauktāśvamekasyām | ṛṣabhaḥ pavamāna ekasyām | svāraṃ vā sauparṇam | punānaḥ soma dhārayetyaiḍamāyāsyamekasyām triṇidhanamāyāsyaṃ tisṛṣu vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārtham antyam ||
[iti mādhyaṃdinaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitaṃ ca kāleyaṃ ca ||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
yaste mado vareṇya iti gāyatraṃ ca hāviṣmataṃ ca pavasvendramaccheti satrāsāhīyaśrudhye | ayaṃ pūṣā rayirbhaga iti gaurīvitam ekasyām | krauñcamekasyām | arkapuṣpamekasyāṃ yacchandasyam | vṛṣā matīnāṃ pavate iti yāmam antyam ||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
antyavarjamekarccāḥ ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma || sarvo daśadaśī ||9||
etābhyāmeva viparyāsam̐ ṣaṇmāsān ||10||
upayantīti śeṣaḥ ||10||
teṣāmekānnatrim̐śśyuttamaḥ || 11 ||
anye māsāstriṃśata eva || 11 ||
iti navamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ || 6||
daśamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ
dvādaśo viṣuvānityuktam | viṣuvantamāha -- 
pavasva vāco agriyaḥ upāsmai gāyatā naro davidyutatyā rucā pavamānasya te kave ||1||
iti bahiṣpavamānam ||
agna ā yāhi vītaye iti rāthantaraṃ trīṇi bārhatāni | agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe ityubhayānyājyānīti vā ||2||
pṛṣṭhasthānagatatvena bṛhataḥ prādhānyāt trīṇi bārhatānīti pakṣaḥ | pavamānagatatve'pi prādhānyāt rāthantarasyāpi prādhānyamityabhiprāyeṇobhayānyājyānīti pakṣaḥ ||2||
satrāṇiviṣuvān
uccā te jātamandhasaḥ gāyatram ekasyām | gauṣūktamekasyām | satrāsāhīyamekasyām | punānaḥ soma dhārayeti samantamekasyām saṃsarpamekasyāṃ yattṛtīyam | dairghaśravasamekasyām rathantaraṃ tisṛṣu | vṛṣā śoṇa iti pārtham antyam ||3||
bṛhacca vāmadevyaṃ ca śyaitasyarkṣu saṃsarpaṃ yatprathamam svāsu kāleyam || 4||
iti pṛṣṭhāni ||
svādiṣṭhayā madiṣṭhayeti gāyatraṃ cāśvasūktaṃ ca | pavasvendraccheti saphaśrudhye | purojitī vo andhasa iti gaurīvitamekasyām tasyāmevāndhīgavam saṃsarpaṃ tisṛṣu yad dvitīyam | sūryavatīṣu kāvam antyam ||5||
[ityārbhavaḥ pavamānaḥ ||]
kāvasaṃsarpe tṛcayoḥ| anye ekarcāḥ ||
yajñāyajñīyamagniṣṭomasāma ||6||
sarvo dvādaśaḥ || 7||
uttaraṃ pakṣamāha -- 
athaite eva pūrve ahanī āvṛtte | tābhyāmuttarābhyāmuttarān ṣaṇmāsān | teṣāmekānnatriṃśī prathamaḥ ||8||
iti | ete eveti anantarokte agniṣṭomasaṃsthe ahanītyarthaḥ | atrāpi māsadvayasya ekānnatriṃśatvamatirātrābhyāṃ saha saṃvatsarapūraṇārthamuktam | asmin satre brāhmaṇoktāḥ abhigarāvupottame ahanī mahāvratasthānīye| na tanmātraśravaṇāt gavāmayanameṣā stomasaṃsthā ca vikṛtaṃ sarpasatramiti vācyam | kalpakāravacanaprāmāṇyāt | yadāha -- sarpasatraṃ gavāmayanaṃ stomasaṃsthāvikṛtamityeka iti || 8||
iti sarpasatraṃ samāptam ||
trisaṃvatsaraṃ satram
gavāmayanaṃ prathamaḥ saṃvatsaro'thādityānāmathāṅgirasām iti trisaṃvatsaramuktam ||
trisaṃvatsarasya gavāmayanena klṛptaḥ prathamaḥ saṃvatsaraḥ ādityānāmayanena
dvitīyo'ṅgirasāmayanena tṛtīyaḥ || 1 ||
asya sūtram -- paurṇamāsī prasavaṃ trisaṃvatsaram ityādi| prasavaḥ prathamasutyā || 1 ||
iti trisaṃvatsaraṃ satraṃ samāptam ||
prajāpateḥ sahasrasaṃvatsaraṃ satram
atirātraḥ sahasraṃ trivṛtaḥ saṃvatsarātirātraḥ iti prajāpateḥ sahasraṃ saṃvatsaram ||
satrāṇi -- viśvasṛjāmayanam
bṛhaspatisavenāviklṛptena klṛptaṃ prajāpateḥ sahasrasaṃvatsaraṃ gaurīvitasvareṇa vā ||1||
iti prajāpateḥ sahasrasaṃvatsaraṃ satraṃ samāptam ||
viśvasṛjāmayanam
pañca pañcāśatastrivṛtaḥ saṃvatsarāḥ pañca pañcāśataḥ pañcadaśāḥ| pañca pañcāśataḥ saptadaśāḥ pañca pañcāśataḥ ekaviṃśāḥ iti viśvasṛjāṃ sahasrasaṃvatsaramuktam |
dṛtivātavatorayanena klṛptaṃ viśvasṛjāmayanam || 1 ||
pakṣāntaramāha -- 
jyotiṣṭomenavāviklṛptena | jyotiṣṭomena vā yathāstomaklṛpteneti
yathāstomaklṛptena || 2||
iti | aviklṛpteneti gaurīvitasvaraniṣedhaḥ | yathāstomaklṛpteneti āditaḥ pañcapañcāśati saṃvatsareṣu jyotiṣṭomasya sarvāṇi stotrāṇi trivṛnti | tataḥ pañca pañcāśanti pañcadaśāni tataḥ pañca pañcāśanti pañcadaśāni, tataḥ saptadaśāni tataḥ ekaviṃśāni | evaṃ saṃvatsarān brāhmaṇoktān stomānnātikramya klṛptenetyarthaḥ | tatra jyotiṣṭomeneṣṭyeva dravyeṣvāvāpoddhārābhyāṃ pavamānānāṃ yathāstomaklṛptirupapādyā | yathā trivṛto jyotiṣṭomasya uccā te jātamandhasa iti gāyatram | yathā daśarātre daśamam | tasyaivaiṣā pratipat kāryā | dīkṣite'pyudayanīyātirātre pratipadvikāro na kārya ityāha -- anyatrodayanīyātirātrāt prāyaṇīyodanīyātirātrayoriti | prāyaṇīyodanīyātirātrayoḥ samādheravaśyakartavyatvāditi bhāvaḥ | tathā ca dvādaśāhasya satratvopapādane nidānam -- samānāhīnaprāyaṇīyodanīyorbhavatīti | samānamitaraṃ yasyāhnaḥ udayapratipat kṛtā tena samānamityarthaḥ || 2||
iti viśvasṛjāmayanaṃ samāptam ||
iti daśamaḥ khaṇḍaḥ || 10||
iti śrīvāmānāryasutavaradarājena viracitāyāmārṣeyakalpavyākhyāyāṃ satreṣu tṛtīyo'dhyāyaḥ ādita ekādaśaḥ || 11 ||
ārṣeyakalpaḥ samāptaḥ||
